### [1] [Supercurrent as a bulk probe for topological phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10814)
*Tatiana de Picoli,Ian Wojtowicz,Jukka I. Vayrynen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于超电流响应（supercurrent response）提出区分平庸与拓扑超导相的新探针：在一维 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合纳米线中，强自旋轨道耦合使拓扑相内超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）减半，从而以非单调信号标记体相变，并可通过动电感（kinetic inductance）测量实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分平庸超导相（trivial superconducting phase）与拓扑超导相（topological superconducting phase）是核心且存在争议的实验难题；现有探针多依赖对局域物理敏感的边界信号（boundary signatures）间接推断体相变。

Method: 研究施加磁场并打开拓扑能隙的一维 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合超导纳米线（one-dimensional Rashba spin-orbit-coupled superconducting nanowire），以超流刚度与超电流响应为体相探测量；并构建低能螺旋模型（low-energy helical model）对结果进行解析理解。

Result: 在强自旋轨道耦合下，拓扑相内的超流刚度（进而超电流）被压低为原来的二分之一，作为化学势（chemical potential）的函数呈现非单调特征，标志体相拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 体相拓扑相变可通过动电感（kinetic inductance）测量被探测，为拓扑超导相鉴别提供不依赖边界信号的手段。

Abstract: Distinguishing trivial from topological superconducting phase remains a central and contested experimental challenge, since most existing probes infer the bulk phase transition indirectly, from boundary signatures that can be sensitive to local physics. Here, we propose an alternative that probes the bulk topological phase and is based on supercurrent response. We study a one-dimensional Rashba spin-orbit-coupled superconducting nanowire with an applied magnetic field that opens a topological gap. We show that for strong spin-orbit coupling, the superfluid stiffness, and consequently the supercurrent, is suppressed by a factor of two inside the topological phase, producing a non-monotonic signature of the bulk phase transition as a function of chemical potential. We further use a low-energy helical model to understand this suppression analytically. Our results show that the bulk topological phase transition can be detected through kinetic inductance measurements.

</details>


### [2] [Amplitude mode in Eliashberg superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10826)
*Maxim Dzero,Emil A. Yuzbashyan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在强耦合Migdal–Eliashberg理论中，声子动力学带来的推迟效应（retardation effects）使Higgs模（Higgs mode）脱离由谱隙设定的配对破缺边（pair-breaking edge）$2\Delta_g$，成为一个尖锐的亚能隙（subgap）激发，从而改变实验可观测的振幅振荡衰减行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在BCS理论中，振幅（Higgs）模与配对破缺阈值简并，其频率等于$2\Delta$，因此仅为边缘奇异性（edge singularity）而非真正的集体模；这解释了在$t=0$突然弱微扰后其振荡按$1/\sqrt{t}$代数衰减的现象。本文旨在探究强耦合理论中声子动力学是否能解除这一简并。

Method: 采用Einstein声子谱下的Migdal–Eliashberg理论Eilenberger方程，在实频率轴上直接计算纵向配对磁化率（longitudinal pair susceptibility），以确定Higgs模的频率；并通过开启/关闭质量重整化（mass renormalization）响应来考察其对位移的贡献。

Result: Higgs模脱离由谱隙设定的配对破缺边$2\Delta_g$并成为尖锐的亚能隙激发。质量重整化响应至少贡献位移的五分之二，也可能贡献全部：若将其关闭，束缚效应（binding）降至计算分辨率以下。约百分之一的位移可将振幅通道约四分之一的谱权重从双准粒子连续谱（two-quasiparticle continuum）转移至离散能级。

Conclusion: 实验观测到的并非位移本身，而是其后果：振幅振荡持续存在，而非按$1/\sqrt{t}$衰减。这表明强耦合推迟效应可显著改变Higgs模的光谱特征与可观测行为。

Abstract: In the BCS theory of superconductivity the amplitude (Higgs) mode is degenerate with the pair-breaking threshold: its frequency coincides with $2Δ$, so it is an edge singularity rather than a true collective mode. This is why after a sudden weak perturbation at $t=0$ its oscillations decay algebraically as $1/\sqrt{t}$. We show that the retardation effects due to the phonon dynamics in the strong-coupling theory lift this degeneracy. The Higgs mode detaches from the pair-breaking edge $2Δ_g$, set by the spectral gap, and becomes a sharp subgap excitation. We determine its frequency from the longitudinal pair susceptibility evaluated directly on the real frequency axis, using the Eilenberger equations for Migdal--Eliashberg theory with the Einstein phonon spectrum. The response of the mass renormalization supplies at least two fifths of the displacement, and possibly all of it: with it switched off, the binding falls below the resolution of the calculation. Its consequences, however, are significant because a percent-level shift transfers about a quarter of the spectral weight of the amplitude channel out of the two-quasiparticle continuum into a discrete energy level. What experiment sees is therefore not the displacement itself but its consequence: amplitude oscillations that persist instead of decaying as $1/\sqrt{t}$.

</details>


### [3] [High Efficiency in Large Supercurrent Rectification with Superconducting Coil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11259)
*Takumi Ichikawa,Daisuke Takegami,Gen Nishijima,Shunsuke Kawakami,Tsutomu Nojima,Yuji Tsuchiya,Satoshi Awaji,Fuyuki Ando,Ken-ichi Uchida,Yuya Hattori,Yoshikazu Mizuguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证了一种线圈状超导丝器件（coil-shaped superconducting wire device），通过外磁场（Hext）与线圈自生磁场的补偿效应产生大整流差异，在Sn10-Pb90焊料丝上实现了79%–84%的高整流效率与125–140 A的大临界电流差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管（superconducting diodes, SDs）需同时具备高整流效率（rectification efficiency, η）与大临界电流差（ΔIc）才能推进超导功率电子（superconducting power electronics）的实际应用，而现有方案难以二者兼得，故需新的器件构型。

Method: 提出线圈状超导丝器件概念，利用外加磁场（Hext）与线圈自身产生磁场之间的补偿（compensation）来诱导大超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect, SDE）；基于Sn10-Pb90焊料丝制备线圈状SD，并开展输运临界电流（Ic）测量。

Result: 在Hext = 500 Oe下（线圈#2）获得η = 79%、ΔIc = 140 A；在Hext = 530 Oe下（线圈#3）获得η = 84%、ΔIc = 125 A，证实大超电流下仍具高整流性能。

Conclusion: 线圈状超导二极管（coil-shaped SDs）所实现的大电流高效率超导二极管效应，对超导功率电子这一潜在领域的发展具有重要价值。

Abstract: Superconducting diodes (SDs) are devices that rectify supercurrent through the difference in the superconducting critical current (Ic) between forward and reverse current directions. For bringing such SDs closer to practical superconducting power electronics applications, a combination of both high rectification efficiency (η) and a large Ic difference (ΔIc) is required. Here, we propose and demonstrate a coil-shaped superconducting wire device concept to induce a large SD effect, arising from the compensation between an external magnetic field (Hext) and the field generated by the coil itself. From transport Ic measurements on coil-shaped SDs based on a Sn10-Pb90 solder wire, we achieved a η of 79% with a ΔIc of 140 A at Hext = 500 Oe (coil #2) and a η of 84% with a ΔIc of 125 A at Hext = 530 Oe (coil #3), confirming high rectification values at large supercurrents. The large-current high-efficiency SDE in the coil-shaped SDs will be highly useful for developing the potential field of supercurrent power electronics.

</details>


### [4] [Strain-engineered long-distance supercurrent in an altermagnetic CrSb based-Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11341)
*Yifan Gao,Jin Yan,Junjie Wei,Yifan Zhang,Zhiyuan Zhou,Tong Zou,Ruiyue Chu,Gui Wang,Kaiwen Shen,Zhiwei Zhang,Fang Chen,Yingfen Wei,Hao Jiang,Xumeng Zhang,Ming Wang,Liyang Liao,Jingli Wang,Yijun Yu,Wu Shi,Qi Yao,Xiaobing Chen,Qihang Liu,Yizheng Wu,Yuhang Li,Xufeng Kou,Cheng Song,Xianzhe Chen,Qi Liu,Xincheng Xie,Ming Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文报道在应变交变磁体（altermagnet）CrSb 外延薄膜中实现长距离 Josephson 超电流输运，横向间隔达 150 nm，超出常规自旋单态相干长度近两个数量级，并伴随磁场诱导的量子干涉图样，证实了本征 Josephson 耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体具有零净磁化与动量依赖自旋劈裂（momentum-dependent spin splitting）的特性，可在无杂散场（stray field）损害的条件下实现非常规超导态，因而成为潜在平台。研究动机在于验证此类材料能否支持长距离无耗散超电流，从而拓展超导自旋电子学（superconducting spintronics）的应用路径。

Method: 采用应变交变磁体 CrSb 外延薄膜（epitaxial thin films）构建基于 CrSb 的 Josephson 结（Josephson junction），并进行横向间隔为 150 nm 的超电流输运测量；同时通过磁场调控观测量子干涉图样以判定耦合性质。

Result: 在 150 nm 横向间隔上观测到无耗散超电流（dissipationless supercurrent），该距离超出常规自旋单态相干长度（spin-singlet coherence length）近两个数量级；并观测到清晰的磁场诱导量子干涉图样（quantum interference pattern），为真实的 Josephson 耦合提供了直接证据。

Conclusion: 结果表明交变磁体可作为在无宏观磁杂散场（macroscopic magnetic stray fields）条件下产生长距离超电流的可行途径，为超导自旋电子学应用开辟了新机会。

Abstract: Altermagnetic materials, characterized by vanishing net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provide a unique platform for realizing unconventional superconducting states without the detrimental stray fields. Here, we demonstrate long-distance Josephson supercurrent transport through epitaxial thin films of the strained altermagnet CrSb. We observe a dissipationless supercurrent across a lateral spacer distance of 150 nm in a CrSb-based Josephson junction, exceeding the conventional spin-singlet coherence length by nearly two orders of magnitude. This supercurrent is further accompanied by a well-defined magnetic-field-induced quantum interference pattern, providing direct evidence for genuine Josephson coupling. Our results reveal altermagnets as a promising route toward generating long-distance supercurrents without macroscopic magnetic stray fields, opening new opportunities for superconducting spintronic applications.

</details>


### [5] [From band reconstruction to Bogoliubov dispersion: How dz2-band enhances iron-based superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11484)
*Jingming Yan,Shendong Su,Guihao Jia,Yucong Peng,Xuanyu Long,Zheng Liu,Pei Ouyang,Qi-Kun Xue,Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope, STM）机械调控铁基超导体晶格，使费米能级（Fermi level）以下的深层 dz2 能带（band）上移并与主导超导能带发生杂化（hybridization），从而增强超导配对强度与超导能隙（superconducting gap）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对铁基超导（iron-based superconductivity）的理解主要建立在费米能级处的电子能带（electronic bands）之上，深层能带（deeper bands）作为潜在影响因素长期被忽视。该工作旨在拓展这一视角，探究深层能带是否可用于增强超导配对强度。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope, STM）对铁基超导体的晶格（crystal lattice）进行机械改变，借助准粒子干涉成像（quasiparticle interference imaging）观测能带杂化及其导致的超导能隙增强。

Result: 观测到费米能级以下深层 dz2 能带相关特征向上移动，并与费米能级处的主要超导能带发生杂化（hybridization）；准粒子干涉成像（quasiparticle interference imaging）给出了该能带杂化及超导能隙（superconducting gap）增强的新证据。

Conclusion: 在深层能带与配对活性能带（pairing-active bands）之间进行关联调控（engineering），是放大超导能隙的有效机制；该工作修正了对与配对相关电子结构的传统认识，并确立了能带杂化（band hybridization）作为优化超导材料的有效途径。

Abstract: Conventional understanding of iron-based superconductivity is usually established based on electronic bands at the Fermi level, leaving deeper bands overlooked as potential factor. Here, we expand this view by demonstrating that a deep-lying dz2 band below the Fermi level can be employed to enhance the superconducting paring strength. By using scanning tunneling microscope to mechanically change the crystal lattice of an iron-based superconductor, we observe an upward shift of the deep-lying dz2 band related feature, leading to hybridization with the primary superconducting bands at the Fermi level. We visualize the novel evidence of this band hybridization and resulting enhanced superconducting gap through quasiparticle interference imaging, providing conclusive evidence that engineering such correlations between deep and pairing-active bands is a potent mechanism for amplifying the superconducting gap. This work refines the conventional view of pairing-relevant electronic structures and establishes band hybridization as an effective way to optimize superconducting materials.

</details>


### [6] [Electric and Heat Transport in One-Dimensional SNS Junctions with Energy-Dependent Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11713)
*Kai Tsutsumi,Shun Uchino*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文基于 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 方程（Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations）的散射方法，研究一维超导-正常金属-超导（SNS）结中相位无关的直流电、热流分量，并比较三类透射谱对非线性输运的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察化学势差与温差共同驱动下 SNS 结的相位无关直流电、热输运行为，并厘清透射谱能量依赖性与多重 Andreev 反射（multiple Andreev reflections）对非线性响应的作用。

Method: 采用基于 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 方程的散射方法（scattering approach），在保留正常区透射能量依赖性的同时纳入多重 Andreev 反射，并对比能量无关型、量子点型与量子点接触（quantum-point-contact）三种透射谱。

Result: 低偏压下，两种电流均呈现与超导能隙及多重 Andreev 反射相关的非线性特征；对温差的响应显著依赖透射谱。小有限偏压下，能量无关型与量子点型的电流对温差依赖较弱，而量子点接触型产生显著非线性响应；在所研究参数范围内，三类透射谱的热流均随温差非线性变化。

Conclusion: 结果凸显透射谱在 SNS 结非线性电、热输运中的关键作用，并为介观费米子超流结（mesoscopic fermionic superfluid junctions）的相关输运研究提供微观参考。

Abstract: We investigate the phase-independent dc components of the electric and heat currents through a one-dimensional superconductor-normal-metal-superconductor (SNS) junction subject to chemical-potential and temperature differences between two superconducting reservoirs. Using a scattering approach based on the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations, we incorporate multiple Andreev reflections while retaining the energy dependence of transmission through the normal region. We compare energy-independent, quantum-dot-type, and quantum-point-contact transmission profiles. At low bias, both currents exhibit nonlinear features associated with the superconducting gap and multiple Andreev reflections. Their responses to a temperature difference depend remarkably on the transmission profile. At a small finite bias, the electric current depends weakly on the temperature difference for the energy-independent and quantum-dot-type profiles, whereas the quantum-point-contact profile produces a pronounced nonlinear response. The heat current depends nonlinearly on the temperature difference for all three profiles over the parameter range investigated. These results highlight the role of the transmission profile in nonlinear electric and heat transport through SNS junctions and provide a microscopic reference for related transport studies in mesoscopic fermionic superfluid junctions.

</details>


### [7] [Pressure-induced double-dome superconductivity in doped kagome metal Cs(V0.86Ta0.14)3Sb5 without charge density wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12257)
*Haochen Sun,Jing Wang,Deng Hu,Zhiwei Wang,Shiyan Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 对无长程电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）序的 Ta 掺杂 Cs(V$_{0.86}$Ta$_{0.14}$)$_3$Sb$_5$ 进行最高 48 GPa 的高压研究，观察到随压力演化的两个超导穹顶（superconducting dome），表明压力诱导的双穹顶超导电性（double-dome superconductivity）是 V 基笼目（kagome）超导体的本征特征，即使长程 CDW 序缺失亦成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V 基笼目超导体 CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ 中电荷密度波（CDW）与超导（superconductivity, SC）存在复杂相互作用，而以 Ta 替代 V 可抑制 CDW 序并增强超导性。为厘清 CDW 序与压力诱导超导行为之间的关系，需要研究无 CDW 序体系在压力下的超导演化。

Method: 采用高压实验手段，对 Ta 掺杂样品 Cs(V$_{0.86}$Ta$_{0.14}$)$_3$Sb$_5$ 施加最高 48 GPa 的压力，并追踪其超导转变温度 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 随压力的变化。该样品具有约 5.29 K 的增强 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 且无 CDW 序。

Result: 低压区表现出与原始 CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ 的“M 型”温度-压力相图不同的非单调演化：$T_\mathrm{c}$ 从常压 5.29 K 略降至 0.33 GPa 的 4.90 K，随后升至 0.61 GPa 的极大值 5.70 K；继续加压后 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 持续下降至 12.3 GPa 的 0.98 K；更高压力下 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 再度上升，在 47.7 GPa 达 4.46 K。该 $T_\mathrm{c}$ 演化确立了压力下的两个超导穹顶，与 CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ 中的情形相似。

Conclusion: 压力诱导的双穹顶超导电性是 V 基笼目超导体的本征属性，其存在不依赖于长程 CDW 序。该结果有助于理解此类体系中 CDW 序与超导电性之间的关联。

Abstract: The vanadium-based kagome superconductor CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ exhibits an intricate interplay between charge density wave (CDW) and superconductivity (SC). Substituting Ta for V will suppress the CDW order and enhance superconductivity. Here, we report a high-pressure study up to 48 GPa on Ta-doped Cs(V$_{0.86}$Ta$_{0.14}$)$_3$Sb$_5$ with enhanced $T_\mathrm{c}$ around 5.29 K and without CDW order. At low pressures, unlike the characteristic $``$M-shaped" temperature-pressure phase diagram of the pristine CsV$_3$Sb$_5$, the Ta-doped compound exhibits a distinct non-monotonic evolution. Initially, $T_\mathrm{c}$ drops slightly from 5.29 K at ambient pressure to 4.90 K at 0.33 GPa, before increasing to a maximum of 5.70 K at 0.61 GPa. Upon further compression, $T_\mathrm{c}$ decreases continuously to 0.98 K at 12.3 GPa. At higher pressures, $T_\mathrm{c}$ rises again, reaching 4.46 K at 47.7 GPa. This evolution of $T_\mathrm{c}$ establishes two superconducting domes under pressure, which are similar to those in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$. Our results demonstrate that pressure-induced double-dome superconductivity is intrinsic to these V-based kagome superconductors, even in the absence of long-range CDW order.

</details>

### [8] [Tau and dark sector searches at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11335)
*Wenzhe Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Belle 与 Belle II 实验为超出标准模型（beyond-the-Standard-Model）的新物理搜寻提供重要平台，本文汇总了 τ 物理与暗扇区（dark sector）四项代表性分析：τ 衰变中 CP 破坏测量、轻子味破坏搜寻、类轴子粒子搜寻以及弱相互作用粒子搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle 与 Belle II 实验在 τ 物理与暗扇区具有独特灵敏度，可用于检验标准模型（Standard Model）之外的新物理。通过多条互补探测通道，可拓宽对稀有衰变与不可见粒子的搜索覆盖。

Method: 报告包含四类分析：基于 $τ^- \to π^- K_S^0 ν_τ$ 的 CP 破坏（CP violation）测量；$τ^- \to \ell^- η$ 搜寻；利用 Belle II 数据通过 $e^+ e^- \to γa (\to γγ)$ 进行的类轴子粒子（axion-like particles）搜寻；以及利用 Belle 数据在 $B \to h X_{\rm inv}$ 衰变中进行的弱相互作用粒子（feebly interacting particles）搜寻。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果或显著性，仅列出所开展的四项测量与搜寻分析。

Conclusion: Belle 与 Belle II 数据在 τ 与暗扇区新物理搜寻中形成互补且具强约束力的研究体系，本汇总报告了相关进展。

Abstract: The Belle and Belle II experiments provide a powerful platform for beyond-the-Standard-Model searches, particularly in tau and dark sectors. This summary reports include a measurement of CP violation in $τ^- \to π^- K_S^0 ν_τ$, a search for $τ^- \to \ell^- η$, a search for axion-like particles via $e^+ e^- \to γa (\to γγ)$ using Belle II data, and a search for feebly interacting particles in $B \to h X_{\rm inv}$ decays using Belle data.

</details>


### [9] [Observation of single top-quark production in the $s$-channel in proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11465)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次以单次实验观测到 $s$ 道（$s$-channel）单顶夸克（single top-quark）产生，显著性达 5.1 个标准差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量该过程对完整刻画顶夸克（top quark）的电弱相互作用（electroweak interactions）及检验新物理（new physics）至关重要，但其截面被强烈压低，此前在大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）上一直难以直接探测。

Method: 利用 ATLAS 探测器在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）$\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV 下采集的质子—质子对撞数据，对应积分亮度（integrated luminosity）$140$ fb$^{-1}$；事例筛选要求恰好一个电子或 μ 子、缺失横向动量（missing transverse momentum）以及恰好两个由 $b$ 夸克引发（$b$-quark-initiated）的喷流；采用图神经网络（Graph Neural Network, GNN）区分信号与顶夸克对（$t\bar{t}$）、$W$+喷流及 $t$ 道单顶夸克本底。

Result: 对网络输出进行似然拟合（likelihood fit）得到观测（预期）信号显著性为 $5.1$（$5.2$）个标准差；测得截面 $\sigma_{t\bar{b}+\bar{t}b} = 10.8 \pm 1.5\,(\text{stat.}) \pm 1.6\,(\text{syst.})\,\text{pb}$，与标准模型（Standard Model）预言一致。

Conclusion: 该结果为 $s$ 道单顶夸克产生提供了单次实验的首次观测证据，并表明其测量截面与标准模型预期相符。

Abstract: The first single-experiment observation of single top-quark production in the $s$-channel is presented. Measurements of this process are essential to fully characterize the electroweak interactions of the top quark and probe for new physics, however its highly suppressed cross-section has so far caused it to evade direct detection at the Large Hadron Collider. The analysis uses proton--proton collision data collected at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $140$ fb$^{-1}$. The selected events contain exactly one electron or muon, missing transverse momentum, and exactly two $b$-quark-initiated jets. A Graph Neural Network is utilized to separate the signal from the top-quark pair, $W$+jets, and $t$-channel single top-quark backgrounds. A likelihood fit to the network output yields an observed (expected) signal significance of $5.1$ ($5.2$) standard deviations. The measured cross-section of $σ_{t\bar{b}+\bar{t}b} = 10.8 \pm 1.5 \text{ (stat.)} \pm 1.6 \text{ (syst.)}\,\text{pb}$ is consistent with the Standard Model prediction.

</details>

Verdugo de Osa,B. Villa Ferre,L. Wan,M. Weber,H. Wei,Z. West,T. Wester,A. J. White,A. Wilkinson,P. Wilson,T. Wongjirad,E. Worcester,M. Worcester,M. Wright,W. Wu,S. Yadav,E. Yandel,T. Yang,G. Yarbrough,L. Yates,S. Yebes,J. Yu,B. Yu,H. Yu,V. Zamazal,B. Zamorano,A. Zeglam,J. Zennamo,J. Zhang,C. Zhang,M. Zhao,M. Zuckerbrot*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SBND（Short-Baseline Near Detector）是一台112吨灵敏质量液氩时间投影室（LArTPC），自2024年起运行并积累了全球最大的中微子-氩相互作用样本；其首年束流运行的探测器性能达到或超过设计指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBND作为费米实验室（Fermilab）助推器中微子束（Booster Neutrino Beam）中的近探测器，是短基线中微子（SBN）计划的一部分，其运行旨在为中微子与BSM物理分析提供高性能探测平台。

Method: 论文描述了探测器的实际建成（as-built）结构，并给出首年束流运行的性能结果，涵盖液氩纯度、TPC读出场、光子探测系统（PMT与X-ARAPUCAs）、宇宙线标记系统（cosmic ray tagger）及数据采集系统（DAQ）等各子系统的表现。

Result: 液氩电子寿命超过30ms（为设计要求的十倍）且在Run 1期间超过95%时间保持；TPC读出噪声达到理论最小值（4米长收集面导线平均388e⁻），99.3%读出通道工作；光产额超过10光电子/MeV，时间分辨率数纳秒，可分辨19ns的束团结构；DAQ在8Hz事件率下稳定运行，2025年首次中微子运行的数据采集效率达98.6%。

Conclusion: 上述结果表明SBND代表了多吨级LArTPC探测器性能的当前最高水平（state of the art）。

Abstract: The Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) is a 112 tonne active mass liquid argon time projection chamber (LArTPC) situated in the Booster Neutrino Beam at Fermilab, forming part of the Short-Baseline Neutrino (SBN) Program. SBND began operation in 2024 and has since accumulated the world's largest sample of neutrino-argon interactions. This paper describes the as-built detector and presents performance results from the first year of beam operation. All detector systems performed at or above design requirements. The liquid argon purity achieved an electron lifetime exceeding 30ms, ten times the design requirement, for more than 95% of Run 1. The TPC wire plane readout achieved noise levels at the theoretical minimum for the given wire geometry (an average of 388e$^-$ for the 4m long collection plane wires) with 99.3% of readout channels live. Together, the liquid argon purity and TPC performance are enabling extremely clean event reconstruction in the SBND TPC. The photon detection system, consisting of passive reflectors and multiple active components (PMTs and X-ARAPUCAs), has achieved a light yield exceeding 10 photoelectrons per MeV throughout the detector volume and a timing resolution of a few nanoseconds, sufficient to resolve the 19ns bunch structure of the Booster Neutrino Beam. A nearly 4$π$ cosmic ray tagger system surrounds the TPC to mitigate cosmic backgrounds in neutrino and BSM physics analyses. The data acquisition system has demonstrated stable performance up to an event rate of 8Hz, and a data collection efficiency of 98.6% was achieved throughout SBND's first neutrino run in 2025. These results demonstrate that SBND represents the state of the art in multi-tonne-scale LArTPC detector performance.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 探测器采集的 (2712.4±14.3)×10^6 个 ψ(3686) 事件，首次观测到 χ_{cJ}→ΛK^0_SΞ̄^0+c.c.（J=0,1,2）衰变，显著性超过 10σ，并给出 χ_{cJ}→ΛK^-Ξ̄^++c.c. 的改进测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅说明利用 BESIII 在 BEPCII 上采集的大统计量 ψ(3686) 数据开展 χ_{cJ} 强子衰变分析，以实现新衰变道的首次观测与已有道的精确测量。

Method: 采用 BESIII 探测器（BESIII detector）在 BEPCII 对撞机上采集的 (2712.4±14.3)×10^6 个 ψ(3686) 事件样本，通过分析 χ_{cJ} 到 ΛK^0_SΞ̄^0+c.c. 与 ΛK^-Ξ̄^++c.c. 末态进行分支比（branching fraction）测量，结果中统计误差与系统误差分别列出。

Result: 首次观测到 χ_{cJ}→ΛK^0_SΞ̄^0+c.c.（J=0,1,2），包含系统不确定性后显著性均大于 10σ；测得相应分支比为 (1.13±0.13±0.13)×10^-4、(0.74±0.08±0.07)×10^-4、(1.16±0.11±0.14)×10^-4；同时改进测量 χ_{cJ}→ΛK^-Ξ̄^++c.c. 分支比为 (1.90±0.08±0.16)×10^-4、(1.40±0.05±0.11)×10^-4、(1.95±0.07±0.15)×10^-4。

Conclusion: χ_{cJ}→ΛK^0_SΞ̄^0+c.c. 三条衰变道被首次确认，显著性超过 10σ；χ_{cJ}→ΛK^-Ξ̄^++c.c. 的分支比得到更精确的测定，为 charmonium（粲偶素）强子衰变研究提供了新的实验数据。

Abstract: By analyzing $(2712.4\pm14.3)\times10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observation of $χ_{cJ}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0 + c.c.$ ($J=0,1,2$) with significances greater than $10σ$, including systematic uncertainties, and improved measurements of $χ_{cJ}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^+ + c.c.$ The branching fractions of these decays are determined to be $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0+c.c.)=(1.13\pm0.13\pm0.13)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0+c.c.)=(0.74\pm0.08\pm0.07)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2}\to ΛK^0_S\bar Ξ^0+c.c.)=(1.16\pm0.11\pm0.14)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^++c.c.)=(1.90\pm0.08\pm0.16)\times10^{-4}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^++c.c.)=(1.40\pm0.05\pm0.11)\times10^{-4}$, and $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2}\to ΛK^-\bar Ξ^++c.c.)=(1.95\pm0.07\pm0.15)\times10^{-4}$. The first uncertainties are statistical, and the second are systematic.

</details>


### [12] [Using Geometric Deep Learning for the Detection of the Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background in Water-Cherenkov Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12042)
*Matthieu Melennec,Andrew D. Santos,Thomas Mueller,Frédéric Magniette*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作将图神经网络（Graph Neural Networks, GNNs）用于弥散超新星中微子背景（Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background, DSNB）经逆β衰变（Inverse Beta Decay, IBD）通道的探测任务，构建首个信号能量重建模型及次级信号信号-本底分类器，并在类Hyper-Kamiokande水切伦科夫探测器模拟中取得与当前最优方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSNB研究有助于理解核心坍缩超新星（Core-Collapse Supernovae）的形成并探究中微子基本性质，但其通量探测困难，需在低能区将信号与众多竞争性本底分离；IBD通道虽具有时间分离的双信号特征，仍亟需有效的探测与重建方法。

Method: 引入图神经网络（GNNs）处理IBD通道的关键探测与重建任务：一是从低能正电子（positron）中重建首个信号的能量；二是针对对应中子俘获（neutron capture）后特征光子发射的次级信号，开发优化的信号-本底分类器。

Result: 在两种类Hyper-Kamiokande水切伦科夫（water Cherenkov）探测器几何的模拟数据上进行测试，所提模型性能与当前最优水平（state of the art）相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明GNN可有效承担DSNB经IBD通道的探测与重建任务，为DSNB研究提供了具备竞争力的深度学习方法。

Abstract: The study of the Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background (DSNB) is an essential approach to improve our understanding of the formation of Core-Collapse Supernovae, as well as investigating fundamental neutrino properties. The detection of the DSNB flux however is a difficult task, as it requires the disentanglement of its signal with many competitive backgrounds in the low energy range. The main DSNB detection channel is the Inverse Beta Decay (IBD), which is characterised by a distinct, time-separated two-signal signature. This work introduces the use of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to handle critical detection and reconstruction tasks for the DSNB through the IBD channel. In particular, we present a model to recover the energy of the first signal from a low energy positron. Additionally, we develop a signal-to-background classifier optimised for the secondary signal, which corresponds to the characteristic photon emission following a neutron capture event. We test our models on two simulations in a Hyper-Kamiokande-like water Cherenkov detector geometry, and show that we obtain performances that are comparable to the state of the art.

</details>


### [13] [Optimising Geometric Deep Learning for Varied Reconstruction Tasks in High Granularity Calorimeters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12058)
*Matthieu Melennec,Frédéric Magniette*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文针对高能物理（high energy physics）探测器中图神经网络（Graph Neural Networks, GNNs）部署成本高的问题，提出利用探测器已知几何结构优化通常具有二次算法复杂度（quadratic algorithmic complexity）的GNN流程，并在多个任务上验证，最终给出模块化架构HIBOU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理发现日益依赖亮度（luminosity）与探测器粒度（detector granularity）的提升，从而获得更大统计量与数据样本，但现有方法与算法难以处理探测器输出。GNN虽能处理这些探测数据的非欧几里得（non-euclidean）特性，其部署却因算法计算复杂度而面临显著困难。

Method: 提出利用探测器已知的几何结构（known geometries）来优化GNN流程中所用算法，这些算法通常具有二次算法复杂度。并在此基础上设计HIBOU——一种通用的模块化架构（modular architecture），为在实验特定重建框架（experiment-specific reconstruction frameworks）中实现这些GNN模块提供蓝图。

Result: 在多个任务上验证了所提优化方法的有效性，包括粒子鉴别（particle identification）、能量回归（energy regression）与实例分割（instance segmentation）。

Conclusion: 利用探测器几何结构优化GNN算法是可行且有效的，HIBOU架构为将优化后的GNN模块集成到实验特定重建框架中提供了通用且模块化的参考方案。

Abstract: In the recent years, high energy physics discoveries have been driven by the increasing of luminosity and/or detector granularity. This evolution gives access to bigger statistics and data samples, but can make it hard to process the detector outputs with current methods and algorithms. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), have been shown to be powerful tools to address these challenges. While GNNs can deal with the non-euclidean nature of the data extracted from these detectors, their deployment presents significant difficulties, mainly from the computational complexity of the algorithms they are based on. We propose to use the known geometries of the detectors to optimise the algorithms used for GNN pipelines, which usually have quadratic algorithmic complexities. We validate the validity of our optimised approach on various tasks, such as particle identification, energy regression and instance segmentation. Finally, we present HIBOU, a generic modular architecture that provides a blueprint for how these GNN blocks are to be implemented within experiment-specific reconstruction frameworks.

</details>


### [14] [Evidence for Normal Neutrino Ordering with Combined Atmospheric Neutrino Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12208)
*Miaochen Jin,Carlos A. Argüelles,Pablo Fernández-Menéndez,Iván Martínez-Soler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文联合分析 Super-Kamiokande（不含钆阶段）、IceCube-DeepCore 与 KM3NeT/ORCA 六检测单元配置的大气中微子（atmospheric neutrino）数据，对大气振荡参数、CP 破坏相位及中微子质量序（neutrino mass ordering）给出约束，并以 3.3σ 排除倒序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气中微子实验（atmospheric neutrino experiments）此前多为单独分析，联合多探测器数据可提升对振荡参数与质量序（mass ordering）的灵敏度，并与加速器（accelerator-based）测量形成独立检验。

Method: 同时拟合所有事件样本，将实验间共享的系统不确定度（systematic uncertainties）视为完全相关，而探测器响应（detector-response）不确定度按各实验独立建模；对质量序偏好用赝实验（pseudoexperiments）进行校准。

Result: 测得 Δm²₃₁ = 2.44^{+0.06}_{-0.04} × 10⁻³ eV²，sin²θ₂₃ = 0.52^{+0.03}_{-0.04}，精度分别为 2.2% 与 6.1%；独立约束 CP 破坏相位 δ_CP = 1.30^{+0.24}_{-0.28} π；质量序比较中正常序（normal ordering）以 Δχ² = 11.0 占优，倒序（inverted ordering）被以 99.95% 置信水平（3.3σ）排除。

Conclusion: 大气中微子联合分析给出的振荡参数精度可与当前加速器测量竞争，并提供对 δ_CP 与质量序的独立约束，倾向于正常序。

Abstract: We present a combined analysis of the latest publicly available atmospheric neutrino data from Super-Kamiokande including all data-taking stages previous to the gadolinium phase, IceCube-DeepCore, and the six-detection-unit configuration of KM3NeT/ORCA. Our fit uses all event samples simultaneously and treats the systematic uncertainties shared among experiments as fully correlated, while detector-response uncertainties are modeled independently for each experiment. %We measure The combined data constrain the atmospheric oscillation parameters to $Δm^2_{31} = 2.44^{+0.06}_{-0.04} \times 10^{-3}\,\mathrm{eV}^2$ and $\sin^2θ_{23} = 0.52^{+0.03}_{-0.04}$, with a precision of $2.2\%$ and $6.1\%$, respectively, which is competitive with current accelerator-based measurements, and independently constrain the $CP$-violating phase to $δ_{CP} = 1.30^{+0.24}_{-0.28}~π$. Finally, comparing the orderings of the neutrino mass spectrum, we find a preference for the normal ordering with $Δχ^2 = 11.0$; further calibrated with pseudoexperiments, we exclude the inverted ordering at $99.95\%$ confidence level ($3.3σ$).

</details>


### [15] [A compact, interrogable Tree Tensor Network Jet Tagger on Lund declustering trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12408)
*Fabrizio Napolitano,Luca Della Penna,Tommaso Tedeschi,Livio Fanò*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种受量子启发的树张量网络（Tree Tensor Network, TTN）喷注标记器（jet tagger），在 Cambridge/Aachen 去簇树（declustering tree）的内部节点间共享同一张量核，仅依赖 Lund 坐标而不使用粒子鉴别或探测器信息。χ=8 模型仅 4014 个可训练参数，却可在夸克/胶子鉴别（quark/gluon discrimination）上接近 LundNet 的原生性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有基于 Lund 去簇树的喷注标记器将 QCD 辐射结构编码进输入，再由图网络处理，但树结构本身并未约束计算。本文追问树结构能否约束计算，以及在此约束下能获得何种新能力，如可解释性与跨递归比较。

Method: 构建一种新型量子启发式树张量网络（TTN），在 Cambridge/Aachen 去簇树的内部节点间共享单个张量核，采用 Lund 坐标，不使用粒子鉴别或探测器级信息；该构造基于对辐射历史而非组分的强先验。由于同一学习映射在每个节点上作用相同，模型可在截断树上评估，并跨递归比较潜空间。

Result: 在粒子级（particle level）的 Pythia 与 Herwig 样本上评估 boosted top tagging 与夸克/胶子鉴别，涵盖原生（Pythia→Pythia）与迁移（Pythia→Herwig）两种配置。χ=8 模型有 4014 个可训练参数，LundNet 约 391,000 个；容量相差两个数量级的情况下，TTN 在夸克/胶子鉴别上的原生性能接近 LundNet，在 top tagging 上略逊，且两任务均表现出与 LundNet 相似的 Pythia→Herwig 迁移行为。此外展示了网络随键维度（bond dimension）和微扰尺度（perturbative scale）变化的行为。

Conclusion: 共享张量核的 TTN 以极少参数实现了接近 LundNet 的喷注标记性能，并可用于定位 Pythia 与 Herwig 喷注表示在 Lund 喷注平面（Lund jet plane）上的分歧区域，揭示生成器依赖的集中区域；这凸显了将辐射历史先验直接嵌入树结构计算的潜力。

Abstract: Jet taggers built on the Lund declustering tree encode QCD radiation structure in the inputs and process it with graph networks. We ask whether the tree itself can also constrain the computation, and what becomes accessible when it does. For this, we construct a novel quantum-inspired Tree Tensor Network (TTN), where a single tensor core is shared among the internal nodes of the Cambridge/Aachen declustering tree, employing Lund coordinates, and with no particle-identification or detector-level information. The construction is based on a strong prior on the radiation history rather than on the constituents, complementary to compact equivariant taggers. Because the same learned map acts identically at every node, the trained model can be evaluated on truncated trees and its latent space compared across recursions, and used to localise where on the Lund jet plane the Pythia and Herwig jet representations diverge, showing the regions of generator-dependent concentration. The $χ=8$ model has 4014 trainable parameters against approximately 391,000 for LundNet. We evaluate boosted top tagging and quark/gluon discrimination at particle level on Pythia and Herwig samples, in both native (Pythia$\to$Pythia) and transfer (Pythia$\to$Herwig) configurations. Despite differing in capacity by two orders of magnitude, the TTN reaches native performance close to LundNet in quark/gluon discrimination, slightly underperforming in top tagging, with a similar Pythia-to-Herwig transfer behavior in both tasks. Finally, we show the behavior of the network as a function of the bond dimension, and as a function of the perturbative scale.

</details>

### [16] [The Hawking and Penrose singularity theorems for low regularity Finsler spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11911)
*Darius Erös,Ettore Minguzzi,Argam Ohanyan,Shin-ichi Ohta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在混合水平/垂直正则性 $C^{(1,2)}$（加权情形）与 $C^{(1,3)}$（非加权情形）的洛伦兹-芬斯勒结构（Lorentz--Finsler structures）上证明霍金与彭罗斯奇点定理（Hawking and Penrose singularity theorems），推广了 $C^1$ 洛伦兹度量的相应结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奇点定理多要求较高正则性，而在低正则性下测地线不再由初始条件唯一确定，加权里奇曲率（weighted Ricci curvature）亦须作分布意义解释。论文旨在填补这一低正则性（特别是加权情形）下的理论空白。

Method: 采用两阶段逼近程序：首先施行保持齐性的卷积（homogeneity-preserving convolution），该工具在半黎曼-芬斯勒（semi-Riemann--Finsler）框架中具有独立价值；其次采用类 Chruściel--Grant 的因果适配逼近（causality-adapted approximation）。

Result: 在 $C^{(1,2)}$（加权）与 $C^{(1,3)}$（非加权）正则性下建立了对应的霍金与彭罗斯奇点定理；对 $C^1$ 洛伦兹度量（Lorentzian metrics），结果将 Graf 的 $C^1$ 奇点定理推广至 $C^1$ 加权情形。

Conclusion: 低正则性（含加权）条件下奇点定理仍成立，所提出的齐性保持卷积与因果适配逼近方法为此类推广提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We prove the Hawking and Penrose singularity theorems for Lorentz--Finsler structures of mixed horizontal/vertical regularity $C^{(1,2)}$ in the $C^1$ weighted case and $C^{(1,3)}$ in the unweighted case. At this regularity, geodesics need not be uniquely determined by their initial conditions, and the (weighted) Ricci curvature must be interpreted distributionally. Our approach relies on a two-stage approximation procedure: first, a homogeneity-preserving convolution, which is of independent interest in the broader semi-Riemann--Finsler setting, and second, a causality-adapted approximation à la Chruściel--Grant. In the special case of $C^1$ Lorentzian metrics, our results extend the $C^1$ singularity theorems of Graf to the $C^1$ weighted case.

</details>

### [17] [A massive right-handed sterile neutrino in three-body ${\bar B}\to D^*\ell^-{\bar N}_R$ decays - a Monte Carlo simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00488)
*Bhubanjyoti Bhattacharya,Thomas E. Browder,Lucien M. Cremaldi,Horacio Crotte Ledesma,Nilakshi Das,Alakabha Datta,Tejhas Kapoor,Kumar Pandey,Alexei Sibidanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究针对 $\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-\bar{N}_R$ 的微分衰变分布，基于 EvtGen 框架开发了可模拟超出标准模型过程的蒙特卡洛事件产生器，并通过与解析结果对比完成验证，最终讨论 RHN 可观测量的敏感度及 Belle II 实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重右手征中微子（heavy right-handed neutrino, RHN）是超出标准模型（beyond-the-Standard-Model, BSM）物理的重要候选，而 $\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-\bar{N}_R$ 为其提供了潜在的实验探针。现有模拟工具可能不足以支持此类过程的实验搜寻，因此需要专门的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo, MC）事件产生器。

Abstract: Upcoming experiments are set to dramatically improve the search for charged lepton flavor violation, with the sensitivity to the rates of $μ\to eee$ decay and $μN\to eN$ conversion expected to improve by four orders of magnitude in the near future. In generic models of partial compositeness, the existing constraints from $μ\to eγ$ and the electric dipole moment (EDM) of the electron require the scale of Higgs compositeness to be above $\sim\mathcal{O}(100)$ TeV. In the $U(2)\times U(1)$ case, $μ\to eγ$ and the electron EDM remain important constraints, while upcoming searches for $μ\to eee$, $μN\to eN$ and flavor-violating $τ$ decays provide complementary probes. We discuss how these accidental symmetries can arise in the infrared compatibly with the dynamical generation of flavor hierarchies in the ultraviolet.

Abstract: We study photon conversion into light particles, such as dilatons, axions, and gravitons, induced by background magnetic fields, and formulate the resulting evolution in terms of the Stokes parameters. Abstract: Tree-level matrix elements sit at the heart of collider event generation, but their cost rises rapidly just where additional resolved radiation becomes most important. Conclusion: 在经典共形阿贝尔-希格斯模型的强超冷相变中，高维算符对成核过程的影响可忽略，高温成核有效理论在该情形下保持适用，其有效范围亦被明确划定。

Abstract: We quantify the impact of higher-dimensional operators in the effective theory for bubble nucleation, focusing on supercooled phase transitions in the classically conformal Abelian-Higgs model. While high-temperature dimensional reduction organizes hard thermal corrections into a tower of operators in a three-dimensional effective field theory, recent results show that dimension-six terms can dominate over higher-loop corrections to the equilibrium thermodynamics of the strongest transitions. To assess their effect on the nucleation rate and the derived near-equilibrium phase-transition observables, we develop a perturbative framework that includes them in the full one-loop nucleation rate. Including higher-dimensional operators both in the bounce action and in the fluctuation determinants, we solve the resulting Sturm-Liouville problem without a derivative expansion, using a modified Gel'fand-Yaglom method. For strongly supercooled transitions, we find that higher-dimensional operators only marginally affect the nucleation rate and the resulting observables. As a byproduct, we delineate the regime of validity of the high-temperature nucleation effective theory in supercooled phase transitions.

</details>


### [31] [Machine Learning Meets High-Energy Nuclear Physics: From Pattern Recognition to Physics-Integrated Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12293)
*Xun Chen,Weiyao Ke,Yu-Gang Ma,Long-Gang Pang,Kai Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本篇小型综述（mini-review）梳理近年高能核物理（high-energy nuclear physics, HENP）中机器学习（machine learning, ML）从事件分类、模式识别等早期应用，转向物理知识直接嵌入数据分析、模拟与物理推断的"物理集成工作流（physics-integrated workflows）"的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期ML应用侧重事件分类、模式识别与选定观测量（observables）的代理模型（surrogate models）；当前阶段则追求将物理知识更直接地纳入数据分析、模拟与物理推断，因此有必要综述已趋于成熟的发展方向。

Method: 综述覆盖重离子碰撞（heavy-ion collisions）、中子星物理（neutron-star physics）、格点量子场论（lattice QFT）与全息或连续场QCD（holographic or continuum QCD）等领域的近期ML应用，重点关注其如何嵌入具体物理工作流，以及对称性、守恒律、因果性、热力学稳定性与拓扑等物理约束的施加方式。

Result: 所综述的进展包括：QCD物质性质（QCD matter properties）的校准贝叶斯提取（calibrated Bayesian extraction）、由重离子与中子星数据推断致密物质状态方程（equation-of-state inference）、生成式事件建模（generative event modeling）、弱物理信号的神经解卷（neural unfolding）、可微逆求解器（differentiable inverse solvers）、规范等变（gauge-equivariant）与基于扩散（diffusion-based）的格点场采样器，以及全息QCD中模型函数的神经重构。

Conclusion: 综述重点不在ML架构本身，而在于其如何进入具体物理工作流、物理约束如何被施加，以及不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）与验证如何决定AI辅助结果能否支撑可靠的物理结论。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) in high-energy nuclear physics (HENP) is entering a new stage in which physical knowledge is incorporated more directly into data analysis, simulation, and physics inference. This mini-review focuses on developments that have matured in the past several years. Whereas earlier applications emphasized event classification, pattern recognition, and surrogate models for selected observables, recent work has moved toward physics-integrated workflows: calibrated Bayesian extraction of QCD matter properties, dense-matter equation-of-state inference from heavy-ion and neutron-star data, generative event modeling, neural unfolding of weak physical signals, differentiable inverse solvers, gauge-equivariant and diffusion-based lattice-field samplers, and neural reconstruction of model functions in holographic QCD. We survey recent applications of ML in heavy-ion collisions, neutron-star physics, lattice QFT, and holographic or continuum QCD. The emphasis is not on ML architectures alone, but on how they enter concrete physics workflows, how physical constraints such as symmetries, conservation laws, causality, thermodynamic stability, and topology are imposed, and how uncertainty quantification and validation determine whether an AI-assisted result can support a reliable physics conclusion.

</details>

### [32] [Beyond Dry Flocking: Shape-Dependent Hydrodynamic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10709)
*Abhishek Thakur,Taniya Khatun,Soumyadeep Kundu,Nitin Kumar,P. Parmananda,Anirban Sain*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以部分涂覆樟脑的三角形纸船作为自驱动 Marangoni 游泳体（Marangoni swimmer），揭示受限系统中流体介质反馈主导的集群（flocking）行为，发现无序-有序转变及罕见的再入型有序-无序转变，并用反应-扩散模型（reaction-diffusion model）耦合游泳体动力学加以重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集群研究通常将周围流体（fluid）仅视为动量汇（momentum sink），把集群系统描述为非动量守恒的"干"活性物质（dry active matter），忽略介质对取向对齐的作用。本文旨在考察流体介质的反馈（该反馈关键依赖游泳体形状）如何影响其对齐行为。

Method: 实验上使用最多三十个部分涂覆樟脑的三角形纸船作为极性游泳体（polar swimmer），构成受限系统；理论上建立樟脑浓度场的反应-扩散模型（reaction-diffusion model），并与游泳体动力学耦合，其中推进力源自表面张力梯度（surface tension gradient）。

Result: 实验观测到随樟脑浓度变化出现的无序-有序转变（disorder-to-order transition），以及随游泳体密度变化出现的非常规再入型有序-无序转变（re-entrant order-to-disorder transition）；所构建的耦合模型成功重现了这些转变。

Conclusion: 由形状依赖的流体动力学相互作用（shape-dependent hydrodynamic interaction）可能在生物体（living organisms）的高效集群中同样发挥核心作用。

Abstract: Flocking results from dynamical alignment among neighbors in a collection of active self-propelling objects. The intervening medium, typically a fluid, however, is not considered to be important for alignment, except for acting as a momentum sink. The flocking system is therefore described as momentum nonconserving "dry" active matter. Here we investigate a confined system of self-propelled Marangoni swimmers where feedback from the fluid medium, which crucially depends on the shape of the swimmers, influences their alignment. Using up to thirty partially camphor-coated triangular paper boats as polar swimmers, we uncover a disorder-to-order transition, and an unusual re-entrant order-to-disorder transition, as functions of camphor concentration and swimmer density, respectively. We reproduce these transitions by formulating a reaction-diffusion model for the camphor field, coupled to the swimmer dynamics, where propulsion forces arise from surface tension gradients. The shape-dependent hydrodynamic interaction discussed here could be central to efficient flocking in living organisms too.

</details>


### [33] [Laser fragmentation in liquid - constructing a generic reaction map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11494)
*Anton Plech,Yogesh Pokhrel,Meike Tack,Matteo Levantino,Sven Reichenberger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过皮秒激光脉冲激发水中金胶体，利用超快原位X射线散射方法追踪其非平衡弛豫路径，构建了涵盖加热、熔化、气泡形成至碎裂的反应图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲激光激发吸收性胶体可产生高度非平衡态物质（non-equilibrium states of matter），其弛豫经由特定的热与非热耗散通道进行；理解这些通道的结构印记对调控与应用至关重要。

Method: 以水中金胶体（gold colloid）为模型体系，采用400 nm皮秒激光脉冲激发其带间吸收带（interband absorption band），并用超快原位X射线散射（ultrafast in situ x-ray scattering）方法进行探测，按时间延迟与激光通量（laser fluence）进行分类分析。

Result: 通过量化晶格温度（lattice temperature）、晶态分数（crystalline fraction）、颗粒尺寸及纳米颗粒周围水的结构响应，构建出反应图谱（reaction map），可辨识加热、颗粒熔化、水泡形成及最终颗粒碎裂等过程。

Conclusion: 颗粒碎裂最终形成以小于3 nm为主导尺寸的纳米团簇（nanoclusters），可用于诊疗学（theranostics）、光子学（photonics）或催化（catalysis）等应用。

Abstract: Excitation of absorbing colloids in liquid by pulsed laser irradiation creates highly non-equilibrium states of matter that relax via defined pathways of thermal and non-thermal dissipation channels. The structural fingerprint of these channels can be probed by ultrafast {\it in situ} x-ray scattering methods and classified as a function of temporal delay of excitation excitation density, or specifically laser fluence. We study the example of photo-excitation of a gold colloid by picosecond laser pulses at the interband absorption band at 400 nm in water. By quantifying lattice temperature, crystalline fraction, particle sizes and the structural response of the water around the nanoparticles a reaction map is constructed that allows to pinpoint heating, particle melting, water bubble formation and finally particle fragmentation to form nanoclusters of predominant sizes of < 3 nm for applications in theranostics, photonics or catalysis.

</details>


### [34] [Autonomous thermodynamic cycles via robotic mobility and sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11667)
*Sofia Kuperman,Ezra Ben-Abu,Yaron Veksler,Anna Zigelman,Sefi Givli,Amir D. Gat*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究提出并实验实现了一种由机器人移动性与感知驱动的自主热力学循环（autonomous thermodynamic cycles），使多稳态充气胶囊（multistable gas-filled capsules）跨越空间温度梯度运行，从而构成可移动的热机以收集并储存能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学循环（thermodynamic cycles）依赖固定热库（fixed thermal reservoirs），被限制在特定位置与温差条件下运行。作者希望突破这一限制，使系统通过机器人移动与感知自主访问空间变化的温度场（spatially varying temperature fields）。

Method: 采用多稳态充气胶囊作为实验载体，使其在系统内跨越热梯度（thermal gradient）循环运动；建立模型描述胶囊能量状态的快速转变，并将胶囊尺度的内能动力学（internal energy dynamics）与机器人宏观导航策略相耦合，以优化兼顾运动成本与能量收集的移动路径。

Result: 模型揭示，胶囊能量状态的快速转变使系统可作为移动热机（mobile heat engine）运行，实现能量的收集与储存；通过尺度关联的路径优化，获得了平衡运动成本与能量收集的运动策略。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当自主系统（autonomous systems）在环境中导航时，热力学循环可以自发涌现，构成一种人工类比物（artificial analog）：类似在空间资源中觅食能量的生物体。这为能量转换在移动与感知条件下的实现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Thermodynamic cycles are the foundation of energy conversion across natural and engineered systems, transforming heat into useful work. However, these cycles traditionally operate between fixed thermal reservoirs, restricting them to specific locations and temperature differences. Here, we introduce autonomous thermodynamic cycles enabled by robotic mobility and sensing, allowing robots to perform thermodynamic cycles by accessing spatially varying temperature fields. We experimentally realize this concept using multistable gas-filled capsules that circulate within the system across a thermal gradient. Our model reveals that rapid transitions in the capsules' energy states allow the system to operate as a mobile heat engine that harvests and stores energy. By linking the capsule-scale internal energy dynamics to the robot's large-scale navigation strategy, we optimize locomotion paths that balance motion cost and energy harvesting. These findings demonstrate that thermodynamic cycles can emerge when autonomous systems navigate their environments, offering an artificial analog of organisms that forage for energy across spatial resources.

</details>


### [35] [Elastic polymer networks of exceptional strength by deconcentrating tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11837)
*Yu Zhou,Chenghai Li,Zheqi Chen,Xianyang Bao,Zhigang Suo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 聚合物网络的强度远低于单链，原因在于高张力集中于少数链段。通过提高承受高张力链段的比例，可实现强度大幅放大，水凝胶强度从约0.05 MPa提升至约10 MPa，且保持低滞后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物网络强度比单条聚合物链低数个数量级（orders of magnitude），根源在于网络将高张力集中在很小一部分聚合物链段（strands）上，因此需要探索提升整体强度的设计途径。

Method: 开发制备高强度水凝胶（hydrogels）的方法，通过招募更大比例的链段承担高张力来放大强度；涉及常规网络、高度缠结网络（highly entangled network）与预拉伸互穿网络（prestretched interpenetrating network）等结构，并将类似放大策略应用于弹性体（elastomers）。

Result: 水凝胶强度由常规网络的约0.05 MPa提升至高度缠结网络的约1 MPa，再提升至预拉伸互穿网络的约10 MPa，同时保持低滞后（low hysteresis）；弹性体亦获得类似强度放大。实验数据提示强度与链段长度之间存在标度关系（scaling relation）。

Conclusion: 通过提高承载高张力链段的比例可显著放大聚合物网络强度，并据此提出构建兼具弹性与高强度聚合物网络的设计原则（design principles）。

Abstract: The strength of a polymer network is orders of magnitude below that of a polymer chain, because the network concentrates high tension in a small fraction of polymer strands. Here we show that the strength of a network can be greatly amplified by recruiting a larger fraction of strands to bear high tension. We develop approaches to fabricate hydrogels of exceptional strength while maintaining low hysteresis. The strength of the hydrogel increases from ~0.05 MPa for a regular network, to ~1 MPa for a highly entangled network, and further to ~10 MPa for a prestretched interpenetrating network. Similar amplifications of strength are achieved for elastomers. Furthermore, experimental data suggest a scaling relation between strength and strand length. This work provides design principles for creating elastic and strong polymer networks.

</details>


### [36] [Fluid deformable surfaces with variable thickness - a Surface Shallow-Water-Helfrich model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11903)
*Jan Magnus Sischka,Axel Voigt*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文在表面 Navier-Stokes-Helfrich 模型基础上引入厚度场，以浅水方程思路替换二维流体的不可延展约束，从而允许表面面积变化，并基于曲面有限元建立数值格式，用于研究封闭曲面的形状演化及形态发生建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上皮组织（epithelial tissues）在形态发生（morphogenesis）中具有基础作用，力学上表现为兼具固体与流体双重性质的薄软材料。已有的表面 Navier-Stokes-Helfrich 模型虽结合了弯曲与表面流体动力学，但未考虑细胞厚度的变化，因此需要引入厚度自由度以更真实地描述这类组织。

Method: 在（自）演化曲面上引入厚度场（thickness field），采用浅水方程（shallow-water equation）的思想进行建模；由此以薄膜流体的不可压缩约束（incompressibility constraint）替代二维流体的不可延展约束（inextensibility constraint）及相应的表面面积守恒。随后基于曲面有限元（surface finite elements）开发数值格式并开展收敛性测试。

Result: 收敛性测试验证了所提数值格式的表现；在保持封闭体积恒定的封闭曲面（closed surfaces）上展示了该模型对形状演化的影响，并讨论了其对形态发生建模的意义。

Conclusion: 通过引入厚度场并将面积守恒替换为薄膜不可压缩约束，模型能够允许表面面积变化，从而更灵活地刻画上皮组织的固-液双重力学行为；该框架对形态发生（morphogenesis）建模具有潜在启示意义。

Abstract: Epithelial tissues play a fundamental role in morphogenesis. Mechanically they can be viewed as thin soft materials exhibiting a solid-fluid duality. The Surface Navier-Stokes-Helfrich model accounts for these properties by combining bending and surface hydrodynamics. In order to account for varying cell thickness we incorporate a thickness field in the spirit of a shallow-water equation, but defined on the (self-)evolving surface. This replaces the inextensibility constraint of the two-dimensional fluid and with it the conservation of surface area by an incompressibility constraint of the thin film fluid allowing for changes in surface area. We develop a numerical scheme based on surface finite elements, perform convergence tests, demonstrate the impact on shape evolution of closed surfaces with a constant enclosed volume and discuss implications on modeling morphogenesis.

</details>


### [37] [A structure-preserving neural density functional for the ions of a polymer electrolyte](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12132)
*Liyao Lyu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种保持对称性、热力学可积性与诺特定理的神经密度泛函，用于非均匀聚合物电解质中离子关联的跨尺度描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测非均匀聚合物电解质（inhomogeneous polymer electrolytes）的结构与响应，需要既保留分子尺度精度、又能跨空间尺度与几何结构迁移的离子关联描述。

Method: 构建神经密度泛函（neural density functional），保留空间对称性、热力学可积性（thermodynamic integrability）与诺特恒等式（Noether identities），在稳定非临界体相中恢复完全屏蔽（perfect screening）；其非线性密度依赖用于捕捉配对闭合（pair closure）遗漏的关联。仅使用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）提供的平面密度与内力剖面训练。

Result: 非线性密度依赖捕捉到浓度依赖关联，包括强耦合下长波长数密度涨落由增强转为抑制的转变（crossover）；泛函可描述未训练盐浓度下的密度剖面，并预测体相结构因子（structure factors）与长波长数响应。它可预测更大域及二维外场中的离子结构，在同一离子数据上优于其他三种神经密度泛函架构，且仅用四分之一训练轮次即保持精度，而最佳替代方法误差增长约三分之二。

Conclusion: 空间可迁移性（spatial transferability）为将分子关联与更大尺度的连续介质预测相连接提供了必要基础。

Abstract: Predicting the structure and response of inhomogeneous polymer electrolytes requires a description of ion correlations that retains molecular-scale accuracy while remaining transferable across spatial scales and geometries. We develop a neural density functional for electrolytes that preserves spatial symmetries, thermodynamic integrability and the Noether identities, with perfect screening recovered in stable, noncritical bulk states. Its nonlinear density dependence captures the concentration-dependent correlations missed by a pair closure, including a crossover from enhanced to suppressed long-wavelength number fluctuations at strong coupling. The functional describes density profiles at an untrained salt concentration and predicts bulk structure factors and the long-wavelength number response. Trained solely on planar density and internal-force profiles from molecular dynamics, the functional predicts ionic structure in larger domains and in two-dimensional external fields. On the same ion data, it is more accurate than three other neural density-functional architectures and keeps its accuracy with a quarter of the training runs, where the errors of the best alternative grow by about two thirds. The spatial transferability provides a necessary foundation for connecting molecular correlations to continuum predictions at larger scales.

</details>

### [38] [Character variety of the five-punctured sphere and the determinantal quintic hypersurface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12092)
*Kazuhiro Hikami*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文利用 skein 理论方法研究五孔球面的 SL2(C) 特征标簇，证明其满足 Fricke-Klein-Vogt 型关系并实现为对称行列式超曲面，同时给出簇变量实现与简单闭曲线的 Poisson 结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画 SL2(C) 特征标簇（character variety）在五孔球面（five-punctured sphere）情形下的代数结构，其中五个孔点可取任意共轭类（conjugacy class），并借助 skein 理论（skein-theoretic methods）与簇代数框架给出显式描述。

Method: 采用 skein 理论方法，针对五孔球面在每个孔点取任意共轭类的 SL2(C) 特征标簇进行分析，建立与 Fricke-Klein-Vogt 型关系、对称行列式超曲面（symmetric determinantal hypersurface）以及簇变量（cluster variables）之间的对应，并显式推导曲面上简单闭曲线（simple closed curves）的 Poisson 结构（Poisson structure）。

Result: 结果表明：Fricke-Klein-Vogt 型关系可实现在对称行列式超曲面上；特征标簇可由簇变量实现；并显式得出简单闭曲线的 Poisson 结构，同时证明簇突变（cluster mutations）诱导特征标簇的自同构（automorphisms）。

Conclusion: 研究为五孔球面 SL2(C) 特征标簇提供了 skein 理论、行列式超曲面与簇变量三种相互关联的描述，并建立了簇突变与特征标簇自同构之间的联系。

Abstract: Using skein-theoretic methods, we study the SL2(C) character variety of the five-punctured sphere with arbitrary conjugacy class at each of the five punctures. We show that a Fricke-Klein-Vogt-type relation is realized as a symmetric determinantal hypersurface. We also give a realization of the character variety in terms of cluster variables. We explicitly derive the Poisson structure of the simple closed curves on the surface, and prove that cluster mutations induce automorphisms of the character variety.

</details>

### [39] [Primordial black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10750)
*V. I. Dokuchaev,Yu. N. Eroshenko,V. V. Nikulin,K. A. Postnov,S. G. Rubin,V. D. Stasenko*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本综述聚焦原初黑洞（primordial black holes）这一由 Zel'dovich 与 Novikov 于 1966 年首次提出的概念，梳理其形成模型及借助天文与天体物理观测进行检验的方法，并重点讨论闭合畴壁（closed domain walls）坍缩与额外空间维度（extra spatial dimensions）相关机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞自 1966 年被首次提出后，近年来受到越来越多的关注。现代电磁波谱天文数据以及引力波（gravitational waves）与宇宙中微子（cosmic neutrinos）观测揭示了一系列可能与在前恒星宇宙时期形成的原初黑洞相关的独特天体物理现象，因此有必要系统梳理相关形成模型与检验手段。

Method: 本文为综述性研究，描述多种原初黑洞形成模型，并总结利用天文与天体物理观测检验这些模型的方法；其中特别详细讨论了通过闭合畴壁坍缩以及宇宙额外空间维度相关效应形成原初黑洞及其团簇的机制。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果，仅指出综述涵盖多种形成模型与相应观测检验方法，并对闭合畴壁坍缩和额外空间维度这两类形成途径进行了详细讨论。文中还提及 Appendix II 提供了 Zel'dovich 与 Novikov 1966 年开创性论文的原文。

Conclusion: 原初黑洞很可能与当前多信使天文观测所揭示的若干独特现象相关；对其形成模型的研究及基于天文、天体物理观测的检验，是当前值得深入探讨的方向，闭合畴壁与额外空间维度机制是其中重点。

Abstract: Primordial black holes, whose formation in the early Universe was first hypothesized by Ya.B.~Zel'dovich and I.D.~Novikov in 1966 (the text of this pioneering article is provided in Appendix II to this review), have attracted increasing attention in recent years. Modern astronomical data across the electromagnetic spectrum, together with observations of gravitational waves and cosmic neutrinos, have revealed a number of unique astrophysical phenomena that may be related to primordial black holes formed in the pre-stellar cosmological epoch. This review describes various models of primordial black hole formation, as well as methods for testing these models with astronomical and astrophysical observations. In particular, we discuss in detail the formation of primordial black holes and their clusters through the collapse of closed domain walls and through effects associated with extra spatial dimensions of the Universe.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限外延已成为可控生长高质量单层二维（2D）材料的新方法，但机制理解目前仅限于晶核吸附与扩散势垒的分析，缺乏对界面力学作用的刻画。

Method: 采用人工智能（AI）辅助的密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究生长于c面蓝宝石（c-plane sapphire）与六方氮化硼（h-BN）之间的单层NbSe2中，范德华限域如何与原子运动耦合，并计算力—电子（force-electronic）景观。

Result: 计算得到作用于Nb和Se的较大机械力分别为+0.070与-0.04 eV/Å，单胞界面相互作用能最高达2.4 eV，与类似衬底上报道的吸附和扩散能量相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明界面力学可能解释受限外延的成功；调控界面力学及相关范德华力化学（vdW mechanochemistry）有望成为实现高通量、高质量材料合成的有效途径。

Abstract: Confined epitaxy has recently emerged as a novel methodology for controlled growth of high-quality monolayer two-dimensional (2D) materials. However, mechanistic understanding remains limited to analysing nucleus adsorption and diffusion barriers. Here, I discover that van der Waals (vdW) confinement not only affects the specific adsorption and diffusion of nuclei on confined substrates but also induces strong coupling to atomic forces at the interface. Using artificial intelligence (AI) assisted density functional theory (DFT) calculations, I study how vdW confinement couples to the atomic motion of monolayer NbSe2 grown between c-plane sapphire and hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN). The calculated force-electronic landscape yields large mechanical forces of +0.070 and -0.04 eV per Angstrom on Nb and Se, respectively, giving rise to a unit-cell interfacial interaction energy up to 2.4 eV, comparable to reported adsorption and diffusion energies on similar substrates. The results indicate that interfacial mechanics may explain the success of confined epitaxy. The findings suggest that tuning interfacial mechanics, and related vdW mechanochemistry, could be a promising route to high-throughput, high-quality materials synthesis.

*Chuqiao Shi,Giovanny Espitia,Yanxing Li,Yi Jiang,Yiyuan Luo,Xu Qi,Kaiwen Yang,Xiaohui Liu,Xiaoqin Li,Chih-Kang Shih,Mit H. Naik,Yimo Han*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超莫尔晶格（supermoire lattices）此前已被发现存在于大角度扭转双层中，并被认为源于公度堆叠基元的周期性排布，但其形成的微观结构机制尚未解决。该研究的动机即在于阐明这一微观机制。

Method: 方法上结合力场模拟（force-field simulations）与实验验证，实验手段为超分辨、层分辨的多层电子叠层成像（super-resolution, layer-resolved multislice electron ptychography）。

Result: 结果识别出皮米（picometer）尺度的层间 Se-Se 排斥为驱动力，由此产生耦合的短程（约 1 nm）莫尔弛豫（moire relaxation）与共存的长程（约 10 nm）超莫尔调制。

Conclusion: 结论指出，即便在皮米尺度上，晶格弛豫在大角度莫尔体系中仍具有关键作用，在理解其涌现的电子与量子现象（emergent electronic and quantum phenomena）时必须显式考虑。

Abstract: Supermoire lattices in large-angle twisted bilayers have recently been shown to emerge from periodic arrangements of commensurate stacking motifs, yet the microscopic structural mechanism governing their formation has remained unresolved. Here, we uncover the microscopic origin of supermoire order as a hierarchical lattice relaxation process. Combining force-field simulations with experimental validation via super-resolution, layer-resolved multislice electron ptychography, our results identify interlayer Se-Se repulsion at the picometer scale as the driving force, giving rise to coupled short-range (about 1 nm) moire relaxation and coexisting long-range (about 10 nm) supermoire modulation. These findings demonstrate that even picometer-scale lattice relaxation remains critical in large-angle moire systems and must be explicitly considered in understanding their emergent electronic and quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [42] [Ceramic Materials: From Atomic Bonding and Processing to Microstructure, Functional Properties, and Biomedical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10874)
*Jairo Rondon,Juan Valera,Alex Velez Cruz,Irela Perez Magin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文为陶瓷材料综述，围绕“制备—微观结构—性能—服役表现”关系链，讨论原子键合与缺陷、晶粒尺寸、孔隙率、相稳定性对力学、热学、电学及生物学行为的影响，并涵盖生物陶瓷与纳米陶瓷、智能陶瓷等新兴方向及主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陶瓷的性能由原子键合（atomic bonding）、晶体结构（crystal structure）、制备（processing）、微观结构（microstructure）与功能特性之间的相互作用决定，因此有必要以系统性的关系框架梳理其行为规律与调控途径。

Method: 采用综述（review）方式，以“制备—微观结构—性能—服役表现”（processing–microstructure–property–performance）关系为主线，分析晶体化学、缺陷、晶粒尺寸、孔隙率与相稳定性的作用，并比较传统烧结（conventional sintering）、溶胶—凝胶（sol–gel）与增材制造（additive manufacturing）等工艺路线。

Result: 离子键（ionic bonding）与共价键（covalent bonding）赋予陶瓷高硬度、热与化学稳定性、耐磨性及多样化电学行为，但同时限制塑性变形，并提高其对缺陷、孔隙、晶界与残余应力的敏感性；文中还评述了氧化铝、氧化锆、磷酸钙、羟基磷灰石、磷酸三钙及生物活性玻璃等生物医学陶瓷（biomedical ceramics）。

Conclusion: 主要挑战仍在于脆性断裂、制备异质性、降解控制、规模化放大以及孔隙率—强度权衡（porosity–strength trade-off）；未来发展需整合先进制造、多尺度表征（multiscale characterization）、预测建模与数据驱动设计。

Abstract: Ceramic materials are inorganic, predominantly nonmetallic solids whose performance is governed by the interplay among atomic bonding, crystal structure, processing, microstructure, and functional properties. Their ionic and covalent bonding provides high hardness, thermal and chemical stability, wear resistance, and diverse electrical behavior, but also limits plastic deformation and increases sensitivity to flaws, porosity, grain boundaries, and residual stresses. This review examines ceramics through the processing--microstructure--property--performance relationship, emphasizing the effects of crystal chemistry, defects, grain size, porosity, and phase stability on mechanical, thermal, electrical, and biological behavior. Conventional sintering, sol--gel processing, and additive manufacturing are discussed as key routes for controlling densification, porosity, geometry, and composition. Biomedical ceramics, including alumina, zirconia, calcium phosphates, hydroxyapatite, tricalcium phosphate, and bioactive glasses, are also reviewed. Emerging topics include nanoceramics, smart ceramics, composites, multimaterial manufacturing, defect engineering, immunomodulatory bioceramics, and sustainable processing. Major challenges remain in brittle fracture, processing heterogeneity, degradation control, scale-up, and the porosity--strength trade-off. Future advances will require integrating advanced manufacturing, multiscale characterization, predictive modeling, and data-driven design.

</details>


### [43] [Data driven screening of CaCu$_5$-type magnetic structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10891)
*Nabaraj Pokhrel,Sheila Whitman,David S. Parker*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一套机器学习辅助流程，用于在CaCu$_5$结构族中快速筛选永磁候选材料，将逾6万种取代组分逐步缩减至439个进行第一性原理计算，最终识别出159种具有正轴向磁各向异性能的化合物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永磁材料（permanent magnet）候选的化学空间庞大，纯第一性原理（first-principles）筛选计算成本过高，亟需高效缩减搜索空间的策略。

Result: 筛出439个候选进入第一性原理计算，DFT结果显示159种化合物具有正轴向磁各向异性能（axial magnetic anisotropy energy），倾向于[001]方向而非所测试的面内方向。

Conclusion: 所得数据集揭示了CaCu$_5$组分空间中磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）的化学趋势，并提供了一批待深入研究的候选材料；更广泛而言，展示了将既有机器学习模型与第一性原理计算结合、系统缩减庞大化学空间的实用策略。

Abstract: We develop a machine learning assisted workflow for the rapid screening of permanent magnet candidates within the CaCu$_5$ structure family. We generated more than 60,000 structures through substitutions. These compositions were screened using a combination of pretrained materials models, Materials Project data, and a machine-learning model for magnetization prediction. Four criteria, namely, metallicity, negative formation energy, energy above the convex hull $\leq 50$~meV/atom, and predicted magnetization above the adopted $\geq 0.75$~T threshold reduced the initial chemical space to 439 candidates for first-principles calculations. Density functional theory calculations revealed that 159 compounds exhibit positive axial magnetic anisotropy energy, favoring [001] over the tested in-plane directions. The resulting dataset reveals chemical trends in magnetic anisotropy across the CaCu$_5$ compositional space and provides a set of candidates for further investigation. More broadly, this work demonstrates a practical strategy for combining existing machine learning models with first-principles calculations to systematically reduce large chemical spaces to tractable sets for computationally intensive materials screening.

</details>


### [44] [Band Tail States: Speed Bump or End of the Road for Two-Dimensional Semiconductors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10953)
*Zherui Han,Robert K. A. Bennett,Davide Donadio,Maritha A. Wang,Tara Peña,Mikaël Cassé,Martin Vanbrabant,Valeriya Kilchytska,Jean-Pierre Raskin,Eric Pop*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出二维（2D）电子器件潜力的充分发挥取决于对能带尾态（band tail states, BTS）的理解与调控，并给出降低其影响的设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与具有陡峭能带边缘（Urbach 能量约 10 meV）的硅（silicon）不同，当前单层二维半导体（monolayer 2D semiconductors）因空间势不均匀性（spatial potential inhomogeneity）而呈现粗糙能带边缘（Urbach 能量约 100 meV），这构成了二维电子学的根本限制。

Method: 将基于物理的电学模型（physics-based electrical model）与实验进行对比，分析能带尾态对单层二维晶体管（monolayer 2D transistors）电学行为的影响，并评估若干缓解策略。

Result: 能带尾态导致有效迁移率（effective mobility）降低以及需要过高栅压的“慢”开启（slow turn-on）行为；通过提高栅极电容（gate capacitance）、应变工程（strain engineering）以及采用双层而非单层二维沟道（bilayer 2D channels），可将该效应缓解，前提是 Urbach 能量降至约 50 meV 以下。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了理论框架与设计指南，以应对上述根本限制，并推动二维半导体成功集成到低功耗纳米电子学（low-power nanoelectronics）中。

Abstract: We propose that the path to realizing the full potential of two-dimensional (2D) electronics lies in understanding and mastering their energy band tail states (BTS). Unlike silicon, which benefits from sharp band edges (Urbach energy $E_\rm U$ ~ 10 meV), today's monolayer 2D semiconductors have rough band edges ($E_\rm U$ ~ 100 meV) caused by spatial potential inhomogeneity. Comparing a physics-based electrical model to experiments, we demonstrate that BTS cause reduced effective mobility and "slow" transistor turn-on with excessive gate voltage in monolayer 2D transistors, which sets a fundamental limit for their energy-efficient operation. We also show that BTS effects in 2D transistors could be mitigated with higher gate capacitance, with strain engineering, and by using bilayer instead of monolayer 2D channels, as long as $E_\rm U$ is reduced below ~ 50 meV. This work provides a theoretical framework and design guidelines to navigate these fundamental limits, and to enable successful integration of 2D semiconductors into low-power nanoelectronics.

</details>


### [45] [Bond-Switching Reconstruction in Bare Ti3C2 MXene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11062)
*Kui Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作表明 MXene（过渡金属碳氮化物）的金属-碳骨架拓扑并非固定，双轴应变下 Ti3C2 可发生键切换重构，生成动态稳定的新相 N 及 N'，且重构受机械手段调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上 MXene 的性质调控依赖成分、表面终端与插层，而金属-碳骨架拓扑被视为固定不变。本文旨在检验该骨架是否可重构，从而为 MXene 引入新的结构自由度。

Result: 获得动态稳定的多形体 N，含 15 原子原胞、六类不同 Ti-C 键与 2.55 Å 的 Ti-Ti 对；相干转变路径需跨越 8.47 eV/胞势垒，先到达低于 N 1.08 eV 的重构极小值 N'，N 相对母相高 7.58 eV/胞，N 与 N' 的反向势垒分别为 1.50 与 1.98 eV/胞。重构淬灭母相自旋极化，形成非磁性金属，费米能级态密度增加近四倍；电荷由 C 向 Ti 转移并与 Ti-Ti 配对相关；多层中重构层形成层间 Ti-C 键；10% 双轴拉伸使相能差减半，约 85 GPa 压力使相序反转。

Conclusion: 键切换重构（bond-switching reconstruction）构成 MXene 中一种可由机械手段调控的结构自由度，其能量学与电子结构可由应变和压力有效调节。

Abstract: MXene properties are typically tuned through composition, surface termination, and intercalation, while the metal-carbon framework topology is considered fixed. Here, we demonstrate that this framework can reconstruct. Unconstrained relaxations of biaxially strained bare Ti3C2 yield a dynamically stable polymorph, N, featuring a 15-atom primitive cell, six distinct Ti-C bond classes, and 2.55 Å Ti-Ti pairs. The coherent transformation pathway crosses an 8.47 eV barrier per cell but first reaches another reconstructed minimum, N', lying 1.08 eV below N, revealing a family of bond-switched networks. Phase N lies 7.58 eV per cell above the parent. Reverse barriers along the calculated pathway are 1.50 and 1.98 eV per cell for N and N', respectively. Reconstruction quenches the parent spin polarization, yielding a nonmagnetic metal with a nearly fourfold increase in Fermi-level density of states. Bader and electron-localization analyses suggest charge redistribution from C toward Ti associated with Ti-Ti pairing. In multilayers, reconstructed layers form interlayer Ti-C bonds. A 10% biaxial tension halves the phase-energy difference, and pressure reverses phase ordering near 85 GPa. These results establish bond-switching reconstruction as a mechanically controllable structural degree of freedom in MXenes.

</details>


### [46] [Light-induced interlayer spacing dynamics via orbital phonon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11212)
*Wenwen Mao,Hanbyul Kim,Angel Rubio,Dongbin Shin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间耦合决定层状范德华过渡金属硫族化物（van der Waals transition metal dichalcogenides）的电子性质，而现有研究缺乏对光诱导层间距调控机制的系统理解。该文旨在探索以轨道选择性激发实现层间距动态调控的可行性及其微观机制。

Method: 采用实时含时密度泛函理论模拟三层 1T'-WSe2 与 1T'-WS2 的光致响应，并构建与晶格动力学耦合的有效 Lindblad 模型；同时配合不同层间距下的静态计算，分析层间距变化对电子结构的影响。

Result: 模拟显示层间距的收缩或膨胀取决于硫族元素 p 轨道（chalcogen p-orbital）密度在层间区域是被耗尽还是累积。1T'-WSe2 中的余弦式位移运动（cosine-like displacive motion）由单一有效激发态描述；而 1T'-WS2 中平衡层间距的移动则需要两个具有不同电声耦合（electron-phonon coupling）与弛豫通道的激发态。静态计算表明层间距变化可改变电子能隙并可能进入不同的电子相。

Conclusion: 该文将轨道重分布（orbital redistribution）、载流子弛豫（carrier relaxation）与层间呼吸运动（interlayer breathing motion）联系起来，确立轨道选择性光激发作为调控层状材料电子性质的可行途径。

Abstract: Interlayer coupling controls the electronic properties of layered van der Waals transition metal dichalcogenides. We investigate light-induced control of the interlayer spacing through orbital-selective excitation in trilayer 1T'-WSe2 and 1T'-WS2. Real-time time-dependent density functional theory simulations show that the interlayer spacing contracts or expands depending on whether chalcogen p-orbital density is depleted from or accumulated in the interlayer region. Effective Lindblad models coupled to the lattice dynamics reproduce these contrasting responses with simplified dynamics. A single effective excited state captures the cosine-like displacive motion in trilayer 1T'-WSe2, whereas the shift of the equilibrium spacing in trilayer 1T'-WS2 requires two excited states with different electron-phonon couplings and relaxation channels. Static calculations at varied interlayer spacings indicate that these spacing changes modify the electronic gaps and could access different electronic phases. These results connect orbital redistribution, carrier relaxation, and interlayer breathing motion, and establish orbital-selective optical excitation as a route to tuning the electronic properties of layered materials.

</details>


### [47] [Giant Magnetization Enhancement at Ultrathin Limit of an Orthoferrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11217)
*Jin Young Oh,Yaolong Xing,Seung Gyo Jeong,Sehwan Song,Woo-suk Noh,Valeria Lauter,Seongjae Choi,Seyoung Kwon,Jaeha Choi,Si Won Kim,Hong Hyun Jeon,Heemin Lee,Cheng-Tai Kuo,Jun-Sik Lee,Chanyoung Lee,Yeonkyu Lee,Jeehoon Kim,Heung-Sik Kim,Sungkyun Park,Sang Ho Oh,Woo Seok Choi*

  <summary>Details</summary>

TL;DR: 研究评估通用机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）能否自动完成晶体学开放数据库（COD）第三层级——物理合理性验证。对约31.7万条结构经M3GNet弛豫，99.0%收敛；但单一描述符阈值无法区分有效与无效结构，且不同模型存在特定误差模式，故MLIP弛豫是有效但依赖模型的筛选工具，宜多模型联合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体学开放数据库（COD）中晶体结构超过50万条，第三层级（物理合理性）验证长期依赖经验启发式（empirical heuristics）或专家判断，在数据库规模与新增结构沉积速度面前既慢又易错，因此需探索自动化方案。

Result: 314 153条（99.0%）在体积无大幅变化下收敛，多数条目的弛豫体积与沉积体积几乎一致。人工检查327条结构显示，无单一描述符阈值（能量、最大力或原子位移）能区分有效与无效结构。多模型比较揭示模型特异性失效：M3GNet扭曲环戊二烯基（cyclopentadienyl）及其他π配体，M3GNet与CHGNet扭曲噻吩（thiophene）与噻唑（thiazole）环，而PET系列模型可保持这些基元；所有模型均检出缺失氢原子，部分模型检出虚假氢、错误原子类型及两处此前未报告的坐标错误，且无单一模型能检出所有误差类型。

Conclusion: MLIP弛豫是一种有用但依赖模型（model-dependent）的筛选工具；收敛性与体积变化可作为简单判据识别不能确证沉积结构的弛豫，但因各模型失效模式互补、单一模型无法覆盖全部误差类型，结合多个模型优于依赖任何单一模型。

Abstract: The Crystallography Open Database (COD) contains over half a million experimentally determined crystal structures. Validation of crystallographic data proceeds at three hierarchical levels, the third of which assesses the physical plausibility of structures. This level has typically relied on empirical heuristics or expert judgment, which are slow and error-prone given the size of crystallographic databases and the rate at which new structures are deposited. Here we assess whether universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) can automate this level of validation. These structures were relaxed with M3GNet, and 314 153 (99.0\%) converged without a large change in cell volume. For most entries the relaxed and deposited volumes are nearly identical. Convergence and volume change thus provide simple criteria for identifying relaxations that do not confirm the deposited structure. A manual inspection of 327 structures showed that no single descriptor threshold (energy, largest force, or atomic displacement) separates valid from invalid structures. A comparison of M3GNet, CHGNet, PET-MAD and PET-OAM on 524 selected structures revealed model-specific failures: M3GNet distorts cyclopentadienyl and other $π$ ligands, and both M3GNet and CHGNet distort thiophene and thiazole rings, whereas the PET models preserve these motifs. All models detected missing hydrogen atoms, and at least some detected spurious hydrogens, incorrectly assigned atom types, and two previously unreported coordinate errors. No single model detected all error types. MLIP relaxation is therefore a useful but model-dependent screening tool, and combining several models is preferable to relying on any single one.

</details>

*Chun-Teh Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种二维线性弹性（linear elasticity）蜂窝结构：由单一不可压缩（incompressible）各向同性弹性固体的正六边形块体经可滑动理想界面连接，可实现体模量（bulk modulus）为零而剪切模量（shear modulus）保持较高，规范化剪切模量上确界 S > 0.8528 > 4/5，优于 Milton 构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松比（Poisson's ratio）接近 -1 仅意味着体模量远小于剪切模量。在固-孔（solid-void）结构中，释放膨胀的机制通常同时削弱抗剪切能力，使两种模量都变小；本文旨在证明二维线性弹性（two-dimensional linear elasticity）中这种剪切损失并非不可避免。

Method: 考虑由单一不可压缩各向同性弹性固体的正六边形块体构成的蜂窝，块体沿整个边由理想界面（ideal interfaces）连接，界面允许沿与边缘法线成一定倾角方向的相对滑动；块体的集体微转动（collective infinitesimal block rotations）产生精确膨胀机制。固-孔实现中用细固体板隔以孔洞替代界面，易弯曲但可传递轴向力。通过显式静力许可应力场（statically admissible stress field）与互补能量原理（complementary energy principle）给出保留剪切模量的严格下界，并用极限论证将该界推广至固-孔混合物。

Result: 蜂窝在体模量为零时保留其组分剪切模量的五分之四以上；此类混合物的规范化剪切模量上确界（supremum）S 满足 S > 0.8528 > 4/5，超过 Milton 构造所达到的值。

Conclusion: 此类膨胀材料（dilational materials）可自由膨胀或收缩而抵抗任何形状变化，有望用作承受热失配或膨胀失配同时传递剪切的夹层，以及在不改变形状的前提下改变尺寸的支架与可展开结构部件。

Abstract: A Poisson's ratio near -1 indicates only that the bulk modulus is small compared with the shear modulus. In solid-void structures, the mechanism that frees the dilation usually weakens the resistance to shear as well, resulting in both moduli becoming small. In two-dimensional linear elasticity, we demonstrate that this loss is not inevitable. We consider a honeycomb of regular hexagonal blocks of a single incompressible isotropic elastic solid, joined along their whole edges by ideal interfaces that allow relative sliding along a direction inclined to the edge normal. Collective infinitesimal block rotations produce an exact dilational mechanism, so the effective bulk modulus vanishes, while sixfold symmetry ensures isotropy. The interfaces, however, transmit traction along their whole length. In a solid-void realization, each interface is replaced with fine solid plates separated by void, which bend easily yet retain their capacity to transmit axial force. Such dilational materials, which expand or contract freely while resisting every change of shape, could serve as interlayers that accommodate thermal or swelling mismatch while still transmitting shear, and as components in stents and deployable structures that change size without changing shape. An explicit, statically admissible stress field and the complementary energy principle yield a rigorous lower bound on the retained shear modulus, and a limiting argument transfers this bound to the solid-void mixtures. The honeycomb retains more than four-fifths of its constituent's shear modulus at zero bulk modulus: the supremum S of the normalized shear modulus of such mixtures satisfies S > 0.8528 > 4/5, exceeding the value attained by Milton's construction.

</details>

*Jubair Hossan Abir,M. R. U. S. Shapon,S. S. B. Pallab,Tanvir Khan,Raihana Shams Islam,Saleh Hasan Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 Janus 半导体（2D Janus semiconductors）可同时整合自旋-谷耦合（spin-valley coupling）、压电性（piezoelectricity）与可调光学响应，为多功能纳米器件提供平台，因此需探索新型组分并明确其可调性。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），结合含自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的 Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof 泛函与 HSE06 杂化泛函（hybrid-functional）计算，并施加双轴应变（biaxial strain）分析能带、Berry 曲率（Berry curvature）与光学吸收。

Conclusion: 组分与应变可作为互补调控手段，用于定制 Cr 基 Janus 单层（Cr-based Janus monolayers）的谷电子学（valleytronic）、光电子学、机电与光催化应用；带边排列满足析氧反应（oxygen evolution reaction）要求但对析氢反应（hydrogen evolution reaction）驱动力不足，需进一步应变调控以实现全解水（overall water splitting）。

HSE06 hybrid-functional calculations retain the direct-gap character, yielding gaps of 1.46 eV for CrSiCN4 and 1.19 eV for CrGeCN4. Opposite out-of-plane spin character and Berry curvature emerge at K and K'. Biaxial strain reduces the respective gaps from 1.59 to 0.86 eV and 1.44 to 0.63 eV, drives direct-to-indirect transitions, and redshifts absorption while preserving valley spin contrast.
