Band-edge alignment satisfies the oxygen evolution reaction requirement but provides insufficient driving force for the hydrogen evolution reaction, motivating further strain-induced band-edge modulation toward overall water splitting. These results establish composition and strain as complementary controls for tailoring Cr-based Janus monolayers for valleytronic, optoelectronic, electromechanical, and photocatalytic applications.

</details>


### [52] [Data-Driven Variable-Exponent Analysis for Photoemission Yield Spectroscopy: An Autonomous Self-Diagnosing Framework Based on Integrated Residual Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11331)
*Shinjiro Yagyu,Takahiro Nagata,Yoshiyuki Nakajima*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于 1/n-Scan 法的数据驱动框架，直接在原始信号空间分析环境空气下的光电子产额谱（PYS），并融合 Akaike 权重与多项残差指标实现自诊断质量评估，可在无发射机制假设下自主识别单组分近似的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化材料发现（automated materials discovery）的推进，使从环境空气（ambient-air）PYS 数据中无监督地提取物理信息变得重要。传统固定指数分析与对数变换受异方差性（heteroscedasticity）困扰，在低信号区域估计不稳定。

Method: 采用基于 1/n-Scan 法的数据驱动分析框架，直接在原始信号空间运算，以规避对数变换带来的几何偏差（geometric bias）。同时集成自诊断质量评估系统，用 Akaike 权重（Akaike weights）量化估计不确定性，并配合归一化平均绝对误差（NMAE）、RMSE-to-MAE 比值（RMR）、Durbin-Watson（DW）统计量与宏观指标 ΔR² 等独立残差指标。

Result: 在空气环境中对不同程度掺杂的 Si 与多晶 Au 参考样品进行验证：重掺杂 p 型 Si 中两个具有不同阈值（thresholds）的发射组分重叠导致单组分近似失效，该现象被判别为统计异常——DW 低于临界值并伴随 ΔR² 上升，而 NMAE 与 RMR 仍正常；该结论由双组分拟合独立确认（ΔAIC ≈ 57，DW 由 0.8 恢复至 2.0）。重掺杂 n 型 Si 的渐进表面演化则被归类为单组分描述内的变化。

Conclusion: 该框架能够区分硬件相关的数据退化与物理模型失配，为闭环自主材料探索（closed-loop autonomous materials exploration）提供稳健且具备自诊断能力的分析引擎。

Abstract: Photoemission yield spectroscopy (PYS) is widely used for evaluating the electronic states of materials. As automated materials discovery advances, unsupervised extraction of physical information from ambient-air PYS data becomes important. Conventional fixed-exponent analyses and logarithmic transformations suffer from heteroscedasticity, which destabilizes estimation in low-signal regions. To address this, we propose a data-driven analysis framework based on the 1/n-Scan method, which operates directly in the original signal space, removing the geometric bias inherent in log-transform approaches. We further integrate a self-diagnostic quality-evaluation system that quantifies estimation uncertainty with Akaike weights, together with independent residual metrics--the normalized mean absolute error (NMAE), the RMSE-to-MAE ratio (RMR), the Durbin-Watson (DW) statistic, and a macroscopic metric ($ΔR^2$)--that distinguish hardware-related data degradation from a physical-model mismatch. Applying the framework to differently doped Si and a polycrystalline Au reference in air, we demonstrate autonomous detection, without assumptions on the emission mechanism, of the breakdown of the single-component approximation in heavily doped p-type Si, arising from the overlap of two emission components with different thresholds, as a statistical anomaly--a decrease in DW below its critical value with an auxiliary increase in $ΔR^2$, despite sound NMAE and RMR--independently confirmed by a two-component fit ($Δ$AIC $\approx$ 57, DW recovering from 0.8 to 2.0). For heavily doped n-type Si, the gradual surface evolution was classified as a change within the single-component description. This framework provides a robust, self-diagnosing analysis engine for closed-loop autonomous materials exploration.

</details>


### [53] [Generation, Accumulation, and Utilization of Data in the Automatically Controlled End-Station of a Soft X-ray Beamline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11476)
*Yasunobu Ando,Masashige Miyamamoto,Keita Hiromori,Takeru Nakashima,Masafumi Horio,Masato Kotsugi,Iwao Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出以数据为中心的研究框架，整合 PIONEER 自动化实验系统、OMNES 元数据管理系统与机器学习分析，实现高通量同步辐射 XAS 数据从生成、积累到利用的连续工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 正快速改变材料探索，但可靠的 AI 辅助研究依赖保留上下文、来源（provenance）与机器可读结构的实验数据；高通量同步辐射测量因而不仅需要自动化数据采集，还需将数据生成、积累与利用连接为连续工作流。

Method: 整合 PIONEER 系统（自动化样品处理、真空转移与扫描 XAS）、OMNES 与机器学习分析。PIONEER 在 NanoTerasu 的 BL08U 软 X 射线束线系统产生大量空间分辨光谱数据；数据存入云端 ARIM-mdx 数据系统，OMNES 管理样品、制备、测量条件、仪器设置、数据位置与分析历史等元数据，采用表格与 JSON 格式并按元数据结构存储、数值与单位分离，基于实验与分析过程间的因果关系以关系数据模型组织记录并可视化为图。

Result: OMNES 将样品、测量、原始数据、处理数据与分析结果关联，保留实验与分析来源并阐明结果如何由先前过程和数据推导；累积光谱可用机器学习方法处理（包括降维与材料分类），结果可回注 OMNES；摘要以已报道的 BN XAS 光谱 ML 分析作为可应用于束线数据的分析工作流示例。

Conclusion: 基于因果关系的数据组织方式可支持保留来源的高通量同步辐射数据的机器学习应用；该框架以 BN XAS 光谱分析为例展示了从束线数据采集到机器学习分析并回注数据的分析工作流。

Abstract: AI is rapidly transforming materials exploration, yet reliable AI-assisted research depends on experimental data that retains their context, provenance, and machine-readable structure. High-throughput synchrotron measurements therefore require more than automated data acquisition: data generation, accumulation, and utilization must be connected within a continuous workflow. Here, we present a data-centric research framework that integrates the PIONEER system, the OMNES, and ML analysis. The PIONEER system automates sample handling, vacuum transfer, and scanning XAS at the BL08U soft X-ray beamline of NanoTerasu, systematically generating large volumes of spatially resolved spectral data. Measurement and analysis files are stored in the cloud-based ARIM-mdx data system, while OMNES manages metadata describing samples, preparation procedures, measurement conditions, instrument settings, data locations, and analysis histories. Tabular and JavaScript Object Notation formats are used according to the metadata structure, with numerical values stored separately from units to facilitate machine processing. OMNES organizes these records based on causal relationships among experimental and analytical processes, linking samples, measurements, raw data, processed data, and analysis results through a relational data model and visualizing their relationships as a graph. This causality-based organization preserves experimental and analytical provenance and clarifies how each result was derived from the preceding processes and data. The accumulated spectra can subsequently be processed using ML methods, including dimensionality reduction and materials classification, and the resulting data can be registered back into OMNES. Previously reported ML analysis of BN XAS spectra is presented as an example of the analytical workflow that can be applied to data acquired at the beamline.

Method: 采用相邻铂层产生的宽带太赫兹自旋轨道力矩（spin-orbit torque）在α-Fe₂O₃中激发磁振子脉冲，并通过相位分辨太赫兹透射（phase-resolved terahertz transmission）进行探测。

Abstract: Ultrafast magnonics explores routes towards energy-efficient information processing by replacing electrons with pulses of terahertz magnons. Here, we demonstrate the launching of 0.4-picosecond magnon pulses in the altermagnetic insulator $α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ (hematite) using broadband terahertz spin-orbit torques by an adjacent platinum layer. Phase-resolved terahertz transmission reveals that these sub-picosecond pulses travel ballistically, coherently and non-dispersively at a constant velocity of 23 nm/ps, transport angular momentum and retain their temporal waveform shape over 160 nm, with an inferred relaxation length in hundreds of nanometers or more. Conclusion: 研究结果表明，FP 可作为一种简单有效的方法，通过缺陷工程（defect engineering）实现陶瓷材料室温塑性的提升。

Abstract: Ceramics are indispensable materials for modern engineering and high-technology applications. However, the intrinsic brittleness at room temperature can cause catastrophic failure and severely limit their widespread applications. Here, we report a flash processing (FP) method based on the coupling of electric and thermal fields to significantly improve the room-temperature plasticity of TiO2 ceramics with a large plastic compressive strain of 15.4% during micropillar compression. Our findings suggest that FP can be a simple and effective method for achieving improved room-temperature plasticity of ceramic materials through defect engineering.

</details>


### [57] [Probing $L_z$ Orbital Angular Momentum via Wavefunction-Interference-Induced Charge Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11633)
*Jonas Erhardt,Sven Schemmelmann,Fabian Schöttke,Cedric Schmitt,Lukas Gehrig,Bing Liu,Domenico Di Sante,Giorgio Sangiovanni,Jörg Schäfer,Peter Krüger,Markus Donath,Ralph Claessen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）的实空间方法，通过原子间干涉效应将轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum, OAM）的 $L_z$ 信息编码到局域态密度（local density of states, LDOS）的空间分布中，从而规避光电子二色性探测的伪影与限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OAM 表征对理解量子材料（如 valley Hall 与 Rashba 体系）及能带拓扑（band topology）至关重要；但传统基于光电子二色性（photoemission dichroism）的 OAM 探测易受干涉伪影影响，且主要局限于占据态（occupied states）。

Method: 利用三角晶格原子单层中的扫描隧道显微镜，结合 $L_z$ 轨道相位与相邻原子 Bloch 相位产生的干涉效应；该干涉使 LDOS 极大值位移至原子间不同的 Wyckoff 位置，从而将 $L_z$ 编码为特征性实空间 LDOS 图案。以 Tl/Si(111) 与 In/SiC(0001) 两种 $L_z$ 序列相反的二维量子材料为示例进行验证。

Result: 在两种体系中均观测到与各自拓扑相（topological phases）相对应的 $L_z$ 依赖 LDOS 积累，成功区分了对比鲜明的 $L_z$ 序列。

Conclusion: 确立了 $L_z$ 依赖的电荷局域化（charge localization）可作为三角晶格中能量孤立态（energetically isolated states）原子阻挫（atomic obstruction）的代理量，适用范围涵盖过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenides）。

Abstract: Orbital angular momentum (OAM) characterization is central to understanding quantum materials such as valley Hall and Rashba systems and, through its connection to Berry curvature, provides key insight into band topology. Conventional OAM detection via photoemission dichroism, however, is susceptible to interference artifacts and is largely restricted to occupied states. Here, we demonstrate a real-space approach to map the $L_z$ OAM character using scanning tunneling microscopy in triangular atomic monolayers. Unlike photoemission, this method exploits interference effects arising from the $L_z$ orbital phase and the Bloch phase associated with neighboring atoms. This shifts the local density of states (LDOS) maxima to distinct Wyckoff positions between atoms, encoding $L_z$ information into characteristic real-space LDOS patterns. We exemplify this approach in 2D quantum materials with contrasting $L_z$ sequences, namely Tl/Si(111) and In/SiC(0001) monolayers, identifying $L_z$-dependent LDOS accumulation associated with distinct topological phases in both systems. Our results establish $L_z$-dependent charge localization as a proxy to atomic obstruction for energetically isolated states in triangular lattices, including transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [58] [The role of density in shaping electron trapping in amorphous silicon nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11643)
*Jonathon Cottom,Emilia Olsson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在固定化学计量比（stoichiometry）条件下，密度（density）通过孔隙率（porosity）与三配位硅前驱体（three-coordinate Si precursor）供给量调控非晶氮化硅（amorphous silicon nitride）的电子俘获（electron trapping）混合构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶氮化硅的电子性质随工艺变化，而工艺往往同时改变化学计量比与密度，二者贡献难以分离；因此需将密度与化学计量比解耦，以建立薄膜结构与电子俘获之间的关联。

Method: 构建并分析499种化学计量构型（stoichiometric configurations），密度范围为2.86–3.19 g cm⁻³；统计孔隙率、可及空隙分数（accessible void fraction）、内部表面积与自由三配位Si前驱体供给，并区分Si悬挂键（Si dangling bonds, K centres）、诱导K中心（induced-K）与极化子（polaronic）俘获通道。

Result: 密度降低使可及空隙分数由8.4%升至12.6%，内部表面积扩大，自由三配位Si前驱体供给增加三倍以上；既有K中心俘获占比由9%升至29%，而诱导K与极化子俘获下降。中性前驱体指纹（neutral precursor fingerprint）可预测留出构型的俘获混合构成并重现其密度依赖；局域补偿（local compensation）控制K中心激活，电子竞争（electronic competition）决定俘获选择，键应变（bond strain）预测极化子弛豫或诱导K形成。

Conclusion: 在固定化学计量比下，密度通过孔隙率与前驱体可用性控制俘获混合构成；各俘获路径涉及不同网络体积，但在整个密度范围内保持其特征深度与弛豫能。

Abstract: The electronic properties of amorphous silicon nitride vary with processing, which often changes stoichiometry and density together. Separating their contributions is essential to connect film structure with electron trapping. Across 499 stoichiometric configurations spanning 2.86-3.19g cm$^{-3}$, porosity controls the trapping mixture. Lower density increases accessible void fraction from 8.4 to 12.6%, expands internal surface and more than triples the supply of free three-coordinate Si precursors. Capture at pre-existing Si dangling bonds (K centres) rises from 9 to 29%, while induced-K and polaronic trapping decline. A neutral precursor fingerprint predicts the trapping mixture in held-out configurations and reproduces its density dependence. Local compensation controls K-centre activation, electronic competition selects capture, and bond strain predicts polaronic relaxation or induced-K formation. Routes involve distinct network volumes but retain their characteristic depths and relaxation energies across the density range. At fixed stoichiometry, density controls the trapping mixture through porosity and precursor availability.

</details>

Conclusion: 以 CO2 为前驱体的热丝金属氧化物沉积可用于制备 WO3 外延薄膜，所得薄膜兼具可见光透明与高能区强吸收的光学特征，为 WO3 薄膜的沉积提供了一种可行途径。

Abstract: The deposition of epitaxial films of WO3 by hot-filament metal-oxide deposition using CO2 as a precursor was demonstrated. The films were obtained at a low growth rate of 0.25 nm/min. </details>


### [60] [Robust skyrmion nucleation and transport via composition gradient in rare earth-transition metal ferrimagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11682)
*Pietro Diona,Luca Maranzana,Sergey Artyukhin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用稀土-过渡金属合金（rare-earth–transition-metal alloys）中的空间成分梯度（compositional gradient），通过图案化激光写入（patterned laser writing）实现磁斯格明子（magnetic skyrmions）的确定性成核与可控输运，并同时抑制斯格明子霍尔效应（skyrmion Hall effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁斯格明子多依赖局域激发方式（如激光加热、自旋轨道力矩（spin-orbit torques）或人工缺陷）成核，其可控输运常受斯格明子霍尔效应限制。该研究旨在通过成分梯度设计同时解决成核与输运控制的难题。

Method: 在稀土-过渡金属合金中，利用图案化激光写入引入空间成分梯度，使材料跨越磁化补偿点（magnetization compensation point），从而定义具有相反磁化方向的区域；再施加垂直方向磁场选择性反转磁化以生成斯格明子。同时，同一成分设计构建了调谐至自旋角动量补偿点（spin angular momentum compensation point）的通道。

Result: 在自旋角动量补偿点通道中，斯格明子可被自旋轨道力矩以高速度驱动，且斯格明子霍尔效应消失。此外，局部成分变化充当钉扎位点（pinning sites），实现阈值控制的俘获与释放。

Conclusion: 稀土-过渡金属合金中的空间成分梯度可同时实现磁斯格明子的确定性成核与可控输运，为抑制斯格明子霍尔效应及实现阈值调控的钉扎行为提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are typically nucleated via localized excitation methods, such as laser heating, spin-orbit torques, or engineered defects, and their controlled transport is often limited by the skyrmion Hall effect. Here, we demonstrate that spatial gradients in rare-earth--transition-metal alloys, achievable through patterned laser writing, enable both deterministic nucleation and controlled transport. The strategy leverages engineered compositional gradient to cross the magnetization compensation point, defining regions with opposite magnetization. An out-of-plane magnetic field selectively reverses the magnetization, generating a skyrmion. Crucially, the same compositional design enables a channel tuned to the spin angular momentum compensation point, where skyrmions are driven by spin-orbit torques at high velocity with vanishing skyrmion Hall effect. Local composition variations act as pinning sites, enabling threshold-controlled trapping and release.

</details>


### [61] [Mode-selective electron-phonon coupling drives charge density waves in the kagome metals YRu$_3$Si$_2$ and LaRu$_3$Si$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11689)
*Wenqian Tu,Run Lv,Dingfu Shao,Yuping Sun,Wenjian Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过机器学习力场驱动的分子动力学结合模式分辨电声耦合分析，揭示了YRu$_3$Si$_2$与LaRu$_3$Si$_2$中创纪录高温电荷密度波（CDW）源于模式选择性电声耦合与非谐声子效应，并给出二者CDW形成与熔化的统一微观图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YRu$_3$Si$_2$与LaRu$_3$Si$_2$拥有kagome金属中已报道最高的CDW转变温度（$T_{\mathrm{CDW}}\approx 800$与400~K），但其微观机制长期未解，尤其是如此高转变温度的来源尚不明确。

Method: 将机器学习力场（machine-learned force field）驱动的分子动力学所提取的非谐声子重整化（anharmonic phonon renormalization），与模式分辨电声耦合（electron-phonon coupling, EPC）分析相结合，并辅以化学键分析。

Result: CDW由模式选择性EPC驱动：Ru面外声子模式耦合Ru-$d_{xz}/d_{yz}$与Si-$p_x/p_y$轨道，声子线宽在$Q_{\mathrm{CDW}}$处尖锐峰值，而无特征的电子磁化率排除了费米面嵌套（Fermi-surface nesting）；非谐声子谱可同时重现实验的$Q_{\mathrm{CDW}}$与$T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$，分子动力学模拟在实空间可视化了CDW熔化；Y$^{3+}$半径更小强化Ru-Ru键并提升晶格刚性，解释了YRu$_3$Si$_2$约两倍高的$T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了kagome金属YRu$_3$Si$_2$与LaRu$_3$Si$_2$中CDW形成与熔化的统一微观图像，指明模式选择性电声耦合与非谐性在高温CDW中的关键作用。

Abstract: YRu$_3$Si$_2$ and LaRu$_3$Si$_2$ host the highest charge density wave (CDW) transition temperatures ever reported in kagome metals, $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}\approx 800$ and 400~K, with propagation vectors $Q_{\mathrm{CDW}}\approx(1/2,0,0)$ and $(1/4,0,0)$, respectively. The microscopic mechanism behind these record values has remained unresolved. Here, we combine anharmonic phonon renormalization extracted from molecular dynamics driven by a machine-learned force field with mode-resolved electron-phonon coupling (EPC) analysis to identify this mechanism. The CDW is driven by mode-selective EPC: the Ru out-of-plane phonon modes couple the Ru-$d_{xz}/d_{yz}$ and Si-$p_x/p_y$ orbitals, and the phonon linewidth peaks sharply at $Q_{\mathrm{CDW}}$, while the featureless electronic susceptibility rules out Fermi-surface nesting. Phonon anharmonicity melts the CDW, and the anharmonic phonon spectra reproduce both $Q_{\mathrm{CDW}}$ and $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$ in good agreement with experiments. Molecular dynamics simulations also visualize the CDW melting in real space. Chemical bonding analysis further shows that the smaller Y$^{3+}$ radius strengthens the Ru-Ru bonds, enhancing lattice rigidity and accounting for the factor-of-two higher $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$ of YRu$_3$Si$_2$. Our results establish a unified microscopic picture of CDW formation and melting in kagome metals YRu$_3$Si$_2$ and LaRu$_3$Si$_2$.

</details>


### [62] [Predicting plasmonic pole representations of complex dielectric spectra using convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11698)
*Dario A. Leon*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network, CNN）的框架，借助多极 Padé 表示（multipole-Padé representation）将介电谱（dielectric spectra）直接反演为等离激元极点结构（plasmonic pole structure），且仅用随机参数生成的合成谱训练即可泛化到第一性原理与实验谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从介电谱中提取能量位置、展宽与谱重等谱学性质对理解多体物理中的集体电子激发至关重要，而传统方法依赖非线性拟合（nonlinear fitting），计算开销大且对初始化与拟合选择高度敏感。

Method: 构建卷积神经网络框架，以多极 Padé 表示下的解析表达式生成随机参数的合成谱作为唯一训练数据，而非使用特定材料数据库；网络学习谱与其特征之间的一般映射，实现单次前向传播（single forward pass）下的直接反演。

Result: 经合成谱训练的网络可泛化至复杂的第一性原理（first-principles）与实验介电谱，并能以高精度提取底层极点参数。

Conclusion: 该方法为传统非线性拟合提供了高效且稳健的替代方案，支持跨材料与应用的高通量、自动化介电谱分析。

Abstract: Extracting spectral properties such as energy position, broadening, and spectral weight from dielectric spectra is critical for interpreting collective electronic excitations in many-body physics. Conventional approaches typically rely on nonlinear fitting procedures that can be computationally demanding and highly sensitive to initialization and fitting choices. In this work, we develop a convolutional neural-network framework for the direct inversion of dielectric spectra into their underlying plasmonic pole structures using a multipole-Padé representation. Rather than training on a constrained database of spectra associated with a specific set of materials, the network is trained entirely on synthetic spectra generated from the analytical multipole-Padé expression with randomized parameters. This enables the network to learn the general mapping between the spectra and their features in an unbiased way, without requiring large, material-specific datasets derived from real materials. We demonstrate that the synthetically trained network generalizes to complex first-principles and experimental dielectric spectra, extracting the underlying pole parameters with high accuracy in a single forward pass. This approach provides an efficient and robust alternative to conventional nonlinear fitting, enabling high-throughput, automated analysis of dielectric spectra across diverse materials and applications.

</details>


### [63] [Interlayer ferromagnetic order revealed through pressure-enhanced interlayer exchange coupling in Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11731)
*Riku Iimori,Takashi Kimura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过静水压（hydrostatic pressure）选择性调控层间耦合，在 Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ 单晶中识别出两个特征温度 $T_{\rm inf1}$ 与 $T_{\rm inf2}$，其压力依赖相反，且层间铁磁序可由微观层间交换耦合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德瓦尔斯铁磁体（van der Waals ferromagnets）虽为层状晶体结构却具有有限层间磁耦合，该耦合对磁性质起关键作用，但其微观机制与可预测性尚不明确。

Method: 对高质量 Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ 单晶进行磁化测量（magnetization measurements），并以静水压（hydrostatic pressure）选择性调控层间耦合；结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）分析层间交换相互作用。

Result: 在居里温度（Curie temperature）以下识别出 $T_{\rm inf1}$ 与 $T_{\rm inf2}$ 两个特征温度，二者压力依赖相反：$T_{\rm inf1}$ 关联热激活畴壁脱钉（thermally activated domain-wall depinning），$T_{\rm inf2}$ 反映层间铁磁序形成；两者压力依赖显著，而层内铁磁序对应的居里温度几乎不随压力变化。第一性原理计算显示压力诱导层间交换作用显著增强，并可合理重现 $T_{\rm inf2}$ 及其压力依赖。

Conclusion: 层间磁有序的出现可由微观层间交换耦合预测，从而为理解与设计层状磁性材料中的层间耦合现象建立了预测性框架（predictive framework）。

Abstract: Van der Waals ferromagnets possess finite interlayer magnetic coupling despite their layered crystal structures, and this coupling plays an important role in determining their magnetic properties. In this study, we performed magnetization measurements on high-quality Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ single crystals while selectively tuning the interlayer coupling using hydrostatic pressure. We identified two characteristic temperatures, $T_{\rm inf1}$ and $T_{\rm inf2}$, below the Curie temperature with opposite pressure dependences: $T_{\rm inf1}$ is associated with thermally activated domain-wall depinning, whereas $T_{\rm inf2}$ reflects the formation of interlayer ferromagnetic order. Both temperatures exhibit pronounced pressure dependences, in sharp contrast to the nearly pressure-independent Curie temperature associated with intralayer ferromagnetic order. First-principles calculations revealed a substantial pressure-induced enhancement of the interlayer exchange interaction and reasonably reproduced $T_{\rm inf2}$ and its pressure dependence. These results demonstrate that the emergence of interlayer magnetic ordering can be predicted from the microscopic interlayer exchange coupling, establishing a predictive framework for understanding and designing interlayer coupling phenomena in layered magnetic materials.

</details>


### [64] [A General Algorithm for Minimal Uncoupled Stress--Strain Calculations of Elastic Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11733)
*S. I. Simak,Utkarsh Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于稀疏结构（sparsity structure）的通用算法，用最少数量的应变构型（strain configurations）从第一性原理（first-principles）应力-应变计算中提取二阶弹性常数（second-order elastic constants），无需目标常数间的代数耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常需为不同晶体对称性分别指定应变集，且可能存在代数耦合或构型冗余。该工作旨在以算法化方式统一构造最小且无耦合的应变集，并实现对最小性的精确验证。

Method: 将对称约化后的弹性刚度矩阵（elastic stiffness matrix）视为辅助二元矩阵，依据其稀疏结构生成可行应变模式；六个独立弹性常数各取自单一应力分量，同一形变产生的附加应力关系用于内部一致性检验。由于六维应变空间仅含 2^6-1=63 个非空应变模式，可对每种弹性对称类型穷举验证最小性。

Result: 该方法在既有高效应变集为最小的情形下可复现这些集合，并在部分对称类中给出优于既有列表的改进集合；推导出覆盖 230 个晶体空间群（crystallographic space groups）全部弹性对称类型的显式应变集，并与已 established 方法进行精度与计算效率的基准对比。

Conclusion: 该构造为弹性常数提取提供了可验证最小化的通用方案；基于应力-应变导数（stress-strain derivatives）的表述使其可借助一致采样的系综平均热力学应力（ensemble-averaged thermodynamic stresses）扩展至有限温度。

Abstract: We have developed a general algorithm for constructing the minimum number of strain configurations required to extract second-order elastic constants from first-principles stress-strain calculations without algebraic coupling between the constants targeted for extraction. The method operates on the sparsity structure of the symmetry-reduced elastic stiffness matrix, represented by an auxiliary binary matrix, and generates admissible strain patterns algorithmically rather than prescribing separate strain sets for individual crystal symmetries. Each independent elastic constant is obtained from a single stress component, while additional stress relations generated by the same deformation provide internal consistency checks. Because the six-dimensional strain space contains only 2^6-1=63 non-empty strain patterns, the minimality of the resulting sets can be verified exactly for every elastic-symmetry type considered. The construction reproduces established high-efficiency strain sets in cases where they are minimal and yields improved sets for symmetry classes in which previously tabulated schemes require additional configurations. Because the formulation is based on stress-strain derivatives, it can also be readily extended to finite temperatures using consistently sampled ensemble-averaged thermodynamic stresses. Explicit strain sets are derived for all elastic-symmetry types spanning the 230 crystallographic space groups, and their accuracy and computational efficiency are benchmarked against established approaches.

</details>


### [65] [Can end-to-end learning from raw electronic structure explain magnetic anisotropy?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11742)
*Rafał Topolnicki,Jan Navrátil,Piotr Błoński*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究检验了直接从电子谱（electronic spectra）学习自旋—轨道（spin–orbit）性质时，模型精度相当但学习到的电子依赖可能不同的问题。通过比较两种架构预测磁各向异性能（MAE），并结合可解释性与受控扰动，指出可信的微观解释需依赖多方面的收敛证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型即使预测精度相近，也可能学到不同的电子依赖关系，仅凭精度不足以判断其学习到的谱学关系是否具有物理相关性。研究以原子尺度磁体（atomic-scale magnets）中的磁各向异性为对象，检验学习到的谱学关系的物理意义。

Method: 对比双向门控循环单元（bidirectional gated recurrent unit）与一维卷积神经网络（one-dimensional convolutional neural network），二者均以自旋和轨道分辨的标量相对论态密度（scalar-relativistic densities of states, SR-DOS）为输入预测磁各向异性能（magnetic anisotropy energy, MAE）。采用 Shapley 加性解释（Shapley additive explanations）识别共享谱特征，将二阶微扰理论（second-order perturbation theory, PT2）导出的 MAE 贡献加入输入，并进行受控谱扰动。

Result: 尽管域内（in-domain, ID）精度相当，两种架构学到的谱依赖仅部分重叠。Shapley 分析识别出与 PT2 一致的合理自旋—轨道耦合路径相关的共享谱特征。加入 PT2 导出的 MAE 贡献对域内性能影响甚微，但可改善跨域迁移（transfer beyond the training domain），且增益因架构而异。受控谱扰动显示，相似的归因模式并不意味着预测 MAE 对谱权重具有相同的函数依赖。

Conclusion: 端到端学习（end-to-end learning）能够识别磁各向异性的候选电子特征，但其可信的微观解释须依赖预测性能、模型对比、物理理论、受控谱干预以及分布偏移（distribution shift）下评估等多方面证据的收敛。

Abstract: Machine-learning models trained directly on electronic spectra can predict spin--orbit-driven properties, yet models with comparable predictive accuracy may learn different electronic dependencies. We examine this problem using magnetic anisotropy in atomic-scale magnets and a validation strategy designed to assess the physical relevance of learned spectral relationships. We compare a bidirectional gated recurrent unit and a one-dimensional convolutional neural network, both trained to predict magnetic anisotropy energy (MAE) from spin- and orbital-resolved scalar-relativistic densities of states (SR-DOS). Despite comparable in-domain (ID) accuracy, the architectures learn only partly overlapping spectral dependencies. Shapley additive explanations identify spectral features shared between the two architectures that can be associated with plausible spin--orbit-coupling pathways consistent with second-order perturbation theory (PT2). Adding PT2-derived MAE contributions to the model inputs has little effect on ID performance but can improve transfer beyond the training domain, with gains differing between architectures. Controlled spectral perturbations further demonstrate that similar attribution patterns do not imply the same functional dependence of the predicted MAE on spectral weight. The contrasting responses provide a functional context for architecture-dependent out-of-domain transfer. End-to-end learning can identify candidate electronic signatures of magnetic anisotropy. Their credible microscopic interpretation, however, rests on convergent evidence from predictive performance, model comparison, physical theory, controlled spectral interventions, and evaluation under distribution shift.

</details>


### [66] [Photoinduced Odd- and Mixed-Parity Magnetism in Collinear Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11807)
*Jin-Yang Li,Ruo-Yu Ning,Ying Zhang,Qiu-Qiu Li,Si Li,Wen-Li Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合 Floquet 理论与第一性原理计算，证明光照可在常规共线反铁磁体（collinear antiferromagnet，如单层 CoS、CoSe）中诱导奇宇称（odd-parity）与混合宇称（mixed-parity）磁性，并产生有限的反常霍尔响应，从而为补偿反铁磁体中的自旋劈裂调控提供普适策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁性（altermagnetism）虽已作为超越铁磁与反铁磁范式的磁序出现，但在完全补偿的共线磁体中实现奇宇称与混合宇称磁性仍是挑战。该研究旨在寻找在常规共线反铁磁体中构筑此类非常规宇称磁性的可行途径。

Method: 以单层 CoS 与 CoSe 为代表性体系，将 Floquet 理论（Floquet theory）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）相结合，考察圆偏振光（circularly polarized light, CPL）、椭圆偏振光（elliptically polarized light, EPL）、单轴应变（uniaxial strain）及外加电场的联合作用。

Result: 圆偏振光诱导奇宇称 f 波自旋劈裂，椭圆偏振光诱导奇宇称 p 波自旋劈裂；单轴应变结合 CPL 可实现 p 波态。电场与 CPL 或 EPL 共同作用可将自旋劈裂转变为混合宇称形式。光照导致的 PT 对称性破缺（$\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry breaking）与自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）共同产生有限的反常霍尔响应（anomalous Hall response）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种在补偿反铁磁体中工程化奇宇称与混合宇称自旋劈裂的通用策略，为光控电子与磁现象提供路径。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, characterized by compensated collinear magnetic moments in real space and momentum-dependent spin splitting with even-parity symmetry, has recently emerged as a distinct form of magnetic order beyond the conventional ferro- and antiferromagnetic paradigms. However, realizing odd- and mixed-parity magnetism in fully compensated collinear magnets remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate that light irradiation can induce odd- and mixed-parity magnetism in conventional collinear antiferromagnets. Taking monolayer CoS and CoSe as representative systems, and combining Floquet theory with first-principles calculations, we show that circularly polarized light (CPL) induces odd-parity $f$-wave spin splitting, whereas elliptically polarized light (EPL) induces odd-parity $p$-wave spin splitting. Uniaxial strain combined with CPL provides an alternative route to realizing the $p$-wave state. More remarkably, applying an electric field together with CPL or EPL transforms the spin splitting into mixed-parity forms. The light-induced breaking of $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry, together with spin-orbit coupling, further gives rise to a finite anomalous Hall response. Our work establishes a versatile strategy for engineering odd- and mixed-parity spin splitting in compensated antiferromagnets, offering a route toward light-controlled electronic and magnetic phenomena.

</details>


### [67] [Solution-Based Synthesis of Fe-Co-Ni Prussian Blue Analogue Powders: A Comparative Structural, Spectroscopic and Thermal Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11830)
*Larissa de O. Garcia,Lindiomar Borges de Avila,Mengmeng Wang,Yann Garcia,Christian K. Muller,Flavio Abreu Araujo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以无添加剂共沉淀法合成 Fe/Co/Ni 三种普鲁士蓝类似物（Prussian Blue Analogues, PBAs）并进行系统表征，结果表明三者的差异不能仅归因于过渡金属种类，前驱体氧化态与洗涤效率同样影响产物组成与热行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对普鲁士蓝（Prussian Blue, PB）及其 Co、Ni 类似物之间的差异，探讨这些差异是否可单纯归因于过渡金属（transition metal）身份的替换。该问题关系到 PBA 类材料组成与性能的可控性与可比性。

Method: 采用无添加剂水相共沉淀（additive-free aqueous co-precipitation）路线，以 K$_4$[Fe(CN)$_6$] 分别与 FeCl$_3$、CoCl$_2$、NiCl$_2$ 反应制备 Fe-PBA、Co-PBA 与 Ni-PBA 三种粉末。表征手段包括 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）、ATR-FTIR 与拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）、FEG-SEM/EDS 以及 TG/SDTA。

Result: 三种组成均呈立方氰基桥连 PBA 骨架（cubic cyanide-bridged framework），表观晶格参数（lattice parameter）分别为 Fe-PBA 10.10 ± 0.02、Co-PBA 10.01 ± 0.02、Ni-PBA 10.10 ± 0.03 Å。ATR-FTIR 主 C≡N 伸缩振动带沿 Fe–Co–Ni 序列由 2062 移至 2071 与 2087 cm$^{-1}$，表明局域氰基环境随组成改变。Fe-PBA 的钾含量低于 Co、Ni 样品，且低温质量损失显著更大。三种衍射图谱中均出现归属为结晶 KCl 的尖锐反射，Ni-PBA 另有未归属反射，指示至少还存在一个额外晶相。

Conclusion: Fe、Co、Ni 基 PBA 之间的差异不能仅归因于过渡金属身份；前驱体氧化态（precursor oxidation state）与洗涤效率（washing efficiency）亦影响所得粉末的组成与热响应。

Abstract: Prussian Blue (Fe-PBA) and its Co and Ni analogues (Co-PBA and Ni-PBA) were synthesized by an additive-free aqueous co-precipitation route using K$_4$[Fe(CN)$_6$] and FeCl$_3$, CoCl$_2$, or NiCl$_2$, respectively. The three powders were systematically compared by X-ray diffraction, ATR-FTIR and Raman spectroscopy, FEG-SEM/EDS, and TG/SDTA. All compositions exhibit the characteristic cubic cyanide-bridged PBA framework, with apparent lattice parameters of 10.10 +/- 0.02, 10.01 +/- 0.02, and 10.10 +/- 0.03 Angstrom for Fe-PBA, Co-PBA, and Ni-PBA, respectively. The dominant C$\equiv$N stretching band in ATR-FTIR shifts from 2062 to 2071 and 2087 cm$^{-1}$ across the Fe-Co-Ni series, indicating composition-dependent changes in the local cyanide environment. Fe-PBA contains less potassium than the Co- and Ni-containing powders and exhibits a substantially larger low-temperature mass loss. Sharp reflections assigned to crystalline KCl are observed in all three diffraction patterns, while additional unassigned reflections in Ni-PBA indicate the presence of at least one further crystalline phase. These results show that differences among Fe-, Co-, and Ni-based PBAs cannot be attributed solely to transition-metal identity, as precursor oxidation state and washing efficiency also influence the composition and thermal response of the resulting powders.

</details>


### [68] [Benchmarking Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials for Temperature-Dependent Elasticity of Binary and High-Entropy Refractory Carbides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11878)
*Miroslav Lebeda,Jan Drahokoupil,Šimon Svoboda,Petr Vlčák*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作针对七种碳化物（五种二元、两种高熵）在300–1200 K范围内，以一致的从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics, AIMD）数据为基准，系统评测了九种通用机器学习原子间势（universal machine-learning interatomic potentials, uMLIPs）预测温度相关弹性性质的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温条件下难熔碳化物（refractory carbides）弹性性质的准确预测对性能评估很重要，但AIMD计算成本高，难以跨成分与温度系统研究；uMLIPs虽高效，但其在该任务上的准确性尚未充分确立，因此需要基准评测。

Method: 以一致AIMD参考数据为基准，对五种二元碳化物和两种高熵（high-entropy, HE）碳化物在300–1200 K进行九种uMLIPs的评测；采用应力–应变分子动力学（stress-strain molecular dynamics）获取弹性常数及体模量、剪切模量与杨氏模量，显式采样热原子运动及非谐效应（anharmonic effects），并将绝对弹性性质与归一化热软化（thermal softening）分别评估。

Result: MACE-MH-1总体平均绝对百分比误差最低（5.7%）；MACE-MH-1与DPA4-Mini对热软化的再现最准确，平均偏差分别为2.5与2.3个百分点。所有模型普遍低估刚度，多数模型相对AIMD参考的较大平衡体积可能是原因之一；C12的模型依赖性误差最大；高熵碳化物的精度与二元碳化物相当，未见化学复杂度带来的精度损失；精度随过渡金属成分变化，第V族碳化物（尤其TaC）最具挑战。

Conclusion: 结果识别出适用于碳化物有限温度弹性的有前景的预训练模型，并表明准确的绝对刚度与热软化必须分别评估。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of temperature-dependent elasticity is important for assessing refractory carbides under high-temperature conditions, but the computational cost of ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) limits systematic investigations across compositions and temperatures. Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) offer an efficient alternative, yet their accuracy for this task remains insufficiently established. Here, we benchmark nine uMLIPs against consistent AIMD reference data for five binary and two high-entropy (HE) carbides between 300 and 1200 K. Elastic constants and the corresponding bulk, shear, and Young's moduli are obtained using stress-strain molecular dynamics, explicitly sampling thermal atomic motion and anharmonic effects beyond thermal expansion alone. We assess absolute elastic properties and normalized thermal softening separately. MACE-MH-1 achieves the lowest overall mean absolute percentage error (5.7%). MACE-MH-1 and DPA4-Mini reproduce thermal softening most accurately, with mean deviations of 2.5 and 2.3 percentage points, respectively. All models generally underestimate stiffness, with larger equilibrium volumes relative to the AIMD reference likely contributing to this trend for most models. C12 exhibits the largest model-dependent errors. The HE carbides are described with accuracy comparable to that of the binary carbides, indicating no apparent accuracy penalty from chemical complexity. Accuracy instead varies with transition-metal composition, with group-V carbides, particularly TaC, presenting the greatest challenge. These results identify promising pretrained models for finite-temperature elasticity in carbides and show why accurate absolute stiffness and thermal softening must be assessed independently.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond special quasirandom structures: free energies from energy cumulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11882)
*Yann L. Müller,Anirudh Raju Natarajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出自由能的能量累积量（energy cumulants）无穷展开，将传统无序相自由能近似视为展开的一阶项，并通过随机构型能量估计高阶累积量，从而在无序相稳定性预测中纳入短程有序（short-range order）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测无序相（disordered phase）的有限温度稳定性需依赖其自由能，但传统做法仅将特殊准随机结构（special quasirandom structure）的能量与理想构型熵（ideal configurational entropy）组合，忽略了降温过程中形成的短程有序，导致近似偏差。

Method: 作者将自由能展开为能量累积量的无穷级数（infinite expansion in energy cumulants），指出传统近似仅对应展开首项；高阶累积量由随机排布（random arrangements）的能量估计，无需蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）或分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟。

Result: 在九元难熔（refractory）体系中，加入二阶累积量使自由能误差降低约一个数量级；该展开可给出含有序—无序转变（order–disorder transitions）、缺陷浓度与短程有序的相图；作者进一步用基础原子间势（foundation interatomic potential）在26种元素范围内筛选锂过量岩盐（lithium-excess rocksalt）氧化物，寻找富含锂渗流所需Li₄团簇的可合成无序相。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于任意晶格与化学组成，使无序相进入常规筛选（routine screening）的可及范围，为无序相稳定性的高通量评估提供了免模拟的展开框架。

Abstract: Predicting the finite-temperature stability of a disordered phase requires its free energy. It is traditionally approximated by combining the energy of a special quasirandom structure (a small cell mimicking a random alloy) with the ideal configurational entropy. This approximation neglects the short-range order that develops on cooling. In this study, we show that the conventional approximation is the first term of an infinite expansion of the free energy in energy cumulants. Higher-order cumulants are estimated from energies of random arrangements, without Monte Carlo or molecular dynamics. In a nine-element refractory system, adding the second cumulant reduced free energy errors by about an order of magnitude. The expansion gives phase diagrams with order--disorder transitions, defect concentrations, and short-range order. To demonstrate its utility, we used a foundation interatomic potential to screen lithium-excess rocksalt oxides across 26 elements for synthesizable disordered phases rich in the Li$_4$ clusters needed for lithium percolation. The approach applies to any lattice and chemistry, and brings disordered phases within reach of routine screening.

</details>


### [70] [Comprehensive study of massively overlapping cascades in common elemental metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11910)
*Aslak Fellman,Jesper Byggmästar,Fredric Granberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 对21种简单晶格金属开展大规模重叠级联（overlapping cascades）分子动力学模拟，结果显示模拟结果对势函数选择高度敏感，且不同晶格类型形成特征性缺陷结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统比较不同晶格结构金属在辐照下的缺陷演化行为，并考察经典解析势与机器学习势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）对大规模重叠级联模拟结果的影响。

Method: 在Be、Al、Ti等21种FCC、BCC、HCP金属中，对每种元素使用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟2000个累积5 keV级联，所用势函数包括多种经典解析势及部分元素的机器学习势。

Result: 重叠级联模拟结果对周期表上势函数的选择非常敏感。FCC金属普遍形成堆垛层错四面体（stacking fault tetrahedra），并常含间隙型Shockley不全位错与Frank位错；BCC金属典型形成间隙型1/2⟨111⟩位错，但在V和Nb中也观察到空位型1/2⟨111⟩位错；HCP金属形成以a型位错为主的复杂结构，可为空位型或间隙型。

Conclusion: 饱和缺陷浓度与材料基本性质之间的关联得到探讨，表明缺陷形成特征与晶格结构及势函数密切相关。

Abstract: Massively overlapping cascades simulations were carried out in 21 elemental metals: Be, Al, Ti, V, Cr, Fe, Co, Ni, Cu, Zr, Nb, Mo, Rh, Pd, Ag, Hf, Ta, W, Pt, Au and Pb. These elements have simple FCC, BCC and HCP lattice structures. For each element, 2000 cumulative 5 keV cascades were simulated using molecular dynamics. The overlapping cascades were simulated using various classical analytical interatomic potentials as well as with machine-learning interatomic potentials for many of the elements. The general conclusion is that results from massively overlapping cascades simulations are very sensitive to the choice of interatomic potential across the periodic table. Furthermore, we see that FCC metals all form stacking fault tetrahedra due to irradiation, and also typically include interstitial type Shockley partial and Frank type dislocations. In BCC materials typically form interstitial 1/2$\langle$1 1 1$\rangle$ dislocations, but vacancy type 1/2$\langle$1 1 1$\rangle$ dislocations were also observed in V and Nb. HCP materials tend to form complex dislocation structures mainly consisting of a-type dislocations that can be of both vacancy or interstitial type. Correlations between saturated defect concentrations and fundamental underlying material properties are explored.

</details>


### [71] [Homogeneous Charge Relaxation in Organic Mixed Ionic-Electronic Conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11977)
*Silan Zhang,Scott T. Keene,Junfu Tian,Georgina E. Lindop,Simon M. Fairclough,Zhengkang Qu,Xian Wei Chua,Christopher Slaughter,Christina J. Kousseff,Caterina Ducati,Iain McCulloch,Henning Sirringhaus,Akshay Rao,George G. Malliaras*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用原位电荷光度法（operando charge photometry）追踪有机混合离子-电子导体（OMIECs）薄膜在偏置与开路弛豫中的电荷行为，揭示水富集通道同时加速离子运动与导致电荷泄漏的双重作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OMIECs 可耦合离子与电子信号，应用于储能、神经形态计算与生物电子学，但偏置移除后电荷在水中如何弛豫、存储态能维持多久尚不明确。

Method: 采用原位电荷光度法（operando charge photometry）追踪 OMIEC 薄膜在偏置与开路弛豫过程中的电荷；结合建模与冷冻电子显微镜（cryo-electron microscopy）分析形貌；并通过调节聚噻吩（polythiophene）端基侧链来调控吸水与离子捕获。

Result: 偏置下电荷以氧化还原前沿（redox fronts）形式移动，移除偏置后则在通道内均匀弛豫；溶胀形成贯穿本体的连通水富集通道，加快离子运动的同时促使存储电荷泄漏。

Conclusion: 端基侧链可调控吸水与离子捕获：体积庞大的疏水侧链实现稳定的可编程状态，亲水侧链则支持快速电生理传感（electrophysiology sensing）。

Abstract: Organic mixed ionic-electronic conductors (OMIECs) couple ionic and electronic signals, enabling energy storage, neuromorphic computing, and bioelectronics. However, how charge relaxes in water once the bias is removed, which determines how long stored states persist, remains unclear. Using operando charge photometry, we tracked charge in OMIEC films during biasing and open-circuit relaxation. Under bias, charges moved as redox fronts. After bias removal, charge instead relaxed uniformly across the channel. Modeling and cryo-electron microscopy indicate that swelling creates connected water-rich pathways through the bulk. These pathways speed ion motion but also let stored charge leak away. Tuning the terminal side chain of a polythiophene controls water uptake and ion trapping. Bulky hydrophobic side chains enable stable programmable states, whereas hydrophilic side chains support fast electrophysiology sensing.

</details>


### [72] [Multifunctional synaptic learning and neuromorphic computing using crystalline TiO$_x$/NiO$_x$ heterojunction-based memory devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11992)
*Dip Manna,Koushik Mondal,Subhajit Karmakar,Pawan Kumar Ojha,Sourav Roy,Biswarup Satpati,Prasanta Karmakar,Supratic Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用 Ag/TiO$_x$/NiO$_x$/p$^{++}$-Si 忆阻器结构模拟生物突触特性，在直流与脉冲偏置下实现无成形双极阻变、巴甫洛夫学习、人工伤害感受器及摩斯电码检测，并在 MNIST 识别中达到约 95% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于模仿生物突触（biological synapse）的多种特性，探索以忆阻器（memristor）结构实现类神经功能的可行性。

Method: 方法为构建 Ag/TiO$_x$/NiO$_x$/p$^{++}$-Si 忆阻器结构，并在直流（dc）与脉冲偏置（pulsed bias）下研究其输运特性；通过结晶态 NiO$_x$ 促进其顶部 TiO$_x$ 的结晶生长，并利用测得的电导-编程脉冲关系提取突触权重（synaptic weight）用于人工神经网络（artificial neural network）。

Result: 结果显示，该异质结构在反向偏置下呈现稳定的无成形（forming free）非易失双极阻变（bipolar resistive switching）特性，具有渐变式 set 与 reset 特征；器件成功实现经典巴甫洛夫学习（Pavlov's learning）、人工伤害感受器（artificial nociceptor）与摩斯电码检测，并在 MNIST 数字识别中达到约 95% 准确率。

Conclusion: 结论指出，NiO$_x$/TiO$_x$ 异质结（heterojunction）在获得可重复的 $I-V$ 特性、进而模拟生物突触多种特性方面起关键作用。

Abstract: An attempt is made here to mimic different properties of biological synapses using Ag/TiO$_x$/NiO$_x$/p$^{++}$-Si memristor structure by studying its different transport properties under dc and pulsed bias. The presence of crystalline NiO$_x$ with smaller gains is found to be helpful to get TiO$_x$ deposited on its top with crystalline properties and larger grains. The heterostructure offers stable bipolar forming free non-volatile resistive switching characteristics under reverse biased condition with gradual set and reset features. These devices are also able to successfully implement the classical Pavlov's learning, study artificial nociceptor and Morse code detection. While incorporating synaptic weights derived from the measured conductance programming pulse relationship, an artificial neural network achieves nearly 95\% accuracy in MNIST digit recognition. In brief, NiO$_x$/TiO$_x$ heterojunction plays the pivotal role in getting such reproducible $I-V$ characteristics for mimicking different properties of biological synapses.

</details>


### [73] [Emergent gold-induced interfacial magnetism in monolayer FePS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12041)
*Giuseppe Buccoliero,Anita Polli,Marli dos Reis Cantarino,Rachel Nickel,Aloïs Arrighi,Roberto Sant,Tristan Riccardi,Valérie Reita,Nedjma Bendiab,Laëtitia Marty,Kurt Kummer,Johann Coraux,Nicholas B. Brookes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在FePS₃与超薄金膜构成的混合异质结构中，金膜作为近邻材料诱导二维反铁磁体的界面磁矩，并通过层数与衬底工程实现原子级精确调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料因原子级洁净界面与极薄特性，是研究强邻近效应（proximity effects）的理想平台，即邻近材料可重塑其基态。本文旨在探究金属衬底而非惰性衬底对二维磁体磁性的主动影响。

Method: 采用金辅助剥离（Au-assisted exfoliation）制备大面积薄片，结合元素选择性X射线吸收成像（element-selective X-ray absorption mapping）实现从单层到块体的逐层研究，并以惰性SiO₂衬底作为参照；辅以共振非弹性X射线散射（resonant inelastic X-ray scattering）与原子多重态计算（atomic multiplet calculations）。

Result: 在9 T与5 K下，金上单层的X射线磁圆二色性（X-ray magnetic circular dichroism）相对SiO₂增强三倍，轨道/自旋磁矩比降低约40%；散射谱显示晶体场（d-d）激发塌缩并伴随大量谱权重转移至电荷转移区。

Conclusion: 界面电荷转移与Fe 3d-Au杂化被识别为上述效应的微观起源；该效应在前三层内衰减并在五层时消失，表明层数与衬底工程结合可作为原子级精确的调控手段。

Abstract: Owing to their atomically clean interfaces and extreme thinness, two-dimensional (2D) materials are ideal hosts for strong proximity effects, whereby contact with an adjacent material reshapes their ground state. Here, we demonstrate such an effect within a hybrid heterostructure, between the 2D antiferromagnet FePS$_3$ and an ultrathin gold film. Rather than a passive support, the latter profoundly changes the nature of the magnetism in the 2D magnet, inducing a sizeable interfacial moment. Large-area flakes from Au-assisted exfoliation, combined with element-selective X-ray absorption mapping, enable a layer-by-layer study from monolayer to bulk, benchmarked against an inert SiO$_2$ substrate reference. At 9 T and 5 K, the monolayer on gold shows a three-fold enhanced X-ray magnetic circular dichroism relative to SiO$_2$, with a $\sim$40% reduction of the orbital-to-spin moment ratio. Resonant inelastic X-ray scattering reveals a collapse of the crystal-field (d-d) excitations and a massive spectral-weight transfer to the charge-transfer region, unveiling the microscopic mechanism behind this change. Supported by atomic multiplet calculations, we identify interfacial charge transfer and Fe 3d-Au hybridization as the origin of these effects, which decay over the first three layers and vanish at the pentalayer, establishing layer number combined with substrate engineering as an atomically precise handle.

</details>


### [74] [Direct Quantification of Molecular Helicity-Dependent Effective Magnetic Fields in Chiral Polypeptide/Ferromagnet Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12145)
*Theo Balland,Buddhika Hondamuni,Shira Yochelis,Meital Ozeri,Simon Sochiera,Angela Wittmann,Yossi Paltiel,Dmitry Budker,Jonas Fransson,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报道了在分子/铁磁多层异质结构中存在由手性诱导的长程磁相互作用。作者用绝缘间隔层将吸附的手性多肽单分子层与铁磁层解耦，证明该作用由对映体依赖的有效磁场介导（可能经由手性声子），并用MOKE显微镜定量测出其大小与符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）此前主要在输运实验与光电子谱中被观测到，而近期实验提示吸附手性分子可影响邻近铁磁层的磁态，但这类手性诱导效应的起源与定量表征仍缺乏。本文旨在探究超越常规直接输运耦合的、手性诱导的长程磁相互作用。

Method: 采用绝缘间隔层将吸附的手性分子与磁体解耦，以验证作用的长程性质；使用具有螺旋结构的自组装手性多肽单分子层作用于铁磁多层。借助磁光克尔效应（MOKE）显微镜，并设置无分子的片上参考区域，通过精确测量局部磁滞回线的可重复移动来直接量化该有效磁场。

Result: 手性多肽单分子层通过一个对映体依赖的有效磁场作用于铁磁多层，该磁场可能由手性声子穿过绝缘层介导。萃取出的有效磁场在相反对映体下符号反转，并与对映体过量（enantiomeric excess）相关。

Conclusion: 结果在杂化异质结构中建立了分子手性与磁响应之间直接且定量的联系，为某一类与CISS相关的效应提供了可实验获取的基准（benchmark）。

Abstract: Chirality-Induced Spin Selectivity (CISS) refers to structural chirality governing spin transport in chiral systems, with experimental signatures reported in both transport experiments and photoemission. Beyond influencing transport, recent experiments indicate that adsorbed chiral molecules can influence the magnetic state of adjacent ferromagnetic layers, suggesting the existence of chirality-induced effects whose origin and quantitative characterization remain lacking. Here, we investigate chirality-induced long-range magnetic interactions in a hybrid molecular/ferromagnetic multilayer beyond the conventional direct transport coupling. We demonstrate the long-range nature by decoupling the adsorbed chiral molecules from the magnet using an insulating spacer. We show that self-assembled monolayers of chiral polypeptides with a helical structure can act on a ferromagnetic multilayer via an enantiomer-dependent effective magnetic field that may be mediated by chiral phonons through the insulating layer. Using Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect (MOKE) microscopy with on-chip reference regions free of molecules, we directly quantify this field by measuring precisely the reproducible shifts of the local hysteresis loops. The extracted effective field reverses sign for opposite enantiomers and correlates with the enantiomeric excess. These results provide a direct and quantitative link between molecular chirality and magnetic response in hybrid heterostructures, establishing an experimentally accessible benchmark for a CISS-related effect.

</details>


### [75] [Specialized machine learning force fields for materials dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12151)
*Yue Wu,Haoyu Wan,Yuan Tian,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述（Review）探讨如何通过物理驱动的专门化（physics-driven specialization）拓展机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）在复杂动力学体系中的适用性，并提出面向特定可观测量的验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练等变图神经网络（pretrained equivariant graph neural networks）虽在广泛化学空间的近平衡性质上具备稳健零样本（zero-shot）性能，但其向复杂材料动力学的迁移仍受限于分布外反应态（out-of-distribution reactive states）、表征偏置（representation biases）与计算扩展瓶颈。

Method: 系统评估 MLIP 设计中的结构性权衡，包括高应变构型欠采样（undersampling）、高阶消息传递架构（high-order message-passing architectures）的过高计算开销，以及开放与场耦合体系对非局域相互作用（nonlocal interactions）的需求；并通过四类应用场景建立面向可观测量的验证框架。

Result: 四类苛刻应用场景为：带电界面（electrified interfaces）、组分波动的开放体系（compositionally fluctuating open systems）、多相演化（multiphase evolution）与大规模断裂（large-scale fracture）；分析指出通用基础模型与任务专用势（task-specific potentials）具有互补作用。

Conclusion: 强调针对性适配必须针对预期可观测量进行严格基准测试（benchmarked），并提出开发物理一致、硬件感知（hardware-aware）力场的路线图，以无缝衔接电子结构精度（electronic-structure accuracy）与宏观材料现象。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are transforming atomistic simulations by accessing unprecedented length and time scales. While pretrained equivariant graph neural networks achieve robust zero-shot performance for near-equilibrium properties across broad chemical spaces, their translation to complex materials dynamics remains fundamentally challenged by out-of-distribution reactive states, representation biases, and computational scaling limits. In this Review, we examine how physics-driven specialization extends the applicability of MLIPs to complex dynamical systems. We systematically evaluate the structural trade-offs in MLIP design: the undersampling of highly strained configurations, the prohibitive computational overhead of high-order message-passing architectures, and the necessity of nonlocal interactions for open and field-coupled systems. Through four demanding application contexts - electrified interfaces, compositionally fluctuating open systems, multiphase evolution, and large-scale fracture - we establish a framework for observable-specific validation. Highlighting the complementary roles of universal foundation models and task-specific potentials, we emphasize that targeted adaptations must be rigorously benchmarked against intended observables. We conclude with a roadmap for developing physically consistent, hardware-aware force fields that seamlessly connect electronic-structure accuracy to macroscopic materials phenomena.

</details>


### [76] [A Universal Polybromide Melt Strategy for the Direct Conversion of Metals into Optoelectronic-Grade Bromide Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12278)
*Arindam Mondal,Mark Baranov,Lonia R. Friedlander,Mariela J. Pavan,Kirill Sobolev Yevgeny Rakita*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种通用、单步、无有机溶剂的合成平台，利用甲基铵多溴化物熔体（methylammonium polybromide melt, PBM）在室温下直接将稳定元素金属薄膜转化为溴化物钙钛矿及相关体系，实现MAPbBr3、MASnBr3、MA2Au2Br6、MA2CuBr4的制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskite）体系通常要求特定金属氧化态（M2+或M+/M3+对），限制了传统合成中的加工灵活性，并使Sn、Cu、Au等多价金属面临稳定性挑战。该研究旨在突破这一限制，建立更灵活且可规模化的合成路径。

Method: 采用甲基铵多溴化物熔体（PBM）作为反应介质，在室温下与Pb、Sn、Au、Cu等元素金属反应，生成相应溴化物钙钛矿相；通过调控PBM化学活性，并借助原位X射线衍射（in situ X-ray diffraction）观测相演化过程中的瞬态中间相。

Result: PBM可成功将元素金属转化为MAPbBr3、MASnBr3、MA2Au2Br6与MA2CuBr4；在两种情形下原位XRD捕获到瞬态中间相。针对易过氧化为Sn4+相的Sn，通过调整PBM组成并结合金属Sn介导的归中反应（comproportionation reaction），抑制了过氧化，在环境条件下获得高质量MASnBr3薄膜。

Conclusion: 氧化电位与歧化电位（oxidation and disproportionation potentials）可作为预测金属与PBM反应结果的有效指标。该方法与卷对卷（roll-to-roll）基础设施完全兼容，为下一代光电子技术的规模化提供低门槛路径。

Abstract: Halide perovskite systems require specific metal oxidation states (M2+ or M+/M3+ pairs), limiting processing flexibility during traditional synthesis and posing stability challenges for multivalent metals like Sn, Cu, and Au. This work anchors a profound paradigm shift: a universal, single-step, organic-solvent free synthetic platform that directly converts stable elemental metal films into bromide perovskite and perovskite-related systems for a wide variety of (opto)electronic applications. The synthetic platform uses methylammonium polybromide melt (PBM) that reacts at room temperature with different elemental metals, including Pb, Sn, Au, and Cu, successfully transforming them into their respective Br-perovskite phases: MAPbBr3, MASnBr3, MA2Au2Br6, and MA2CuBr4. It is shown that the PBMs chemical activity can be tuned, and in situ X-ray diffraction captures in two cases transient intermediate phases during phase evolution. For Sn, which tends towards overoxidation to Sn4+ phase, changes in the PBMs composition coupled with a metallic Sn mediated comproportionation reaction allowed suppressing overoxidation, and formation of high quality MASnBr3 films under ambient conditions. Overall, oxidation and disproportionation potentials are found to be useful metrics for predicting M:PBM reaction outcomes. Industrially, this approach should be fully compatible with roll-to-roll infrastructure, providing a low-barrier pathway to scale next-generation optoelectronic technologies.

</details>


### [77] [The Stability of Rutile Oxides for Oxygen Evolution Catalysis: From Mechanistic Understanding to New Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12297)
*Katarina Kretschmer,Michael H. Eikerling,Tobias Binninger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该计算研究通过比较块体金红石型IrO2、RuO2及相关MO2体系的电子结构，利用源自晶体轨道的电子描述符区分金属-氧键的共价与离子贡献，从而揭示二者稳定性差异的电子起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物电解质水电解（polymer electrolyte water electrolysis）被视为绿氢生产的关键技术，而阳极析氧反应（oxygen evolution reaction, OER）的基准电催化剂IrO2受铱稀缺性与高成本制约。RuO2活性相当但稳定性不及IrO2，其实际应用受限，因此需理解二者稳定性差异的本质原因。

Method: 采用计算研究（computational study），分析块体IrO2与RuO2中的金属-氧键（metal-oxygen bond）强度。通过对比块体金红石型（rutile）IrO2、RuO2及若干MO2体系的电子结构，借助晶体轨道衍生的电子描述符（crystal-orbital-derived electronic descriptors）区分共价与离子贡献。

Result: 研究区分了金属-氧键中的共价（covalent）与离子（ionic）贡献，为IrO2与RuO2稳定性差异的电子起源提供了解释性见解。

Conclusion: 该工作从电子结构层面阐明了RuO2稳定性劣于IrO2的可能来源，可为寻找耐久、低成本替代材料或大幅降低铱载量提供理论依据。摘要未提供具体定量结论。

Abstract: Polymer electrolyte water electrolysis is considered as a pivotal technology for green hydrogen production. Iridium dioxide (IrO2) serves as the benchmark electrocatalyst for the anodic oxygen evolution reaction (OER) in these cells. However, the scarcity and cost of iridium drives efforts to find durable and cost-effective replacements or drastically reduce the iridium loading. While ruthenium dioxide (RuO2) offers comparable activity, its practical utility is impacted by its stability being inferior to IrO2. The presented computational study analyzes the metal-oxygen bond strengths in bulk IrO2 and RuO2 to provide insights into the electronic origins of their differing stabilities. Through a comparative analysis of the electronic structure of bulk rutile IrO2, RuO2, and selected MO2 systems, it is able to discriminate the covalent and ionic contributions to the metal-oxygen bond using crystal-orbital-derived electronic descriptors.

</details>

### [78] [Fiber product of condensable algebras and critical points of boundary phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12079)
*Hanlin Lin,Hao Zheng*

Main category: math.CT

TL;DR: 该工作提出 2+1 维拓扑序（topological orders）边界相变临界点的数学描述：给定两个边界相 $\mathcal{C}_A$ 与 $\mathcal{C}_B$ 及其拉格朗日代数（Lagrangian algebras）$A$、$B$，临界点对应纤维积（fiber product）$D = A \times_{M} B$，并证明 $D$ 仍为可凝聚代数（condensable algebra），同时在 $Z(Vec_G^\omega)$ 中给出可凝聚代数的替代分类与分裂现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 2+1 维拓扑序中边界相变的临界点，为边界相变提供代数化的数学描述框架。该问题涉及拓扑序中不同边界相之间的转变机制及其代数不变量。

Method: 方法上，针对 2+1 维拓扑序 $\mathcal{C}$ 中两个边界相 $\mathcal{C}_A$、$\mathcal{C}_B$（对应拉格朗日代数 $A$、$B$），构造基于 $A$-$B$-代数 $M$ 的纤维积 $D = A \times_{M} B$ 作为临界点的候选描述；并证明 $D$ 的可凝聚性。此外，对 $Z(Vec_G^\omega)$ 中可凝聚代数给出替代分类，并在若干 Abelian 与非 Abelian 群 $G$ 的例子上进行显式计算。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）证明了纤维积 $D = A \times_{M} B$ 仍为可凝聚代数，从而支持其作为边界相变临界点的数学描述；（2）给出了 $Z(Vec_G^\omega)$ 中可凝聚代数的另一种分类，并揭示了某些代数凝聚过程中出现的分裂现象（splitting phenomenon）；（3）在覆盖 Abelian 与非 Abelian 群 $G$ 的多个 $Z(Vec_G^\omega)$ 例子中完成了可凝聚代数的显式计算。

Conclusion: 结论表明，2+1 维拓扑序边界相变的临界点可由拉格朗日代数的纤维积统一刻画，且该构造具有可凝聚性这一良好性质；同时，$Z(Vec_G^\omega)$ 中可凝聚代数的分类与分裂现象为理解凝聚过程提供了进一步的结构性认识。摘要未给出更多关于物理实现或实验预测的结论。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a mathematical description of the critical point of boundary phase transitions in 2+1D topological orders. Given two boundary phases, $\mathcal{C}_A$ and $\mathcal{C}_B$, with corresponding Lagrangian algebras $A$ and $B$ in a 2+1D topological order $\mathcal{C}$, respectively, we propose that the critical point of the phase transition between these two phases, if it exists, corresponds to $D = A \times_{M} B$, which is the fiber product of $A$ and $B$ over $A$-$B$-algebra $M$. We prove that $D$ is also a condensable algebra. Additionally, we present an alternative classification of condensable algebras in $Z(Vec_G^ω)$ and reveal splitting phenomenon that arises during the condensation of certain algebras. We also perform explicit computations for the condensable algebras within several examples of $Z(Vec_G^ω)$, covering both Abelian and non-Abelian groups $G$.

</details>
