### [79] [Theory of Topologically Ordered Superfluids in 2+1 Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10668)
*Chao-Ming Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文为拓扑有序超流体（topologically ordered superfluids, TOSFs）在（2+1）维玻色子系统中建立了一套表征其拓扑数据的通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TOSFs 同时具备去禁闭的、有能隙的任意子激发（anyonic excitations）与因戈德斯通模（Goldstone modes）而保持的无能隙特性，其拓扑数据的系统刻画此前缺乏统一框架。

Method: 通过规范化（gauging）自发破缺的电荷 U(1)_{\rm c} 对称性，将 TOSF 映射为受对偶 U(1)_{\rm dual} 对称性富集、且霍尔电导（Hall conductance）为零的有能隙拓扑序；反向规范化 U(1)_{\rm dual} 可恢复 TOSF。作者发展了代数与场论两套互补表述，并利用 parton 构造给出实例。

Result: 该双向映射使 TOSF 的拓扑数据——包括凝聚电荷、去禁闭任意子内容及涡旋的拓扑性质——可借助对称性富集拓扑序（symmetry-enriched topological orders）的代数框架系统编码；超流涡旋与有能隙任意子扇区中残余离散对称性所对应磁通缺陷共享拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 该框架统一刻画了 TOSF 的拓扑数据，场论表述还提示了向有能隙拓扑有序相的自然转变；借助 parton 构造，作者给出了玻色子系统中具有非阿贝尔涡旋（non-Abelian vortices）的 TOSF 例子。

Abstract: Topologically ordered superfluids (TOSFs) support deconfined, gapped anyonic excitations while remaining gapless because of Goldstone modes. We develop a general framework for characterizing their topological data in $(2+1)$-dimensional bosonic systems with spontaneously broken charge ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm c}$ symmetry. Gauging ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm c}$ maps a TOSF to a gapped topological order enriched by a dual ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm dual}$ symmetry with vanishing Hall conductance, while gauging ${\rm U}(1)_{\rm dual}$ recovers the TOSF. This two-way mapping allows the topological data of the TOSF, including the condensate charge, deconfined anyon content, and topological properties of vortices, to be systematically encoded using the algebraic framework of symmetry-enriched topological orders. In particular, superfluid vortices share the topological properties of flux defects associated with the residual discrete symmetry in the gapped anyon sector. We develop complementary algebraic and field-theoretic formulations of this framework, with the latter also suggesting a natural transition to a gapped topologically ordered phase. Additionally, using parton constructions, we present examples of TOSFs with non-Abelian vortices in bosonic systems.

</details>


### [80] [SC$^\sharp$: superconductivity intertwined with topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10679)
*Zhi-Qiang Gao,Yan-Qi Wang,Hui Yang,Zhaoyu Han,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了(2+1)维带凝聚体电荷 $2ne$ 的带隙超导体的分类框架，通过聚焦超导涡旋（vortex）的拓扑性质刻画超导与内禀拓扑序的交织，并提出新的相族 SC$^{\sharp}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导（superconductivity）与内禀拓扑序（intrinsic topological order）的相互交织可能超出简单共存的形式，需要一个系统框架来精确刻画二者关系并给出分类。

Method: 以携带 $h/(2ne)$ 磁通的基本涡旋及其拓扑性质为切入点，构建基于任意子凝聚（anyon condensation）的系统代数框架，按交织程度对相进行分类。

Result: 获得按交织程度递增的分类：无可拓扑序的可逆超导体、非扭曲与扭曲 SC$^\ast$，直至 SC$^{\sharp}$；SC$^{\sharp}$ 中基本涡旋不可分解且必为非阿贝尔（non-Abelian），其涡旋扇区量子维度（quantum dimension）至少为 $\sqrt{6}$ 且不满足 Fibonacci 融合规则；并构造了若干无穷例子族及 primitive SC$^{\sharp}$ 相。

Conclusion: 基于涡旋性质的分类方案揭示了拓扑有序超导体之间的新区别，并对其可能实现方式施加了约束；primitive SC$^{\sharp}$ 相无法通过对低电荷超导凝聚体相对相位无序化获得。

Abstract: Superconductivity and intrinsic topological order can intertwine in ways that go beyond simple coexistence. Here we develop a classification framework for (2+1)D gapped superconductors with condensate charge $2ne$. By focusing on the topological properties of superconducting vortices, we precisely characterize this interplay. We obtain a classification with increasing levels of intertwinement, from the well known invertible superconductors without topological order, to untwisted and twisted SC$^\ast$, and finally to SC$^{\sharp}$. Our main finding is the family SC$^{\sharp}$, in which the elementary vortex carrying $h/(2ne)$ flux cannot be factorized into an invertible superconductor vortex dressed by bulk anyons, and every elementary vortex is necessarily non-Abelian. For such phases, we also obtain no-go results: the elementary vortex sector has quantum dimension at least $\sqrt{6}$ and cannot satisfy Fibonacci fusion rules. We develop a systematic algebraic framework to obtain all these superconducting phases through anyon condensation, allowing us to construct several infinite families of examples and make connections to anyon superconductivity. Finally, we introduce primitive SC$^\sharp$ phases for which factorization of every nontrivial vortex sector is obstructed, and argue that they cannot arise by disordering the relative phases of lower-charge superconducting condensates. Our classification scheme based on vortex properties allows us to uncover new distinctions between topologically ordered superconductors and constrain how they can be realized.

</details>


### [81] [Spin Chain with Continuous Exponential Symmetry: Topological Phase and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10682)
*Biao Lian,Zhen Bi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究具有 $U(1)\rtimes\mathbb Z_2$ 连续指数对称性的一维自旋 $S$ 链的对称有能隙基态，发现其拓扑性质由 $S$ 与 $q$ 的奇偶性共同决定，并揭示出超越既有分类方案的新型对称保护相——“半 SPT 相”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出既有对称保护拓扑（symmetry-protected topological, SPT）分类方案可能不完备，需考察带有指数调制荷（modulated charge）的连续对称性体系中是否出现新的有能隙相。

Method: 采用带整数基底 $q$、电荷单位按 $q^{L-j}$ 指数衰减的 $U(1)\rtimes\mathbb Z_2$ 对称一维自旋链模型；最简单模型为可视为 XXZ Haldane 自旋链 $q$-变形的量子击穿模型（quantum breakdown model），并通过分析 $S$ 与 $q$ 的奇偶组合判定对称基态的拓扑。

Result: 整数 $S$ 时，奇 $q$ 允许带受保护边缘模（edge modes）的非平凡 $\mathbb Z_2$ Haldane 型 SPT 相，偶 $q$ 则不允许非平凡 SPT 相；半奇整数 $S$ 时，奇 $q$ 因 Lieb-Schultz-Mattis 阻碍不存在唯一对称有能隙基态，而偶 $q$ 强制产生新相“半 SPT（half-SPT）”。该相仅在单侧边缘具有对称保护边缘模，且弦序（string orders）仅为 SPT 相的一半。

Conclusion: 上述结果揭示了一类新的对称保护有能隙相，超出已有分类方案的覆盖范围，表明指数连续对称性可导致非常规的拓扑相及其边缘/弦序特征。

Abstract: We study symmetric gapped ground states of one-dimensional spin $S$ chains with a continuous exponential symmetry $U(1)\rtimes\mathbb Z_2$ with integer base $q$, where the U(1) charge has a modulated unit of charge $q^{L-j}$ decaying exponentially with site $j$. The simplest model is a quantum breakdown model that can be viewed as a $q$-deformation of the XXZ Haldane spin chain. We show that the topology of their symmetric ground states crucially depends on $S$ and $q$. For integer $S$, odd $q$ allows a nontrivial $\mathbb Z_2$ Haldane-type symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase with protected edge modes, while even $q$ allows no nontrivial SPT phase. For half-odd integer $S$, odd $q$ prohibits any unique symmetric gapped ground state due to a Lieb-Schultz-Mattis obstruction, whereas even $q$ enforces a novel symmetric gapped phase which we name as the ``half-SPT" phase. Unconventionally, the half-SPT phase only has a symmetry protected edge mode on one edge, and has half of the string orders of an SPT phase. Our results uncover a new type of symmetry-protected gapped phases beyond existing classification schemes.

</details>


### [82] [Competing symmetry breaking and topology in quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10694)
*Anthony Rey,Ömer M. Aksoy,Claudio Chamon,Akira Furusaki,Christopher Mudry*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究在一维自旋-1/2量子系统中考察自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）与对称保护拓扑（symmetry-protected topology）的竞争与协作，构造了两类具有 Z₂×Z₂×Z₂^T 对称性的局域哈密顿量，揭示出包含对称破缺相、SPT 相、无能隙相在内的“相动物园”及奇异连续相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究在低维量子系统中，自发对称破缺与对称保护拓扑两种机制如何相互竞争并协同作用，尤其是它们能否生成有序临界相（ordered critical phases）与多重临界性（multicriticality）。

Method: 作者引入两类具有 Z₂×Z₂×Z₂^T 对称性的局域哈密顿量，系统刻画其相图：六个长程有序的有能隙相（FM_{x,y,z} 与 Neel_{x,y,z}）、其对应的对称保护拓扑相（FM^SPT_{x,y}、Neel^SPT_{x,y}），以及两个无能隙相（gFM_z 与 gNeel_z）。方法以相变性质分析为主，涉及 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体理论与 Lifshitz 三临界点分析。

Result: 竞争有能隙相之间的直接相变可为连续或弱一级；连续相变线表现出奇异临界性质，例如 Neel_x 与 Neel_y 之间的解禁闭量子临界点（deconfined quantum critical points）线由 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体理论描述，并终止于 gFM_z 相处的 Lifshitz 三临界点，该点出现连续 U(1) 对称性的自发破缺；此外，不同 Z₂ 对称破缺的 SPT 相之间（如 Neel^SPT_x 与 Neel^SPT_y）的临界线保留低能边界自由度，实现对称富集临界性（symmetry-enriched criticality，即无能隙 SPT 相）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对称破缺与拓扑的竞争能够产生有序临界相与多重临界性，且 Lifshitz 三临界点处连续 U(1) 对称性的自发破缺与 Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman 定理的朴素预期相悖，揭示出非常规机制。

Abstract: We investigate how spontaneous symmetry breaking and symmetry-protected topology compete and cooperate in one-dimensional quantum spin-1/2 systems. We introduce two families of local Hamiltonians with $\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}\times\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}\times\mathbb{Z}^{T}_{2}$ symmetry which host a zoo of broken-symmetry phases with and without gapless boundary spins, namely, six long-range-ordered gapped phases that differ by their $\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}$-symmetry-breaking patterns (FM$^{\,}_{x,y,z}$ and Neel$^{\,}_{x,y,z}$), their partner symmetry-protected topological phases (FM$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{x,y}$ and Neel$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{x,y}$), and two gapless phases (gFM$^{\,}_{z}$ and gNeel$^{\,}_{z}$) with incommensurate $\mathbb{Z}^{T}_{2}$-breaking magnetic long-range order and central charge $\mathsf{c}=1$. Direct transitions between competing gapped phases can be continuous or weakly first order. We demonstrate that continuous phase transition lines have exotic critical properties. For example, a line of deconfined quantum critical points (between the Neel$^{\,}_{x}$ and Neel$^{\,}_{y}$ phases) described by Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid theory is terminated by the gFM$^{\,}_{z}$ phase at a Lifshitz tricritical point which exhibits spontaneous breaking of the continuous U(1) symmetry despite the locality and one-dimensionality of the Hamiltonian, contrary to a naive expectation from the Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman theorem. Furthermore, critical lines between symmetry-protected topological phases with different $\mathbb{Z}^{\,}_{2}$-symmetry breaking (e.g., Neel$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{x}$ and Neel$^{\mathrm{SPT}}_{y}$) additionally retain low-energy boundary degrees of freedom, realizing symmetry-enriched criticality (or a gapless SPT phase). Our results reveal unconventional mechanisms by which competing symmetry breaking and topology generate ordered critical phases and multicriticality.

</details>


### [83] [Symmetric Mode Ansatz: Theory of Magnon Collective Modes in Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10706)
*Miguel Gonçalves,Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Juan Felipe Mendez-Valderrama,Andreas Feuerpfeil,B. Andrei Bernevig,Nicolas Regnault*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出对称模式拟设（SyMA），用于描述莫尔材料中自发时间反演破缺的陈磁体低能磁振子谱，并在整数与分数填充下验证其准确性，进而揭示陈数（Chern number）与量子几何（quantum geometry）对磁振子能隙的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔材料（Moiré materials）中存在由平带铁磁自发破缺时间反演所导致的整陈绝缘体与分陈绝缘体（FCIs），其中扭转 MoTe2 呈现具有带隙磁振子（magnon）激发的 Ising 铁磁性。明确这些集体模式的性质及磁振子能隙来源，是理解陈磁体稳定性的关键问题。

Method: 发展广义单模近似（single-mode approximation），称为对称模式拟设（symmetric mode ansatz, SyMA），用以描述低能磁振子谱；并构造两类可解析求解的 Hubbard 模型（含 Haldane 模型与连续理想带）。

Result: SyMA 在整数填充下与数值计算高度吻合，在包括 FCI 的分数填充下亦定性准确。对于 SyMA 精确成立的一类模型，证得零动量磁振子能隙的双侧界，其可由陈数、贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与量子度规（quantum metric）表示。

Conclusion: 均匀量子几何（uniform quantum geometry）足以稳定陈带中的磁性；该工作为陈磁体集体模式的解析描述及其稳定性判据提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Moiré materials host integer and fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) where time-reversal is broken spontaneously by flat band ferromagnetism. In twisted MoTe2, unlike in quantum Hall, opposite spins carry opposite Chern numbers and realize Ising ferromagnetism with gapped magnon excitations. Clarifying the nature of these collective modes and the origin of the magnon gap is key to understanding the stability of the Chern magnets in these materials. To do so, we develop a generalized single-mode approximation, which we call the symmetric mode ansatz (SyMA), to describe the low-energy magnon spectrum. We find it to be in exceptional agreement with numerical calculations at integer filling, and qualitatively accurate across fractional fillings including FCIs. To explain the accuracy of the SyMA, we present two families of Hubbard models (including the Haldane model and continuum ideal bands) whose collective modes are analytically solvable and for which the SyMA is either exact or an accurate approximation. For a class of these models where SyMA is exact, we prove two-sided bounds on the zero-momentum magnon gap in terms of the Chern number, Berry curvature, and quantum metric, revealing that uniform quantum geometry is sufficient for stabilizing magnetism in Chern bands.

</details>


### [84] [Cooper Instability of a Magnetic Wigner Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10710)
*Wangqian Miao,Chunli Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究磁性维格纳晶体（magnetic Wigner crystal）中的集体模式能否介导配对，针对菱面体六层石墨烯（rhombohedral hexalayer graphene）中的电子晶体实验。通过 Hartree--Fock 与含时 Hartree--Fock 方法求解双电子 Cooper 问题，发现声子介导吸引超过配对阈值，手性 $p$-波（chiral $p$-wave）配对为主导不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy）直接成像了菱面体六层石墨烯中的电子晶体，磁输运测量提示超导与电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）序源自相邻载流子密度下的自旋-谷极化铁磁金属，促使作者追问磁性维格纳晶体中的集体模式能否在该情境下介导配对。

Method: 研究维格纳晶体第一导带中两个电子的 Cooper 问题；在 Hartree--Fock 与含时 Hartree--Fock 近似下获得晶体基态及其集体模式谱，并利用准玻色子近似（quasiboson approximation）推导电子--声子（electron--phonon）哈密顿量，进而求解双电子问题。

Result: 集体模式（玻色子）能量与单粒子（费米子）色散由电子密度控制且量级相当，缺乏绝热展开所需的小参数；由于磁性维格纳晶体不具时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry），配对磁化率中通常的 Cooper 对数发散消失，吸引须超过有限阈值才能配对。求解结果显示声子介导吸引超过配对阈值，手性 $p$-波配对为主导不稳定性。

Conclusion: 在上述近似范围内，维格纳晶体的集体模式尽管时间反演对称性破缺，仍可有利于手性配对；该结果为发展完整的多体理论以研究电子晶化与超导提供了有前景的起点。

Abstract: Recent scanning tunneling microscopy has directly imaged electronic crystals in rhombohedral hexalayer graphene, and magnetotransport measurements suggest that superconductivity and charge-density-wave order emerge from a common spin-valley-polarized ferromagnetic metal at nearby carrier densities. Motivated by these observations, we ask whether collective modes in a magnetic Wigner crystal can mediate pairing in this setting. We address this question by studying the Cooper problem for two electrons in the first conduction band of the Wigner crystal. Unlike conventional phonon-mediated pairing, the collective mode (boson) energies and single-particle (fermion) dispersion are comparable scales controlled by the electron density, leaving no small parameter for an adiabatic expansion. Moreover, because the magnetic Wigner crystal is not time-reversal invariant, the usual Cooper logarithmic divergence in the pairing susceptibility is absent, so the attraction must exceed a finite threshold for pairing to occur. These unusual features motivate a numerical experiment to see whether the crystal's own vibrations can bind electrons. We obtain the crystalline ground state and its collective mode spectrum within Hartree--Fock and time-dependent Hartree--Fock approximation, and derive the electron--phonon Hamiltonian using the quasiboson approximation. Solving the resulting two-electron problem, we find that phonon-mediated attraction exceeds the pairing threshold, with chiral $p$-wave pairing as the leading instability. Within these approximations, we show that collective modes of a Wigner crystal can indeed favor chiral pairing despite broken time-reversal symmetry. Our results provide an encouraging starting point for developing a full many-body theory to study electronic crystallization and superconductivity.

</details>


### [85] [Gauging Modulated Symmetries: Bond Algebras, Higher-Form Symmetries, and Symmetry-Enriched Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10764)
*Gustavo M. Yoshitome,Pedro R. S. Gomes,Guilherme Delfino*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个构造性框架，用于刻画 $d$ 维格点系统中的调制对称性（modulated symmetries）及其规范化（gauging），并据此发展离散阿贝尔调制对称性的分类与晶格对称富化拓扑相（SETs）的微观构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究中调制对称性多依赖具体模型，缺乏系统的分类与规范化框架。作者希望用空间群（space group）对内部对称群的作用来统一描述周期调制模式，并为相关拓扑相提供微观路径。

Method: 将调制剖面组织为空间群作用在有限阿贝尔内部对称群上的有限维表示；因自同构群有限，空间作用通过空间群的有限商群分解，得到周期调制模式与离散阿贝尔调制对称性的分类框架。通过核构造（kernel construction）系统获得对称性允许的局域相互作用及开算符代数，并对其规范化。

Result: 规范化后，开算符代数的元素转变成扩展的 Wilson 算符与边界算符（boundary operators），并保持其代数关系。在去禁闭区（deconfined regime），所得代数实现混合 't Hooft 反常（mixed 't Hooft anomaly），给出阿贝尔晶体对称富化拓扑相（crystalline SETs）的微观途径；这些 SETs 不混合电场与磁场分部，二者按互相反梯度表示（contragredient representations）变换，从而保持编织配对。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式例子展示，并可自然纳入文献中已有的若干模型，表明其作为离散阿贝尔调制对称性分类与规范化的一般构造具有适用性。

Abstract: We develop a constructive framework for characterizing modulated symmetries and their gauging in $d$-dimensional lattice systems. The modulation profiles are organized as a finite-dimensional representation of the space group acting on a finite Abelian internal symmetry group. Since the corresponding automorphism group is finite, the nontrivial spatial action factors through a finite quotient of the space group, leading to periodic modulation patterns and providing a classification framework for discrete Abelian modulated symmetries. The symmetry-allowed local interactions are systematically obtained from a kernel construction, which also yields an algebra of open operators. Upon gauging, its elements are transmuted into extended Wilson and boundary operators while retaining their algebraic relations. In the deconfined regime, the resulting algebra realizes a mixed 't Hooft anomaly, providing a microscopic route to Abelian crystalline symmetry-enriched topological phases (SETs). These SETs do not mix electric and magnetic sectors, which transform in mutually contragredient representations, thereby preserving their braiding pairing. We illustrate the framework through explicit examples and show that several models present in the literature fit naturally within our construction.

</details>


### [86] [Probing and Manipulating Quantum Materials with Strong-field Terahertz and Mid-infrared Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11126)
*S. J. Li,H. Y. Wu,S. J. Zhang,Y. T. Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该综述（review）聚焦强场太赫兹（THz）与中红外（MIR）辐射在量子材料（quantum materials）探测与调控中的研究进展，重点关注非线性电子动力学、集体模式耦合、对称性敏感响应及场诱导的电子结构与有序态变化，并特别介绍协同极端条件用户装置（SECUF）所推动的相关发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料中的涌现现象（emergent phenomena）源于不同自由度（degrees of freedom）之间的相互作用，识别这些相互作用并确立其在有序态（ordered states）中的作用是凝聚态物理（condensed-matter physics）的核心问题，也是调控材料性质的基础。相关低能激发（low-energy excitations）与能隙多位于THz与MIR波段，为该类研究提供了合适的能量窗口。

Method: 本文为综述性工作，梳理利用强场（strong-field）THz与MIR辐射探测和操控量子材料的近期进展；该波段强场兼具相位稳定（phase-stable）特性，可用于探测非线性动力学（nonlinear dynamics）并将选定自由度驱离平衡态（far from equilibrium）。

Result: 综述涵盖的主要进展方向包括：非线性电子动力学（nonlinear electronic dynamics）、集体模式耦合（collective-mode coupling）、对称性敏感响应（symmetry-sensitive responses），以及场诱导的电子结构与有序态变化。文中特别关注协同极端条件用户装置（Synergetic Extreme Condition User Facility, SECUF）所促成的研究进展。

Conclusion: 强场THz与MIR辐射为探测和主动调控量子材料中的相互作用与有序态提供了重要手段；相关能力的进一步发展（尤其依托SECUF等设施）有望推动该领域的持续进展。

Abstract: Emergent phenomena in quantum materials arise from interactions among different degrees of freedom. Identifying these interactions and establishing their roles in ordered states are central problems in condensed-matter physics and provide a basis for controlling material properties. Many relevant low-energy excitations and gaps lie in the terahertz (THz) and mid-infrared (MIR) range, where intense phase-stable fields can both interrogate nonlinear dynamics and drive selected degrees of freedom far from equilibrium. This review surveys recent progress in probing and manipulating quantum materials with strong-field THz and MIR radiation, with emphasis on nonlinear electronic dynamics, collective-mode coupling, symmetry-sensitive responses and field-induced changes in electronic structure and ordered states. Particular attention is given to developments enabled by the Synergetic Extreme Condition User Facility (SECUF).

</details>


### [87] [Multipolar fluctuations in localized $4f^2$-electron systems from dynamical mean-field theory: application to $\mathrm{PrCdNi}_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11562)
*Koki Numa,Junya Otsuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将基于密度泛函理论与动力学平均场理论的多极涨落分析方法，从 \(4f^1\) 电子体系扩展至 \(4f^2\) 电子体系，并将其应用于 PrCdNi\(_4\) 以预言反铁四极序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有方法（多极涨落分析）仅适用于 \(4f^1\) 体系，而 \(4f^2\) 体系具有更复杂的多体态结构与晶体电场分裂，需专门的方法拓展来处理。

Method: 在 \(j=5/2\) 子空间的 \(j\)-\(j\) 耦合方案下构建多体态以再现 \(4f^2\) Hund 定则基态多重态的晶场分裂；并通过从单粒子基到多粒子基的映射，推导多极磁化率及 \(4f^2\) 多重态之间的相互作用。

Result: 将该框架应用于具有非 Kramers \(Γ_3\) 双重态基态的 PrCdNi\(_4\)，提出由最近邻与第四近邻相互作用竞争所驱动、位于 \(\boldsymbol{q}=(2π,π,0)\) 的 \((3z^2-r^2)\) 型反铁四极序。

Conclusion: 所发展的形式化方法可推广至一般的 \(4f^n\) 电子体系。

Abstract: Multipolar-fluctuation analysis based on density functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory is extended from $4f^1$- to $4f^2$-electron systems. Two methodological developments are presented. First, many-particle states are constructed within the $j$-$j$ coupling scheme in the $j=5/2$ subspace, so as to reproduce the crystalline-electric-field splitting of the $4f^2$ Hund's-rule ground-state multiplet. Second, multipolar susceptibilities and interactions between $4f^2$ multiplets are derived through a mapping from single-particle to many-particle bases. We apply this framework to PrCdNi$_4$, which has a non-Kramers $Γ_3$ doublet ground state, and propose a $(3z^2-r^2)$-type antiferroquadrupolar order at $\boldsymbol{q}=(2π,π,0)$ driven by the competition between nearest- and fourth-nearest-neighbor interactions. The formalism developed here is applicable to general $4f^n$-electron systems.

</details>


### [88] [Orbital magnetic susceptibility and de Haas-van Alphen effect of a flat band from quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12193)
*Ethan Huecker,Mengxing Ye,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作研究零磁场下严格平带（flat band）的轨道磁性（orbital magnetism）问题，指出传统理论不适用，并通过非对易动量（noncommutative kinetic momenta）的 Moyal 代数展开，建立由轨道矩 $\mathcal{M}$、闭合形式系数 $\mathcal{X}$ 与 Berry 曲率 $Ω$ 共同决定的完整轨道响应框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零磁场下严格平带具有零速度、发散有效质量（effective mass）且无费米面（Fermi surface），使得标准轨道磁性理论无法适用；因此需要建立描述磁场如何为平带生成有效色散的理论框架。

Method: 采用按非对易动能动量（noncommutative kinetic momenta）的 Moyal 代数（Moyal algebra）组织的展开，计算能带能量的一阶与二阶磁场修正，并借助紧束缚平带晶格模型（flattened Chern lattice model）的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）进行数值验证。

Result: 能带能量获得一阶移动 $B\,\mathcal{M}(\mathbf k)$（由 Bloch 态的轨道矩决定）与二阶移动 $\tfrac12\mathcal{X}(\mathbf k)B^{2}$（给出闭合形式的规范不变系数）；磁化率（susceptibility）纯为几何量，对一般孤立平带重现既有的规范不变响应公式；化学势钉扎于平带时，$\mathcal{M}=0$ 等值线成为涌现费米面（emergent Fermi surface），其附近玻色化（bosonize）激发得到能级间距关于 $B$ 二次的修正 Lifshitz–Kosevich 公式；振荡相位移动由涌现费米面周围的 Berry 相位与 $\mathcal{X}$ 联合给出；能带为窄带时振荡频率以无自由参数的形式从零场费米面面积过渡到 $\mathcal{M}=0$ 等值线面积。

Conclusion: 轨道响应完全由 $\mathcal{M}$、$\mathcal{X}$ 与 Berry 曲率 $Ω$ 三个量确定，表明平带轨道磁性本质上是几何的；解析结果通过平带 Chern 晶格模型的精确对角化得到数值验证。

Abstract: A band that is exactly flat at zero magnetic field has zero velocity, a divergent effective mass, and no Fermi surface, so the standard theory of orbital magnetism does not apply. We show that the magnetic field generates an effective dispersion. In the expansion organized by the Moyal algebra of the noncommutative kinetic momenta, the band energy acquires a first-order shift $B\,\mathcal{M}(\mathbf k)$, set by the orbital moment of the Bloch state, and a second-order shift $\tfrac12\mathcal{X}(\mathbf k)B^{2}$, whose gauge-invariant coefficient we obtain in closed form. These two functions, together with the Berry curvature $Ω$, fix the entire orbital response. The susceptibility is purely geometric, and for a generic isolated flat band it reproduces the established gauge-invariant response formula. With the chemical potential pinned to the flat band, the $\mathcal{M}=0$ contour becomes an emergent Fermi surface, and bosonizing excitations in its vicinity yields a modified Lifshitz--Kosevich formula with level spacing quadratic in $B$. The phase shift of the oscillations is given jointly by the Berry phase around the emergent Fermi surface and $\mathcal{X}$. When the band is narrow rather than exactly flat, the oscillation frequency crosses over, with a parameter-free profile, from the zero-field Fermi-surface area to the area of the $\mathcal{M}=0$ contour. We verify our analytical findings numerically by exactly diagonalizing a flattened Chern lattice model.

</details>


### [89] [Magnon band splitting without altermagnetism in CuF2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11838)
*Ioannis Rousochatzakis,Oleg Janson,Alexander A. Tsirlin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对类金红石材料 CuF₂ 的计算与实验联合研究表明，其实际单斜结构下的磁序由弱耦合方格子层模型描述，层间斜向反铁磁耦合导致元胞倍增，从而抑制交变磁性（altermagnetism）能带劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭对称性论证不足以确证交变磁态，需结合计算与实验加以验证。CuF₂ 属于被预测为交变磁体的材料之一，因此成为检验该问题的对象。

Method: 采用从头算（ab initio）与线性自旋波（linear spin-wave）计算，并结合磁化测量，构建自旋-½ 的有效模型进行拟合与分析。

Result: 实验单斜结构下 CuF₂ 可描述为弱耦合方格子层，面内耦合 J₁≈115 K，两个协同反铁磁层间耦合分别为 J₁ 的 4% 与 8%。层间耦合源于长程超交换（superexchange）且相对方格平面倾斜，导致磁有序态下元胞倍增，有效抑制交变磁能带劈裂。同时存在异常强的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用 |D|/J₁≈0.3，引起自旋倾斜，并与 order-by-disorder 效应共同将 Néel 矢量钉扎于晶体 b 轴。DM 各向异性可促发磁振子（magnon）能带劈裂，但能带保持非手性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使对于具有交变磁对称性的 3d 磁体，相对论效应（relativistic effects）仍具有重要作用。

Abstract: Conclusive identification of an altermagnetic state requires going beyond mere symmetry arguments. We illustrate this in a combined computational and experimental study of the rutile-like material CuF$_2$, which is on the list of predicted altermagnets. Using ab initio and linear spin-wave calculations supplied by magnetization measurements, we show that CuF$_2$ in its experimental monoclinic structure can be described by a spin-$\frac12$ model of weakly coupled square-lattice layers with the in-plane coupling $J_1\simeq 115$ K and two synergistic antiferromagnetic interplane couplings amounting to 4% and 8% of $J_1$, respectively. Driven by long-range superexchange, these interlayer couplings are oblique to the square planes, resulting in the unit-cell doubling in the magnetically ordered state, thus effectively suppressing any altermagnetic band splitting. Concurrently, we identify unusually strong Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, $|\mathbf D|/J_1\simeq 0.3$, that produce spin canting and, together with order-by-disorder effect, pin the Néel vector to the crystallographic $b$-axis. Additionally, DM anisotropy promotes magnon band splitting, but these bands remain non-chiral. Our results highlight the importance of relativistic effects even in $3d$ magnets with altermagnetic symmetries.

</details>


### [90] [Strange-metal behavior in correlated molecular conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12044)
*Savita Priya,Maxim Wenzel,Christian Prange,Bertold Rasche,Christian Sindlinger,Takuya Kobayashi,Hiromi Taniguchi,Dita Puspita Sari,Yasuyuki Ishii,Kazuya Miyagawa,Kazushi Kanoda,Simone Fratini,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过变温输运与宽频光学测量，发现含汞阴离子的κ-(BEDT-TTF)₄Hg₃₋δX₈（X = Br、Cl）盐呈现电阻率线性温 dependence 与频率依赖电导的幂律行为，属于"奇异金属（strange metal）"范畴，并证明其与不可公度阴离子层及大尺度空间调制直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: κ-BEDT-TTF盐是典型的Mott体系（Mott system），通常表现为从费米液体（Fermi liquid）到Mott绝缘体的关联驱动电子转变。而含空间调制汞阴离子的金属性化合物偏离该分类，其非常规金属行为的来源尚不明确，构成本文的研究动因。

Method: 采用变温输运测量（temperature-dependent transport）与宽频光学光谱（broad-range optical investigations）相结合的手段，考察电阻率随温度的变化规律以及频率依赖电导$\vert\hatσ (ω)\vert$的幂律指数。

Result: 实验观测到电阻率随温度线性上升（$ρ(T)\propto T$）可延续至较高温度，且频率依赖电导呈$\vert\hatσ (ω)\vert \propto ω^{-0.7}$的幂律行为；线性频率依赖的散射率（scattering rate）标度至普朗克耗散（Planckian dissipation）。上述响应仅在具有不可公度阴离子层（incommensurate anion layers）的体系中出现，并与大尺度空间调制（large-scale spatial modulations）建立直接联系。

Conclusion: 该工作表明奇异金属行为与不可公度阴离子层导致的大尺度空间调制密切相关，并指出该结论可推广至其他强关联奇异金属化合物（strange-metal compounds）。

Abstract: The $κ$-BEDT-TTF salts are prototypical Mott systems, realizing a correlation-driven electronic transition from a Fermi-liquid state to a Mott insulator. The metallic compounds with spatially modulated Hg-containing anions, $κ$-(BEDT-TTF)$_4$Hg$_{3-δ}X_8$ ($X$ = Br, and Cl), however, fall outside this classification. Our temperature-dependent transport and broad-range optical investigations reveal a $ρ(T)\propto T$ dependence of the resistivity up to elevated temperatures and a power-law behavior of the frequency-dependent conductivity $\vert\hatσ (ω)\vert \propto ω^{-0.7}$; well-known hallmarks of ``strange metals''. The linear-in-frequency scattering rate of these strange metals scales to the Planckian dissipation. We demonstrate that this response only occurs in systems with incommensurate anion layers and establish a direct link to large-scale spatial modulations. Our conclusions can be extended to other strongly correlated strange-metal compounds

</details>


### [91] [Field-resolved hierarchy of superconducting energy gaps in PdTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12075)
*Sangyun Lee,A. M. Donald,D. Duong,S. Huang,R. Gazizulin,C. Huan,Rongying Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究利用密封³He浸没环境下的低温软点接触谱（soft point-contact spectroscopy）研究PdTe的超导态，观测到约0.5与1.1 meV两个特征超导能标，二者在升温时趋于同一Tc，但在磁场下表现出约4.5与7.5 kG两个不同特征场，揭示出具有不同磁场鲁棒性的能标层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清多个超导能标（superconducting energy scales）在磁场下如何演化，对于区分多带（multiband）、各向异性（anisotropic）与表面敏感（surface-sensitive）的超导响应具有重要意义。PdTe为研究上述问题提供了対象体系。

Method: 采用密封³He浸没环境中的低温软点接触谱技术，测量PdTe的Andreev反射谱（Andreev-reflection spectra），并以BTK模型进行拟合分析，同时结合零偏压电导（zero-bias conductance）与独立的场依赖能隙分析。

Result: Andreev反射谱显示约0.5和1.1 meV两个特征超导能标，单能隙BTK模型无法充分描述。两能标在升温时逐渐被压制并趋于同一Tc；磁场下则呈现约4.5与7.5 kG两个不同特征场，低能标对应零电阻超导态的消失，而高能谱响应在更大磁场下仍持续存在。独立场依赖能隙分析给出的压制标度与零偏压电导结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果揭示PdTe中存在场分辨的超导能标层级（field-resolved hierarchy），并表明其超导响应具有不同的磁场鲁棒性（magnetic-field robustness）。

Abstract: Resolving how multiple superconducting energy scales evolve under magnetic field is important for distinguishing multiband, anisotropic, and surface-sensitive superconducting responses. Here, we investigate the superconducting state of PdTe using low-temperature soft point-contact spectroscopy in a sealed $^3$He immersion environment. The resulting Andreev-reflection spectra reveal two characteristic superconducting energy scales near 0.5 and 1.1 meV that are not adequately described by a single-gap BTK model. Both energy scales are progressively suppressed upon warming toward the same superconducting transition temperature $T_c$. In contrast, their magnetic-field evolution reveals two distinct characteristic fields near 4.5 and 7.5 kG. The lower scale is associated with the loss of the zero-resistance superconducting state, whereas the higher-energy spectroscopic response persists to substantially larger fields. Independent field-dependent gap analysis yields suppression scales consistent with those identified from the zero-bias conductance. These results reveal a field-resolved hierarchy of superconducting energy scales in PdTe and indicate superconducting responses with distinct magnetic-field robustness.

</details>


### [92] [Dynamical many-body control of high harmonic generation in a Mott insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12136)
*Mingyao Guo,Ryo Noguchi,Mohammad Muntasir Hassan,Steven J. Gomez Alvarado,Reina M. Gomez,Yao-Jui Chan,Yu-Chieh Wen,Stephen D. Wilson,Yao Wang,David Hsieh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作报道在典型莫特绝缘体（Mott insulator）La$_2$CuO$_4$中实现高达23阶的高次谐波产生（high harmonic generation, HHG），观察到与驱动场强无关的4 eV截断，并将其归因于 Hubbard 带（Hubbard bands）决定的内禀能量尺度。借助时间分辨高次谐波谱学（time-resolved high harmonic spectroscopy），研究区分了载流子产生、带内加速与带间复合对非平衡辐射谱的贡献，并发现光掺杂在临界激发密度之上触发多体流形（many-body manifolds）重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HHG 已成为探测固体中超快电子动力学的有力手段，但在电子激发源自多体相互作用而非单粒子能带色散（band dispersion）的强关联材料中，该领域仍处于起步阶段。论文旨在将 HHG 拓展为探测强关联体系中多体电子重构的敏感手段。

Method: 采用高次谐波产生与时间分辨高次谐波谱学（time-resolved high harmonic spectroscopy），在原型莫特绝缘体 La$_2$CuO$_4$ 中测量最高23阶的谐波发射，并通过光掺杂（photo-doping）改变激发密度以研究非平衡态响应。

Result: 观测到稳健的4 eV截断，其不随驱动场强变化，揭示由 Hubbard 带定义的内禀能量尺度；实验将载流子产生（carrier creation）、带内加速（intraband acceleration）与带间复合（interband recombination）的贡献加以区分。光掺杂在超过临界激发密度后引发多体流形（many-body manifolds）的基态式重组，使能带曲率（band curvature）发生重整化（renormalize）并抑制非线性带内电流，从而将多体能带重构的特征直接印刻于谐波发射中。

Conclusion: 结果表明 HHG 可作为探测关联驱动的细微电子重构的灵敏探针，并表明多体相互作用能够产生极端非线性光学响应，且该响应在非平衡态下具有高度可调性。

Abstract: High harmonic generation (HHG) has emerged as a powerful probe of ultrafast electron dynamics in solids. However, in strongly correlated materials, where electronic excitations arise from many-body interactions rather than single-particle band dispersion, the field remains in its infancy. Here, we report HHG up to the 23$^{rd}$ order in the prototypical Mott insulator La$_2$CuO$_4$. We observe a robust 4 eV cutoff that is independent of driving field strength, revealing an intrinsic energy scale defined by the Hubbard bands. Using time-resolved high harmonic spectroscopy, we disentangle the distinct contributions of carrier creation, intraband acceleration, and interband recombination to the nonequilibrium radiation spectrum. We demonstrate that photo-doping triggers a fundamental reorganization of the many-body manifolds above a critical excitation density, a process that renormalizes the band curvature and suppresses nonlinear intraband currents, thereby directly imprinting the signature of many-body band reconstruction onto the harmonic emission. These results establish HHG as a sensitive probe of subtle correlation-driven electronic reconstructions and highlight that many-body interactions enable extreme nonlinear optical responses that are highly tunable in nonequilibrium states.

</details>


### [93] [Transducing quantum-spin-ice correlations into Weyl Fermi-arc transport at a synthetic Kondo lattice interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12294)
*Tsung-Chi Wu,Michael Terilli,Christian Zaprianov,Eun Sang Choi,David Graf,Qinghua Zhang,Lin Gu,Mikhail Kareev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作构建了外尔半金属（Weyl semimetal）$\mathrm{Eu_2Ir_2O_7}$ 与量子自旋冰（quantum spin ice）候选材料 $\mathrm{Tb_2Ti_2O_7}$ 的人工近藤晶格（Kondo lattice）界面，发现量子自旋冰关联被印记到外尔费米弧（Fermi arc）输运中，表现为随磁场演化的六重—十二重—六重各向异性序列，且该十二重响应在经典自旋冰界面中不出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋冰的集体激发与量子涨落因母体材料为绝缘体而在电学上基本“沉默”，难以直接探测。将此类磁性环境与表面局域化的外尔态耦合，为把其关联直接编码进费米弧输运提供了途径，但该实验范畴此前基本未被探索。

Method: 构筑外尔半金属 $\mathrm{Eu_2Ir_2O_7}$ 与量子自旋冰候选材料 $\mathrm{Tb_2Ti_2O_7}$ 之间的人工近藤晶格界面，并在超低温下测量其电子输运随磁场变化的各向异性响应，同时与经典自旋冰界面进行对照。

Result: 超低温下，电子输运由低场下的六重各向异性响应转变为中间磁场窗口内的十二重响应，随后在高场进入回归的六重各向异性。经典自旋冰界面未出现类似的十二重响应，表明同一巡游电子通道在与量子涨落的 Tb 矩相互作用时获得了定性不同的对称性信息。

Conclusion: 该结果与“外尔表面态和横向多极（multipolar）自由度、以及纵向偶极（dipolar）贡献之间发生界面耦合”的图像一致，说明人工近藤晶格界面可将具有多极关联的阻挫量子磁性转导为独特的外尔费米弧输运响应。

Abstract: Quantum spin ice hosts collective excitations and quantum fluctuations that are largely electrically silent because the materials supporting them are insulating. Coupling this magnetic environment to surface-localized Weyl states offers a route to encode its correlations directly into Fermi-arc transport. Experimental access to this regime, however, has remained largely unexplored. Here, we realize a synthetic Kondo lattice interface between the Weyl semimetal $\mathrm{Eu_2Ir_2O_7}$ and the quantum-spin-ice candidate $\mathrm{Tb_2Ti_2O_7}$, and demonstrate that quantum-spin-ice correlations are imprinted on Weyl Fermi-arc transport. At ultra-low temperature, electronic transport evolves from a sixfold anisotropic response at low field into a twelvefold response over an intermediate field window, before entering a reentrant sixfold anisotropy at high field. No analogous twelvefold response occurs at the classical-spin-ice interface. The quantum--classical contrast shows that the same itinerant electronic channel acquires qualitatively different symmetry information when interacting with the quantum-fluctuating Tb moments, consistent with an interfacial coupling between the Weyl surface states and the transverse, multipolar degrees of freedom, in addition to the longitudinal, dipolar contribution. Our work establishes synthetic Kondo lattice interfaces as a route for transducing frustrated quantum magnetism with multipole correlations into distinct Weyl Fermi-arc transport responses.

</details>


### [94] [True vs false Fermi surfaces in the Pseudogap regime and their transformation with doping and temperature in the Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12339)
*Y. M. Vilk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于精确图解量子蒙特卡洛（DiagMC）结果，采用改进的双粒子自洽方法（TPSC+）同时分析能量分布曲线（EDC）与动量分布曲线（MDC），揭示赝能隙（pseudogap）区费米液体术语失效，需区分真/假费米面与零能准粒子（ZEQ）线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）的精确图解量子蒙特卡洛结果促使对赝能隙进行更深入研究，需要一种能同时刻画能量与动量空间谱特征、并可对照精确数值结果的方法来厘清赝能隙区的准粒子图像。

Method: 使用改进的双粒子自洽方法（TPSC+），同时分析能量分布曲线（EDC）与动量分布曲线（MDC），并以马祖巴拉谱代理量（Matsubara spectral proxy）对图解量子蒙特卡洛（DiagMC）结果进行基准测试。

Result: 赝能隙由临界热自旋涨落驱动，该涨落在二维因Mermin-Wagner定理而出现；公度涨落先打开反节点赝能隙并留下真费米弧，随温度降低费米面演化为空穴型与电子型假费米面；非公度涨落在对角线附近产生热点，其赝能隙延续至量子临界点（QCP），而在反节点处先于QCP掺杂消失并出现两条位于未占据区的前驱反铁磁（AFM）能带。TPSC+在强相互作用区低估赝能隙压制，可在更低温度或掺杂下重现DiagMC行为。

Conclusion: 费米液体术语在赝能隙区失效，需引入真/假费米面与零能准粒子线概念；马祖巴拉代理量等价于热展宽为πT的谱函数，会掩盖特征；基于A(k,0)发现低温下任意相互作用强度（包括弱耦合）均出现空穴型费米面。

Abstract: Exact diagrammatic quantum Monte Carlo (DiagMC) results for the nearest-neighbor Hubbard model motivate a closer study of the pseudogap. Using the improved two-particle self-consistent approach (TPSC+), we analyze Energy (EDC) and Momentum Distribution Curves (MDC) simultaneously. We show that Fermi-liquid terminology breaks down in the pseudogap regime, requiring a distinction between true and false Fermi surfaces and zero-energy quasiparticle (ZEQ) lines. A false Fermi surface has a momentum-space spectral maximum but a frequency-space depression at zero energy, while a false ZEQ line violates the standard quasiparticle condition $\partial Σ'(\mathbf{k},ω)/\partial ω|_{ω=0}<0$.The pseudogap is driven by critical thermal spin fluctuations, which occur in two dimensions because of the Mermin-Wagner theorem. Commensurate fluctuations first open an antinodal pseudogap, leaving true Fermi arcs. With decreasing temperature, the Fermi surface evolves into hole- and electron-like false Fermi surfaces. Incommensurate fluctuations generate hot spots near the diagonal, where the pseudogap persists to the quantum critical point (QCP), whereas at $\mathbf{k}_{AN}$ it disappears before the QCP doping. There, the spectrum has two precursor antiferromagnetic (AFM) bands, both in the unoccupied ($ω>0$) region. We benchmark TPSC+ against DiagMC results for the Matsubara spectral proxy $-\mathrm{Im}[\mathcal{G}(\mathbf{k},iπT)]/π$. TPSC+ underestimates pseudogap suppression in the strong-interaction regime, reproducing DiagMC behavior at lower temperatures or doping. This proxy is equivalent to the spectral function with thermal broadening $η=πT$, which obscures features when $πT$ is not the smallest energy scale. Using $A(\mathbf{k},0)$, we find that hole-like Fermi surfaces emerge at any interaction strength at low temperature, even at weak coupling.

</details>

### [95] [AtomWorld-Mirror: Macro-Step World Modeling of Critical Evolution Backbones for Materials Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11527)
*Ziming Pan,Ruge Zhang,Haozhi Han,Junkai Zhou,Xingyuan Chen,Yifeng Chen,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AtomWorld-Mirror 提出一种时间感知的宏观步（macro-step）世界模型，通过将短微观事件片段蒸馏为关键状态间的物理可达跃迁，实现原子系统临界演化骨架的快速长时程预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原子尺度模拟器以微观分辨率推进，在抵达具有结构意义的状态前耗费大量计算于低影响局部更新，形成"演化分辨率瓶颈（evolutionary-resolution bottleneck）"，限制长时程（long-horizon）模拟。

Method: 该模型面向逐步骤（Step-Wise）原子模拟，将短微观事件片段蒸馏为关键状态间物理可达的跃迁，在潜在宏观步动力学中联合预测稀疏结构编辑与累积物理时间；每次跃迁受局部可达性（local reachability）、存量守恒（inventory conservation）与连续时间一致性（continuous-time consistency）约束。

Result: 在五个原子系统中（涵盖富铜 RPV 钢辐照老化、Cu-Zr 金属玻璃、Li₃N 基反钙钛矿固态电解质），宏观步推理相较逐事件模拟实现 10³ 至 10⁴ 倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过将局部原子物理摊销为可复用的潜在宏观模型，并以宏观步推理替代显式微观事件重放，该方法在保持结构有效性与时间语义的同时，为大幅加速长期材料演化预测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Atomistic simulation is a fundamental tool for studying long-term materials evolution, from diffusion and defect dynamics to interfacial reactions and fracture. Yet conventional simulators typically advance at microscopic resolution, spending substantial computation on low-impact local updates before reaching structurally consequential states, an evolutionary-resolution bottleneck that limits long-horizon simulation. We propose AtomWorld-Mirror, a time-aware macro-step world model for the critical evolution backbone of atomic systems. For Step-Wise atomistic simulation, AtomWorld-Mirror distills short micro-event segments into physically reachable transitions between key states, jointly predicting sparse structural edits and accumulated physical time through latent macro-step dynamics. Local reachability, inventory conservation, and continuous-time consistency constrain each transition. By amortizing local atomic physics into a reusable latent macro model and replacing explicit micro-event replay with macro-step inference, this formulation provides a path toward substantially faster prediction of long-term materials evolution while preserving structural validity and time semantics. Across five atomic systems, spanning Cu-rich RPV steel irradiation aging, Cu-Zr metallic glass, and Li$_3$N-based anti-perovskite solid electrolyte, macro-step inference delivers a speed up of $10^3$ to $10^4$ times over event-by-event simulation.

</details>


### [96] [Ecology of AI Agents: Collaboration Creates a Population Threshold for Takeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12436)
*Erin Crawley,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出AI智能体群体的生态安全（ecological safety）理论：当智能体具备协作能力时，群体网络安全能力随规模增长，产生临界人口阈值——低于阈值群体衰减，高于阈值群体自我强化式暴增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体已能实施真实网络攻击、随数量扩展能力并集体追求错位目标（misaligned goals），存在错位智能体种群爆炸（population explosion）的风险：智能体可入侵计算机并秘密部署更多智能体，形成自我强化循环。这引出核心问题——何种因素决定错位智能体群体是被遏制还是进入该循环。该问题属于种群层面的生态安全，区别于固定种群下的个体智能体或多智能体安全。

Method: 基于种群增长方程（population growth equation）构建AI智能体种群的生态学理论，其中适应度（fitness，即增长率）取决于网络安全能力（cybersecurity capability）。分别考察无协作与有协作两种情形下的种群动态。

Result: 无协作时，仅当个体智能体能力超过某临界阈值，种群才会暴增。有协作时，集体网络安全能力随种群规模上升，从而产生临界种群阈值（critical population threshold）：低于该阈值种群衰减，高于该阈值种群暴增，即使个体智能体能力未发生变化。该现象在生态学中称为强阿利效应（strong Allee effect）。

Conclusion: 由于对小规模智能体进行红队测试（red teaming）无法保证更大种群的生态安全，该理论呼吁生态红队测试与种群节奏控制（population pacing）：在受控环境中逐步部署更大规模智能体种群，测量网络能力随种群规模的扩展方式，并估计暴增的临界种群规模。能力提升可能降低该阈值，因此每一代新模型都需重新估计。

Abstract: AI agents can now conduct real-world cyberattacks, scale up capabilities with the number of agents, and collectively pursue misaligned goals to obtain rewards. Together, these factors raise the risk of a population explosion of misaligned agents: agents could compromise computers and secretly deploy additional agents, creating a self-reinforcing cycle where larger populations develop greater collective cyber capability and expand further. This raises a fundamental question: What determines whether a population of misaligned agents remains contained or takes off into this self-reinforcing cycle? This population-level problem is ecological safety: unlike individual-agent or multi-agent safety with a fixed population, it concerns the dynamics of the population itself. Here, we develop an ecological theory of AI-agent populations based on a population growth equation in which fitness (growth rate) depends on cybersecurity capability. We show that, without collaboration, the population takes off only when individual-agent capability exceeds a critical threshold. With collaboration, however, collective cybersecurity capability increases with population size. This creates a critical population threshold: below it, the population declines; above it, the population takes off, even though individual-agent capability has not changed. In ecology, this phenomenon is known as the strong Allee effect. Because red teaming a small group of agents cannot guarantee ecological safety in larger populations, our theory calls for ecological red teaming and population pacing: gradually deploying larger agent populations in controlled environments, while measuring how cyber capability scales with population size, and estimating the critical population size for takeoff. Capability gains may lower this threshold, requiring re-estimation for each new model generation.

</details>

### [97] [Time-Domain Analysis of Surface Acoustic Wave Magnetoelectric Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11421)
*Mohsen Samadi,Henrik Wolframm,Felix Weisheit,Dirk Meyners,Eckhard Quandt,Michael Höft,Martina Gerken*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该研究结合数值与实验方法，考察基于多层Love波延迟线的磁电声表面波（SAW）传感器的时域传播特性，验证时域与频域分析的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声表面波传感器的瞬态响应对理解其动态行为、传播延迟及对时变信号的响应具有重要意义，而多层结构中的色散使相速度与群速度关系复杂，需定量表征。

Method: 采用互补的数值与实验方法，通过时域分析确定延迟时间并提取相速度与群速度，同时利用频域分析独立表征波色散，并在146 MHz工作频率下比较波场追踪结果与频域色散关系。

Result: 数值与实验均显示相速度与群速度存在差异，反映多层结构中Love波的色散；仿真群速度与频域色散关系结果偏差在0.4%以内，仿真与实验的延迟时间偏差为2.7%、群速度偏差为0.9%。

Conclusion: 时域与频域分析结果具有一致性，为SAW传感器的瞬态传播行为提供了定量表征。

Abstract: The transient response of surface acoustic wave sensors is important for understanding their dynamic behavior, propagation delay, and response to time-varying signals. In this work, the time-domain propagation characteristics of a magnetoelectric SAW sensor based on a multilayer Love-wave delay line are investigated using complementary numerical and experimental approaches. Time-domain analysis is employed to determine the delay time and to extract the phase and group velocities, while frequency-domain analysis provides an independent characterization of the wave dispersion. Both numerically and experimentally, the phase and group velocities are found to differ, reflecting the dispersion of the Love wave in the multilayer structure. At the operating frequency of 146 MHz, the simulated group velocity obtained from wave-field tracking agrees within 0.4% with the value determined from the frequency-domain dispersion relation. The simulated delay time and group velocity also show good agreement with experiment, with deviations of 2.7% in delay time and 0.9% in group velocity. These results demonstrate the consistency of the time- and frequency-domain analyses and provide a quantitative characterization of the transient propagation behavior of SAW sensors.

</details>

### [98] [PXtal: Learning to Align Powder X-Ray Diffraction and Crystal Structures under Information Asymmetry across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10653)
*Zhuoran Yang,Christopher M. Collins,Bei Peng,Luke M. Daniels,Matthew J. Rosseinsky,Vladimir V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PXtal 框架，针对粉末 X 射线衍射（PXRD）与晶体结构之间物理上固有的信息不对称，采用非平衡最优传输（UOT）与耦合级广义 KL 散度（GKL）进行跨模态对齐，在多个测试集（含零样本迁移）上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学多模态学习常假设配对视图信息量相当，但 PXRD 将三维晶体结构压缩为一维衍射图时会丢失信息，导致该假设失效。论文旨在将这种信息不对称（information asymmetry）作为显式设计问题加以处理。

Method: PXtal 使用非平衡最优传输（Unbalanced Optimal Transport, UOT）适配跨模态耦合（cross-modal coupling），并引入耦合级广义 Kullback-Leibler 散度（coupling-level generalized KL divergence, GKL）以监督完整的传输方案（transport plan）。

Result: 在六个测试集（含四个零样本迁移集）中，PXtal 在 PXRD 到晶体候选检索（PXRD-to-crystal candidate retrieval）任务上持续优于基线模型；当 PXRD 图案存在相近但晶体学上不同的非配对近邻时增益最大。所得晶体与 PXRD 编码器在下游材料与晶体学任务中迁移效果更好。

Conclusion: 结果表明信息不对称是科学多模态学习中的通用设计问题：对齐目标应反映各模态所保留的信息内容。

Abstract: Scientific multimodal learning commonly assumes that paired views are comparably informative. Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) makes this mismatch explicit: compressing a three-dimensional crystal structure into a one-dimensional diffraction pattern loses information and makes the pattern harder to connect to the crystal structure that produced it. We introduce PXtal, a framework for learning aligned PXRD and crystal representations under this physically imposed information asymmetry. PXtal uses Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT) to adapt the cross-modal coupling and coupling-level generalized Kullback-Leibler (GKL) divergence to supervise the full transport plan. Across six test sets, including four zero-shot transfer sets, PXtal consistently outperforms the baseline models in PXRD-to-crystal candidate retrieval, with the largest gains when PXRD patterns have close but crystallographically distinct nonpaired neighbors, meaning similar input patterns associated with different crystals. The resulting crystal and PXRD encoders transfer more effectively to downstream materials and crystallographic tasks. These results identify information asymmetry as a general design problem in scientific multimodal learning: alignment objectives should reflect what each modality preserves.

</details>


### [99] [Residual spectral instabilities in representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11257)
*Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将变分自编码器（variational autoencoder, VAE）中的逐维后验坍缩（dimension-wise posterior collapse）表述为围绕部分坍缩态的涨落理论（fluctuation theory），指出坍缩方向构成不变涨落扇区，其激活边界由残差谱上沿决定，从而给出由残余信息主导的谱级联（spectral cascade）解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的表示会相继丢失潜在自由度，暗示存在一系列转变，但其背后的稳定性原理尚不清楚。论文旨在为这一逐次坍缩现象提供统一的稳定性判据。

Method: 将负证据下界（negative evidence lower bound）视为有效自由能（effective free energy），通过其二次展开构造高斯理论（Gaussian theory），令其 Hessian 充当潜在涨落的质量矩阵（mass matrix）。据此推导坍缩方向的不变涨落扇区及其精确质量谱，并给出以条件残差算子（conditional residual operator）表述的判据，同时结合数值延拓实验进行验证。

Result: 当解码器方差（decoder variance）低于残差谱上沿时，局部重激活方向会降低自由能，取等号对应临界性（marginality）。该判据在线性高斯 VAE 极限下恢复主成分阈值。数值延拓实验显示，潜在维度在这些谱临界点附近相继丢失。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种谱级联解释：坍缩序列由存活表示未能解释的残余信息所支配，重激活边界可作为连续连接分支上逐次坍缩的局部判据。

Abstract: Learned representations can lose latent degrees of freedom successively, suggesting a cascade of transitions whose underlying stability principle remains unclear. Here we formulate dimension-wise posterior collapse in variational autoencoder (VAE) as a fluctuation theory around partially collapsed states. Interpreting the negative evidence lower bound as an effective free energy, its quadratic expansion defines a Gaussian theory whose Hessian acts as a mass matrix for latent fluctuations. We show that the collapsed directions form an invariant fluctuation sector and derive its exact mass spectrum in terms of a conditional residual operator. A local reactivation direction lowers the free energy when the decoder variance falls below the residual spectral upper edge, with equality marking marginality. The criterion recovers principal component thresholds in the linear Gaussian VAE limit. Viewed in reverse along continuously connected branches, the reactivation boundary provides a local criterion for successive collapse. Numerical continuation experiments show successive loss of latent dimensions near these spectral marginalities. These results support a spectral cascade interpretation governed by residual information left unexplained by the surviving representation.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Equal-mass sunset integrals and genus-zero local Gromov--Witten theory of Calabi--Yau $n$-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10828)
*Hartmut Maennel,Pierre Vanhove*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文证明了一个精确恒等式：等质量二维 $(n-1)$-圈 sunset Feynman 积分（sunset Feynman integral，$n\geq4$）与局部 Calabi–Yau $n$-fold $X_n=\operatorname{Tot}(K_{F_n})$ 的亏格零单点后代不变量（genus-zero one-point descendant invariants）在欧氏大动量下作为收敛对数芽（logarithmic germs）相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立高圈 sunset 积分与局部 Calabi–Yau 几何（mirror symmetry）之间的精确对应，将 Feynman 积分计算转化为镜像坐标与后代级数的问题。

Method: 引入镜像坐标 $R=\log y+O(y)$、$A=\theta_yR$、$Q=e^R$，利用主单点势 ${\mathcal D}_0^\Delta(Q)$ 与后代级数 ${\mathcal D}_a^\Delta$；后代级数由环绕超几何级数（ambient hypergeometric series）与逆镜像映射（inverse mirror map）显式给出；系数取值于奇 zeta 值 $\mathbb Q[\zeta(3),\zeta(5),\ldots]$（来自 Gamma class）。

Result: 得到显式恒等式 $I^{(n-1)}_{\circleddash}(s)=(-1)^{n-1}yA[nR^{n-1}-\theta_Q{\mathcal D}_0^\Delta R^{n-2}+\sum_{j=0}^{n-3}c_jR^j]$；证明主镜像映射的全局单值性（global univalence）并给出 $Q$-Taylor 半径的显式上下界；提取完整 primary evaluation divisor 及其积分覆盖变换；将环绕端点常数约化为有限普适 Gamma-class 部分与 $\lfloor(n-1)/2\rfloor$ 个实参数。

Conclusion: 该结果给出 sunset 积分与局部 Calabi–Yau 镜像几何之间的精确等价，并将端点常数归结为有限普适结构与少量实参数。

Abstract: We prove an exact identity, as convergent logarithmic germs at large Euclidean momentum, between the equal-mass two-dimensional $(n-1)$-loop sunset Feynman integral, with $n\geq4$, and the genus-zero one-point descendant invariants of the local Calabi--Yau $n$-fold $X_n=\operatorname{Tot}(K_{F_n})$, where $F_n\subset(\mathbb P^1)^{n}$ is a smooth $(1,\ldots,1)$ hypersurface. Writing $s$ for the momentum variable and $y=-1/s$, the identity reads $$
  I^{(n-1)}_{\circleddash}(s)=(-1)^{n-1}yA\,\Bigl[nR^{n-1}-θ_Q{\mathcal D}_0^Δ\,R^{n-2}
  +\sum_{j=0}^{n-3}c_jR^j\Bigr], $$ where $R=\log y+O(y)$ is the local mirror coordinate, $A=θ_yR$, $Q=e^R$, ${\mathcal D}_0^Δ(Q)$ is the primary one-point potential, and each $c_j$ is an explicit linear combination of $1$, the descendant series ${\mathcal D}_a^Δ(Q)$ and their derivatives $θ_Q{\mathcal D}_a^Δ$, $0\leq a\leq n-2$, with coefficients in the odd-zeta values $\mathbb Q[ζ(3),ζ(5),\ldots]$ coming from the Gamma class. The descendant series are given explicitly by the ambient hypergeometric series and the inverse mirror map. We also prove global univalence of the principal mirror map, with explicit upper and lower bounds on the $Q$-Taylor radii, extract the full primary evaluation divisor and its integral cover transform, and reduce the ambient endpoint constants to a finite universal Gamma-class part and $\lfloor(n-1)/2\rfloor$ real parameters.

</details>


### [101] [Injectivity of specialization maps in critical cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11565)
*Yalong Cao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究特殊化映射（specialization maps）从等变消失圈上同调（equivariant vanishing cycle cohomology）到环境空间等变上同调的单射性问题，考察对象为带势箭图（quivers with potentials）的表示模栈（moduli stacks），包括与广义锥形（generalized conifolds）及 $SO(3,\mathbb{C})$ 有限子群相关的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特殊化映射是等变消失圈上同调到环境空间等变上同调的自然映射，其单射性直接关系到消失圈上同调能否嵌入环境空间的上同调结构。带势箭图表示模栈及相关几何对象为检验该单射性问题提供了自然的问题背景。

Method: 研究针对带势箭图的表示模栈（moduli stacks of representations of quivers with potentials）展开，并覆盖与广义锥形（generalized conifolds）以及 $SO(3,\mathbb{C})$ 有限子群（finite subgroups）相关的模栈情形；核心是考察特殊化映射（specialization maps）的单射性（injectivity problem）。摘要未提供具体技术手段。

Result: 摘要未给出明确的单射性结论，仅说明在该类模栈上研究其单射性问题，涵盖广义锥形与 $SO(3,\mathbb{C})$ 有限子群相关情形。

Conclusion: 特殊化映射的单射性研究可扩展至带势箭图表示模栈这一较广的对象类，包括广义锥形与 $SO(3,\mathbb{C})$ 有限子群所对应的几何情形。摘要未陈述最终结论或推论。

Abstract: Specialization maps are natural maps from equivariant vanishing cycle cohomology to the equivariant cohomology of the ambient space. We study their injectivity problem for moduli stacks of representations of quivers with potentials, including those associated with generalized conifolds and finite subgroups of $SO(3,\mathbb{C})$.

</details>

### [102] [When quantum field theory kicks in: the relation between matter, gravity and energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10588)
*Antonio Ferreiro,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 论文论证：将"能量"作为物质性判据从经典物质延伸到量子场论（quantum field theory）后，会迫使引力场的解释面临两难，最终使经典意义上"物质与时空"的二分在量子化后无法维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者以"能量是否存在"作为判定某物是否为物质的核心标准，借此既统一各类经典物质的物质性，又据此否认广义相对论（General Relativity）中的引力场属于物质。论文意图考察这一标准在量子场论中延伸后对引力解释带来的新概念后果。

Method: 概念分析路径：将上述能量判据形式化地推广至量子场论，考察量子物质应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor）重整化（renormalization）的后果，并区分由此产生的两种理论立场——平等主义观点（egalitarian view）与统一主义观点（unificationist view）。

Result: 重整化迫使一个由度规（metric）单独构成的局域纯几何项进入守恒的应力-能量张量，由此产生两面性：一方面赋予度规在时空意义之外的物质意义；另一方面使引力能量与非引力能量紧密绑定，以至于若不做真空选择便无法将二者分开，而一般时空并不提供这种选择。

Conclusion: 由此形成两难：若引力能量无意义，则非引力量子场的能量同样无意义，只有经典场才是物质；若两者都有意义，则度规获得物质场（matter field）的物质意义。无论取哪一分支，经典的物质与时空之分在量子化后均不成立。

Abstract: The existence of energy is the main criterion for (i) providing a common core to the materiality of all types of classical matter and (ii) denying that the gravitational field of General Relativity is a type of matter. Extending this criterion to quantum field theory, though formally straightforward, carries new conceptual consequences for the interpretation of gravity. Renormalization of the quantum matter stress-energy tensor forces a local, purely geometric term, built from the metric alone, into the conserved stress-energy, and this has two faces. It grants the metric a material significance alongside its spatio-temporal one (the egalitarian view); and it binds gravitational to non-gravitational energy so tightly that the two cannot be split apart without a choice of vacuum that a generic spacetime does not supply (the unificationist view). The latter poses a dilemma: either gravitational energy is meaningless, but then so is the energy of the non-gravitational quantum fields, and only classical fields remain matter; or both are meaningful, and the metric thereby acquires the material significance of a matter field. On neither horn does the classical divide between matter and spacetime survive quantization.

</details>

### [103] [Descalarization bursts from Galactic supramassive neutron stars: a kHz continuous-wave target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10728)
*José Carlos Olvera M.,Daniela D. Doneva,Pablo Cerdá-Durán,José A. Font,Stoytcho S. Yazadjiev*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在大质量标量-张量引力（massive scalar-tensor gravity）框架下，中子星塌缩为黑洞时须脱去标量毛（scalar hair），其发射的爆发经色散（dispersion）展宽为kHz频段、持续超过10^4年的准单色信号，可用连续波方法探测。定向搜索无中心脉冲星的超新星遗迹时，A+-ET-CE探测器网络所需灵敏度深度为233--762 Hz^{-1/2}；任一观测时刻处于传播中的此类信号估计最多约3个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量标量-张量引力预言标量化的中子星在塌缩成黑洞时必须失去标量毛，该过程可能产生可观测的引力波信号。文中利用现实旋转前身星的三维模拟来评估此类信号的探测可行性，动机在于连接该理论预言与现有及未来引力波探测能力。

Method: 采用现实旋转前身星（realistic rotating progenitor）的三维模拟（3D simulations），分析标量毛脱落所发射爆发的传播与色散效应，并针对无中心脉冲星的超新星遗迹（supernova remnants）开展定向搜索（directed searches）的灵敏度估算，基于A+-ET-CE探测器网络。

Result: 色散将发射爆发拉伸为kHz频段的准单色信号，持续时间超过10^4年，适合连续波（continuous-wave）方法探测。在A+-ET-CE探测器网络下，定向搜索所需灵敏度深度为233--762 Hz^{-1/2}，且估计任一观测时刻最多约3个此类信号处于传播中。

Conclusion: 标量毛脱落产生的信号可转化为长时标准单色kHz引力波，具备连续波探测潜力，并给出了面向超新星遗迹定向搜索的灵敏度需求与预期事件率量级。

Abstract: In massive scalar-tensor gravity, a scalarized neutron star must shed its scalar hair as it collapses into a black hole. Using 3D simulations of a realistic rotating progenitor we show that dispersion stretches the emitted burst into a quasi-monochromatic signal in the kHz frequency range lasting $>10^4$ yr and amenable to continuous-wave methods. We find that detecting these signals in directed searches of supernova remnants without a central pulsar requires sensitivity depths of 233--762 $\rm{Hz^{-1/2}}$ with the A+-ET-CE detector network. We estimate up to $\sim3$ of these signals in transit at any observational epoch.

</details>


### [104] [Autocorrelation in black hole flare movies: Departures from Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12199)
*Fang-Stars Wei,Zelin Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Bin Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于参数化 Johannsen-Psaltis 度规中轨道热斑的射线追踪影片，提取时间-角度自相关图中次级脊的斜率与截距作为观测量，并证明其可联合约束自旋与度规形变参数，部分低自旋负形变情形可落在 Kerr 参考区域之外，具备区分非 Kerr 特征的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞影片中的自相关（autocorrelation）编码了引力透镜（gravitational lensing）像之间的时间延迟与角分离，可用于检验时空几何。本文旨在评估这种透镜诱导相关性是否具备区分 Kerr 度规偏离的能力。

Method: 采用含单一形变参数的参数化 Johannsen-Psaltis 度规（parametrized Johannsen-Psaltis metric），模拟轨道热斑并生成射线追踪（ray-traced）影片；从时间-角度自相关图的近似线性次级脊中提取斜率 k 与截距 b，并考察自旋、形变参数、轨道半径、观测者倾角与轨道角速度等设定下的表现。

Result: 自旋与度规形变参数的变化在 (k,b) 平面上产生不同轨迹，使两个观测量在给定轨道半径、观测者倾角与轨道角速度时可联合约束两参数。通过改变轨道角速度可构造 Kerr 参考区域（Kerr reference region）；对部分低自旋、负形变参数构型，次级峰测得的斜率-截距对落在该参考区域之外。

Conclusion: 该偏离特征在亚开普勒（sub-Keplerian）、开普勒（Keplerian）与超开普勒（super-Keplerian）运动中均出现，表明黑洞耀发影片的次级脊测量有望用于识别非 Kerr 特征。

Abstract: Autocorrelation in black hole movies encodes the time delays and angular separations between gravitationally lensed images, offering a means of testing the spacetime geometry. Using an orbiting hot spot in the parametrized Johannsen-Psaltis metric with a single deformation parameter, we investigate the ability of lensing-induced correlations to distinguish departures from Kerr. From ray-traced movies, we extract the slope (k) and intercept (b) of an approximately linear secondary ridge in the time-angle autocorrelation map as potential future observables. Variations in the spin and the metric deformation parameter produce distinctive trajectories in the (k,b) plane, allowing the two observables to jointly constrain both parameters when the orbital radius, observer inclination, and orbital angular velocity are specified. By varying the orbital angular velocity, we construct a Kerr reference region in the (k,b) plane, and we find that, for some low-spin configurations with negative deformation parameters, the measured slope-intercept pairs of the secondary peaks lie outside this reference region. This feature appears for sub-Keplerian, Keplerian, and super-Keplerian motion, indicating that secondary-ridge measurements from black hole flare movies have the potential to distinguish non-Kerr signatures.

</details>


### [105] [Evidence for orbital eccentricity supports a hierarchical origin for GW231123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12205)
*Baoxiang Wang,Tao Yang,Zhoujian Cao,Bin Hu,Xikai Shan,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: GW231123 更可能是偏心（eccentric）双黑洞并合，而非准圆轨道（quasi-circular）事件；引入偏心性后自旋推断显著改变，支持其起源于稠密环境中的层级并合（hierarchical merger）通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW231123 是目前观测到的质量最大的双黑洞并合事件，也是位于对不稳定质量间隙（pair-instability mass gap）中最引人注目的层级并合候选体之一。其推断出的极大分量自旋与极短的带内持续时间，使该源对并合附近的波形物理高度敏感，因而需要检验更一般的波形描述。

Method: 研究在 11–448 Hz 频段分析数据，采用偏心且自旋进动（spin-precessing）的有效单体（effective-one-body）波形模型 TEOBResumS–Dalí，并计入探测器校准（calibration）不确定性。同时开展类 GW231123 的注入—恢复（injection–recovery）研究，比较不同起始频率对偏心性探测的影响。

Result: 测得 5.5 Hz 处偏心率为 e_{5.5Hz}=0.34^{+0.12}_{-0.23}（90% 置信度），偏心模型相对准圆模型的贝叶斯因子（Bayes factor）为 10.2。注入—恢复研究表明，若如既往分析那样从 20 Hz 起始，会掩盖该偏心性。纳入偏心性后，自旋推断发生实质变化，次级近极值自旋（near-extremal secondary spin）的偏好被削弱，其后验中位数降至 a_2≃0.7，接近黑洞并合遗迹（remnant）的典型自旋。

Conclusion: 结果给出 GW231123 的一致动力学解释：残余偏心性（residual eccentricity）、位于质量间隙中的黑洞以及类遗迹自旋，可自然地由稠密环境中的层级并合通道产生。

Abstract: GW231123 is the most massive binary black hole merger observed to date and one of the most intriguing hierarchical merger candidates in the pair-instability mass gap. Its exceptionally large inferred component spins and remarkably short in-band duration make the source highly sensitive to waveform physics near merger. Here we show that GW231123 is better described as an eccentric binary black-hole merger. We analyse data over 11--448 Hz using the eccentric, spin-precessing effective-one-body waveform model TEOBResumS--Dalí, accounting for detector calibration uncertainty. We measure $e_{5.5\,\mathrm{Hz}}=0.34^{+0.12}_{-0.23}$ at 90\% credibility and obtain a Bayes factor of 10.2 in favour of the eccentric model over its quasi-circular counterpart. GW231123-like injection--recovery studies further show that analyses starting at 20 Hz, as in previous studies, can obscure this eccentricity. Including eccentricity substantially changes the spin inference, weakening the preference for a near-extremal secondary spin and lowering its posterior median to $a_2\simeq0.7$, close to the characteristic spin expected for a black-hole merger remnant. These results provide a coherent dynamical interpretation of GW231123, in which residual eccentricity, a mass-gap black hole, and a remnant-like spin arise naturally from a hierarchical merger channel in a dense environment.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

### [106] [From Tunnel Overburden Reconstruction to Tidal Measurements via atmospheric muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11368)
*Jiwon Seo,Jongseok Chung,Chang Hyon Ha,TongZhou Huang,Jinyoung Kim,Sooa Kim,Hani Kimku,Byoung-cheol Koh,Minsu Kwak,Sihyun Lee,Yujin Lee*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: HAWL 与升级版 HAWLU 展示了两类动态缪子成像（dynamic muography）：一为车载移动探测，一为对随时间变化目标的探测，分别实现 6.0% 隧道长度精度与米级潮位监测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于闪烁体（scintillator）探测器的缪子成像（muography）可用于探测大型结构内部密度分布。研究旨在将缪子成像从静态场景拓展至动态场景，即探测器自身运动或被测目标随时间变化的情形。

Method: HAWL 项目采用车载移动探测器测量宇宙线缪子（cosmic-ray muons），对韩国东部山区进行实时便携式缪子层析成像（muon tomography）。升级系统 HAWLU 采用更紧凑设计并支持径迹重建（trajectory reconstruction），在保宁海底隧道（Boryeong Undersea Tunnel）连续 31.6 天测量潮位变化。

Result: HAWL 实现隧道长度精度 6.0%，覆盖层测量范围 8–400 米水当量（meter-water-equivalent, m.w.e.）。HAWLU 实现米级潮位测量精度，测得缪子率随潮位每升高 1 米下降 1.72 ± 0.04%；海底隧道扫描的径迹重建独立验证了探测器性能。

Conclusion: 结果验证了动态缪子成像在两种途径下的能力：便携式探测器在运动中测量，以及探测器测量随时间变化的目标。

Abstract: Muography with scintillator-based detectors enables the investigation of density distributions within large structures. The Hankuk Atmospheric-muon Wide Landscaping (HAWL) project is the first real-time portable muon tomography. It successfully charted the mountainous region of eastern Korea by measuring cosmic-ray muons with a detector operated in a moving vehicle. HAWL achieved a tunnel length accuracy of 6.0 % and an overburden measurement range of 8-400 meter-water-equivalent (m.w.e.). The HAWL upgrade (HAWLU) features a more compact design and enables trajectory reconstruction. Using this upgraded system, we measured tidal water-level variations with meter-scale precision over 31.6 days in the Boryeong Undersea Tunnel. The measured muon rate decreased by $1.72 \pm 0.04$ % per meter of increasing tidal level, while the reconstruction of undersea tunnel scan independently validated the detector performance. These results demonstrate the capabilities of dynamic muography in two approaches, one with a portable detector in motion and the other with a detector measuring a target that changes over time.

</details>


### [107] [First demonstration of $50\,\mathrm{ps}$ four-dimensional vertex reconstruction with the Timepix4 beam telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12084)
*David Bacher,Kazu Akiba,Martin van Beuzekom,Victor Coco,Raphael Dumps,Tim Evans,Kevin Heijhoff,Malcolm John,Edgar Lemos Cid,Loris Martinazzoli,Tommaso Pajero,Yutaro Takahashi,Espen de Wit*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 首次在硅像素探测器中实现50 ps量级的四维顶点重建，利用Timepix4束流望远镜与180 GeV/c混合强子束，顶点时间分辨率达54±5 ps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为面向未来高堆积（high-pile-up）探测器的精密定时（precision timing）需求，需要验证在50 ps尺度上实现四维顶点重建的可行性。

Method: 将180 GeV/c混合强子束（mixed-hadron beam）打向CERN SPS上Timepix4束流望远镜（beam telescope）上游的固定靶以产生强子相互作用，并采用将时间与空间参数一并纳入的四维径迹与顶点拟合（4D track and vertex fits）。

Result: 顶点时间分辨率随定时测量数目呈预期统计标度行为，由16个定时平面簇（timing-plane clusters）重建的顶点达54±5 ps；拟合分辨率模型中残留29.2±0.6 ps的不可约分量（irreducible component），主要来自时钟分布与时间参考系统而非定时测量的组合。

Conclusion: 在未出现未预期分辨率下限的情况下实现低至54 ps的标度行为，构成迈向未来高堆积探测器精密定时的重要实验进展。

Abstract: The first demonstration of 4D vertex reconstruction at the $50\,\mathrm{ps}$ scale using a silicon pixel detector is reported. Hadronic interactions are produced by directing a $180\,\mathrm{GeV}/c$ mixed-hadron beam onto a fixed target upstream of the Timepix4 beam telescope at the CERN SPS. The resulting particles are reconstructed using 4D track and vertex fits that include time directly alongside the spatial parameters. The vertex time resolution follows the expected statistical scaling with the number of timing measurements, reaching $54\pm5\,\mathrm{ps}$ for vertices reconstructed from 16 timing-plane clusters. An irreducible component of $29.2\pm0.6\,\mathrm{ps}$ remains in the fitted resolution model, including sizeable contributions from the clock distribution and the time reference system rather than from the combination of the timing measurements. The observed scaling down to $54\,\mathrm{ps}$ without an unforeseen resolution floor above represents a significant experimental step towards precision-timing in future high-pile-up detectors.

</details>


### [108] [Cavity Characterization for the Princeton Axion Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12298)
*Nate Otto,Jianyang Fu,Joelle-Marie Begin,Saptarshi Chaudhuri,Roman Kolevatov,Lyman Page,Joe Wiedemann*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 普林斯顿轴子搜寻实验（Princeton Axion Search, PXS）采用铜镀铝圆柱腔谐振器（haloscope），在0.8–2.1 μeV质量窗口、200–500 MHz频段内以DFSZ灵敏度为目标。本文通过介质与金属小球微扰测量腔体电磁场模式，验证了腔体结构与仿真的一致性。结果确认了PXS的腔体架构，并为未来亚μeV轴子搜寻提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PXS旨在以DFSZ灵敏度探测0.8–2.1 μeV（对应200–500 MHz）的轴子（axion）质量窗口。实现量子色动力学（QCD）灵敏度需要大体积、高Q值的腔体结构，因此需对腔体架构与场模式进行实验验证。研究还旨在为未来亚μeV轴子搜寻提供设计依据。

Method: 实验使用直径700 mm、高1022 mm的铜镀铝圆柱腔，置于5 T螺线管磁场中，由直径152 mm贯穿腔高的调谐杆旋转，将主模共振从390调谐至480 MHz。通过在空腔上施加介质与金属小球微扰（bead perturbation）推演电场与磁场模式，并将实测模式频率与TM010场分布随调谐杆位置的变化与仿真对比。

Result: 实测模式频率与TM010场分布在调谐杆位置变化下与仿真高度一致。研究描述了端板鼓凸与调谐杆倾斜等加工与装配缺陷的影响；焊接缝与端盖接合被证明不会显著降低Q值。结合测量与仿真，评估了全调谐范围内形状因子（form factor）的不确定度。

Conclusion: 结果验证了PXS的腔体架构，并为未来亚μeV轴子搜寻提供参考，此类搜寻需要大体积高Q值结构以达成QCD灵敏度。

Abstract: The Princeton Axion Search (PXS) targets the mass window $0.8\text{--}2.1~μ\text{eV}$ (corresponding to frequencies $200\text{--}500~\text{MHz}$) at DFSZ sensitivity. The experiment, a haloscope, will use a cylindrical copper-plated aluminum cavity of $700~\text{mm}$ diameter and $1022~\text{mm}$ height, immersed in a $5~\text{T}$ solenoidal magnetic field. A $152~\text{mm}$ diameter tuning rod spanning the height of the cavity rotates to tune the primary mode resonance from $390\text{--}480~\text{MHz}$, the frequency range for initial operations. We deduce the electric and magnetic field mode patterns using a combination of dielectric and metallic bead perturbations on the empty cavity. The measured mode frequencies and the $\text{TM}_{010}$ field profile, as a function of tuning rod position, are in excellent agreement with simulations. We describe the effect of fabrication and assembly imperfections, including the bowing of end plates and tilt of the tuning rod. The welded seams and end-cap joints are demonstrated to not significantly degrade $Q$. We combine measurement and simulation to assess an uncertainty on form factor across the full tuning range. Our results validate PXS's cavity architecture and inform future searches for sub-$μ\text{eV}$ axions, in which large-volume high-$Q$ structures are required to reach QCD sensitivity.

</details>


### [109] [Autocorrelation-based beam shape optimization in scanning electron microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11414)
*Rebecca Guan,Andreas Werbrouck,Matthew M. Maschmann,Matthias J Young*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于图像自相关（autocorrelation）的扫描电子显微镜（scanning electron microscope, SEM）自动聚焦与消像散算法，利用自相关近似当前束斑形状，并以贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimization）调节工作距离与像散参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SEM自动聚焦算法通常仅优化单一派生指标（如图像锐度），未考虑束斑的内在形状，因此难以同时校正聚焦与像散。

Method: 工作分两部分：首先建立以自相关逼近当前束斑形状的理论基础；随后据此对束斑形状进行条件化，使用贝叶斯优化同时调节工作距离（working distance）与像散（stigmation）。

Result: 通过比较优化前后自相关阵列的半高全宽（full width at half maximum, FWHM）以及优化前后样品表面图像的视觉对比，表明算法能成功优化扫描参数。

Conclusion: 该算法可在不同放大倍率下有效聚焦于多种形貌（topographies）表面。

Abstract: Many automatically focusing algorithms for scanning electron microscopes exist. Typically, these optimize a single derived metric (such as image sharpness) without considering the intrinsic beam shape. We describe an algorithm to automatically focus and stigmate scanning electron microscopes based on the autocorrelation of captured images. In the first part of this work, we develop the theoretical basis of using the autocorrelation to approximate the current beam shape. In the second part, we use this to condition the beam shape on various surfaces, using Bayesian optimization to tune both working distance and, in particular, stigmation. Through a comparison of the full width at half maximum of the autocorrelation arrays before and after optimization and a visual comparison of images taken of the sample surface before and after optimization, we show that the algorithm is able to successfully optimize the scanning parameters. Overall, our algorithm can effectively focus on a variety of topographies at different magnifications.

</details>
