# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Explicit Robin Green's Functions, Resonance Spectra, and Impedance Recovery on Annuli and Spherical Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39643)
*Ming Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文针对环形与球壳区域上带双独立 Robin 阻抗（Robin impedance）的 Helmholtz 方程（Helmholtz equation），构造了显式闭式 Green 函数（Green's function），并系统刻画了共振谱（resonance spectrum）的结构、渐近行为与反问题可辨识性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环形与球壳域的双阻抗边值问题在声学、电磁与屏蔽腔体等场景中常见，但缺乏显式闭式解与对阻抗依赖关系的严格谱分析。论文旨在给出可机精度计算的核函数，并揭示共振谱对两阻抗的双线性结构及其反问题含义。

Method: 利用 Graf 加法定理（Graf's addition theorem）与超球加法定理（hyperspherical addition theorem）将每个角模态（angular mode）化为显式 2×2 线性系统，共振由两阻抗的双线性特征行列式（characteristic determinant）决定；并推导谱支的单调性、四角渐近、低频无共振带与高频间距律，辅以基于 Jacobian 的灵敏度与条件数判据。

Result: 证明谱支在两阻抗上为正、单重且严格递增；给出阻抗平面四角的一阶渐近，系数由边界质量与极限本征函数的法向导数给出，并在 Dirichlet–Dirichlet 角给出显式混合二阶系数（径向模态闭式为 2π/(R₂−R₁)³）；建立低频无共振带、三维二阶阈值展开与全域有理逼近；证明带壳曲率修正的普适高频间距律；反问题中一个非径向模态的两个共振频率经显式二次方程至多产生两组候选阻抗，第三个共振确定物理解，并识别径向球模态不可辨识的反射对称障碍。

Conclusion: 核函数与谱可计算至机器精度，各渐近区间均经数值验证，所得核可作为有限元与边界元（finite-element / boundary-element）验证的参考解。

Abstract: This paper constructs explicit closed-form Green's functions for the Helmholtz equation on annular and spherical-shell domains with two independent Robin impedances on the inner and outer boundaries. Graf's and the hyperspherical addition theorems reduce each angular mode to an explicit $2\times2$ linear system, and the resonance spectrum is governed by a characteristic determinant bilinear in the two impedances. This bilinearity has a direct inverse-problem consequence: two resonant frequencies of one non-radial angular mode generate at most two candidate impedance pairs via an explicit quadratic equation, a third resonance selects the physical pair, and an exact reflection-symmetry obstruction identifies where the radial spherical mode cannot recover the impedances. Spectrally, we prove the branches positive, simple and strictly increasing in both impedances; derive first-order asymptotics at the four corners of the impedance plane, with coefficients given by boundary masses and normal derivatives of the limiting eigenfunctions, and an explicit mixed second-order coefficient at the Dirichlet--Dirichlet corner, which for the radial mode evaluates in closed form to $2π/(R_2-R_1)^3$; establish a low-frequency resonance-free band, with a second-order threshold expansion explicit in dimension three and a rational approximation accurate over the whole impedance range; and prove the universal high-frequency spacing law with a shell-curvature correction. Jacobian-based sensitivity and conditioning criteria are included. The kernels and spectra are computable to machine precision, all asymptotic regimes are confirmed numerically, and the kernels provide reference solutions for finite-element and boundary-element validation.

</details>


### [111] [A Complete Proof of Mueller--Ho Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12203)
*Senping Luo,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究经典与移位 theta 函数（Gaussian lattice sums）在模群（modular group）意义下的极小值问题，证明存在三个阈值 0<α_a<α_b<α_c<1，给出 θ(1;z)+αJ(z;a,b) 最小值的分段解析结构，从而完整证明 Mueller 与 Ho (2002) 关于玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose–Einstein condensates）的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出该研究源自玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose–Einstein condensates）中的 Mueller–Ho 猜想（2002），复变量 z 与向量 (a,b) 分别对应双组分玻色气体（two-component Bose gas）的总涡旋形状与相对位置。论文动机即在于给出该猜想的完整严格证明。

Method: 定义经典 theta 函数 θ(s;z) 与移位 theta 函数 J(z;a,b)（均为高斯格和），并考察组合量 θ(1;z)+αJ(z;a,b) 在 z∈H、(a,b)∈R² 上的极小值，且在模群（modular group）意义下进行分类讨论。

Result: 证明存在三个阈值 0<α_a<α_b<α_c<1，使最小值（modulo modular group）呈现五个区间：α∈[0,α_a) 时为 (e^{iπ/3};1/3,1/3)；α=α_a 时为 (e^{iπ/3};1/3,1/3) 或 (e^{iφ_{α_a}};1/2,1/2)；α∈(α_a,α_b) 时为 (e^{iφ_α};1/2,1/2)，其中 φ_α∈(φ_{α_a},π/2)；α∈[α_b,α_c] 时为 (i;1/2,1/2)；α∈(α_c,1] 时为 (iy_α;1/2,1/2)，y_α∈(1,√3] 且 y_1=√3。

Conclusion: 上述极小值结构完全证明了 Mueller 与 Ho (2002) 在玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚中提出的猜想。

Abstract: For $s>0$, and $z\in\mathbb H=\{z=x+iy:y>0\}$, define
  $θ(s;z)=\sum_{m,n\in\mathbb Z}e^{-πs\big(m^2y+\frac{(mx-n)^2}{y}\big)}$ and $
  J(z;a,b)=\sum_{m,n\in\mathbb Z}e^{-π\big(m^2y+\frac{(mx-n)^2}{y}\big)}
  \cos\bigl(2π(ma+nb)\bigr)$ for the classical and shifted theta functions (Gaussian lattice sums), respectively.
  We prove that, up to the modular group, there exist three thresholds $0<α_a<α_b<α_c<1$ such that \begin{equation}\aligned\nonumber \Minima_{z\in\mathbb H,\,(a,b)\in\mathbb R^2}\Big(θ(1;z)+αJ(z;a,b)\Big)= \begin{cases} \;\big(e^{iπ/{3}};1/3,1/3\big), &\hbox{if}\;\; α\in[0,α_a),\\ \;\big(e^{iπ/{3}};1/3,1/3\big)\;\hbox{or}\;\big(e^{i{\varphi_{α_a}}};1/2,1/2\big), &\hbox{if}\;\; α=α_a,\\ \;\big(e^{i{\varphi_α}};1/2,1/2\big),\;\varphi_α\in(\varphi_{α_a},\fracπ{2}),\;\; &\hbox{if}\;\; α\in(α_a,α_b),\\ \;\big(i;1/2,1/2\big),\; &\hbox{if}\;\; α\in[α_b,α_c],\\ \;\big(iy_α;1/2,1/2\big),\; y_α\in(1,\sqrt3],\;\;y_1=\sqrt3,\;\;&\hbox{if}\;\; α\in(α_c,1], \end{cases} \endaligned\end{equation} where the complex variable $z$ and vector $(a,b)$, represent the total vortex shapes and relative positions of the two-component Bose gas, respectively. Consequently, this completely proves the conjecture of Mueller and Ho~\cite{Mue2002} (2002) arising in Bose--Einstein condensates.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Omnisolver: An extensible interface to Ising spin-glass and QUBO solvers: adding a distributed GPU brute-force plugin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10542)
*Konrad Jałowiecki,Jakub Pawłowski,Bartłomiej Gardas,Łukasz Pawela*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该软件更新为 Omnisolver 引入首个插件 omnisolver-bruteforce，实现 QUBO 与 Ising 实例在 CUDA GPU 上的精确穷举搜索，并通过 Ray 分布式框架跨多 GPU、多主机扩展，附带 float32 路径的数值稳定化。在稠密随机 Ising 实例上，8× NVIDIA H100 可将穷举求解推进至 N=60，约 3.15 天完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典启发式算法（如模拟分岔机，Simulated Bifurcation Machine）需要可靠的精确解作为基准，而现有穷举工具在多 GPU 扩展性与数值稳定性方面不足。该更新旨在将单 GPU 暴力内核升级为具统一接口、可分布扩展的 ground-truth oracle（真值预言机）。

Method: 方法包含三部分：将原单 GPU 暴力内核（Computer Physics Communications 260, 107728, 2021）封装为具统一 Python 与 CLI 接口的一等插件；构建基于 Ray 的分布式框架，把搜索拆分为 2^k 个固定变量子问题并分发至多 GPU 与多主机，由控制器收集合并部分结果；通过补偿式增量更新、周期性精确能量重锚定和最优缓冲区刷新，对 float32 快速基态路径进行数值稳定化（N ≥ 40 自动启用）。

Result: 在稠密随机 Ising 实例上，8× NVIDIA H100（96 GB）GPU 可实现至 N=60 的穷举求解，N=60 时约耗时 3.15 天，与经验倍增规则 t_{N+1}=2 t_N 基本吻合；分布式采样器自 N ≳ 44 起相对单张 H100 达到约 8× 的完整加速。

Conclusion: 该插件作为实用 ground-truth oracle，可为模拟分岔机等最先进经典启发式算法提供精确参考解；公共采样器 API 未发生变更。

Abstract: This software update extends Omnisolver with \texttt{omnisolver-bruteforce}, a first-class plugin for exact exhaustive search of QUBO and Ising instances on CUDA-enabled GPUs. The update contributes three components: packaging of the single-GPU brute-force kernel of [Computer Physics Communications 260, 107728, 2021] as a first-class Omnisolver plugin with a uniform Python and CLI interface, a Ray-based distributed framework that splits the search into $2^{k}$ fixed-variable subproblems and dispatches them across multiple GPUs and hosts, with a controller that gathers and merges partial results, and numerical stabilization of the fast \texttt{float32} ground-state path through compensated incremental updates, periodic exact energy re-anchoring, and best-buffer refresh. Stabilization activates automatically for $N \geq 40$ leaving the public sampler API is unchanged. On dense random Ising instances we measure exhaustive solve times up to $N=60$ on $8\times$ NVIDIA H100 (96 GB) GPUs, reaching $\approx\!3.15$ days at $N=60$ in close agreement with the empirical $t_{N+1}=2\,t_N$ doubling rule, with the distributed sampler reaching the full $\approx\!8\times$ speedup over a single H100 from $N\!\gtrsim\!44$ onwards. The plugin thereby serves as a practical ground-truth oracle for state-of-the-art classical heuristics such as the Simulated Bifurcation Machine.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Dirac operators on periodic planar graphs: the right definition advocated by flat bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10964)
*Koichi Kato,Daniel Parra,Serge Richard*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究周期平面图上的大质量离散狄拉克型算子，借助Floquet–Bloch分解纠正了忽略面所导致的虚假平带，并给出正确狄拉克–Hodge算子谱的还原公式，最终应用于阿基米德格与Lieb格的平带对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期平面图上的离散狄拉克型算子谱结构（尤其平带）尚需严格刻画，且朴素处理中忽略面（face）可能引入与图几何无关的伪谱特征，需要澄清正确的算子及其谱来源。

Method: 采用Floquet–Bloch分解（Floquet–Bloch decomposition）分析周期平面图上的大质量离散狄拉克型算子；针对正确的狄拉克–Hodge算子（Dirac–Hodge operator）$D_m$ 建立谱还原，将 $σ(D_m)$ 用顶点上的图拉普拉斯（graph Laplacian）$-Δ_V$ 与面上的拉普拉斯 $-Δ_F$ 表达。

Result: 首先证明忽略面会导致与图几何无关的虚假平带（spurious flat bands）。对正确的 $D_m$，建立其谱通过 $-Δ_V$ 与 $-Δ_F$ 的还原；由此得出 $D_m$ 携带平带当且仅当上述两个标量拉普拉斯之一携带平带。该还原被应用于阿基米德格（Archimedean lattices）上 $D_m$ 的平带研究，并与Lieb格上的结果进行对比。

Conclusion: 正确处理面自由度对于刻画周期平面图上的离散狄拉克算子谱至关重要；$D_m$ 的平带存在性可完全归结为标量拉普拉斯 $-Δ_V$ 或 $-Δ_F$ 的平带，从而为不同格（如阿基米德格与Lieb格）的平带分析提供统一框架。

Abstract: Massive discrete Dirac-type operators on periodic planar graphs are investigated. Using Floquet--Bloch decomposition, we first show that neglecting the faces leads to spurious flat bands that are unrelated to the graph's geometry. Then, for the correct Dirac--Hodge operator $D_m$, we establish a spectral reduction expressing $σ(D_m)$ through the graph Laplacian on vertices $-Δ_V$ and the Laplacian on faces $-Δ_F$. As a consequence, $D_m$ carries a flat band precisely when one of these scalar Laplacians does. We finally apply this reduction to the study of flat bands of the operator $D_m$ on Archimedean lattices and contrast the outcome with that on the Lieb lattice.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Quantum simulation of material-specific Hubbard models with alkaline-earth atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10713)
*Henning Schlömer,Reuben R. W. Wang,Bo Xing,Susanne F. Yelin,H. R. Sadeghpour*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于费米碱土原子（fermionic alkaline-earth atoms）的集成量子模拟架构，可编程调控多轨道材料哈密顿量（multi-orbital Hamiltonian）的关键要素，并以双层镍酸盐 La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ 的四带低能哈密顿量为基准进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟的长期目标是忠实再现关联量子材料（correlated quantum materials）的低能哈密顿量，从而在固态实验、电子结构理论与可编程多体系统之间建立直接联系。现有方案难以在单一平台内同时再现多轨道材料哈密顿量的全部关键要素。

Method: 采用长寿命钟态（clock states）编码合成轨道（synthetic orbitals），利用其差分极化率（differential polarizabilities）产生轨道选择性隧穿；接触相互作用提供多轨道 Hubbard 与 Hund 耦合；在超窄钟跃迁上施加空间结构化的相干驱动实现对称性选择的轨道间杂化（inter-orbital hybridization）；钟失谐独立控制轨道能级偏移。原子本身仍处于最低光晶格带的常规 s 波 Wannier 轨道。

Result: 以双层镍酸盐 La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ 的四带低能哈密顿量为苛刻基准，自洽计算表明，第一性原理建模得到的微观参数可在单一实验可达的晶格配置中再现，并能在较大参数范围内独立编程。

Conclusion: 该结果为将材料衍生的多轨道哈密顿量转换为可编程超冷原子量子模拟器（ultracold-atom quantum simulators）提供了通用路径。

Abstract: A long-term goal of quantum simulation is to faithfully reproduce the low-energy Hamiltonians of correlated quantum materials, establishing a direct connection between solid-state experiment, electronic-structure theory, and programmable many-body systems. Here, we introduce an integrated quantum-simulation architecture based on fermionic alkaline-earth atoms that enables programmable control over the key ingredients of multi-orbital material Hamiltonians. Long-lived clock states encode synthetic orbitals, their differential polarizabilities generate orbital-selective tunneling, and contact interactions provide multi-orbital Hubbard and Hund couplings. Spatially structured coherent driving on the ultra-narrow clock transition further realizes symmetry-selective inter-orbital hybridization, while the clock detuning independently controls orbital energy offsets. In this way, the spatial symmetries of solid-state orbitals can be encoded directly into the effective Hamiltonian, even though the atoms themselves remain in conventional $s$-wave Wannier orbitals of the lowest optical-lattice bands. We demonstrate the resulting integrated control using the four-band low-energy Hamiltonian of the bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ as a demanding benchmark. Self-consistent calculations show that the microscopic parameters obtained from first-principles modeling can be reproduced within a single experimentally accessible lattice configuration and independently programmed over substantial parameter ranges. Our results establish a general route for translating material-derived multi-orbital Hamiltonians into programmable ultracold-atom quantum simulators.

</details>


### [115] [Universal Drude Weights in One-dimensional Repulsive Fermi Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11137)
*Xiangguo Yin,Dawei Niu,Shuang Zhang,Shizhong Zhang,Yunbo Zhang,Xi-Wen Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 基于贝特拟设（Bethe ansatz）与广义流体力学（generalized hydrodynamics），严格建立了排斥型一维双组分连续费米气体（Yang-Gaudin 模型）中电荷、自旋与能量输运的 Drude 权重与热力学量之间的普适关系，并揭示其与零温相边界的对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积量子气体中的输运性质此前理解不足，尤其是电荷、自旋、能量输运的 Drude 权重与热力学量之间的关系缺乏严格结论，本文旨在填补该空白并为超冷原子实验提供理论基础。

Method: 以贝特拟设与广义流体力学为工具，对一维排斥型双组分连续费米气体（Yang-Gaudin 模型）进行解析推导，得到电荷 Drude 权重 $D_{nn}$、$D_{nm}$、$D_{ne}$ 的热力学表达式，并研究零温与低温下的自旋 Drude 权重 $D_{mm}$、$D_{me}$。

Result: 解析结果给出 $D_{nn}$、$D_{nm}$、$D_{ne}$ 分别等于粒子密度、磁化强度以及温度乘以熵密度，构成弹道输运（ballistic transport）的标志并可推广至其他一维连续可积系统；自旋 Drude 权重刻画对磁场梯度的磁化与能量流响应，揭示自旋-电荷耦合（spin-charge coupling）特征；$D_{nm}$、$D_{mm}$、$D_{me}$ 在 $\mu\text{-}H$ 平面上的分布可映射出零温相边界。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了可积量子气体输运理解的空白，并为未来超冷原子实验提供直接的理论基础。

Abstract: Building on the Bethe ansatz and generalized hydrodynamics, we rigorously establish universal relations between Drude weights governing charge, spin, and energy transport and thermodynamic properties for the one-dimensional repulsive two-component continuum Fermi gas - the paradigmatic integrable Yang-Gaudin model. We analytically derive thermodynamic expressions for charge Drude weights: $D_{nn}$, $D_{nm}$, $D_{ne}$ (responses of particle, magnetization and energy currents to a chemical potential gradient) equal the particle density, magnetization, and temperature times entropy density, respectively. These relations constitute a hallmark of ballistic transport, and can be generalized to other 1D continuum integrable systems. Furthermore, we investigate spin Drude weight ($D_{mm}$, $D_{me}$) at zero and low temperatures. They characterize the magnetization and energy current responses to a magnetic field gradient, revealing an essential spin-charge coupling feature in quantum transport. We find that the Drude weights $D_{nm}$, $D_{mm}$, and $D_{me}$ across the $μ\text{-}H$ plane remarkably map out the zero-temperature phase boundary. This work fills a critical gap in the understanding of transport within integrable quantum gases and furnishes a direct theoretical foundation for future ultracold-atom experiments.

</details>


### [116] [Nonlinearity management of matter-wave vector solitons of Bose-Einstein condensates in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11536)
*F. Kh. Abdullaev,J. S. Yuldashev,M. Ögren*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究二维物质波矢量孤子（matter-wave vector solitons）在非线性管理（nonlinearity management）下的演化，通过时间快速调制种间相互作用的 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程取平均，导出有效非线性量子压力，发现其可抑制坍缩并实现矢量孤子动力学稳定化，数值模拟验证了孤子寿命的显著延长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究强而快速的时间调制（即非线性管理）能否稳定二维矢量物质波孤子，并理解其抑制坍缩的机制。

Method: 对种间相互作用随时间强快速调制的矢量 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程（vector Gross-Pitaevskii equation）进行时间平均，导出平均化方程，并据此分析二维矢量物质波孤子的动力学稳定化；同时用原始含时方程进行全数值模拟验证。

Result: 平均化过程中出现了依赖于另一组分粒子数的有效非线性量子压力（effective nonlinear quantum pressure）；该非线性量子压力在平均系统中抑制了坍缩（collapse），从而形成稳定的矢量孤子。

Conclusion: 非线性管理可实现对二维矢量物质波孤子的动力学稳定化；相比未管理情形，管理显著延长了孤子寿命，原含时 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程的全数值模拟证实了这一点。

Abstract: The evolution of matter-wave vector solitons in two dimensions under nonlinearity management is studied. The averaged over strong and rapid modulations in time of the inter-species interactions vector Gross-Pitaevskii equation is derived. The averaging gives the appearance of the effective nonlinear quantum pressure depending on the population of the other component. Using this system of equations, the dynamical stabilization of the two-dimensional vector matter-wave solitons under the management of the mean-field nonlinearity is investigated. It is shown that the nonlinear quantum pressure arrests the collapse in the averaged system, giving rise to stable vector solitons. Full numerical simulations of the original, time-dependent system of Gross-Pitaevskii equations confirm that the management strongly prolongs the lifetime of these solitons compared to the unmanaged case.

</details>


### [117] [Constructing Large and Structured Decoherence-Free Subspaces in Hybrid Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12348)
*Catalin-Mihai Halati,Tony Jin,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个构建具有非平凡结构的大尺度无退相干子空间（decoherence-free subspace）的框架，基于量子物质与耗散玻色模式耦合的混合量子系统（hybrid quantum system），利用李代数（Lie algebra）表示论构造具有物理意义零态（null state）的多体算符，并在一维定向隧穿光学腔模型中展示指数级多的稳态、长程关联、体积律纠缠（volume-law entanglement）、非互易李雅普林斯金效应（Liouvillian skin effect）与η配对（η-pairing）强对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无退相干子空间是量子信息与开放量子系统的核心资源，但通常结构平凡、维度受限，缺乏可控的多体关联结构。该文旨在通过耗散这一全局约束，构造具有非平凡结构与涌现对称性的、维度指数大的无退相干子空间，从而为耗散工程复杂量子关联提供途径。

Method: 将量子物质耦合到一个耗散玻色模式，长时极限下耗散通过筛出物质零态对物质施加全局约束。借助李代数的表示论构造具有物理上感兴趣零态的多体算符，并据此按底层代数结构选择模型。显式算例为一维量子粒子的定向隧穿算符（directed tunneling operator）与有损耗光学腔的耦合，其零态由sl(2,C)代数确定。采用解析与数值方法刻画费米子与玻色子的零态，并通过含时矩阵乘积态（matrix product state, MPS）模拟研究一般初态下长时态的涌现。

Result: 在定向隧穿算例中，无退相干子空间的对称性源自sl(2,C)代数，导致指数级数量的稳态。零态展现出长程动力学关联（long-range kinetic correlations）与体积律纠缠，凸显其复杂量子特性；定向隧穿的非互易性在多体区域引发受对称性约束的李雅普林斯金效应（Liouvillian skin effect）；该开放光-物质系统具有源自无自旋η配对代数（spinless η-pairing algebra）的强对称性。含时MPS模拟证实这些非平凡长时性质可在耗散动力学中从一般初态涌现。

Conclusion: 该框架为以可控方式通过耗散工程复杂量子关联并利用非互易性开辟了途径，表明基于李代数表示论挑选模型可系统性地设计具有丰富对称性与纠缠结构的无退相干子空间。

Abstract: We develop a framework to construct large decoherence-free subspaces with a non-trivial structure. The construction is based on hybrid quantum systems in which quantum matter is coupled to a dissipative bosonic mode. Dissipation imposes a global constraint on the matter by selecting the matter null states in the long-time limit. The decoherence-free subspaces spanned by matter null states can exhibit exotic quantum properties and emergent symmetries that do not characterize the full Liouvillian dynamics. We show how to construct many-body operators with physically interesting null states by employing representation theory of Lie algebras. This approach allows us to choose models based on their underlying algebraic structure. We consider explicitly the example of coupling a lossy optical cavity to the directed tunneling operator of quantum particles in one-dimension. In this case, the symmetries of the decoherence-free subspace originate in an $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{C})$ algebra, leading to an exponentially large number of steady states. We characterize the null states for both fermionic and bosonic particles, employing analytical and numerical methods. The states exhibit several properties that underline their complex quantum nature: long-range kinetic correlations and volume-law entanglement. The nonreciprocal nature of the directed tunneling dynamics leads to the emergence in the many-body regime of a symmetry-constrained Liouvillian skin effect. The open light-matter system exhibits a strong symmetry stemming from spinless $η$-pairing algebras. We show how the non-trivial properties of the long-times states emerge in the dissipative dynamics from generic initial states, by performing time-dependent matrix product state simulations. Our framework opens avenues for dissipatively engineering complex quantum correlations and harnessing nonreciprocity in a controlled manner.

</details>

### [118] [Lyapunov spectrum of random neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12426)
*David G. Clark*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作在大 $N$ 极限下计算了具有随机非对称耦合的非线性递归神经网络（recurrent neural network）的李雅普诺夫谱（Lyapunov spectrum），并证明其混沌是广延的（extensive）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对随机非对称耦合非线性递归神经网络，需要刻画其高维混沌的完整谱结构及其是否随系统规模广延增长。

Method: 基于一个有限 $N$ 恒等式，将李雅普诺夫指数的累积分布表示为切空间动力学（tangent-space dynamics）的响应函数，其中切轨迹由最小范数条件而非初始条件选取；再用空腔方法（cavity method）在大 $N$ 下通过自洽单点问题确定该响应函数。

Result: 在大 $N$ 极限下得到该网络的李雅普诺夫谱，并确立其混沌是广延的，同时可获取动力学的微分同胚不变（diffeomorphism-invariant）性质。

Conclusion: 该分析建立了随机非对称递归神经网络中广延混沌的谱刻画，并提供了接触微分同胚不变动力学性质的途径；作者注明该工作与 AI 模型 GPT-6 Astra 和 Claude Opus 5.5 合作完成。

Abstract: The Lyapunov spectrum of a nonlinear recurrent neural network with random asymmetric couplings is calculated in the limit $N\to\infty$. The calculation is based on a finite-$N$ identity that expresses the cumulative distribution of Lyapunov exponents as a response function of the tangent-space dynamics, with the tangent trajectory selected by a minimum-norm condition rather than by an initial condition. A cavity method then determines this response function at large $N$ through a self-consistent single-site problem. This result establishes that the chaos in this network is extensive and gives access to diffeomorphism-invariant properties of the dynamics. This work was done in collaboration with the AI models GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5.

</details>


### [119] [Short-Range Vector Spin Glasses: Parisi Hierarchies and Real-Space Ultrametricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12441)
*Akash Vijay,Jaewon Kim,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文引入一个定义在 $D$ 维超立方晶格上的 $N$ 分量矢量自旋无序模型，其最近邻键上带随机矩阵耦合、元胞面上带随机张量耦合。在大 $N$ 极限下，模型支持热力学稳定的一步、全阶及一步全阶复本对称破缺（replica symmetry breaking, RSB）相。空间局域性通过红外机制约束这些有序相，并给出 Parisi 层级（Parisi hierarchy）的实空间关联长度诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场自旋玻璃理论以复本对称破缺（replica symmetry breaking, RSB）为组织原则，但在有限空间维度的真正短程系统中能否出现连续 Parisi 层级（Parisi hierarchy），此前尚未解决。该问题构成研究的核心动机。

Method: 构建 $N$ 分量矢量自旋在 $D$ 维超立方晶格上的无序模型：最近邻键采用随机矩阵耦合，初等元胞面（plaquette）采用随机张量耦合。在大 $N$ 极限下求解其相图，并借助 Imry-Ma 机制、Ward 恒等式（Ward identity）保护的复本 Goldstone 模（replica Goldstone mode）以及 $1/N$ 领头阶分析，考察空间局域性对有序相的红外约束。

Result: 模型存在热力学稳定的一步、全阶（full）与一步全阶（one-full）RSB 相，以及复本对称（replica-symmetric）顺磁相与铁磁相。空间局域性通过两种红外机制约束有序相：(i) 淬火随机各向异性经 Imry-Ma 机制破坏 $D\leq4$ 的均匀铁磁性；(ii) 连续 Parisi 层级产生由 Ward 恒等式保护其无能隙性的复本 Goldstone 模，在 $1/N$ 领头阶表现为自由自旋波带，导致 $D\leq2$ 的红外发散涨落及连续破缺相的 Mermin-Wagner 不稳定性。此外，Parisi 层级获得实空间诠释：对互重叠为 $q$ 的两个纯态，特征长度 $\xi(q)$ 刻画其冻结磁化图样差异保持关联的尺度；在 Parisi 层级的连续区域，$\xi(q)$ 与温度无关并随 $q$ 单调递减。纯态的超度量（ultrametric）组织对应实空间关联长度的超度量层级：在 Parisi 树根附近分离的态通过长波冻结图样相区别，距根较远处分离的态仅在渐次更短的尺度上相区别。

Conclusion: 研究表明，连续 Parisi 层级可在有限维短程系统中热力学稳定存在，但受空间局域性通过 Imry-Ma 机制与复本 Goldstone 模施加的红外约束限制。纯态的超度量层级同时体现为实空间关联长度的超度量层级，从而为 Parisi 层级提供了直接的实空间解释。

Abstract: Replica symmetry breaking (RSB) is the organizing principle of mean-field spin-glass theory, yet whether a continuous Parisi hierarchy can arise in a genuinely short-range system in finite spatial dimension remains unresolved. Here we introduce a disordered model of $N$-component vector spins on a $D$-dimensional hypercubic lattice, with random matrix couplings on nearest-neighbor links and random tensor couplings on elementary plaquettes. In the large-$N$ limit, the model supports thermodynamically stable one-step, full, and one-full RSB phases, alongside replica-symmetric paramagnetic and ferromagnetic phases. We find that spatial locality constrains these ordered phases through two distinct infrared mechanisms. ($i$) Quenched random anisotropy destroys uniform ferromagnetism for $D\leq4$ through an Imry-Ma mechanism. ($ii$) A continuous Parisi hierarchy gives rise to replica Goldstone modes whose gaplessness is protected by a Ward identity. At leading order in $1/N$, these modes behave as a band of free spin waves, producing infrared-divergent fluctuations in $D\leq2$ and a Mermin-Wagner instability of the continuously broken phase.
  Remarkably, the Parisi hierarchy also acquires a direct real-space interpretation. For two pure states with mutual overlap $q$, a characteristic length $ξ(q)$ describes how far the differences between their frozen magnetization patterns remain correlated. Throughout a continuous sector of the Parisi hierarchy, $ξ(q)$ is independent of temperature and decreases monotonically with $q$. The ultrametric organization of pure states is therefore accompanied by an ultrametric hierarchy of real-space correlation lengths: states that separate near the root of the Parisi tree differ through long-wavelength frozen patterns, whereas states that separate farther from the root differ only on progressively shorter scales.

</details>

### [120] [Planck-scale black hole remnants inside neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10589)
*Kimet Jusufi,Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于T对偶启发的有效几何（T-duality-inspired effective geometry），考察中子星物质中普朗克尺度正则黑洞遗迹（Planck-scale regular black-hole remnant）的演化。结论显示：在基准吸收截面假设下，该类遗迹实际上近乎惰性，无法在宇宙年龄内摧毁中子星；而流体吸积（hydrodynamic accretion）出现的质量门槛约为10^11–10^12 kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于明确区分几何结果与关于吸收/发射的假设，以评估普朗克尺度黑洞遗迹能否在中子星内部增长并影响中子星存续。该问题涉及黑洞遗迹与致密物质相互作用的可行性判据。

Method: 采用T对偶启发的有效几何描述静态黑洞族，并分别处理几何量与吸收、发射假设。使用常量灰体Stefan–Boltzmann近似（constant-greybody Stefan–Boltzmann approximation）推导霍金功率（Hawking power），并结合正则描述、基准面积尺度吸收截面（area-scale absorption cross section）及流体吸积的平均自由程判据进行分析。

Result: 表面引力温度（surface-gravity temperature）在小幅质量增加后按(M−M_ext)^{1/2}增长；常量灰体近似下霍金功率正比于(M−M_ext)^2，可给出稳定下平衡点与不稳定增长阈值。但当l_0=ℓ_Pl且吸收效率为量级1时，形式下平衡的过剩质量仅10^−45–10^−46 kg，与单个中子俘获不一致。在单位系数的基准面积尺度截面下，平均俘获间隔为5.4×10^8 yr，初始无辐射增长时标为9.1×10^27 yr；流体吸积在代表性物质参数下对应质量约10^11–10^12 kg，零点长度修正在飞米尺度约为10^−40。

Conclusion: 在所述假设下，中子星内的普朗克质量遗迹实际上呈惰性，若无额外供给，无法在宇宙年龄内摧毁中子星。稳态俘获–弛豫态（stationary capture–relaxation state）还需有效的微观发射律，仅凭零温条件不足以确立。文中进一步讨论了条件性增长阈值、不稳定平衡附近的临界慢化（critical slowing）、暗物质供给、角动量转移及中子星存续约束的要求。

Abstract: We examine a Planck-scale regular black-hole remnant in neutron-star matter using a T-duality-inspired effective geometry and explicitly separate geometrical results from assumptions about absorption and emission. Within the static family, the surface-gravity temperature grows as $(M-M_{\rm ext})^{1/2}$ after a small mass increase. A constant-greybody Stefan--Boltzmann approximation gives a Hawking power proportional to $(M-M_{\rm ext})^2$ and can produce a stable lower balance point and an unstable growth threshold under stated hypotheses. For $l_0=\ell_{\rm Pl}$, however, the formal lower equilibrium has an excess mass of only $10^{-45}$--$10^{-46}\,\mathrm{kg}$ for order-unity absorption efficiency, so the canonical description is inconsistent with individual neutron captures. For a fiducial area-scale absorption cross section with unit coefficient, the mean capture interval is $5.4\times10^8\,\mathrm{yr}$ and the initial nonradiating growth timescale is $9.1\times10^{27}\,\mathrm{yr}$. Under this assumption, a Planck-mass remnant inside a neutron star is therefore effectively inert and, without additional feeding, cannot destroy the star within the age of the Universe. A stationary capture--relaxation state additionally requires an effective microscopic emission law; it is not established by zero temperature alone. Hydrodynamic accretion requires control of wave effects and a mean-free-path criterion; its onset corresponds to masses of order $10^{11}$--$10^{12}\,\mathrm{kg}$ for representative matter parameters. Direct zero-point-length corrections there are negligible, of order $10^{-40}$ at a femtometre. We discuss conditional growth thresholds, critical slowing near an unstable balance, dark-matter feeding, angular-momentum transfer, and the requirements for neutron-star survival constraints.

</details>


### [121] [Geometrothermodynamics of Black Hole Shadows: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10638)
*Hernando Quevedo,Jose M. Ladino,Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,Sasha A. Zaldivar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述黑洞阴影（black-hole shadow）与几何热力学（geometrothermodynamics, GTD）之间的对应关系，指出二者虽定义于不同空间，但依赖相同的宏观黑洞参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞阴影与几何热力学分别属于光学观测与热力学几何两个领域，二者空间定义不同却共享同一组宏观黑洞参数，因此值得系统梳理其潜在对应关系。

Method: 针对 Reissner–Nordström（RN）、Kerr 与 Kerr–AdS 黑洞，综述三种 GTD 度规所揭示的热力学结构（含 Davies 不稳定性、极值与退化极限、正则曲率零点）与阴影构型的映射；并建立从阴影可观测量重建热力学的局部雅可比秩（Jacobian-rank）判据，讨论阴影—微观结构（shadow–microstructure, SM）图。

Result: 三种 GTD 度规揭示了互补的热力学结构，并可映射至阴影构型；Kerr–AdS 将该对应扩展至非对称阴影与多维平衡态；局部雅可比秩判据为从阴影可观测量重建热力学提供了条件；SM 图可作为 GTD 分类平衡态的光学表征。

Conclusion: 以人马座 A*（Sagittarius A*）为例说明该框架的反问题应用：阴影观测可约束热力学状态，并为识别可区分不同 GTD 度规结构的额外度规依赖可观测量提供基础。

Abstract: Black-hole shadows and geometrothermodynamics (GTD) are defined in different spaces but depend on the same macroscopic black-hole parameters. We review their correspondence for Reissner--Nordström (RN), Kerr, and Kerr--AdS black holes. The three GTD metrics reveal complementary thermodynamic structures, including Davies instabilities, extremal and degenerate limits, and regular curvature zeros, which can be mapped to shadow configurations. Kerr--AdS extends this correspondence to asymmetric shadows and multidimensional equilibrium states. We formulate a local Jacobian-rank criterion for thermodynamic reconstruction from shadow observables and discuss shadow--microstructure (SM) diagrams as optical representations of GTD-classified equilibrium states. The application to Sagittarius A* illustrates the inverse problem: shadow observations constrain thermodynamic states and provide a basis for identifying additional metric-dependent observables that could distinguish among the GTD metric structures.

</details>


### [122] [Impact of site-dependent noise configurations on Einstein Telescope science at low frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10669)
*Francesco Crescimbeni,Matteo Di Giovanni,Davide Rozza,Andrea Contu,Alessandro Cardini,Domenico D'Urso,Carlo Giunchi,Luca Naticchioni,Marco Olivieri,Mike Lindner,Andreas Rietbrock,Paolo Pani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究评估站点相关牛顿噪声（Newtonian noise）对爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）科学性能的影响，比较三角与2L几何构型在三种站点噪声场景下的表现。结果表明，10Hz以下的低频灵敏度提升可显著增强四类科学目标的表现，而低频灵敏度恶化可能抵消由10km增至15km臂长带来的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波天文台（third-generation gravitational-wave observatory）ET的科学潜力高度依赖低频段灵敏度，而低频段受站点相关牛顿噪声影响显著。因此需量化不同站点噪声配置对ET科学目标的影响，以支撑站点选择与噪声缓解决策。

Method: 对比基线三角构型与2L探测器几何构型（臂长分别为10km与15km），采用来自Euregio Meuse-Rhine地区、Lausitz与Sardinia三处测量的站点相关噪声场景，并基于贝叶斯参数估计（Bayesian parameter estimation）量化四类科学目标的表现差异。

Result: 所采用灵敏度之间的差异主要集中在10Hz以下，因而对依赖轻物体早期旋近（early inspiral）或低频段大质量系统的科学目标影响最大。在考虑的构型中，只要两个L形干涉仪中至少一个达到与Sardinia站点相当的噪声水平，2L网络通常优于三角构型；而10Hz以下灵敏度的严重退化会大幅削弱、甚至在某些情形下抵消从10km增至15km臂长所预期的科学收益。

Conclusion: 为充分发挥ET在10Hz以下尚待探索频段的科学潜力，站点选择与低频噪声缓解与网络几何构型同等重要。低频灵敏度的改善可全面提升四类科学目标（中等质量黑洞双星探测与参数估计、早期旋近的后牛顿广义相对论检验、大质量残余黑洞铃宕光谱学、双中子星并合的早期预警与天区定位）的表现。

Abstract: We investigate how site-dependent Newtonian noise configurations affect the scientific performance of the Einstein Telescope (ET), a third-generation gravitational-wave observatory. We compare the baseline triangular and 2L detector geometries, with arm lengths of 10km and 15km, respectively, under three site-dependent noise scenarios constructed from measurements in the Euregio Meuse-Rhine region, Lausitz, and Sardinia. The differences among the adopted sensitivities are concentrated mainly below 10Hz and thus have the greatest impact on science cases that rely on the early inspiral of light objects, or on massive systems whose characteristic frequencies lie in the low-frequency band. We quantify these effects for four ET science cases: (1) detection and parameter estimation of intermediate-mass black-hole binaries; (2) post-Newtonian tests of General Relativity based on the early inspiral; (3) black-hole ringdown spectroscopy with massive remnants; and (4) early-warning and sky-localization capabilities for binary neutron-star mergers. Using Bayesian parameter estimation, we find that improved low-frequency sensitivity can significantly enhance ET's scientific performance across all four cases. Within the configurations considered here, a 2L network in which at least one of the two L-shaped interferometers attains a noise level comparable to that assumed for the Sardinian site generally provides better performance than the triangular configurations. Conversely, strong degradation of the sensitivity below 10Hz can substantially reduce, and in some regimes offset, the scientific gains expected from increasing the arm length from 10km to 15km. These results highlight the importance of site selection and low-frequency noise mitigation, alongside network geometry, for fully realizing ET's scientific potential in the largely unexplored frequency band below 10Hz.

</details>


### [123] [High-Accuracy Semi-Analytic Mode Functions, Ultraviolet Regularity, and Particle Creation in the Hybrid Approach to mLQC-I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10802)
*Jamal Saeed,Rui Pan,Anzhong Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作为 mLQC-I（modified loop quantum cosmology，改进圈量子宇宙学）中混合方法下的标量宇宙学扰动构建高精度半解析模式函数，用于研究紫外正则性与引力粒子产生；三阶一致渐近解结合自适应缺陷修正达到亚百分比乃至 0.00001% 精度，并区分有限波数与严格紫外极限的渐近处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需为 mLQC-I 中跨越多阶段的标量扰动演化提供受控的模式函数，以支撑原初功率谱（primordial power spectrum）与非高斯关联（non-Gaussian correlation）的未来精密计算。同时需厘清有限波数匹配与严格紫外极限之间的张力，判断紫外发散是否为表观现象。

Method: 在混合框架内将演化划分为渐近 pre-de Sitter 分支、含量子反弹（quantum bounce）的预暴胀区与暴胀三段；在中间区构造完整三阶一致渐近解（third-order uniform asymptotic solution）并引入自适应缺陷修正（adaptive defect correction）。此外比较远收缩分支所选 de Sitter 真空与在反弹处施加的四阶绝热态（fourth-order adiabatic state）。

Result: 三阶近似对代表性高波数模式的复模式精度达亚百分比；缺陷修正解在测试低波数范围内与数值演化吻合至 0.00001% 水平。将领先连接外推至无穷波数会产生虚假的常数负频系数与表观紫外发散；大波数 de Sitter 模式则在能量-动量张量重整化（energy-momentum tensor renormalization）所需阶数内与四阶绝热展开一致，且光滑精确演化保持该紫外结构。有限与中等模式仍可发生非绝热 Bogoliubov 混合（Bogoliubov mixing）与粒子产生。

Conclusion: 有限模式粒子产生与紫外正则性可以共存。紫外论证亦可推广至 dressed-metric 方法对应的大波数区间，但其定量模式函数须以该方法的有效质量重新计算。

Abstract: We construct high-accuracy semi-analytic mode functions for scalar cosmological perturbations in mLQC-I within the hybrid approach to study ultraviolet regularity and gravitational particle creation. The evolution comprises an asymptotic pre-de Sitter branch, a pre-inflationary region containing the quantum bounce, and inflation. In the intermediate region, we construct the complete third-order uniform asymptotic solution and introduce adaptive defect correction. The third-order approximation achieves sub-percent complex-mode accuracy for representative higher-wavenumber modes; defect-corrected solutions agree with numerical evolution at the 0.00001% level over the tested low-wavenumber range. Retaining amplitudes and phases provides a controlled basis for future precision calculations of primordial power spectra and non-Gaussian correlations. We compare the de Sitter vacuum selected in the remote contracting branch with a fourth-order adiabatic state imposed at the bounce. Finite-wavenumber matching and the strict ultraviolet limit require different asymptotic treatments: extrapolating the leading connection to infinite wavenumber yields a spurious constant negative-frequency coefficient and an apparent ultraviolet divergence. Instead, the large-wavenumber de Sitter mode agrees with the fourth-order adiabatic expansion through the order required for energy-momentum tensor renormalization, and smooth exact evolution preserves this ultraviolet structure. Finite and intermediate modes can nevertheless undergo nonadiabatic Bogoliubov mixing and particle creation. Finite-mode particle production thus coexists with ultraviolet regularity. The ultraviolet argument also extends to the dressed-metric approach in its corresponding large-wavenumber regime, while quantitative mode functions must be recalculated using its effective mass.

</details>


### [124] [Constitutive Geometry of the Effective Vacuum Sector of Pseudo-Complex General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10836)
*F. Weber,P. O. Hess,C. A. Zen Vasconcellos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在伪复广义相对论（pseudo-complex general relativity, pcGR）框架下，探讨能否用协变本构结构（covariant constitutive structure）内蕴地刻画有效真空应力，而非将其唯象地当作奇异流体处理。对静态伪复 Schwarzschild 解，有效真空张量自然分解为各向同性部分与二维横向子空间几何相关的纯横向部分，从而导出基于横向度规（transverse metric）的本构表述，并证明对数横向面积应变（logarithmic transverse area strain）在特定局域本构变量类中为最小标量本构坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正引力理论（modified theories of gravity）中的有效真空区常以各向异性应力-能量张量（anisotropic stress-energy tensor）表示，即便其来源是几何性而非物质性的。这引出核心问题：此类有效应力能否通过协变本构结构被内蕴刻画，而不必作为奇异流体进行唯象建模。作者选择在 pcGR 中处理该问题，因为其约束变分表述（constrained variational formulation）虽确认有效真空源源于几何，却无法唯一确定对应的几何约束区。

Method: 针对静态伪复 Schwarzschild 解（static pseudo-complex Schwarzschild solution），分析有效真空张量的结构分离。将有效真空张量分解为各向同性贡献与纯横向贡献，后者关联于与类时及径向方向正交的二维子空间的内蕴几何。据此建立以横向度规（transverse metric）为基础的本构表述，并将其无穷小形变（infinitesimal deformation）唯一分解为迹（trace）与无迹剪切（traceless shear）两种模式。

Result: 有效真空张量可自然分离为各向同性贡献与纯横向贡献；横向部分的无穷小形变唯一地分解为迹模式与无迹剪切模式，而静态解仅具有各向同性的横向响应。在那些代数依赖于横向度规、且其一阶变分生成迹投影算子（trace projector）并具有状态无关归一化（state-independent normalization）的局域本构变量类中，对数横向面积应变（logarithmic transverse area strain）被确定为最小的标量本构坐标。

Conclusion: 在 pcGR 的静态伪复 Schwarzschild 情形下，有效真空应力可借助横向度规的本构结构内蕴刻画，无需诉诸奇异流体的唯象描述。对数横向面积应变构成该本构变量类中的最小标量本构坐标，为有效真空区的本构表述提供了基础。

Abstract: Effective vacuum sectors in modified theories of gravity are often represented by anisotropic stress-energy tensors, even when the underlying source is geometric rather than material. This raises a broader question: can such effective stresses be characterized intrinsically through a covariant constitutive structure, rather than modeled phenomenologically as exotic fluids? We address this question in pseudo-complex general relativity (pcGR), where a constrained variational formulation identifies the effective vacuum source as geometric in origin but does not uniquely determine the corresponding geometric constraint sector. For the static pseudo-complex Schwarzschild solution, we show that the effective vacuum tensor separates naturally into an isotropic contribution and a purely transverse contribution associated with the intrinsic geometry of the two-dimensional subspace orthogonal to the timelike and radial directions. This decomposition leads to a constitutive formulation based on the transverse metric. Its infinitesimal deformations split uniquely into trace and traceless shear modes, with the static solution having only an isotropic transverse response. Within the class of local constitutive variables that depend algebraically on the transverse metric and whose first variation generates the trace projector with stateindependent normalization, the logarithmic transverse area strain is the minimal scalar constitutive coordinate.

</details>


### [125] [Probing the Black Hole Singularity with a Quantum String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10861)
*Harpreet Singh,Malay K. Nandy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文以Kantowski-Sachs度规描述黑洞内部，并在其中引入弦（string）传播，通过正则量子化导出Wheeler-DeWitt方程，微扰求解至一阶并检验波函数正则性，结果表明DeWitt边界条件在零阶与一阶均成立，暗示黑洞奇点在此量子框架内被消解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近经典奇点处时空曲率可达普朗克（Planck）或弦（string）尺度，广义相对论的经典描述失效，因此需要引入弦论自由度刻画该区域的量子行为。该研究旨在探究黑洞经典奇点附近的量子效应。

Method: 采用Kantowski-Sachs度规描述黑洞内部，并在其中放置传播的弦，弦动力学由Polyakov作用量（Polyakov action）表述；对组合系统作正则量子化（canonical quantization）得到Wheeler-DeWitt方程。该方程在径向与角向弦部分可分离，但剩余几何与弦自由度存在非平凡耦合，故采用微扰方法（perturbative approach）求解至一阶波函数。

Result: 零阶解中角向部分由一般Heun函数（general Heun function）描述，几何部分以Bessel函数表示；一阶波函数由含零阶解的源项控制，其显式表达式由补函数（complementary function）与特解积分（particular integral）给出。波函数在奇点与视界处（零阶与一阶）均被确认正则；视界处正则性对一个本征值施加离散化条件，另一本征值则由波函数的角向周期性（azimuthal periodicity）量子化。

Conclusion: 零阶与一阶解均满足DeWitt边界条件（DeWitt boundary condition），表明在此量子框架内黑洞奇点得到消解（resolution of the black hole singularity）。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the quantum behaviour near the classical singularity of a black hole. We describe the black-hole interior by the Kantowski-Sachs metric, with a string propagating in this region. This approach is motivated by the fact that, near the classical singularity, spacetime curvature reaches Planck or string scales, necessitating a description in terms of string-theoretic degrees of freedom. The string dynamics is formulated via the Polyakov action, and the combined system is canonically quantized to derive the corresponding Wheeler-DeWitt equation. Although the equation is separable in the radial and azimuthal string sectors, the remaining geometric and string degrees of freedom are nontrivially coupled. To address this, we employ a perturbative approach and obtain the wave function up to first order. At the zeroth-order, the angular part is described by the general Heun function, while the geometric part is expressed in terms of the Bessel function. The first-order wave function is governed by a source term containing the zeroth-order solution, and we obtain its explicit expression in terms of the complementary function and the particular integral. We confirm regularity of the wave function at both zeroth and first order, at the singularity as well as at the horizon. Regularity at the horizon imposes a discretization condition on one eigenvalue, while another eigenvalue is quantized due to the azimuthal periodicity of the wave function. Importantly, both zeroth- and first-order solutions satisfy the DeWitt boundary condition, implying resolution of the black hole singularity within this quantum framework.

</details>


### [126] [Observational Signatures of Dyonic Black Holes in Non-Linear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10866)
*Sobhan Kazempour,Sichun Sun,Zhiqing He,Chengye Yu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究考察非线性电动力学（non-linear electrodynamics, NLED）中静态球对称双荷黑洞（dyonic black hole）的薄盘吸积与辐射谱特征，并通过 EHT 对 Sgr A* 的角直径约束进行统计检验，发现 NLED 与纯磁模型均与观测数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典麦克斯韦理论（Maxwell theory）中电磁对偶要求电荷相等，而 NLED 的耦合参数会引入新的引力势并改变强场区几何。为评估此类替代模型的观测可检验性，需研究其吸积与辐射特征。

Method: 计算粒子在圆轨道上的角速度、比能（specific energy）与比角动量（specific angular momentum），并求解零测地线（null geodesic）轨道以确定光子球（photon sphere）与黑洞阴影（black hole shadow）的光学边界。同时利用事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）对 Sgr A* 的视界尺度角直径限制开展 χ² 拟合优度检验。

Result: NLED 的修正导致 ISCO 半径收缩，使辐射谱热峰向更小半径移动，与标准 Schwarzschild 及 Reissner-Nordström 解相比，局部有效温度升高。χ² 检验表明，双荷 NLED 模型与纯磁模型均与实验数据具有良好统计一致性。

Conclusion: 在强场区，NLED 耦合显著改变黑洞几何与吸积辐射特性，但所研究的双荷 NLED 与纯磁模型在现有 EHT 对 Sgr A* 的角直径约束下仍具观测有效性。

Abstract: We study the observational features of thin-disk accretion and emission spectra for static, spherically symmetric dyonic black holes in non-linear electrodynamics (NLED). In contrast to classical Maxwell theory, where electromagnetic duality assumes equal charges, the coupling parameters in NLED introduce a new gravitational potential and modify the geometry in the strong-field regime. We also study the kinematic characteristics of particles on circular orbits, such as their angular velocity, specific energy, and specific angular momentum. It is found that modifications of NLED lead to a shrinkage of the ISCO radius, resulting in a shift of the radiation spectrum thermal maximum towards smaller radii and, consequently, an increase in the local effective temperature in comparison with the standard Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström solutions. Additionally, we compute null-geodesic orbits to determine the optical edge of both the photon sphere and the black hole shadow. In order to assess the observational validity of the aforementioned alternatives, we carry out a $χ^2$ goodness-of-fit test through the angular diameter constraints at the horizon scale collected by the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) concerning Sagittarius A* ($\text{Sgr A}^*$). We prove that both these dyonic NLED and purely magnetic models have good statistical agreement with experimental data.

</details>


### [127] [Accounting for detector calibration uncertainty in black-hole ringdown analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11052)
*Rowina S. Nathan,Shingo Hido,Neil Lu,Mallika R. Sinha,Sizheng Ma,Ling Sun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出两种基于准正规模有理滤波器（QNMRF）的铃宕（ringdown）分析方法，用以在探测器校准误差不可忽略时估计残余黑洞的质量与自旋，避免由此产生的对广义相对论的虚假偏离证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测器校准系统误差（calibration systematics）会在未经修正时对残余黑洞质量与自旋的测量产生偏置，进而可能造成反对广义相对论（general relativity）的虚假证据。铃宕阶段的准正规模（quasinormal modes）频率与阻尼时间仅由残余质量与自旋决定，因此需在存在校准误差的情况下仍能正确估计这些参数。

Method: 采用准正规模有理滤波器（quasinormal-mode rational filter, QNMRF）提出两种互补方法：当校准误差被良好测量时，先对应变（strain）施加最佳估计的校准修正再做标准分析；当误差未知时，联合采样残余质量、自旋及频率依赖的校准误差，并在不确定性先验上做边缘化（marginalizing）。验证方式为将数值相对论（numerical-relativity）波形 SXS:BBH:0305 注入有色探测器噪声。

Result: 在整体匹配滤波信噪比（matched-filter signal-to-noise ratio）约60、模拟校准误差为幅度 $\pm25\%$、相位 $\pm25^\circ$ 的条件下，对校准不确定性做边缘化可在 90% 可信区间（credible interval）内恢复真实质量与自旋，而忽略校准误差会得到有偏估计。应用于 GW250207（Hanford 探测器响应未被良好表征期间记录的事件）时，仅使用铃宕部分即恢复了与旋近-并合-铃宕（inspiral-merger-ringdown）预测一致的残余参数。

Conclusion: 对校准误差进行修正或边缘化，可使仅基于铃宕数据的残余质量与自旋估计免受探测器校准系统误差的偏置，并在实际事件 GW250207 中与完整波形预测保持一致。

Abstract: Detector calibration systematics can bias measurements of a remnant black hole's mass and spin, producing spurious evidence against general relativity if unaccounted for. The ringdown phase in the gravitational-wave signal produced by a binary black-hole merger encodes the remnant properties: at sufficiently late times, the signal is well described by a superposition of quasinormal modes, each with a frequency and damping time set only by the remnant mass and spin. Here we present two complementary approaches for analyzing the ringdown portion of the data to estimate the remnant mass and spin in the presence of a nonnegligible detector calibration error, using the quasinormal-mode rational filter (QNMRF). With a well-measured error, we apply the best-estimated calibration correction to the strain before conducting the standard analysis; with an unknown error, we instead jointly sample the remnant mass, spin and the frequency-dependent calibration error, marginalizing over an uncertainty prior. By injecting the numerical-relativity waveform SXS:BBH:0305 into colored detector noise, at a full-signal matched-filter signal-to-noise ratio of $\sim60$ and with simulated calibration errors of $\pm25\%$ in magnitude and $\pm25^\circ$ in phase, marginalizing over the calibration uncertainty recovers the true mass and spin within the 90\% credible interval, whereas the estimated values are biased if the calibration errors are ignored. We apply the method to GW250207, an event recorded while the Hanford detector response was not well characterized, and recover a remnant consistent with the inspiral-merger-ringdown prediction using the ringdown portion alone, both by correcting for a best-estimated calibration error and by marginalizing over a broad uncertainty prior.

</details>


### [128] [Chameleon Gravity with an Environmental Frequency Dependent on Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11207)
*Raziyeh Zaregonbadi,Nasim Saba*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在变色龙（chameleon）机制框架下引入势能中附加的二次环境项（由密度依赖的背景频率刻画），针对反幂律势推导修正的平衡构型，指出存在“频率主导区”，并给出频率主导、薄壳屏蔽（thin shell screening）、第五力抑制与弱场近似的解析一致性条件；数值示例表明该修正密度标度可增强第五力抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修改引力（modified gravity）的标量场理论受实验室与太阳系环境下未观测到长程第五力（fifth force）的严格限制。变色龙机制通过使标量场平衡值及有效质量依赖环境物质密度来规避该限制；本文旨在考察一种有效唯象扩展，以纳入超出常规物质耦合的未解析环境效应。

Method: 在标量势中加入附加的二次环境贡献，其特征为密度依赖的背景频率；该背景频率不同于标量扰动的频率，用于参数化未解析的环境效应。对反幂律（inverse power law）标量势，推导修正的平衡构型；对平衡态附近的线性扰动给出满足 Klein Gordon 色散关系（dispersion relation）与密度依赖有效质量的分析，并推导与频率主导、薄壳屏蔽、第五力抑制及弱场近似相关的解析一致性条件。

Result: 识别出频率主导区（frequency dominated regime），其中环境贡献主导场的密度依赖性。线性扰动服从含密度依赖有效质量的 Klein Gordon 色散关系；避免快子不稳定性（tachyonic instability）要求环境耦合为正。针对代表性参数选取的数值示例显示，相对于常规变色龙情形，修正后的密度标度可增强第五力抑制。

Conclusion: 势能中二次环境贡献为变色龙框架提供了可解析处理的唯象扩展：在频率主导条件下可改变场的密度标度并增强第五力抑制，同时需满足正环境耦合及薄壳屏蔽、弱场近似等一致性条件。

Abstract: Scalar field theories of modified gravity are strongly constrained by the absence of detectable long range fifth forces in laboratory and Solar System environments. The chameleon mechanism addresses this problem by making the equilibrium value and effective mass of the scalar field dependent on the ambient matter density. In this work, we investigate an effective phenomenological extension of the chameleon framework in which the scalar potential contains an additional quadratic environmental contribution characterized by a density dependent background frequency. This background frequency is distinct from the frequency of scalar perturbations and parametrizes unresolved environmental effects beyond the conventional matter coupling. For an inverse power law scalar potential, we derive the modified equilibrium configuration and identify a frequency dominated regime in which the environmental contribution controls the density dependence of the field. Linear perturbations about the environmental minimum obey a Klein Gordon dispersion relation with a density-dependent effective mass, and the absence of tachyonic instability requires a positive environmental coupling. We further derive analytical consistency conditions associated with frequency dominance, thin shell screening, fifth-force suppression, and the weak field approximation. Numerical illustrations for representative parameter choices demonstrate how the modified density scaling can enhance fifth-force suppression relative to the conventional chameleon case.

</details>


### [129] [Late-Time Background Expansion and Cosmographic Signatures of Finsler-Randers-Sasaki Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11269)
*Thunga S,Devaraja Mallesha Naik,Manaswini B S,V Venkatesha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究对数型与指数型 Finsler–Randers–Sasaki（FRS）宇宙学模型的晚期膨胀历史与几何诊断，利用宇宙计时器、BAO 与 Pantheon+ Ia 型超新星数据进行约束，并结合 SH0ES 先验校准。两模型背景膨胀历史相近，但状态查找器（statefinder）诊断可显著区分其高阶运动学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察两类 FRS 宇宙学模型（对数型与指数型）能否一致描述晚期宇宙膨胀，并检验仅凭背景膨胀历史是否足以区分不同几何构造。为此需借助多类观测数据与几何诊断工具进行系统对比。

Method: 采用宇宙计时器（cosmic chronometers）、重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillations, BAO）与 Pantheon+ Ia 型超新星（Type Ia supernovae）联合约束两类 FRS 模型，分别进行基准拟合与引入 SH0ES 距离阶梯先验的校准拟合。随后开展宇宙学运动学重建（cosmographic reconstruction）与状态查找器（statefinder）诊断。

Result: 未校准情形下，两种模型对晚期膨胀历史的描述相互一致；引入 SH0ES 先验后，推断的哈勃常数（Hubble constant）升高，声学尺度（acoustic scale）的推断值亦被修正。运动学重建显示在中等红移处存在由减速到加速的平滑过渡；在观测相关红移范围内，两模型均表现为非幽灵（non-phantom）、类精质（quintessence-like）的有效暗能量（dark energy）组分。状态查找器诊断可区分二者：对数模型较接近标准平坦 ΛCDM 不动点，指数模型则沿独特的动力学轨迹演化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，尽管两类 FRS 构造的背景膨胀历史相似，其高阶运动学特征可存在显著差异，说明背景层面的观测一致性不足以判定模型优劣，需借助状态查找器等几何诊断加以区分。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the late-time expansion history and geometric diagnostics of logarithmic and exponential Finsler--Randers--Sasaki (FRS) cosmological models. We constrain both scenarios using cosmic chronometers, Baryon acoustic oscillations, and Pantheon+ Type~Ia supernovae, considering baseline fits as well as configurations calibrated with the local SH0ES distance-ladder prior. In the uncalibrated case, both profiles provide consistent descriptions of the late-time expansion history. Incorporating the SH0ES prior increases the inferred Hubble constant and modifies the inferred acoustic scale, reflecting the interplay between the local distance-ladder calibration and the BAO distance scale. Cosmographic reconstructions reveal a smooth transition from deceleration to acceleration at intermediate redshifts. Over the observationally relevant redshift range, both models exhibit a non-phantom, quintessence-like effective dark-energy sector. Finally, statefinder diagnostics distinguish the two FRS geometries: the logarithmic model remains relatively close to the canonical flat $Λ\mathrm{CDM}$ fixed point, whereas the exponential model follows a distinct dynamical trajectory. These results demonstrate that, despite their similar background expansion histories, the two FRS constructions can exhibit substantially different higher-order kinematic signatures.

</details>


### [130] [Neutrino oscillations inside ultrarelativistic matter: on the role of the Tolman VI spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11319)
*Daniele Gregoris*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态球对称时空（self-gravitating geometry）中由爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equations）自洽导出的、由超相对论物质支撑的中微子振荡（neutrino oscillations），解析计算了中微子程函（eikonal）与自旋光学（spinoptics）相位振荡，并提出以 Tolman VI 度规为背景识别裸奇点（naked singularities）的观测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子在光子与超相对论等离子体中的传播、以及弯曲时空中的中微子振荡虽已被广泛研究，但二者在自引力几何中的结合此前似乎未被考虑。本文旨在填补该空白，并区分引力效应与电弱（electroweak）效应。

Method: 基于测地线运动（geodesic motion）与几何对称性，解析计算中微子程函相位与自旋光学相位振荡；将共振现象与背景物质解释相联系，并通过与热场论（thermal field theory）渐近行为对比来验证其启发式方法，同时论证结果非坐标假象（coordinate artifacts）。

Result: 给出了纯引力场与纯物质场效应以及温度对中微子振荡的影响；指出若中微子撞击曲率奇点（curvature singularity），自旋光学相位移动会显著影响味转换概率（flavor conversion probability）；并在不同温度与密度条件下定性讨论了恒星内部与宇宙微波背景（Cosmic Microwave Background）中的中微子振荡。

Conclusion: 在 Tolman VI 度规背景下，提出一种识别宇宙中裸奇点的观测方法：中微子将同时处于物理重叠、味平衡（flavor-equilibrium）与量子力学混杂状态。研究还分别审视了几何对称性与位形物理特征的理论作用。

Abstract: While both neutrino propagation in photons and ultrarelativistic plasmas and neutrino oscillations in curved spacetimes have been studied extensively, their combination in a self-gravitating geometry appears not to have been considered previously. In this paper, we will investigate neutrino oscillations inside a static spherically symmetric spacetime, derived self-consistently from Einstein's equations, supported by ultrarelativistic matter. By relying on the geodesic motion and geometrical symmetries of the configuration, we will compute analytically the neutrino eikonal and spinoptics phase oscillations. Next, we will relate resonance phenomena to the physical interpretation of the background material inside which neutrinos travel for disentangling gravitational versus electroweak effects. We will inspect the effects of the pure gravitational and pure matter fields, and of the temperature in the neutrino oscillations; we will explain why our results are not coordinate artifacts, and we will certify our heuristic method by comparing with the asymptotic behaviours predicted by thermal field theory approaches. We will raise awareness on the relevance of the spinoptics phase shift entering the flavor conversion probability, should the neutrinos hit a curvature singularity. Theoretically, we will scrutinize the separate roles of geometrical symmetries versus physical features of the configuration, while astrophysically we will qualitatively discuss neutrino oscillations inside stars and in the Cosmic Microwave Background at different temperature and density conditions. Our research puts forward, in the context of the Tolman VI metric, an observational method for identifying naked singularities in our Universe: neutrinos would simultaneously be physically overlapped, in flavor-equilibrium and quantum-mechanically scrambled.

</details>


### [131] [Pre-geometric Black Holes with Spherical Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11357)
*Giuseppe Meluccio,Egor Pluzhnikov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究具有球对称性的前几何（pre-geometric）黑洞构型，通过对应原理（correspondence principle）给出不同前几何理论在真空中的精确解，其形式为（反）德西特-施瓦西度规（(anti-)de Sitter-Schwarzschild metric）的前几何类比，并在某一情形下呈现普朗克尺度（Planckian size）的（反）德西特核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前几何引力（pre-geometric Gravity）关注普朗克尺度（Planck scale）附近时空的非度规（non-metric）特征，引力相互作用经由自发对称性破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）机制作为低能有效理论出现。本文动机在于修正黑洞几何中出现的经典奇点（classical singularities）。

Method: 研究球对称（spherical symmetry）的前几何黑洞构型，利用对应原理（correspondence principle）求解不同前几何理论在真空中的精确解，并分析前几何版本的伯克霍夫定理（Birkhoff's theorem）。

Result: 所得精确解为（反）德西特-施瓦西度规（(anti-)de Sitter-Schwarzschild metric）的前几何类比，据此预期此类黑洞几何由此涌现；其中一种情形下，前几何解还呈现出普朗克尺度（Planckian size）的（反）德西特核心。主要结果辅以对前几何版伯克霍夫定理的分析。

Conclusion: 前几何理论可在真空中给出球对称黑洞的精确解，其低能形式对应（反）德西特-施瓦西几何，并可能以普朗克尺度的（反）德西特核心替代经典奇点。

Abstract: Pre-geometric Gravity investigates the non-metric features of spacetime near the Planck scale, from which the gravitational interaction arises as a low-energy effective theory via a mechanism of spontaneous symmetry breaking. With a view of amending the classical singularities found in black hole geometries, in this work we study pre-geometric black hole configurations with spherical symmetry, and present exact solutions for different pre-geometric theories in vacuum via the correspondence principle. These solutions are the pre-geometric analogue of the (anti-)de Sitter-Schwarzschild metric, from which such type of black hole geometry is thus expected to emerge. In one case, the pre-geometric solution also exhibits an (anti-)de Sitter core of Planckian size. The main result is supplemented with an analysis of the pre-geometric version of Birkhoff's theorem.

</details>


### [132] [Probing hairy Kerr black holes through quasi-periodic oscillations II: a study based on the resonance models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11378)
*Anirban Dasgupta,Supragyan Priyadarshinee,Indrani Banerjee,Subhash Mahapatra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在引力解耦（gravitational decoupling）框架下的旋转毛状黑洞（hairy black holes）时空中，检验九个共振模型（resonance models）并联合先前两个运动学模型，对同一批高频准周期振荡（HFQPO）数据开展系统比较，发现当前精度尚无法排除毛状克尔（hairy Kerr）情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毛状黑洞因引入质量、电荷、自旋之外的额外自由度，为检验偏离克尔（Kerr）范式提供框架；本文旨在扩展此前（Paper I）仅基于两个运动学高频准周期振荡模型的分析，纳入更多共振模型以系统评估毛状克尔时空的可检验性。

Method: 研究对象为引力解耦框架下构造的一类旋转毛状黑洞，其时空满足爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein field equations）且具有守恒、符合强能量条件（strong energy conditions）的能量-动量张量。在Paper I的运动学高频准周期振荡模型基础上，本文进一步将九个共振模型与同一批六个黑洞源的高频准周期振荡观测数据进行拟合，共计对十一个模型（两个运动学模型加九个共振模型）在毛状克尔框架内进行比较，并采用赤池信息量准则（Akaike Information Criterion）及与已有独立自旋估计的一致性检验进行评估。

Result: 基于两个运动学模型与九个共振模型的综合分析表明，现有精度的高频准周期振荡数据尚不能排除毛状克尔情形；通过赤池信息量准则与推断自旋同先前独立自旋估计的一致性检验，为各源不同高频准周期振荡模型的相对表现提供了系统评估框架。

Conclusion: 结果凸显高频准周期振荡在探测黑洞毛发（black hole hair）方面的潜力，同时表明需更精确的观测以及对准周期振荡（QPOs）物理起源的更深入理解。

Abstract: Hairy black holes (BHs), characterized by nontrivial scalar or matter fields, introduce additional degrees of freedom beyond mass, charge, and spin, providing a framework for testing deviations from the Kerr paradigm. We investigate a class of rotating hairy black holes constructed within the gravitational decoupling framework, whose spacetime satisfies the Einstein field equations with a conserved energy-momentum tensor consistent with the strong energy conditions. In our previous work (Paper I; Dasgupta et al. 2026), we studied their horizon structure and test-particle dynamics and confronted two kinematic high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillation (HFQPO) models with observations of six black hole sources. Building on that analysis, we here test nine resonance models against the same HFQPO data, enabling a systematic comparison of eleven models (two kinematic and nine resonance) within the hairy Kerr framework. A comprehensive analysis based on the two kinematic models (Paper I) and the nine resonance models reveals that the HFQPO data with the current level of precision cannot rule out the hairy Kerr scenario. By implementing the Akaike Information Criterion and by examining the consistency of the inferred spins with previous independent spin estimates, our analysis provides a systematic framework for assessing the relative performance of different HFQPO models for each source. These results highlight the potential of HFQPOs to probe black hole hair and the need for more precise observations and a better understanding of the physical origin of QPOs.

</details>


### [133] [Probing Kerr spacetime via interferometric signatures of higher-order images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11391)
*Fabiano Feleppa,Fabio Aratore,Valerio Bozza,Lorenzo Albanese*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在强偏折极限下解析推导了慢转 Kerr 黑洞中紧致源的复可见度函数，并给出高阶像位置与自旋、源运动的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶像（higher-order images）携带黑洞时空几何信息，长基线干涉（long-baseline interferometry）有望探测其特征，但缺乏对紧致源情形的解析刻画。

Method: 采用强偏折极限（strong deflection limit），在黑洞自旋线性阶推导有限尺寸源的复可见度函数（complex visibility function）；先处理静态赤道源，再通过在各像对应的推迟源位置（retarded source position）取值，推广至圆赤道轨道上的紧致源。

Result: 对静态赤道源，得到无量纲 Kerr 自旋参数（dimensionless Kerr spin parameter）与同宇称序列相邻高阶像角位移的直接关系；对轨道源，参考系拖曳（frame dragging）与源运动以相反角方向改变像位置，最终结构由二者竞争决定，并在长基线可见度图样上留下不同印记。

Conclusion: 结果为在干涉特征中识别黑洞自旋与源运动印记提供了分析框架，可用于长基线观测中高阶像的解读。

Abstract: Higher-order images produced by photons that orbit close to the unstable photon region of a black hole encode information about the spacetime geometry and leave characteristic signatures accessible with long interferometric baselines. We develop an analytical description of these signatures for a compact source in the spacetime of a slowly rotating Kerr black hole. Using the strong deflection limit, we derive the complex visibility function for a source of finite size at linear order in the black-hole spin. For a static equatorial source, we obtain a direct relation between the dimensionless Kerr spin parameter and the angular displacement of successive higher-order images belonging to the same parity sequence. We then extend the construction to a compact source on a circular equatorial orbit by evaluating each image contribution at the corresponding retarded source position. Frame dragging and source motion modify the angular positions of the images and leave distinct imprints on the long-baseline visibility pattern. For the configuration considered here, these effects act in opposite angular directions, so that the resulting structure is determined by their competition. Our results provide an analytical framework for identifying the imprints of black-hole spin and source dynamics in the interferometric signatures of higher-order images.

</details>


### [134] [Note on decoherence of the cosmological background by thermal perturbations in the language of open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11397)
*Alexander Kulyabin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在玩具模型中，将宇宙学扰动视作热浴（thermal bath）环境，用开放量子系统（open quantum systems）框架研究均匀自由度（homogeneous degrees of freedom）的退相干（decoherence），通过有效二阶耦合纳入宇宙学反作用（cosmological backreaction）并推导主方程（master equation），发现热贡献对应的退相干时标可短于 Hubble 时标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有方法多从半经典（semiclassical）总波函数出发对扰动求迹（tracing out），本文意图改用纳入宇宙学反作用的有效二阶耦合来研究扰动热浴对宇宙均匀自由度的退相干影响。另一动机是探讨此类框架下由退相干诱导的指针态（pointer-state）结构能否为早期宇宙半经典态的选择提供判据。

Method: 构建空间平坦的半经典暴胀宇宙玩具模型，其中含处于慢滚（slow-roll）状态的时钟场（clock field），标量宇宙学扰动处于假定的热态（thermal-like state）并充当环境。不采用 Markov 近似（Markov approximation），通过有效二阶耦合纳入扰动的宇宙学反作用，推导主方程；耦合常数由量子化扰动模式的热关联函数（thermal correlation functions）确定。

Result: 在一组代表性参数估计下，热贡献对应的特征退相干时标（decoherence timescale）可以短于 Hubble 时标（Hubble timescale）。

Conclusion: 该框架下热扰动环境能够有效退相干宇宙均匀自由度，且其退相干时标可短于 Hubble 时标；文章进一步讨论了退相干诱导的指针态结构是否可作为早期宇宙半经典态选择判据的问题。

Abstract: We study, within a toy model, how a thermal bath of cosmological perturbations can decohere the homogeneous degrees of freedom of the universe, treating the perturbations as an environment in the framework of open quantum systems. In contrast to several earlier approaches, instead of tracing out the perturbations from a semiclassical ansatz for the total wave function, we incorporate their cosmological backreaction through an effective second-order coupling and derive a master equation. Our toy model consists of a spatially flat semiclassical inflationary universe with a clock field in the slow-roll regime, while scalar cosmological perturbations in an assumed thermal-like state constitute the environment. One of the features of the model is the absence of the Markov approximation. The resulting coupling constant is determined by thermal correlation functions of the quantized perturbation modes. Within this framework, we find that, for a representative set of parameter estimates, the characteristic decoherence timescale corresponding to the thermal contribution can be shorter than the Hubble timescale. In addition, we address the question of whether the pointer-state structure induced by the decoherence in such a framework could provide a criterion for the choice of semiclassical states in the early universe.

</details>


### [135] [Bulk-boundary decompositions and exact perfect fluid stellar distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11518)
*Sudan Hansraj,Christian G. Boehmer,Ndumiso Buthelezi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Böhmer–Jensko（BJ）提出将 Ricci 标量（Ricci scalar）分解为体项与边界项并以此构建引力场论。本文忽略非动力学边界项，在共形静态度规（conformostatic metric）下推导理想流体恒星场方程，重点研究体项拉格朗日量的纯二次情形，并探讨可行物理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BJ 关于 Ricci 标量体项与边界项分解及其拉格朗日量泛函形式的建议，为构建新引力理论提供了出发点。因边界项对天体物理建模而言是非动力学的（non-dynamical），故仅由体项构造理论具有研究价值；且线性体项函数等价于 Einstein 理论，需探究更高阶复杂度以寻找新物理。

Method: 在共形静态度规（conformostatic metric）背景下，求取恒星理想流体分布（stellar perfect fluid distribution）的场方程；先分析体项函数的纯二次情形（pure quadratic case），并尝试对几何势函数（geometric potential functions）作简化选择（如势随半径反比变化）以处理压强各向同性方程（equation of pressure isotropy）。

Result: 纯二次情形的场方程远较 Einstein 方程复杂，压强各向同性方程即使对单个几何势函数作简单选择也难以求解；势随半径反比变化的情形（potentials varying inversely with the radius）无法产生可行模型，故改为假定两势成比例关系（proportional relationship），从而构造出满足基本物理要求的各向同性流体壳层（shell of isotropic fluid）；对等温流体（isothermal fluid，密度与压强均按半径平方反比律）的研究显示其流体行为与对应的 Einstein 模型相当。

Conclusion: 在 BJ 体项分解框架下，纯二次拉格朗日量的可行情景依赖于对几何势函数施加特定约束（如成比例关系），由此可得物理上合理的各向同性流体壳层；等温流体情形与 Einstein 模型结果可比。边界项在本文的天体物理建模中不参与动力学。

Abstract: The decomposition of the Ricci scalar in terms of bulk and boundary terms gives rise to an interesting gravitational field theory as proposed by Böhmer and Jensko (BJ). BJ went on to suggest functional forms of the bulk contributions in the Lagrangian. For the purposes of astrophysical modelling the boundary terms are non-dynamical and the entire theory is built from the bulk contribution only. The field equations for a stellar perfect fluid distribution are obtained against the background of a conformostatic metric. Since the linear form of the function of the bulk term is equivalent to Einstein we probe the next level of complexity namely the pure quadratic case. The field equations are substantially more complicated than Einstein's equations. The equation of pressure isotropy proves intractable even with simple choices of one of the geometric potential functions. Accordingly we analyse the case of the potentials varying inversely with the radius. Viable models do not arise in this case and so we postulate a proportional relationship between the potentials. It turns out that a physically reasonable shell of isotropic fluid may be constructed that satisfies elementary physical requirements. Finally we investigate the isothermal fluid where both density and pressure go as the inverse square law of the radius. This results in fluid behaviour that is comparable to that in the corresponding Einstein model.

</details>


### [136] [Holographic dark energy from generalized entropies and extended infrared cut-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11616)
*Ke Chen,Simone D'Onofrio,Sergei D. Odintsov,Tanmoy Paul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究基于广义熵（generalized entropy）泛函与局域红外截断（infrared cut-off）的全息暗能量（holographic dark energy）模型，构建九个模型并用DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+与Planck压缩CMB数据约束，结果显示数据偏好高阶红外截断，但无显著偏离Bekenstein-Hawking面积律的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有全息暗能量模型多基于Bekenstein-Hawking面积律与简单红外截断，广义熵与高阶截断的组合效应尚缺乏系统性观测检验，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 将三种熵（Barrow熵及三参数、五参数广义熵）分别与Granda-Oliveros（GO）截断及其两种扩展（加入γH^{-2}项与γ\ddot{H}/H项，后者使暗能量演化方程升为二阶）组合，得到九个模型；通过MCMC分析结合DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+与Planck压缩CMB似然进行约束，并用Akaike与Bayesian信息准则比较。

Result: Hubble常数（Hubble constant）在所有模型中保持稳定；H^{-2}项在各情形下均不受约束，仅在远期未来才起作用，且从不优于GO截断；\ddot{H}/H项对Barrow与S_5施加紧致约束并显著改善拟合，但对S_3无效；最佳拟合为Barrow熵配合\ddot{H}/H截断，而Barrow指数在所有情形下仍与零相容；对S_3与S_5的暗能量状态方程（equation of state, EoS）分析发现类精质（quintessence-like）行为与幻影穿越（phantom crossing）。

Conclusion: 数据偏好高阶红外截断，但未显示对Bekenstein-Hawking面积律的显著偏离。

Abstract: We study holographic dark energy models constructed from generalized entropy functionals and local infrared cut-offs. Three entropies (Barrow and the three and five-parameter generalized entropies) are each combined with the Granda-Oliveros (GO) cut-off and two extensions, one adding a $γH^{-2}$ term and one a $γ\ddot{H}/H$ term, the latter raising the dark-energy evolution equation to second order. The nine resulting models are constrained against DESI DR2 BAO, Pantheon+ and a Planck compressed CMB likelihood through an MCMC analysis, and compared using the Akaike and Bayesian information criteria. The Hubble constant is stable across all models. The $H^{-2}$ term is unconstrained in every case, as it becomes relevant only in the far future, and never improves on the GO cut-off. The $\ddot{H}/H$ term, instead, is tightly constrained for Barrow and $S_5$, where it improves the fit substantially, but not for $S_3$. The best fit is obtained for Barrow entropy with the $\ddot{H}/H$ cut-off, yet the Barrow exponent remains compatible with zero in all cases. The data thus favour a higher-order infrared cut-off, while showing no significant evidence for a departure from the Bekenstein-Hawking area law. We also investigate the dark energy equation of state (EoS) of $S_3$ and $S_5$, discovering quintessence-like behavior and phantom crossing.

</details>


### [137] [FLRW-Compatible Tensors and Perfect-Fluid Universality in Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11626)
*Cem Dalkırmaz,Yaghoub Heydarzade*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明在 $(D+1)$ 维 FLRW 时空上，满足空间均匀性与各向同性的任意阶协变张量场（当 $D>N$ 时）可有限分解为由度规 $g_{\mu\nu}$ 与单位类时向量 $u_\mu$ 构成的多项式，其标量系数仅依赖宇宙时；由此给出修正引力中理想流体结构与任意阶张量分类的普适性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于 FLRW 背景上二阶（rank-two）张量普适性的结果，但缺乏对任意阶张量场在空间均匀性与各向同性约束下结构的系统性分类；该研究旨在为修正引力场方程（modified gravity equations）中出现的理想流体（perfect-fluid）形式提供更高阶的张量基础。

Method: 在配备 Levi-Civita 联络（Levi-Civita connection）的 $(D+1)$ 维 FLRW 时空（Friedmann–Lemaître–Robertson–Walker spacetimes）上研究协变张量场，对在空间均匀性与各向同性群下不变的协变 $N$ 阶张量场（$D>N$）进行代数分解，构造由 $g_{\mu\nu}$ 与 $u_\mu$ 组成的单项式展开，标量系数仅依赖宇宙时（cosmic time）。

Result: 证明满足条件的 $N$ 阶张量场存在有限的单项式分解；作为直接推论，当 $D>2$ 时，爱因斯坦型重排（Einstein-like rearrangement）中出现的任意二阶修正项均具有有效理想流体（effective perfect-fluid）形式。

Conclusion: 该结果给出支撑 FLRW 背景上修正引力方程理想流体结构的任意阶张量分类（arbitrary-rank tensorial classification），补充了现有的二阶普适性结果，并讨论了其在广义 Friedmann 方程与修正引力中测地偏离方程（geodesic deviation equation）的应用。

Abstract: We study FLRW-compatible tensor fields on Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker spacetimes in $(D+1)$ dimensions equipped with Levi-Civita connection. We prove that any covariant rank-$N$ tensor field invariant under the spatial homogeneity and isotropy group, with $D>N$, admits a finite decomposition into monomials constructed from the metric tensor $g_{μν}$ and the unit timelike vector $u_μ$, with scalar coefficients depending only on cosmic time. As a direct consequence, any rank-two correction term appearing in an Einstein-like rearrangement of the FLRW background field equations has the effective perfect-fluid form for $D>2$. Thus, the result provides an arbitrary-rank tensorial classification underlying the perfect-fluid structure of modified gravity equations on FLRW backgrounds, complementing existing rank-two universality results. Applications to generalized Friedmann equations and to the geodesic deviation equation in modified gravity are also discussed.

</details>


### [138] [Beyond the Dolgov-Kawasaki instability: Covariant scalaron stability, entropy transport, and inner horizons in f(R) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11649)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文区分了度规 $f(R)$ 引力中的两类问题：Dolgov-Kawasaki 不稳定性所检验的局部标量子响应，与强场视界动力学。作者利用 $F=f_R$ 与 Legendre 势 $\mathcal U(F)$ 建立分层框架，证明背景输运仅依赖 $(f,f_R)$，而标量子质量依赖可独立调节的 $f_{RR}$，并给出带电解及宇宙学检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dolgov-Kawasaki 不稳定性（Dolgov-Kawasaki instability）仅检验局部标量子响应，无法确定强场视界动力学，二者此前未被明确区分。作者旨在通过 $F=f_R$ 与 Legendre 势的表述，将正则性、背景输运、标量子稳定性与非线性内视界演化拆分为可分别处理的问题。

Method: 采用 $F=f_R$ 与 Legendre 势 $\mathcal U(F)=FR-f$ 的变量表述，引入标量子恢复系数 $m_{\rm sc}^2=(F-Rf_{RR})/(3f_{RR})$；利用精确球对称 Wald 面积输运恒等式（Wald-area transport identities）消去标量子 Hessian，并在 Einstein 框架下区分纵向动理学聚焦与势依赖的交叉聚焦。以 $f(R)=R+\alpha R^2$ 中的 Reissner-Nordström 解作为精确判别实例，并用变 $F$ 带电解与宇宙学检验推广至非恒定 $F$ 情形。

Result: 在固定常曲率背景与无迹物质条件下，输运仅依赖 $(f,f_R)$，而标量子质量取决于可独立调节的 $f_{RR}$。Reissner-Nordström 在 $f(R)=R+\alpha R^2$ 中给出精确判别：相同背景输运可与相反的标量子质量符号共存。对透射至非退化 Cauchy 视界的非零有质量标量子尾，条件性的固定背景估计给出对广义熵聚焦的非可积贡献；该估计未建立非线性质量膨胀（mass inflation）或特定可延拓类。

Conclusion: 结果为耦合正则性、背景输运、标量子稳定性与非线性内视界演化提供了分层框架。所给估计仅限于固定背景，不构成对非线性质量膨胀或特定可延拓类的判定。

Abstract: The Dolgov-Kawasaki instability tests the local scalaron response in metric $f(R)$ gravity, but does not determine strong-field horizon dynamics. We separate these questions using $F=f_R$ and the Legendre potential $\mathcal U(F)=FR-f$. The scalaron restoring coefficient is $m_{\rm sc}^2=(F-Rf_{RR})/(3f_{RR})$, whereas exact spherical Wald-area transport identities cancel the scalaron Hessian. Their Einstein-frame interpretation distinguishes longitudinal kinetic focusing from potential-dependent cross-focusing. We prove that, at fixed constant-curvature background with tracefree matter, transport depends only on $(f,f_R)$, while the scalaron mass depends on the independently adjustable $f_{RR}$. Reissner-Nordström in $f(R)=R+αR^2$ gives an exact discriminator: identical background transport coexists with opposite scalaron mass signs. A charged solution with varying $F$ and a cosmological check test the identities beyond this constant-$F$ sector. For a nonzero massive scalaron tail transmitted to a nondegenerate Cauchy horizon, a conditional fixed-background estimate gives a nonintegrable contribution to generalized entropy focusing. This estimate does not establish nonlinear mass inflation or a particular extendibility class. The results provide a layered framework for coupling regularity, background transport, scalaron stability, and nonlinear inner-horizon evolution.

</details>


### [139] [Nonreciprocal black holes with primary hair in beyond Horndeski gravity: Exact solutions and stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11712)
*Sebastian Bahamonde,Theodoros Nakas,Simon Iteanu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种系统方法，可在位移对称（shift-symmetric）与 $Z_2$ 对称的超越 Horndeski（beyond Horndeski）理论中构造精确、静态、渐近平坦的黑洞解，且不依赖限制性的互反条件 $g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多依赖互反条件（reciprocal condition）$g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1$，限制了可用几何的普遍性；本文旨在放松该约束，以获得由两个独立度规函数描述的更一般时空，并厘清超越 Horndeski 理论中不同类型的标量毛（scalar hair）。

Method: 发展一种新的系统性构造方法，用于位移对称与 $Z_2$ 对称的超越 Horndeski 理论；所得解空间包含来自不同理论分支的两类毛发，并主要针对第二分支导出若干精确黑洞解族。稳定性方面，通过分析轴向扇区（axial sector）与偶宇称扇区的单极（monopole，$\ell=0$）模，进行线性微扰研究。

Result: 解空间包含两类定性不同的毛发：一支对应 khronometric hair，另一支为真正的标量毛；两者的标量轮廓均定义了非平凡的优选叶状结构（preferred foliation），但位移对称 Noether 流及其对应传播自由度在前者为平凡、在后者非零。第二分支的解一般为奇异的，并带有与独立标量荷（scalar charge）相关的主标量毛（primary scalar hair）；这些解可被正则化，得到规则黑洞或孤子（solitonic）构型，此时标量毛变为次级的（secondary）。对代表性含标量毛解，微扰分析给出在所考虑模式下线稳定性的证据。

Conclusion: 不依赖互反条件可构造更一般的精确黑洞解，并揭示超越 Horndeski 理论中两类本质不同的毛发来源；奇异解可正则化为规则黑洞或孤子，且代表性解在所考察的轴向与单极微扰模式下表现出线性稳定性。

Abstract: We develop a novel systematic method for constructing exact, static, and asymptotically flat black-hole solutions in shift-symmetric and $Z_2$-symmetric beyond Horndeski theories. In contrast to previous studies, our approach does not rely on the restrictive reciprocal condition $g_{tt}g_{rr}=-1$, allowing for more general geometries characterized by two independent metric functions. The resulting solution space contains two qualitatively distinct types of hair originating from different branches of the theory. One branch is characterized by khronometric hair, while the other by genuine scalar hair. In both, the scalar profile defines a nontrivial preferred foliation, but the shift-symmetry Noether current and the associated propagating degree of freedom are trivial for the former and nonvanishing for the latter. Focusing mainly on the second branch, we derive several distinct families of exact black-hole solutions. In general, these solutions are singular and exhibit primary scalar hair associated with an independent scalar charge. Nevertheless, we demonstrate that they can be regularized to yield regular black-hole or solitonic configurations, for which the scalar hair becomes secondary. We further investigate the stability properties of these solutions under linear perturbations by analyzing the axial sector and the monopole $(\ell=0)$ mode of the even-parity sector. For representative solutions with scalar hair, the perturbative analysis provides evidence for linear stability under the modes considered.

</details>


### [140] [Testing Extra-Dimensional Gravitational-Wave Polarizations with Compact-Binary Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11855)
*Hai-Tian Wang,Dicong Liang,Lijing Shao,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于五例致密双星事件的频域贝叶斯分析，在引入一个额外空间维度并考察张量波几何投影产生表观张量、矢量与标量极化（polarization）的框架下，广义相对论（general relativity, GR）参考点在所有情形中均落在估计的90%最高后验密度区域内，未发现额外维度的观测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超四维广义相对论理论中额外空间维度自然出现，但直接观测证据仍缺乏。引力波（gravitational waves）提供了独特的探测手段：几何投影到可观测三维空间子空间可产生表观张量、矢量与标量极化。

Method: 考虑一个额外空间维度与双模张量波假设（two-mode tensor-wave ansatz），其投影极化分量为两个底层 GR 张量波形的固定线性组合，并经标准探测器天线方向图（antenna patterns）进入应变测量。对五例致密双星事件进行频域贝叶斯分析，约束保持传播位于可观测子空间的两个非对易旋转次序下的两个旋转角；另对两例事件分析允许传播向额外维度倾斜的四种次序。

Result: 对 GW250114，在两角拟合中 GR 分位数上界为 Q_GR ≃ 0.6%；三角拟合中经对第三个角度边缘化（marginalizing）后为 Q^marg_GR ≃ 4.6%。在整个事件样本中，GR 参考点在所有情形下均位于估计的 90% 最高后验密度（highest-posterior-density）区域内。

Conclusion: 在所考察的旋转次序与投影极化框架下，观测数据与广义相对论预言一致，尚无支持额外空间维度存在的证据；GR 参考点始终处于90%最高后验密度区域内。

Abstract: Extra spatial dimensions arise naturally in theories beyond four-dimensional general relativity (GR), but direct observational evidence remains elusive. Gravitational waves offer a distinctive probe: geometric projection onto the observable three-dimensional spatial subspace can produce apparent tensor, vector, and scalar polarizations. We consider one additional spatial dimension and a two-mode tensor-wave ansatz for which the projected polarization components are fixed linear combinations of the same two underlying GR tensor waveforms. These components enter the measured strain through the standard detector antenna patterns. A frequency-domain Bayesian analysis of five compact-binary events constrains two rotation angles for two noncommuting orderings of rotations that preserve propagation within the observed subspace. Two events are further analyzed for four orderings that allow propagation to tilt into the extra dimension. For GW250114, the maximum GR quantiles among the tested rotation orderings are $Q_{\mathrm{GR}}\simeq0.6\%$ for the two-angle fits and $Q^{\mathrm{marg}}_{\mathrm{GR}}\simeq4.6\%$ for the three-angle fits after marginalizing over the third angle. Across the full event sample, the GR reference point lies inside the estimated 90% highest-posterior-density region in every case.

</details>


### [141] [Sharing of Gaussian tripartite steering in de-Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11874)
*Xin-Ze Song,Xiao-Li Huang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在连续变量（continuous-variable）框架下研究德西特空间（de-Sitter space）中高斯三分量子导引（Gaussian tripartite quantum steering）的重分布与方向性，揭示曲率对导引的抑制、鲁棒性与跨宇宙视界生成效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德西特曲率会如何影响多体量子关联的分布与方向性尚不清晰，尤其是三分导引相对两体导引是否具有不同的退化或存续行为，以及宇宙学视界两侧的模式间能否产生量子导引，均需系统刻画。

Method: 通过Bogoliubov变换（Bogoliubov transformation）将Bunch-Davies真空（Bunch-Davies vacuum）用开图模式（open-chart modes）表示，推导所得多体高斯态（multipartite Gaussian state）的协方差矩阵（covariance matrix），并分析一对二（one-to-two）与二对一（two-to-one）两类导引构型。

Result: 德西特曲率总体上抑制可及模式间原有的导引，但影响强烈依赖导引方向：部分构型在有限曲率下发生突然死亡（sudden death），而指向非引力模式的二对一导引在整个参数范围内保持有限，显示三分导引相对两体情形的鲁棒性。最大导引不对称性（steering asymmetry）出现在某一方向发生突然死亡的临界点，标志双向导引向单向导引的转变。此外，曲率可生成涉及宇宙视界（cosmological horizon）分隔模式的导引，依分划不同呈持续单向、非对称双向行为，或由单向到双向的突然诞生（sudden-birth）转变。

Conclusion: 德西特曲率不仅削弱既有的量子关联，还跨因果不相连区域重新分配并生成具有方向性的多体量子导引。

Abstract: We investigate the redistribution and directional properties of Gaussian tripartite quantum steering in de-Sitter space within the continuous-variable framework. By expressing the Bunch-Davies vacuum in terms of the open-chart modes through a Bogoliubov transformation, we derive the covariance matrix of the resulting multipartite Gaussian state and analyze both one-to-two and two-to-one steering configurations. We find that de-Sitter curvature generally suppresses the steering initially shared among the accessible modes, but its influence depends strongly on the steering direction.
