In particular, while some steering configurations undergo sudden death at finite curvature, two-to-one steering toward a nongravitational mode remains finite throughout the parameter range considered, demonstrating a robustness of tripartite steering absent in its bipartite counterpart. Remarkably, the maximum steering asymmetry occurs at the critical point at which steering in one direction undergoes sudden death, marking the transition from two-way to one-way steering. We further show that the de-Sitter gravitational field can generate tripartite steering involving modes separated by the cosmological horizon. Depending on the steering partition, this curvature-induced steering exhibits persistent one-way or asymmetric two-way behavior, as well as a sudden-birth transition from one-way to two-way steering. These results demonstrate that de-Sitter curvature not only degrades preexisting quantum correlations but also redistributes and generates directional multipartite steering across causally disconnected regions.

</details>


### [142] [Testing General Relativity with Binary Lens Microlensing Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11949)
*Xinyang Jin,Shuo Cao,Xuanni Chen,Yalong Nan,Xuchen Tai,Shuxun Tian*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出利用双星微引力透镜（binary microlensing）结合干涉天体测量（interferometric astrometry）测量极弱引力场中的参数化后牛顿参数（PPN parameter）γ，基于ASASSN-22av事件的模拟可将γ精度估计至约23%，未来有望提升至约3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论（General Relativity, GR）已在星系与太阳系尺度被精确检验，但在极弱引力场（无量纲牛顿势Φ~10^-8）中仍约束不足，需要新方法探测该弱场区域并测量单位质量所产生的空间曲率。

Method: 将微引力透镜推断的透镜质量（M_lens^GR）与干涉天体测量确定的动力学质量（M_dyn）相结合，通过双星微引力透镜事件测定PPN参数γ；针对此前受背景暗弱与角间距极小（约毫角秒量级）限制的问题，借助GRAVITY+提升的灵敏度与天体测量精度开展天体测量模拟。

Result: 基于双星微引力透镜事件ASASSN-22av的天体测量模拟，直接估算得到γ的精度约为23%；在更多具备精确动力学/透镜质量测定的双星微引力透镜事件基础上，该极弱场GR检验精度可进一步提升至约3%。

Conclusion: 双星微引力透镜结合GRAVITY+干涉天体测量可成为探测极弱引力场、测量PPN参数γ的有效途径，并具有随样本增多而显著提升检验精度的潜力。

Abstract: Einstein's General Relativity (GR), which has been precisely tested on galaxy and solar system scales, is still poorly constrained in extremely weak gravitational fields (with dimensionless Newtonian potentials of $Φ\sim 10^{-8}$). In this Letter, we propose a new method to probe such a weak-field regime of gravity and measure the spatial curvature generated per unit mass. Specifically, binary microlensing events provide a laboratory to measure the parameterized post-Newtonian (PPN) parameter ($γ$), by combining the lensing mass ($M_{\rm lens}^{\rm GR}$) inferred from microlensing with the dynamical mass ($M_{\rm dyn}$) determined from interferometric astrometry. Astrometric tracking of binary lenses was previously hindered by their faintness and minuscule angular separations (typically a few milliarcseconds). This limitation has been alleviated by the deployment of GRAVITY+, which pushes the instrument's sensitivity and astrometric precision to unprecedented heights. Based on astrometric simulations of the binary microlensing event ASASSN-22av, we directly estimate $γ$ at the precision of ~23%. Such test of GR in the extremely weak-field regime can be further improved to much higher precision (~3%), based on more binary microlensing events with precise determination of the dynamical/lens mass.

</details>


### [143] [Gravitational radiation from a photon on the light ring: multipole fluxes and the logarithmic divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12082)
*Enrico Barausse*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 无质量粒子沿 Schwarzschild/Kerr 黑洞的圆形零测地线运动时，引力波辐射在所有多极矩（multipole）ℓ 上仅按 1/ℓ 衰减，导致瞬时总能流对数发散；文章用 Zerilli–Regge–Wheeler 与 Teukolsky 方程解析导出该结果，并修正了 1973 年同步辐射公式的奇宇称项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往对黑洞周围粒子引力波辐射的认识通常假定大 ℓ 处存在指数压低，而沿光环（light ring）运动的无质量粒子是否遵循该规律及其谱形来源尚不明确；同时经典同步辐射公式的奇偶宇称项准确性有待检验。

Method: 以零应力–能量张量（null stress-energy tensor）为源，从 Zerilli–Regge–Wheeler 方程与 Teukolsky 方程出发进行解析推导，分离无穷远辐射与视界吸收两部分功率，并导出 Kerr 光环自旋因子 g(a) 的闭合形式。

Result: 辐射功率满足 Ė_ℓ → g(a) κE²/(M²ℓ)，κ=0.063653，g=27M²(r₀−M)/[r₀²(r₀+3M)]，其中 r₀ 为 Kerr 光环半径；1/ℓ 行为源于光子与准正规模（quasinormal modes）的近共振以及无质量粒子源中的相消效应（使每极功率相对普适源减少 ℓ² 因子）。领头阶有一半辐射落入黑洞，并给出非自旋情形下等分的第一阶修正；有质量近光环轨道中 ℓ 依赖转为指数截断。验证了 Breuer–Ruffini–Tiomno–Vishveshwara (1973) 偶宇称主导项，但指出其奇宇称项偏小四倍。

Conclusion: 沿光环无质量粒子的引力波谱呈 1/ℓ 型而非指数压低，其物理根源为模式近共振与无质量源特异的相消；研究结果经数值解验证，Schwarzschild 情形达 ℓ=12800、Kerr 情形达 ℓ=800，并修正了既有同步辐射公式。

Abstract: A massless particle on the circular null geodesic of a Schwarzschild or Kerr black hole radiates gravitational waves at all multipoles $\ell$ with a power that falls off only as $1/\ell$, so that the total instantaneous power is logarithmically divergent. We derive this result analytically from the Zerilli--Regge--Wheeler and Teukolsky equations with a null stress-energy tensor as source, and find that both the power radiated to infinity and the power absorbed by the horizon approach $\dot{E}_\ell\to g(a) κE^2/(M^2\ell)$, where $E$ is the energy of the particle, $M$ the mass of the black hole and $a$ its spin, with $κ=0.063653$ and a spin factor $g(a)$, for which we obtain, for any spin, the closed form $g=27M^2(r_0-M)/[r_0^2(r_0+3M)]$ in terms of the radius $r_0$ of the Kerr light ring. The $1/\ell$ behavior has two causes: the near-resonance of the photon with the quasinormal modes, which prevents any exponential suppression at large $\ell$, and a cancellation in the source that is specific to massless particles, which reduces the power per multipole by a factor $\ell^2$ with respect to a generic source. At leading order half of the radiation falls into the black hole, and we compute the first correction to this equal split in the non-spinning case. The same analysis describes massive particles on orbits close to the light ring, where the mass turns the $1/\ell$ law into an exponential cut-off. We recover the dominant, even-parity term of the geodesic synchrotron radiation formulae for massive particles of Breuer, Ruffini, Tiomno and Vishveshwara (1973), but we find that their odd-parity term is too small by a factor of four. All results are checked against numerical solutions, up to $\ell=12800$ in Schwarzschild and $\ell=800$ in Kerr.

</details>


### [144] [All theories of gravity with second-order traced field equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12097)
*Ángel J. Murcia*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文对具有二阶导数迹方程（traced equations of motion）的微分同胚不变引力理论（diffeomorphism-invariant theories of gravity）进行了完整分类，并证明了关于由黎曼张量（Riemann tensor）代数构造的此类理论形式的猜想，同时分类了所有迹方程为二阶的多项式拟拓扑引力（polynomial quasi-topological gravities）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力理论中高阶导数项通常引入不稳定性，而迹方程（traced field equations）的阶数可作为筛选良性理论的判据。作者旨在系统刻画满足二阶迹方程的微分同胚不变引力理论，并检验相关形式猜想。

Method: 采用理论分类（classification）方法：对所有具有二阶导数迹运动方程的微分同胚不变引力理论进行归类；对由黎曼张量代数构造、迹方程为二阶的理论，证明其形式的猜想；并据此分类二阶迹方程的多项式拟拓扑引力。

Result: 实现了对二阶迹方程微分同胚不变引力理论的完整分类；对黎曼张量代数构造理论的猜想给出了肯定证明；完成了对二阶迹方程多项式拟拓扑引力的分类。

Conclusion: 二阶迹方程构成约束引力理论形式的有力条件，可用于系统分类微分同胚不变引力及拟拓扑引力，相关猜想得到证实。

Abstract: We classify all diffeomorphism-invariant theories of gravity which possess traced field equations of motion of second order in derivatives. We also prove in the affirmative a conjecture regarding the form of theories constructed algebraically from the Riemann tensor with second-order traced field equations and classify all polynomial quasi-topological gravities with second-order traced field equations.

</details>


### [145] [The variance of the CMB temperature gradient: a dependence on the spatial orientation and anisotropy of a multiply connected Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12179)
*Ralf Aurich,Martin J France,Frank Steiner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与模型无关（model-independent）的方法，基于 rho 统计量（rho-statistics）与掩模扫描（mask-sweeping）流程，在 CMB 温度图中揭示各向异性与几何模式，并据此给出宇宙具有环面拓扑（toroidal topology）时约 3 个哈勃长度（Hubble lengths）的有限尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示 CMB 温度图中潜在的各向异性（anisotropy）与几何模式，并检验 3-环面拓扑（3-torus topology）宇宙所预期的几何特征。作者希望以不依赖具体模型的方式探测这些微弱但持续存在的结构。

Method: 方法基于 rho 统计量与掩模扫描过程，在 768 个掩模取向下遍历整个 CMB，并在大量环面（toroidal）模拟图上检验所探测模式是否与预期的天空圆环（circles-in-the-sky）布局在几何上相符。

Result: 结果显示，所探测模式在大规模环面图集合上与预期天空圆环布局几何匹配；据此重新验证了立方环面尺寸 L 与归一化温度梯度方差标准差 rho 之间的线性关系，并得出宇宙有限尺寸 s 约为 3 个哈勃长度。此外，统计各向同性（statistical isotropy）的偏离极为微弱，几乎不影响 rho 与 L 的线性关系。

Conclusion: 结论指出，立方环面类拓扑为宇宙施加周期性条件（可视为周期性边界条件），约束整体不均匀度并对应零平均曲率（zero average curvature）。rho 不仅是宇宙空间有限性及其尺寸的统计特征（无论拓扑为单连通或复连通），还可借助掩模扫描流程刻画 CMB 的各向异性模式。

Abstract: In the present work we develop a model--independent method to reveal the underlying patterns of anisotropy and geometry in CMB temperature maps. This method, based on the rho-statistics and a process of mask--sweeping, unveils faint but persistent geometrical and periodic structures characterising the anisotropy of the CMB in a universe with 3-torus topology. We assess that the patterns detected over large ensembles of toroidal maps match geometrically to the layouts of expected circles-in-the-sky. The mask--sweeping method explores the whole CMB over 768 mask orientations. Thus we are able to re-verify the linear relation between L the size of the cubic torus and rho the standard deviation of the normalised variance of the CMB temperature gradient. This confers a finite size s of approximately 3 Hubble lengths to our Universe with toroidal topology. Furthermore, it is shown that the violation of statistical isotropy is so faint that this has almost no influence on the linear relation between rho and L. A topology like the cubic toroidal one imposes to our Universe periodic conditions (that can be seen as periodic boundary conditions) which constrain the global degree of inhomogeneity and a zero average curvature. We demonstrate here that rho is not only a statistical signature of the spatial finiteness and size of our Universe (may its topology be simply or multiply connected) but also outlines the patterns of anisotropy of the CMB with the help of the mask--sweeping process.

</details>


### [146] [Swirling spacetimes in higher dimensions: Vacuum backgrounds and black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12372)
*José Barrientos,Adolfo Cisterna,Carlos Herdeiro,Konstantinos Pallikaris*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作利用维度约化理论的隐藏对称性，在五维真空广义相对论中构造出含两个独立旋转参数的高维旋转时空，并揭示其多向渐近结构与方向性曲率奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维旋转解（如 Myers–Perry）通常难以通过对称性方法系统生成，而隐藏对称性（hidden symmetries）与陪集空间（coset-space）约化为构造此类真空解提供了有力工具，故本文旨在借此框架构造更一般的旋转时空并考察其渐近行为。

Method: 直接从陪集空间表述出发，在五维沿两个独立轴向 Killing 向量（axial Killing vectors）作维度约化，得到 SL(3,R)/SO(3) sigma 模型与含两个独立旋转参数的真正五维背景，并将其变换用于生成双旋转的 Schwarzschild–Tangherlini 与 Myers–Perry 几何。

Result: 新背景具有多向渐近结构（multidirectional asymptotic structure）；在径向–角向关联极限（correlated radial-angular limits）下出现方向性曲率奇异性（directional curvature singularities），而固定角度的大半径极限无法显现；并给出在有限仿射参数（finite affine parameter）内到达该奇异渐近端的因果测地线（causal geodesics）。

Conclusion: 该构造的最大轴向延拓（maximally axial extension）表明：纯轴向约化到三维以五维为最大，六维可额外容纳一个非平凡平移形变（translational deformation），更高维仅贡献平坦旁观方向（flat spectator directions）。

Abstract: We construct higher-dimensional swirling spacetimes in vacuum General Relativity using the hidden symmetries of the dimensionally reduced theory. Working directly with the coset-space formulation, we perform in five dimensions a reduction along the two independent axial Killing vectors, obtaining an $SL(3,\mathbb{R})/SO(3)$ sigma model and a genuinely five-dimensional background with two independent swirling parameters. The same transformation yields doubly swirling Schwarzschild--Tangherlini and Myers--Perry geometries. A distinctive feature of the new background is its multidirectional asymptotic structure. Correlated radial-angular limits reveal directional curvature singularities that are invisible in the usual large-radius limit at fixed angle. We further exhibit causal geodesics that reach such singular asymptotic ends in finite affine parameter. Finally, we describe the maximally axial extension of this construction to higher dimensions: five dimensions are maximal for an entirely axial reduction to three dimensions, while six dimensions admit an additional nontrivial translational deformation, with further dimensions contributing only flat spectator directions.

</details>


### [147] [A Pairwise Surrogate for Gravitational-Wave Spectra from Highly Relativistic Vacuum Bubble Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12389)
*Malte Buschmann,Toby Opferkuch*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于成对（pairwise）碰撞的代理模型（surrogate model），将微观碰撞动力学与多气泡转变几何分离，用降维的(1+1)维模拟构建双气泡引力波谱库并结合几何权重，以极低成本重建多气泡系统的引力波谱，并可覆盖直接模拟难以达到的高洛伦兹助推（large-boost）区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空一阶相变（vacuum first-order phase transition）产生的引力波可探测相对论性气泡碰撞（bubble collision）的非线性动力学。然而，要预测其引力波谱，需在含大量气泡的体积内分辨被洛伦兹收缩（Lorentz-contracted）的标量气泡壁，使大助推区域的直接格点模拟（lattice simulation）计算代价过高。

Method: 作者发展了一种基于模拟的成对代理模型，将微观碰撞动力学与多气泡相变几何解耦：利用对称性约化的(1+1)维标量场模拟建立双气泡引力波谱（含碰撞后非线性演化）库，再以考虑其他气泡屏蔽（screening）效应的几何权重进行组合。

Result: 双气泡谱以及三气泡、多气泡系统重建谱均与完整(3+1)维格点模拟结果进行了对照检验。代理模型不仅计算成本可低数个量级，还能获得超出直接多气泡模拟实际能力的强相对论碰撞。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究引力波谱的振幅与形状如何依赖碰撞助推（collision boost）和标量势（scalar potential）提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from vacuum first-order phase transitions probe the nonlinear dynamics of relativistic bubble collisions. Predicting their spectrum requires resolving Lorentz-contracted scalar bubble walls throughout a volume containing many bubbles, making the large-boost regime prohibitively expensive for direct lattice simulations. We develop a simulation-based pairwise surrogate that separates the microscopic collision dynamics from the geometry of the multi-bubble transition. This utilizes symmetry-reduced $(1+1)$-dimensional scalar-field simulations to build a library of two-bubble gravitational-wave spectra, including the nonlinear post-collision evolution. These spectra are combined with geometric weights that account for screening by other bubbles. We test the two-bubble spectra and the reconstructed spectra of three- and many-bubble systems against full $(3+1)$-dimensional lattice simulations. Not only can the surrogate be orders of magnitude cheaper computationally, but it also provides access to highly relativistic collisions beyond the practical reach of direct many-bubble simulations. This provides a route to studying how the spectral amplitude and shape depend on the collision boost and scalar potential.

</details>


### [148] [Listening to the Horizon: Probing Near-Horizon Reflectivity with GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12429)
*R. Prasad,Subhodeep Sarkar,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用 GW250114 中新识别的直接波（Direct Wave, DW）构建延迟回声模板，对残余天体的视界反射率（horizon reflectivity）进行零假设检验，未发现回声证据，给出 90% 置信上限 |R_s| < 0.06。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验天体物理黑洞的视界性质是引力波天文学（gravitational-wave astronomy）的核心目标。DW 是否为近视界物理（near-horizon physics）提供信息尚不明确，但可作为新的探针。若残余天体是具部分反射表面的奇异致密天体（exotic compact object, ECO），内落辐射会被反射并穿隧泄漏，产生延迟回声。

Method: 基于 DW 构建相应的回声模板（echo template），并以观测到的 DW 进行归一化；随后在经准正规模滤波（quasinormal-mode-filtered）的数据中搜索该回声信号。表面反射率记为 |R_s|，范围内表面位置从 0.1 M_f 延伸至距假想视界一个普朗克长度（Planck length）。

Result: 未发现回声证据，贝叶斯因子对数 ln B = -2.9；得到 90% 置信上限 |R_s| < 0.06，对应反射能量占比低于 0.4%。

Conclusion: 该零检验（null test）将瞬发铃宕（ringdown）信号转化为对视界反射率的直接检验，为近视界物理提供约束，并支持残余天体接近克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole）的假设。

Abstract: Testing the horizon properties of astrophysical black holes is a central goal of gravitational-wave astronomy. The recently identified Direct Wave (DW), a prompt non-quasinormal component of GW250114, provides a new probe of near-horizon physics. While it remains unclear whether the DW itself carries horizon information, we show that it enables a novel null test of horizon reflectivity. If the remnant is an exotic compact object (ECO) with a partially reflecting surface, inward plunge radiation is reflected and leaks back through the gravitational potential barrier, producing a delayed DW echo whose amplitude is set by the surface reflectivity ($|\mathcal{R}_s|$). We construct the corresponding echo template, normalized by the observed DW, and search for it in quasinormal-mode-filtered data. We find no evidence for an echo ($\ln\mathcal{B}=-2.9$) and obtain the 90\% credible bound $|\mathcal{R}_s|<0.06$, corresponding to a reflected energy fraction below $0.4\%$, for surfaces from $0.1\, M_f$ to a Planck length from the would-be horizon. Our method thus turns the prompt ringdown signal into a direct test of horizon reflectivity.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [A nonclassical law of the wall in superfluid helium-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10923)
*Mikai Hulse,Musfequs Salehin,Sosuke Inui,Wei Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该研究通过分子标记测速（molecular tagging velocimetry）与压降测量，在机械驱动的 He II 管道流中首次可视化近壁速度场，发现正常流体速度仍遵循对数壁律（law of the wall），但有效卡门常数（Kármán constant）不足经典值的一半，且在所研究温度范围内几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典湍流的对数壁律是稳健的标度律，但其在超流 $^4$He（He II）等量子流体中是否依然成立尚不清楚。由于 He II 的黏性正常流体（normal fluid）与无黏超流体（superfluid）在固壁处遵循根本不同的边界条件，二者运动又可能通过量子化涡旋（quantized vortices）耦合，近壁流动的组织机制仍是量子流体动力学（quantum-fluid hydrodynamics）的开放问题。

Method: 在机械驱动的 He II 管道流中，采用分子标记测速（molecular tagging velocimetry）可视化近壁速度场，雷诺数（Reynolds number）最高达 $1.26\times10^6$；同时由压降测量独立确定壁面剪切应力（wall shear stress）。

Result: 正常流体速度形成稳健的对数剖面，表明尽管 He II 具有二流体（two-fluid）特性，壁律描述依然成立。但所得壁律明显非经典：有效卡门常数（Kármán constant）小于经典值的一半，且在所研究温度范围内几乎不变。该不变性出现在两组分相对份额显著变化的条件下，指示二者间存在强动力学耦合。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了经典湍流的普适律如何被量子流体动力学所改变，并为涉及 He II 输运的低温冷却系统（cryogenic cooling systems）的设计与运行提供指导。

Abstract: The logarithmic law of the wall is one of the most robust scaling laws of classical turbulence, yet whether it survives in a quantum fluid such as superfluid $^4$He (He II) remains unknown. At a solid wall, the viscous normal-fluid and inviscid superfluid components of He II obey fundamentally different boundary conditions, yet their motions can be coupled through quantized vortices. How these competing effects organize the near-wall flow remains an open question in quantum-fluid hydrodynamics. Here we visualize the near-wall velocity field in mechanically driven He II pipe flows at Reynolds numbers up to $1.26\times10^6$ using molecular tagging velocimetry and independently determine the wall shear stress from pressure-drop measurements. We find that the normal-fluid velocity develops a robust logarithmic profile, showing that a law-of-the-wall description persists despite the two-fluid nature of He II. Strikingly, however, the resulting wall law is distinctly nonclassical: the effective Kármán constant is less than half its classical value and remains nearly unchanged across the temperature range studied. This invariance, despite substantial changes in the relative fractions of the two components, indicates strong dynamical coupling between them. These results not only reveal how a universal law of classical turbulence is transformed by quantum hydrodynamics, but also provide valuable guidance for the design and operation of cryogenic cooling systems involving He II transport.

</details>


### [150] [Three-dimensional dopant distribution from a single, out-of-focus spectroscopic image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11053)
*Michael Deimetry,Timothy Petersen,Matthew Weyland,Scott Findlay*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用离焦扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）图像中掺杂原子呈现的圆盘半径与掺杂深度的关系，结合几何分析、统计检测与贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference），从单张离焦图像中重建掺杂原子的三维分布，并在Fe掺杂尖晶石上实现约2纳米的深度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度器件的持续小型化使确定掺杂原子（dopant）的三维分布愈发重要。现有方法在测量深度分布方面存在局限，因此需要一种简便且稳健的手段来表征掺杂原子的空间分布。

Method: 在离焦（out-of-focus）光谱扫描透射电子显微镜（spectroscopic scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM）图像中，掺杂原子表现为半径与掺杂深度相关的圆盘，据此通过简单的几何分析（geometric analysis）从单张离焦图像确定掺杂三维分布。同时借助Si掺杂石墨烯（Si-doped graphene）的数值实验，验证统计检测（statistical detection）与贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）方法，以量化置信度与不确定度并提升低剂量下的稳健性。

Result: 数值实验验证了统计检测与贝叶斯推断在量化置信度和不确定度、提升低剂量稳健性方面的有效性；Fe掺杂尖晶石（Fe-doped spinel）的原理验证实验数据实现了约2纳米以内的掺杂深度反演精度。

Conclusion: 基于离焦STEM图像中掺杂原子圆盘半径与深度的几何关系，可从单张图像确定掺杂原子的三维分布，并具备统计置信度与不确定度量化能力，实验上达到约2纳米的深度精度，为掺杂三维表征提供了概念验证。

Abstract: Increasing miniaturization of nanoscale devices makes determining the three-dimensional distribution of dopants increasingly important. In out-of-focus spectroscopic scanning transmission electron microscopy images, we show that dopant atoms appear as disks with radii relating to dopant depth, allowing the three-dimensional distribution of dopants to be determined from simple geometric analysis of a single out-of-focus image. Through numerical experiments on Si-doped graphene we validate statistical detection and Bayesian inference methods to quantify confidence and uncertainties while making the analysis more robust at lower dose. We present proof-of-concept experimental data from an Fe-doped spinel, retrieving dopant depth to within around 2 nanometres.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Sine beta is tail trivial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10849)
*Theodoros Assiotis,Kohei Suzuki*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明了正弦贝塔点过程（sine beta point process）对所有 beta>0 具有尾平凡性（tail triviality），并由此推出极值性、L^2 遍历性以及碰撞二分的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尾平凡性刻画点过程在无穷远处渐近独立的行为，是理解其刚性结构与唯一性的核心。已有工作（Assiotis 的唯一性定理、Dumaz–Malvy 的相关估计等）提供了相关工具，但尚缺对所有 beta>0 的统一证明。

Method: 证明的关键在于一个一致熵界（uniform entropy bound），该界利用了 Dereudre 等人（2021）的 DLR 方程，以及 Qu–Valko（2025）与 Assiotis–Najnudel（2026）给出的有界两点相关密度，从而证明尾事件是平移不变的；再结合 Assiotis（2026）的唯一性定理或由 Dumaz–Malvy（2026）相关估计导出的平移混合性得到结论。

Result: 得到三项应用：(a) 在无平稳性与有限能量条件的固定核吉布斯测度（fixed kernel Gibbs measures）全凸类中，正弦贝塔具有极值性（extremality）；(b) 对所有 beta>0，相关无穷粒子可逆扩散具有 L^2 遍历性（L^2-ergodicity）；(c) 对该扩散得到以 beta=1 为阈值的几乎必然碰撞/非碰撞二分（collision/noncollision dichotomy）。

Conclusion: 结论是正弦贝塔点过程对所有 beta>0 均尾平凡，且该性质可转化为极值性、遍历性与碰撞二分等多项结构性结果，凸显尾平凡性作为统一工具的作用。

Abstract: We prove that the sine beta point process is tail trivial for every beta >0. The key ingredient in the proof is a certain uniform entropy bound, that makes use of the DLR equations of Dereudre et al. (2021) and the bounded two-point correlation density obtained by Qu and Valko (2025) and Assiotis and Najnudel (2026), that allows us to show that tail events are translation invariant. Tail triviality then follows either from the uniqueness theorem of Assiotis (2026) or from translation mixing, which we derive from the correlation estimates of Dumaz and Malvy (2026). As consequences, we obtain the following applications: (a) extremality of sine beta in the full convex class of fixed kernel Gibbs measures without stationarity and finite-energy conditions; (b) L^2-ergodicity of the associated infinite-particle reversible diffusion for every beta>0; and (c) the almost-sure collision/noncollision dichotomy with threshold beta=1 for the diffusion.

</details>


### [152] [Exponential decay of fractional moments in the $H^{2|2}$ model on $\mathbb Z^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10982)
*Jinglin Wang,Xiaolin Zeng*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在 $\mathbb Z^2$ 上的 $H^{2|2}$ 模型（$H^{2|2}$ model）中证明：在零能量处，格林函数（Green function）的分数矩（fractional moment）随距离指数衰减，适用于任意常边权 $W>0$ 与 $0<s<1/2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为 $H^{2|2}$ 模型及其关联随机薛定谔算子（random Schrödinger operator）的无限体积格林函数，关注零能量处分数矩的衰减行为，以刻画局域化（localization）相关的性质。

Method: 核心技术工具是关于 $\mathbb E[e^{u_x/2}]$ 对边权（edge weights）导数的定量下界（quantitative lower bound），其中 $u$ 为钉扎有效场（pinned effective field）。

Result: 证明了零能量处 $\mathbb E[G(x,y)^s]$ 随 $x$ 与 $y$ 之间距离呈指数衰减，对所有常数边权 $W>0$ 及所有 $0<s<1/2$ 成立。

Conclusion: 结果给出了 $H^{2|2}$ 模型在零能量处格林函数分数矩的指数衰减，表明该性质在任意常数边权下普遍成立，其推导依赖于钉扎有效场期望导数的新下界。

Abstract: For the $H^{2|2}$ model on $\mathbb Z^2$, we prove that, at energy zero, fractional moment of Green functions, $\mathbb E[ G(x,y)^s]$ decays exponentially in the distance between $x$ and $y$, for every constant edge weight $W>0$ and every $0<s<1/2$. Here $G$ is the infinite-volume Green function at energy zero of the associated random Schrödinger operator. The main ingredient is a quantitative lower bound on the derivative of $\mathbb E[e^{u_x/2}]$ with respect to the edge weights, where $u$ is the pinned effective field.

</details>


### [153] [On the asymptotic behavior of the number variance of random points on the unit torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11687)
*Christoph Aistleitner,Siegfried Hörmann,Maryna Manskova*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文以单位环面上的独立同分布（i.i.d.）随机点列为对象，在高精度下计算了数方差（number variance）$V_N(s)$ 的分布，并确定了随机序列典型实现下“泊松型（Poissonian）”渐近行为 $V_N(s)\sim Ns$ 失效的精确阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数方差是刻画确定性序列“伪随机”行为的重要统计量，涵盖从全局到局部的各个尺度；然而文献中似乎缺乏对真正随机情形（i.i.d. 随机点）下数方差分布及几乎必然渐近行为的精确描述，即便在单位环面均匀分布这一相对简单的设定中也是如此。

Method: 关键的技术工具来自 Komlós–Major–Tusnády 定理（Komlós–Major–Tusnády theorem）与 Karhunen–Loève 分解（Karhunen-Loève decomposition），并据此在整个较宽的参数 $s$ 范围内高精度地推导数方差的分布。

Result: 论文给出了数方差在较宽参数 $s$ 范围内的高精度分布，并确定了刻画“泊松型”渐近 $V_N(s)\sim Ns$ 在典型随机序列实现下发生破缺的精确阈值（以 $s$ 表示）。

Conclusion: 研究补充了文献中对真正随机情形下数方差分布与几乎必然渐近行为的精确描述，明确了泊松型渐近的适用范围及其在典型随机实现下的失效阈值。

Abstract: The study of the distribution of real sequences in the unit torus has a long and rich history. There are many notions which describe the ``pseudo-random'' behavior of a given deterministic sequence (often of arithmetic origin), such as equidistribution (which describes the distribution on a ``global scale'') or the pair correlation (which is a ``local-scale'' statistics). Another popular and important statistics, which can be applied to all ranges from global via intermediate to local, is the number variance $V_N(s)$, which describes the mean-square fluctuation of the number of elements of an initial segment of the sequence in an interval of length $s$, when moving this interval around the torus. In the terminology of quantum chaology, where such statistics are used to describe the distribution of the energy levels of a system, pseudo-random behavior is called Poissonian behavior. Since the number variance is an important indicator to qualify pseudo-random behavior, it is quite surprising that the literature does not seem to contain a precise description of the distribution and of the almost sure asymptotic behavior of the number variance in the truly random case (i.i.d.\ random points), even in the comparatively simple setup of uniformly distributed points on the unit torus. In the present paper we calculate the distribution of the number variance with high precision throughout a wide range of the parameter $s$, and determine the precise threshold (in terms of $s$) where the ``Poissonian'' asymptotics $V_N(s) \sim N s$ breaks for a typical realization of a random sequence. Key technical input comes from the Komlós--Major--Tusnády theorem and the Karhunen-Loève decomposition.

</details>


### [154] [Strong RSW estimates for the FK-Ising model on s-embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11990)
*Dmitry Chelkak,Yanqing Wei*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文将 Duminil-Copin、Manolescu 与 Tassion 的“尺度归纳（induction over scales）”方案推广至 s-嵌入（s-embeddings）上的 FK-Ising 模型，在一致有界几何（uniformly bounded geometry）假设下证明了强 RSW（strong RSW）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 RSW 类结果多针对规则网格上的 Ising 或 FK-Ising 模型，而 s-嵌入为更一般的离散曲面提供了统一框架。作者希望在更一般的几何设置下建立强 RSW，从而覆盖包括近临界（near-critical）Ising 模型在内的多种情形。

Method: 核心方法是把 Duminil-Copin、Manolescu 与 Tassion 的尺度归纳方案适配到 s-嵌入上的 FK-Ising 模型，并在切向四边形（tangential quads）满足一致有界几何假设（所有边长与 1 可比、所有角度有下界）的条件下进行论证。

Result: 证明在不利边界条件（unfavorable boundary conditions）下，粗糙拓扑四边形（rough topological quadrilaterals）存在穿越，即强 RSW 成立。该结果带来若干标准推论，包括臂事件（arm events）的准可乘性（quasi-multiplicativity）与簇（clusters）的分形性质（fractal properties）。

Conclusion: 所证结果可应用于多种设定，例如通过选取合适的 s-嵌入，可覆盖低于关联长度尺度（correlation length scale）的规则网格上近临界 Ising 模型。这表明 s-嵌入框架是推广 RSW 型结论的有效工具。

Abstract: We adapt the 'induction over scales' scheme of Duminil-Copin, Manolescu, and Tassion to the FK-Ising model on s-embeddings. Namely, we prove the existence of crossings of rough topological quadrilaterals with unfavorable boundary conditions, known as 'strong' RSW, under the 'uniformly bounded geometry' assumption (all edge lengths are comparable to 1 and all angles are bounded from below) on the tangential quads that form an s-embedding. This result has a number of standard consequences like quasi-multiplicativity of arm events and fractal properties of the clusters. It can be applied in a variety of setups including near-critical Ising models on regular grids below the correlation length scale by considering appropriate s-embedding thereof.

</details>


### [155] [Anomalous scaling limit of a Brownian particle in a log-correlated potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12335)
*Scott Armstrong,Ahmed Bou-Rabee,Tuomo Kuusi*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 研究 $\mathbb{R}^d$（$d\geq2$）中梯度由对数关联高斯势（log-correlated Gaussian potential）给出的布朗粒子扩散，在弱无序（weak disorder）下证明其收敛到标度极限（scaling limit），该极限律关于布朗运动奇异，不变测度（invariant measure）为高斯乘法混沌（Gaussian multiplicative chaos）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未显式陈述研究动机，仅说明所研究的对象为具对数关联高斯势梯度的布朗粒子及其弱无序行为。

Method: 采用重整化群归纳法（renormalization group induction）：在每一尺度上，椭圆算子（elliptic operator）被一个拉普拉斯算子（Laplacian）良好近似，其有效扩散率（effective diffusivity）按尺度的幂次衰减。

Result: 在弱无序条件下证明了向标度极限的收敛，该极限律关于布朗运动（Brownian motion）奇异，且不变测度为高斯乘法混沌；并精确计算了二维情形下有效扩散率的衰减指数（exponent）。

Conclusion: 对数关联随机环境下布朗粒子的弱无序标度极限由高斯乘法混沌刻画，且重整化群归纳中的有效扩散率幂律指数在二维可精确求出。

Abstract: We study a Brownian particle in $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d\geq2$, with drift given by the gradient of a log-correlated Gaussian potential. At weak disorder, we prove convergence to a scaling limit whose law is singular with respect to Brownian motion and whose invariant measure is given by Gaussian multiplicative chaos. The proof uses a renormalization group induction: at each scale, the elliptic operator is well approximated by a Laplacian with effective diffusivity decaying as a power of scale. We compute this exponent exactly in dimension two.

</details>


### [156] [Directed fractal percolation and non-Lipschitz variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12366)
*Shirshendu Ganguly,Victor Ginsburg,Kaihao Jing*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 在分形背景（以Mandelbrot分形渗流为玩具模型）中，本文量化分析了由高斯自由场驱动的最后通过渗流（LPP）问题，证明通过时间与线性增长之间存在几乎多项式的差距，并引入"不可求长定向渗流"这一新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机i.i.d.无序中具快速衰减尾部的无向与有向几何模型预期行为相似并属于KPZ普适类，但分形噪声背景预期显著不同；Liouville量子引力是其典型例子。为理解由高斯自由场驱动的LPP，作者以Mandelbrot分形渗流作为刻画关键特征的玩具模型展开研究。

Method: 发展多尺度框架（multi-scale framework），对分形渗流中的最后通过渗流（LPP）进行量化分析，并考察定向路径的Lipschitz性质对通过时间的限制。

Result: 证明通过时间（passage time）相对线性增长存在几乎多项式的差距（almost polynomial gap），其障碍在于定向路径的Lipschitz性质；并证明可构造穿越开点的定向路径，只要其在空间方向上以指数速度移动。

Conclusion: 受[Cha96]关于分形渗流连通子集Hausdorff维数严格大于1的结果启发，作者引入并命名"不可求长定向渗流"（unrectifiable directed percolation）模型，表明在分形渗流中定向路径需指数快速移动方能穿越开点，推进了对高斯自由场驱动LPP的理解。

Abstract: While models of undirected and directed geometry in random i.i.d. disorder with rapidly decaying tails are expected to behave similarly and exhibit features of the Kardar--Parisi--Zhang universality class, the scenario when the background noise is fractal is expected to be significantly different. A canonical example of the latter is Liouville Quantum Gravity, where the planar Gaussian free field forms the fractal background. A toy model capturing some of the essential features is given by Mandelbrot's fractal percolation, a random Cantor set. In the simplest setting, dyadic cubes of different scales are retained independently with probability $p$ (a parameter of the model), and the intersection of all retained cubes forms the set of open sites. Connectivity properties of such sets have been intensely studied. Across [CCD88, Cha95], a surprising result was proven. Namely, while there is a phase transition for connectivity of the fractal percolation set (like in usual bond percolation), directed percolation never occurs. Results of a similar spirit have been obtained in geometric measure theory as well. As a step towards understanding last passage percolation (LPP) driven by the Gaussian free field (initiated in [GGN24]), we study the problem of quantifying LPP in fractal percolation. Developing a multi-scale framework, we show an almost polynomial gap from linear growth of the passage time. The obstruction to high LPP values is the Lipschitz nature of directed paths. Along these lines, [Cha96] showed that any connected subset of fractal percolation must have Hausdorff dimension strictly larger than one. We show that one can construct directed paths passing through open sites in fractal percolation provided they move exponentially fast in the spatial direction---a model we introduce and term as unrectifiable directed percolation.

</details>


### [157] [On Large Drift Enhancement for the Relaxation Time of Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12425)
*Alain-Sol Sznitman,Klaus Widmayer*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在一般的解析框架下证明了一个一般性结果，刻画在存在大不可压缩漂移型项时平均量之 $L^2$-弛豫时间的渐近消失现象，核心工具为奇异值间隙方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画大不可压缩漂移型项（incompressible drift-like term）对平均量弛豫行为的影响，其解析框架类比于 Constantin-Kiselev-Ryzhik-Zlatos (2008) 与 Coti Zelati-Delgadino-Elgindi (2020) 的既有设定。

Method: 方法上采用 Chatterjee (2025) 提出的奇异值间隙方法（singular value gap method），并结合 Eberle-Lörler (2026) 对该方法的进一步发展，在较宽的解析设定中建立一般性证明。

Result: 在广泛的解析设定下，得到了一个刻画平均量 $L^2$-弛豫时间（$L^2$-relaxation time）渐近消失的一般性结果；奇异值间隙方法在其中起到关键作用。

Conclusion: 该结果将漂移型项导致弛豫时间渐近消失的现象推广至一般解析框架，并显示奇异值间隙方法在此类问题中的适用性。

Abstract: In this note we prove in a broad analytic set-up a general result characterizing the asymptotic vanishing of the $L^2$-relaxation time of averages in the presence of a large incompressible drift-like term. The broad analytic set-up is analogous to that of Constantin-Kiselev-Ryzhik-Zlatos '08 or of Coti Zelati-Delgadino-Elgindi '20. A crucial role is played by the singular value gap method of Chatterjee '25, further developed by Eberle-Lörler '26.

</details>


### [158] [Explicit upper bounds on the threshold of one-dimensional long-range percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11872)
*Guy Amit*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究一维格点上的键渗流（bond percolation），其中相距 r 的两点以概率 p_r = C/r^{1+σ} 相连（0<σ<1）。作者利用具有重尾（heavy-tailed，Sibuya）步长分布的随机单调路径族与二阶矩方法，给出了临界阈值 C_c(σ) 的显式上界，并在 σ→0 时得到渐近形式 C_c ≤ σ + O(σ²)，同时给出定向渗流（oriented percolation）阈值的渐近确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型存在无限簇的临界值 C_c(σ) 并无精确解，已知结果仅有 Schulman 的下界 C_c ≥ 1/[2ζ(1+σ)] 与数值估计。本文旨在填补上界缺失的空白，给出显式且解析可用的上界以界定 C_c 的取值区间。

Method: 将二阶矩方法（second-moment argument）应用于一族随机单调路径，其步长服从重尾的 Sibuya 分布；两条此类路径仅相交有限次，其重叠部分通过更新理论（renewal theory）精确计算。进一步采用粗粒化（coarse-grained）版本，以格点块替代单个格点。

Result: 得到封闭形式的上界：当 σ→0 时 C_c ≤ σ + O(σ²)，且当 σ < 0.667 时该界小于 1。由于路径单调，该界对定向渗流同样成立，并渐近确定定向阈值：σ→0 时 C_c^→ ζ(1+σ) → 1。粗粒化版本给出对所有 σ<1 均成立的显式界 C_c < 1。结果与已知数值 C_c 进行了比较。

Conclusion: 本文为一维长程键渗流及定向渗流的临界阈值提供了显式上界，弥补了此前仅有下界与数值估计的不足，并给出 σ→0 极限下的渐近阈值确定。所提二阶矩与更新理论框架结合粗粒化，可系统性地导出对全部 σ<1 有效的界。

Abstract: We study bond percolation on the one-dimensional lattice in which two sites at distance $r$ are connected with probability $p_r=C/r^{1+σ}$, where $0 < σ< 1$. An infinite cluster exists if $C$ exceeds a critical value $C_c(σ)$, which is not known exactly: the available results are the lower bound of Schulman, $C_c\ge1/[2ζ(1+σ)]$, and numerical estimates. Here we prove explicit upper bounds. We apply a second-moment argument to a family of random monotone paths whose steps have a heavy-tailed (Sibuya) distribution. Two such paths meet only a finite number of times, and their overlap is computed exactly by renewal theory. The result is a closed-form bound, which gives $C_c\leσ+O(σ^2)$ as $σ\to0$ and is below $1$ for $σ< 0.667$. Since the paths are monotone, the bound holds also for oriented percolation, and it determines the oriented threshold asymptotically: $C_c^{\rightarrow}ζ(1+σ)\to1$ as $σ\to0$. A coarse-grained version of the argument, with blocks of sites in place of sites, gives an explicit bound $C_c < 1$ for every $σ< 1$. We compare the bounds with the known numerical values of $C_c$.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

### [159] [Magnetic localization and spectral asymmetry of a charged particle at a screw dislocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10555)
*Claudio Furtado,Omar Mustafa,Hassan Hassanabadi,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究均匀磁场中位于螺旋位错（spiral/screw dislocation）外部的带电粒子，发现局部欧氏但全局螺旋识别的几何通过守恒量耦合影响动力学，得到可精确求解的量子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注螺旋位错（screw dislocation）这一拓扑缺陷对带电粒子量子行为的影响，旨在揭示几何全局性质（而非局部曲率）如何通过守恒量耦合进入动力学。

Method: 在均匀磁场（uniform magnetic field）中处理螺旋位错外部的带电粒子；利用守恒量组合 \(\mathcal{J}=\ell-\beta k\) 消去循环坐标（cyclic coordinates），将径向运动化为含反平方项与谐振约束项的约化量子问题并精确求解。

Result: 当 \(q\mathcal{B}_0\neq0\) 时谱为等间距，存在取向依赖的能量移动；径向概率密度依赖 \(\mathcal{J}\)，且螺旋修正量子数取相反值时空间分布相同但能量不同。径向尺度满足 \(\sqrt{\langle r^2\rangle_n}\propto |q\mathcal{B}_0|^{-1/2}\)，由磁长度（magnetic length）决定局域尺度；当 \(q\mathcal{B}_0=0\) 时约束项消失，问题退化为无标度的反平方系统。

Conclusion: 螺旋参数通过守恒轴向与方位角量的全局耦合进入动力学，而磁场提供径向约束，其取向决定谱的不对称性（spectral asymmetry）。

Abstract: We study a charged particle moving outside a spiral (screw) dislocation in a uniform magnetic field. Although the geometry is locally Euclidean, its global screw identification couples the conserved axial and azimuthal quantities through the combination \(\mathcal{J}=\ell-βk\) After eliminating the cyclic coordinates, the radial motion is governed by an inverse-square term determined by $\mathcal{J}$ and a harmonic confining term generated by the magnetic field. The corresponding reduced quantum problem is exactly solvable for $q\mathcal{B}_0\neq0$, yielding an equally spaced spectrum with an orientation-dependent energy shift. The radial probability density depends on $\mathcal{J}$, and opposite values of the screw-modified quantum number have identical spatial profiles but different energies for a fixed magnetic-field orientation. The radial size scales as \(\sqrt{\langle r^2\rangle_n}\propto |q\mathcal{B}_0|^{-1/2}\), with the magnetic length setting the localization scale. At $q\mathcal{B}_0=0$, the confining term vanishes, and the problem reduces to a scale-free inverse-square system. Thus, the screw parameter enters the dynamics through the global coupling of the conserved axial and azimuthal quantities, whereas the magnetic field provides radial confinement and its orientation determines the spectral asymmetry.

</details>


### [160] [Millisecond phonon lifetimes in piezoelectric nanomechanical resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10700)
*Oliver A. Hitchcock,Matthew P. Maksymowych,Kaveh Pezeshki,Takuma Makihara,Erik Szakiel,Chou-Wei Kiang,Daniel Rugar,Amir H. Safavi-Naeini*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过化学修复离子铣削表面并结合荫罩金属化工艺，制备出铌酸锂声子晶体谐振器，在3.6 GHz与毫开尔文温度下推断内部声子寿命达21毫秒、品质因子超4×10^8，证明此前微秒级寿命源于外部耦合而非材料本征损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千兆赫兹压电纳米机械谐振器（piezoelectric nanomechanical resonator）是量子信息处理与精密传感的潜在平台，但声子寿命（phonon lifetime）长期局限于微秒量级，通常被归因于压电材料更高的本征损耗（intrinsic loss）。本文旨在检验这一损耗限制是否为根本性限制。

Method: 采用受近期铌酸锂（lithium niobate）缺陷介导损耗（defect-mediated loss）研究启发的制备流程，将离子铣削（ion-milled）表面的化学修复（chemical restoration）与荫罩金属化（shadow-mask metallization）工艺相结合，制备铌酸锂声子晶体谐振器（phononic crystal resonator）；并通过系统改变电极与谐振器间距，分析寿命的来源。

Result: 在3.6 GHz、毫开尔文（millikelvin）温度下，推断内部声子寿命最高达21毫秒，对应品质因子（quality factor）超过4×10^8（f·Q_i > 10^18）。随电极—谐振器间距的系统变化表明，实测寿命由器件与测量电路之间的外部耦合（external coupling）决定，而非材料损耗。

Conclusion: 在去除制备诱导的表面损伤（surface damage）后，铌酸锂可获得与最优机械材料相媲美的本征品质，同时保持其本征压电平台（intrinsically piezoelectric platform）与强机电耦合（electromechanical coupling）的特性，说明既有寿命限制并非根本性。

Abstract: Piezoelectric nanomechanical resonators operating at gigahertz frequencies are a promising platform for quantum information processing and precision sensing, but phonon lifetimes have been limited to the microsecond range, widely attributed to the higher intrinsic loss of piezoelectric materials. Here we show that this limitation is not fundamental. Using a fabrication process informed by recent studies of defect-mediated loss in lithium niobate, we combine chemical restoration of ion-milled surfaces with a shadow-mask metallization process to produce lithium niobate phononic crystal resonators with inferred internal phonon lifetimes of up to 21 milliseconds at 3.6 gigahertz, corresponding to quality factors exceeding $4\times10^8$ ($f\cdot Q_i > 10^{18}$) at millikelvin temperatures. By systematically varying the electrode-to-resonator spacing, we demonstrate that the measured lifetime is set by external coupling between the device and the measurement circuit rather than material loss. This establishes that lithium niobate, once fabrication-induced surface damage is removed, achieves an intrinsic quality comparable to the best mechanical materials while remaining an intrinsically piezoelectric platform with strong electromechanical coupling.

</details>


### [161] [Hydrodynamic Anomalous Hall Effect in a Two-Dimensional Dirac Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10733)
*A. Levchenko,D. A. Pesin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在二维有质量狄拉克费米液体中，电子-电子碰撞通过贝里曲率（Berry curvature）产生的双粒子坐标移动可对反常霍尔电流（anomalous Hall current）产生贡献；该贡献在领先玻恩阶（leading Born order）与相互作用强度无关，且其频率与无序极限不可交换，dc极限下随动量弛豫率反比增长，从而在无序强度趋于零时仍保留动量弛豫机制的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超洁净导体中电子-电子碰撞可使输运进入流体力学（hydrodynamic）区间，而贝里曲率带中的此类碰撞还能产生双粒子坐标移动并影响反常霍尔电流，这一机制此前缺乏系统的动力学理论刻画。摘要旨在为弱接触相互作用与弱短程无序的二维有质量狄拉克费米液体建立该贡献的动力学理论。

Method: 采用动力学理论（kinetic theory）方法，研究对象为二维有质量狄拉克费米液体（two-dimensional massive Dirac Fermi liquid），设定弱接触相互作用与弱短程无序。计算在领先玻恩阶进行，并分析电子-电子碰撞积分（collision integral）的动量零模（momentum zero mode）所导致的频依赖性，考察dc极限与洁净极限的次序。

Result: 所得响应在领先玻恩阶与相互作用强度无关；其对反常霍尔电导率（anomalous Hall conductivity）的贡献与内禀反常霍尔电导率的热修正相当。由于动量零模存在，反常霍尔响应呈现非平凡的频率依赖：频率趋于零（dc）与无序趋于零的极限不可交换。在dc极限下，动量对输运场的响应随动量弛豫率（momentum relaxation rate）反比增长，使任意弱的能量依赖杂质散射通过与守恒动量及电子-电子散射所弛豫模式的耦合产生有限修正；若先取洁净极限再取零频极限，该修正消失。

Conclusion: 相互作用诱导的霍尔响应（interaction-induced Hall response）即使在总无序强度趋于零时仍保留关于动量弛豫机制的信息，体现了dc极限与洁净极限次序不可交换的物理后果。

Abstract: In ultraclean conductors, electron-electron collisions can make transport hydrodynamic. In a Berry-curved band, these collisions can also produce two-particle coordinate shifts, which contribute to the anomalous Hall current in the presence of a transport electric field. We develop a kinetic theory of this contribution for a two-dimensional massive Dirac Fermi liquid with weak contact interactions and weak short-range disorder. The computed response is independent of interaction strength at leading Born order. Its contribution to the anomalous Hall conductivity is comparable to the thermal correction to the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity. The existence of the momentum zero mode of the electron-electron collision integral leads to a nontrivial frequency dependence of the anomalous Hall response considered in this work. In particular, the limits of vanishing frequency (dc) and vanishing disorder do not commute. In the dc limit, the momentum response to the transport field grows inversely with the momentum relaxation rate. This allows arbitrarily weak, energy-dependent impurity scattering to produce a finite correction by coupling conserved momentum to the modes relaxed by electron-electron scattering. This correction disappears when the clean limit is taken before the zero-frequency limit. As a result, the interaction-induced Hall response retains information about the mechanism of momentum relaxation even as the overall disorder strength tends to zero.

</details>


### [162] [EPR as a probe for the mechanisms of chirality-induced spin selectivity in photoinduced charge transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10839)
*Arianna Cantarella,D. K. Andrea Phan Huu,Paolo Santini,Michael R. Wasielewski,Alessandro Chiesa,Stefano Carretta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过计算不同微观模型所对应自由基对态（radical pair states）的电子顺磁共振谱（electron paramagnetic resonance spectra, EPR），为手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity, CISS）模型提供与实验直接比较的验证途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对电荷转移中手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）已提出多种模型，但鲜有与实验直接对比，而此类对比对模型验证及该领域后续进展至关重要。

Method: 研究与不同微观模型相关的一大类自由基对态（radical pair states），计算其相应的电子顺磁共振谱（electron paramagnetic resonance spectra）。

Result: 所计算的谱学结果揭示了不同自由基对态的独特特征（peculiar signatures），并可用于评估其可实验探测性（experimental accessibility）。

Conclusion: 该途径有助于识别验证既有微观模型所需的实验条件，从而推动CISS模型的实验检验与领域发展。

Abstract: Several models for chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) in charge transfer have been proposed, but rarely compared directly with experiments. This step is essential for their validation and future progress of the field. Here we investigate a broad class of radical pair states associated with different microscopic models by computing the related electron-paramagnetic resonance spectra. This allows us to evidence peculiar signatures of different radical pair states, to study their experimental accessibility and identify conditions to verify proposed microscopic models.

</details>


### [163] [Reading Altermagnetic Domains with Photon Drag](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10970)
*Sayed Ali Akbar Ghorashi,Andrew M. Rappe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究交错磁体（altermagnet）的光子拖曳（photon-drag）响应，发现其角度依赖同时编码磁序对称性与Néel畴信息，并给出全光学磁读取路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限光子动量可在中心对称空间群中产生二阶光电流（second-order photocurrents），而传统体光伏效应（bulk photovoltaic effect）在此被禁止；交错磁体兼具中心对称磁序与自旋劈裂，其光子拖曳响应的对称性特征尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 通过对称性分析与微观机制分解，计算交错磁体的光子拖曳电流，并按角度谐波（angular harmonics）分离畴偶（domain-even）与畴奇（domain-odd）部分；区分线偏振与圆偏振下的位移（shift）、注入（injection）、费米面（Fermi-surface）和接触主值（contact principal-value）等贡献。

Result: 对四方d波交错磁体，畴偶与畴奇电流分别出现在4n与4n+2角谐波中；单畴上旋转90°的两次测量可分离畴奇电流。线偏振下仅位移项为畴奇，圆偏振下畴奇通道转为注入、费米面与接触主值项。镜像对称固定各谐波角相位，可区分d_{x^2-y^2}、d_{xy}与g波序。常规共线反铁磁体无畴奇光子拖曳电流，四方铁磁体中两部分共享同一谐波。自旋轨道耦合（SOC）虽非交错磁自旋劈裂所必需，却是观测畴奇电荷电流的关键。

Conclusion: 交错磁性在光子拖曳中呈现独特对称性指纹，为交错磁体的全光学磁读取与磁畴探测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Finite photon momentum allows second-order photocurrents in centrosymmetric space groups, where the conventional bulk photovoltaic effect is forbidden. Here we study the photon-drag response of altermagnets and show that its angular dependence reveals both the symmetry of the magnetic order and the Néel domain of the sample. For example, when a crystal rotation exchanges the two domains, the domain-even and domain-odd parts of the current appear in different angular harmonics, $4n$ and $4n+2$ for tetragonal $d$-wave altermagnets, so that two measurements rotated by $90^\circ$ on a single domain isolate the domain-odd current. We also find that these microscopic contributions exhibit opposite domain parities depending on light polarization. Under linear light, only the shift term is domain odd, while under circular light, the domain-odd channel switches to the injection, Fermi-surface, and contact principal-value terms. Mirror symmetries fix the angular phase of each harmonic and distinguish $d_{x^2-y^2}$, $d_{xy}$, and $g$-wave order. In contrast, conventional collinear antiferromagnets show no domain-odd photon-drag current, and in tetragonal ferromagnets the two parts share the same harmonics. Although the altermagnetic spin splitting does not require spin-orbit coupling (SOC), SOC is essential to observe the domain-odd charge current. Our work uncovers distinct signatures of altermagnetism in photon drag and provides a route to all optical magnetic readouts of altermagnets.

</details>


### [164] [Cooperative STT and SOT switching in perpendicular magnetic tunnel junctions: Role of the pulse-end magnetization state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11191)
*Zeyi Zhu,Ding Ma,Kai Zhang,Mingwei Wu,Yi Huang,Jiarui Niu,Shuai Zhang,Mengdi Yin,Ka-San Mak,Lina Chen,Bo Liu,Tiejun Zhou,Yongbing Xu,Ronghua Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种无外场（field-free）的自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）与自旋转移矩（spin-transfer torque, STT）协同翻转方案，通过微磁模拟与解析宏观自旋模型揭示其非线性翻转边界及脉冲末端态对翻转可靠性的决定作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SOT 翻转在脉冲结束时磁化仍处于近面内区域，最终态对脉冲后弛豫（post-pulse relaxation）高度敏感且易发生回跳（back-switching）。研究旨在通过引入垂直 STT 偏置轨迹，实现无需外场的确定性翻转。

Method: 采用微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）在 J_STT–J_SOT 参数空间中刻画翻转行为，并建立基于电流诱导平衡态稳定性分析的解析宏观自旋模型（analytical macrospin model），分析电流密度、吉尔伯特阻尼 α 与单轴各向异性 K_u 对临界边界的影响。

Result: 模拟揭示出非线性翻转边界，源于两种力矩的不同动力学角色：SOT 主导激发与动力学分界线（dynamical separatrix）的跨越，STT 控制末端轨迹与最终态选择。解析模型复现了临界边界随电流密度、α 与 K_u 的变化趋势，并区分出动态反阻尼（dynamic anti-damping）与静态失稳（static instability）分支。

Conclusion: 脉冲时长、阻尼、各向异性及 STT–SOT 平衡的系统分析表明，可靠超快翻转既需在脉冲结束前充分激发以跨越分界线，也需精确控制脉冲末端磁化态以抑制脉冲后弛豫。脉冲末端态而非瞬时力矩幅度，是决定耦合 STT–SOT 系统翻转速度与可靠性的决定性因素。

Abstract: Spin--orbit torque MRAM (SOT-MRAM) is a leading candidate for next-generation nonvolatile memory, offering high speed, endurance, and architectural compatibility. However, in conventional SOT switching, the magnetization remains near the in-plane region at pulse termination, making the final state highly sensitive to post-pulse relaxation dynamics and prone to back-switching. To overcome this, we propose a field-free scheme in which the transverse SOT drives large-angle precessional excitation while the perpendicular spin-transfer torque (STT) biases the trajectory toward the reversed $-z$ state. Micromagnetic simulations reveal a nonlinear switching boundary in the $J_{\mathrm{STT}}$--$J_{\mathrm{SOT}}$ parameter space, originating from the distinct dynamical roles of the two torques: SOT primarily governs the excitation and crossing of the dynamical separatrix, whereas STT controls the terminal trajectory and final-state selection. An analytical macrospin model, based on the stability analysis of the current-induced equilibrium, reproduces the critical-boundary trends as functions of current density, Gilbert damping $α$, and uniaxial anisotropy $K_\mathrm{u}$, and distinguishes dynamic anti-damping and static instability branches. Systematic analyses of pulse duration, damping, anisotropy, and the STT--SOT balance further demonstrate that reliable ultrafast switching requires not only sufficient excitation to cross the separatrix before pulse termination, but also precise control of the pulse-end magnetization state to minimize post-pulse relaxation. These results establish that the pulse-end state, rather than the instantaneous torque amplitude, is the decisive factor governing switching speed and reliability in coupled STT--SOT systems.

</details>


### [165] [Enhancement of the Topological Hall Effect through Engineering the Skyrmion Size and Shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11244)
*Kazuma Murata,Ryunosuke Terasawa,Hiroaki Ishizuka*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在弱耦合（weak-coupling）极限下，理论研究了稀疏分布斯格明子（skyrmion）所致的拓扑霍尔效应（topological Hall effect），并揭示其大小与形状对霍尔效应的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑霍尔效应是探测磁结构（magnetic texture）的重要电学手段，但其幅度偏小限制了实际应用，因此需要理解斯格明子的尺寸与形状如何影响该效应。

Method: 采用基于角动量本征态（angular-momentum eigenstates）的形式化方法，在可接受的计算时间内对稀疏斯格明子体系进行系统分析。

Result: 在电子平均自由程（mean-free path）与斯格明子尺寸相当或更大的洁净材料中，霍尔效应强烈依赖斯格明子的尺寸与形状；当 $k_F\lambda\sim 1$ 时效应最大，且其幅度可通过斯格明子的畴壁宽度（domain-wall width）调控。

Conclusion: 研究为斯格明子尺寸与形状对输运性质（transport properties）的影响提供了基础，并表明拓扑霍尔效应可通过工程化这些性质进行调控。

Abstract: The topological Hall effect of skyrmions is a promising electrical probe of magnetic textures, but its small magnitude limits applications. In this work, we theoretically investigate the topological Hall effect induced by sparsely distributed skyrmions in the weak-coupling limit, focusing on how the size and shape of the skyrmion affect the Hall effect. To this end, we used a formalism based on angular-momentum eigenstates, which enables systematic analysis within reasonable computation time. We found that, in clean materials where the electron mean-free path is comparable to or larger than the skyrmion size, the Hall effect depends considerably on the size and shape of the skyrmion. In particular, the Hall effect is largest when $k_Fλ\sim 1$, and its magnitude can be tuned by the domain-wall width of skyrmions. These findings provide a basis for investigating how the shape and size of skyrmions affect transport properties and demonstrate that the topological Hall effect is controllable by engineering these properties.

</details>


### [166] [Displacement-Field-Induced Ionization of Defect States in High-Quality hBN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11297)
*Ilia Begichev,Daria Belotcerkovtceva,Iakov Reznikov,Sergey Slizovsky,Weijie Lu,Nansi Zhou,Ian Babich,Makars Siskins,Kostya S. Novoselov,Vladimir I. Falko,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Alexey I. Berdyugin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究以六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）封装的单层石墨烯（monolayer graphene）作为电荷传感器，揭示 displacement field 会电离 hBN 带隙中的缺陷态，导致额外的电子掺杂并加剧载流子密度不均匀性，打破 hBN 在电子学上惰性的常规假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hBN 封装被广泛用于制备超洁净范德华（van der Waals, vdW）异质结，且此类器件常在强面外 displacement field 下工作；但通常假设 hBN 保持电子惰性，忽略了即使高质量 hBN 晶体中也存在的缺陷态。该研究旨在检验这一假设。

Method: 采用封装于 hBN 晶体之间的单层石墨烯作为灵敏的电荷传感器（charge sensor），通过施加并增大面外 displacement field，探测 hBN 缺陷态对该场的响应，并统计不同 hBN 晶体中的缺陷体密度。

Result: 增大 displacement field 引起石墨烯与 hBN 之间显著的电荷转移，即使在中等的 displacement field（约 0.5 V nm⁻¹）下也产生 10–20×10⁹ cm⁻² 的额外电子掺杂，同时增加石墨烯层的载流子密度不均匀性（carrier-density inhomogeneity）。两种效应均与 hBN 带隙内缺陷态的离子化（ionization）一致，其体密度约为 1–7×10⁻⁵ nm⁻³，并随不同 hBN 晶体而变化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，hBN 中由 displacement field 诱导的缺陷离子化会改变范德华异质结的电子响应，对器件设计及实验结果的解读具有直接影响。

Abstract: Encapsulation of two-dimensional (2D) materials with hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) is widely used to fabricate ultraclean van der Waals (vdW) heterostructures for optoelectronic and quantum transport studies. These devices are often operated under strong out-of-plane displacement fields that tune the electronic properties of the active 2D material, while the surrounding hBN is typically assumed to remain electronically inert. This assumption, however, neglects defect states that are present even in high-quality hBN crystals. Here, we probe their response to the applied displacement field using monolayer graphene encapsulated between hBN crystals as a sensitive charge sensor. Increasing the displacement field induces notable charge transfer between graphene and hBN, producing additional electron doping of 10-20x10^9 cm^(-2) even at the moderate displacement field (~0.5 V nm^(-1)), while simultaneously increasing carrier-density inhomogeneity of the graphene layer. Both effects are consistent with ionization of defect states within the hBN bandgap, with the bulk density ~1-7x10^(-5) nm^(-3) varying across different hBN crystals. Our results show that displacement-field-induced defect ionization in hBN can modify the electronic response of vdW heterostructures, with direct implications for device design and interpretation of experiments.

</details>


### [167] [Magnonic cavity-enhanced quantum magnetometry with nitrogen-vacancy centre ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11350)
*Nikhil Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种芯片级（chip-scale）氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）色心磁强计，以一维 YIG/CoFeB 磁振子晶体（magnonic crystal）中的单个结构缺陷构成无源磁振子腔（magnonic cavity），并由集成永磁体提供偏置场。微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulation）给出位于 2.5–3.0 GHz 带隙内 2.910 GHz 的缺陷局域自旋波模式，配合 Tavis-Cummings/Lindblad 模型预测灵敏度可达 72 pT/√Hz（优化条件下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 NV 色心集合体（NV centre ensemble）磁强计虽可在室温工作，但其灵敏度通常仅从单一共振提取，且依赖外部微波（microwave）与偏置场（bias-field）硬件，限制了芯片级集成。该研究旨在通过磁振子晶体中的缺陷腔实现微波与偏置场的片上集成，并利用多条共振边沿提升灵敏度。

Method: 采用微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulation）计算一维 YIG/CoFeB 磁振子晶体的能带结构与缺陷局域模式，并与独立的平面波（plane-wave）计算对照。以 Tavis-Cummings/Lindblad 主方程（master equation）描述微波经该模式耦合至 NV 集合体的过程，预测光学探测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance, ODMR）谱与外差（homodyne）响应；结果与闭式稳态解及近似解析 ODMR 理论相互验证。

Result: 模拟显示缺陷局域自旋波模式位于 2.910 GHz，处于 2.5–3.0 GHz 带隙内，距 NV 零场分裂（zero-field splitting）40 MHz，能带结构与平面波计算吻合。主方程解与闭式外差稳态解高度一致（R² > 0.9999），与近似解析 ODMR 理论峰值位置偏差在 0.012 MHz 以内。临界耦合读出（critically coupled readout）下，结合三条边沿给出 93 pT/√Hz 的预期灵敏度，较最佳单边沿提升 √3 倍；灵敏度对 NV-磁振子耦合（NV-magnon coupling）的映射显示在 g_ens/2π ≈ 0.2–0.4 MHz 附近存在最优值，对应 0.64 MHz 自旋线宽（spin linewidth）时 72 pT/√Hz、0.17 MHz 时 19 pT/√Hz，热噪声极限（thermal limit）分别为 6.6 与 1.7 pT/√Hz。

Conclusion: 天线-模式耦合（antenna-mode coupling）、NV-磁振子耦合与自旋线宽被识别为器件设计的主要调控杠杆。该方案表明，基于磁振子晶体缺陷腔的芯片级 NV 磁强计可在无需外部微波与偏置场硬件的前提下实现高灵敏度，并可通过多边沿读取获得额外增益。

Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centre ensembles are leading room-temperature magnetometers, but their sensitivity is usually extracted from a single resonance and relies on external microwave and bias-field hardware. This work proposes a chip-scale NV magnetometer built around a one-dimensional YIG/CoFeB magnonic crystal, in which a single structural defect forms a passive magnonic cavity and integrated permanent magnets supply the bias field. Micromagnetic simulations show a defect-localised spin-wave mode at 2.910 GHz inside the 2.5-3.0 GHz magnonic band gap, 40 MHz from the NV zero-field splitting, with a band structure that matches an independent plane-wave calculation. A Tavis-Cummings/Lindblad model in which the microwave reaches the NV ensemble through this mode predicts a hyperfine-resolved optically detected magnetic resonance spectrum that is strong only within the mode linewidth, and a dispersive homodyne response with one steep edge per $^{14}$N hyperfine line. The master-equation solutions agree with the closed-form homodyne steady state ($R^2 > 0.9999$) and with an approximate analytic ODMR theory (peak positions within 0.012 MHz). For a critically coupled readout with room-temperature electronics, combining the three edges gives a projected sensitivity of 93 pT/$\sqrt{\rm Hz}$, a $\sqrt{3}$-fold gain over the best single edge. Mapping the sensitivity against the NV-magnon coupling reveals an optimum near $g_{\rm ens}/2π\approx 0.2$-$0.4$ MHz, where it reaches 72 pT/$\sqrt{\rm Hz}$ for a 0.64 MHz spin linewidth and 19 pT/$\sqrt{\rm Hz}$ for 0.17 MHz, with thermal limits of 6.6 and 1.7 pT/$\sqrt{\rm Hz}$. The antenna-mode coupling, the NV-magnon coupling and the spin linewidth are therefore the main design levers.

</details>


### [168] [Tunable Ultrastrong Magnon-Magnon Coupling and Squeezed States in a Triaxial Van der Waals Antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11859)
*Xiao Xiao,Zishuang Li,Zui Tao,Lina Chen,Ping Liu,Shuai Zhang,Haozhe Wang,Yong Pu,Ronghua Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在手性（chiral）范德华反铁磁体 $CrPS_4$ 中实现了可宽范围调控的超强磁振子-磁振子耦合（ultrastrong magnon-magnon coupling, USC），并通过完整量子模型将相互作用分解为三个微观通道，进而建立了将 USC 参数与可测量量子现象（压缩磁振子真空态）相联系的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现并量化超强磁振子-磁振子耦合（USC）是量子磁子学（quantum magnonics）的关键挑战；现有工作缺乏材料特异的量子框架，也缺少将 USC 参数与可观测非经典量子态直接挂钩的方案。

Method: 在范德华反铁磁体 $CrPS_4$ 中，研究手性（右手与左手）磁振子模式与声学（acoustic）或光学（optical）磁振子模式之间的 USC；采用完整量子模型，将相互作用分解为共转项 $g_1$（模式杂化）、反共转项 $g_2$（双模压缩）以及手性区特有的自平方项 $g_3$（单模压缩），并通过模拟研究杂化模式的手性与极化演化及由热噪声驱动的磁化动力学。

Result: 归一化耦合比达到 $g/f_g \sim 0.41$，进入 USC 区；模拟可视化了杂化模式的手性与极化演化，并展示了如何从热噪声驱动的磁化动力学中直接提取正交涨落（quadrature fluctuations）与高达约 9.7 dB 的压缩因子（squeezing factor）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了材料特异的 USC 量子框架，以及在低阻尼（low-damping）二维磁体中探测和利用非经典磁振子态的实用路径，通向量子增强自旋电子学（quantum-enhanced spintronics）与混合量子信息平台。

Abstract: Achieving and quantifying ultrastrong magnon-magnon coupling (USC) remains a key challenge for quantum magnonics. Here, we demonstrate widely tunable USC between chiral (right- and left-handed) and acoustic or optical magnon modes in the van der Waals antiferromagnet $CrPS_4$. Using a full quantum model, we decompose the interaction into three microscopic interaction channels: the co-rotating term $g_1$ (mode hybridization), the counter-rotating term $g_2$ (two-mode squeezing), and, uniquely in the chiral regime, the self-squared term $g_3$ that mediates single-mode squeezing. The normalized coupling ratio reaches $g/f_g$ $\sim$ 0.41, entering the USC regime. Most significantly, we establish a complete protocol linking these USC parameters to a measurable quantum phenomenon: a squeezed magnon vacuum state. Our simulations not only visualize the chirality and polarization evolution of the hybrid modes but also demonstrate how to directly extract quadrature fluctuations and a substantial squeezing factor (up to $\sim$ 9.7 dB) from thermal noise-driven magnetization dynamics. This work provides a material-specific quantum framework for USC and a practical pathway to detect and exploit non-classical magnon states in low-damping two-dimensional magnets, opening a route toward quantum-enhanced spintronics and hybrid quantum information platforms.

</details>


### [169] [Magnon dynamics driven by the symplectic quantum metric in a chiral soliton lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11982)
*Sidhartha Chatterjee,Andreas Haller,Peter P. Orth,Thomas L. Schmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维量子自旋链中量子几何（quantum geometry）对磁振子（magnon）波包运动的影响，揭示玻色型磁振子系统受辛结构（symplectic structure）和赝幺正性支配，其波包速度获得正比于辛量子度规（symplectic quantum metric）动量导数的几何贡献，并在近平坦能带中主导群速度（group velocity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度规驱动的输运现象在费米子系统（fermionic systems）中已被广泛研究，但玻色型磁振子系统中的对应动力学尚不清晰。论文旨在考察量子几何对一维量子自旋链中磁振子波包运动的作用，特别是辛结构与赝幺正变换带来的差异。

Method: 采用半经典（semiclassical）方法，在以手性孤子晶格（chiral soliton lattice）为背景的系统中，推导磁振子波包对弱且缓变微扰的响应，并求解所得到的运动方程以刻画波包轨迹。

Result: 磁振子波包速度获得一个几何贡献，其正比于辛量子度规的动量导数；在近平坦能带（nearly flat band）情形下，该几何贡献超过常规群速度。求解运动方程在实时间内得到有界轨迹，其相空间面积（phase-space area）由辛量子度规决定。

Conclusion: 玻色型磁振子系统的动力学显著受辛结构与磁振子Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量变换的赝幺正性（pseudo-unitarity）影响，辛量子度规可主导纵向磁振子动力学并控制波包轨道的相空间结构，为量子几何在玻色型系统中的输运作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of quantum geometry on the motion of magnon wave packets in one-dimensional quantum spin chains. In contrast to fermionic systems, where transport phenomena driven by the quantum metric have been extensively studied, we show that the dynamics in bosonic magnon systems are strongly affected by the symplectic structure and the pseudo-unitarity of the transformation of the magnon Bogoliubov-de Gennes Hamiltonian. A chiral soliton lattice provides a setting in which the symplectic quantum metric strongly influences the longitudinal magnon dynamics. We derive the semiclassical response of a magnon wave packet in such a system to a weak, slowly varying perturbation and demonstrate that the wave packet velocity acquires a geometric contribution proportional to the momentum derivative of the symplectic quantum metric, which dominates over the conventional group velocity for a nearly flat band. Solving the resulting equations of motion, we obtain a bounded trajectory in real time with a phase-space area determined by the symplectic quantum metric.

</details>


### [170] [Field-Free Reconfigurable Spin Logic in Compositionally Graded MnxCoAl Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12018)
*Haohang Cheng,Pengju Wang,Hongrui Qin,Rongkun Han,Dong Pan,Dahai Wei,Jianhua Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作通过垂直组分梯度（vertical composition gradient）工程，在Mn1.8-2.5CoAl/Pt异质结构中实现零磁场下的确定性自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）翻转、多态存储与可重构自旋逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子学逻辑存内（logic-in-memory）有望降低数据传输能耗并加速计算，但SOT翻转通常依赖外加磁场，限制其实际集成。因此需要探索无场（field-free）SOT翻转与可重构逻辑的实现途径。

Method: 研究对比垂直组分梯度Mn1.8-2.5CoAl/Pt与均匀组分Mn1.8CoAl/Pt、Mn2.5CoAl/Pt对照器件，通过SOT翻转测试及Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）相关测量表征有效场特征，并调节电流脉冲的幅度、极性与序列以实现不同翻转模式。

Result: Mn1.8-2.5CoAl梯度器件在零外磁场下表现出确定性SOT翻转，DMI测量显示有限的特征有效场尺度，提示手性磁化翻转过程参与；垂直梯度引入的磁不均匀性可调制畴成核与扩展。该器件还展现零磁场下稳定的中间霍尔电阻态累积多态翻转，而均匀组分对照器件无类似稳定多态行为；通过脉冲调控可在二值与多值翻转模式间切换。

Conclusion: 在同一霍尔条（Hall-bar）器件中、无外磁场条件下实验实现了AND、OR、NAND、NOR布尔运算。结果表明垂直组分工程可将Mn-Co-Al Heusler异质结构从场辅助SOT翻转拓展至无场磁化控制、多态操作与可重构自旋逻辑。

Abstract: Spintronic logic-in-memory provides a route to reducing data-transfer energy consumption and accelerating computation by integrating nonvolatile storage and logic functions within a single physical unit. Here, we investigate field-free spin-orbit-torque (SOT) switching and reconfigurable spin logic in a vertically composition-graded MnxCoAl/Pt heterostructure. In contrast to the uniform-composition Mn1.8CoAl/Pt and Mn2.5CoAl/Pt control devices, the Mn1.8-2.5CoAl gradient device exhibits deterministic SOT switching under zero external magnetic field. Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI)-related measurements reveal a finite characteristic effective-field scale, indicating the involvement of chiral magnetization-reversal processes. Meanwhile, the vertical composition gradient introduces magnetic inhomogeneity that can modify domain nucleation and propagation, and the combined effects are consistent with the observed field-free switching behavior. The composition-gradient device further exhibits accumulative multistate switching with stable intermediate Hall-resistance states under zero magnetic field, whereas the uniform-composition controls do not show comparable stable multistate behavior. By tailoring the amplitude, polarity, and sequence of current pulses, the device can be tuned between binary and multilevel switching modes, enabling the experimental realization of AND, OR, NAND, and NOR Boolean operations within the same Hall-bar device without any external magnetic field. These results extend Mn-Co-Al Heusler heterostructures from field-assisted SOT switching toward field-free magnetization control, multistate operation, and reconfigurable spin logic through vertical composition engineering.

</details>


### [171] [Symmetry-Dependent Polarity Reversal of Bulk Spin-Orbit Torque in Single-Layer MnCoGa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12031)
*Rongkun Han,Mingsong Zhang,Dahai Wei,Yuan Lu,Wenxu Zhang,Jianhua Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在半金属Heusler合金Mn1.6Co1.4Ga（MCG）单层中实现体自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT），并证明晶格应变可调控SOT极性反转，确立应变诱导的结构对称性破缺为调控单层体SOT的自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋电子学器件需精确控制SOT的大小与极性以操纵磁化，但此前体SOT调控机制及相关自由度尚需在单层体系中加以明确。

Method: 在单层MCG Heusler合金中进行厚度依赖（3–20 nm）的SOT驱动磁化翻转测量，并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）分析其拓扑能带结构下的内禀自旋霍尔电导（spin Hall conductivity, SHC）。

Result: 厚度3–20 nm范围内均可实现SOT驱动磁化翻转；强应变超薄区与较厚薄膜间的翻转极性相反。计算显示MCG具有可观内禀SHC，源于拓扑能带结构，并经四方畸变（tetragonal distortion）增强但不改号。

Conclusion: SOT极性反转并非源于SHC变号，而是晶格畸变诱导的对称性破缺改变了自旋流向净体SOT的转换过程；应变调控的结构对称性可作为调节磁性单层体SOT的额外自由度。

Abstract: Controlling the magnitude and polarity of spin-orbit torque (SOT) is essential for manipulating magnetization in spintronic devices. Here, we realize bulk spin-orbit torque (SOT) in a single-layer Mn1.6Co1.4Ga (MCG) Heusler alloy and achieve lattice strain-controlled reversal of SOT polarity. SOT-driven magnetization switching is achieved over a thickness range of 3-20 nm, with the switching polarity reversed between the strongly strained ultrathin regime and thicker films. First-principles calculations reveal a sizable intrinsic spin Hall conductivity (SHC) in MCG originating from its topological band structure, which is enhanced by the tetragonal distortion without changing sign. Together with the thickness-dependent structural evolution, we demonstrate that the reversal of SOT polarity does not originate from a sign change of the SHC, but rather from lattice distortion induced symmetry breaking that modifies the conversion of spin current into a net bulk SOT, thereby controlling its polarity. These results establish strain-controlled structural symmetry as an additional degree of freedom for tuning bulk SOT in magnetic single layers.

</details>


### [172] [Measurement-induced enhancement of the $X$-parity lifetime in a Majorana tetron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12223)
*Katharina Laubscher,Jay D. Sau,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该理论工作受近期Majorana tetron器件实验（arXiv:2507.08795）启发，研究耦合量子点-tetron系统在静电噪声下的线间宇称寿命。结果表明测量点耦合可产生能级劈裂、将噪声诱导跃迁移至有限频率，从而稳定被测态；在测量构型下X宇称切换寿命可达微秒量级，远高于孤立的裸tetron相干时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验通过辅助读出量子点的量子电容测量了Majorana tetron器件的线间X宇称寿命，这一测量结果为理论解释提出了需求。论文由此出发，探究耦合点-tetron系统在静电噪声下的宇称动力学，并解释测量构型下观测寿命的来源。

Method: 采用点-tetron耦合系统的最小模型（minimal model）并结合静电噪声进行研究：在色散区（dispersive regime）将读出点积分掉，动力学约化为有效两能级系统；在近共振区（near-resonant regime）则保留全部四个低能态参与动力学。以无序纳米线器件所代表的噪声强度为参数，计算X宇称切换寿命。

Result: 与测量点的耦合本身通过产生能级劈裂显著改变宇称动力学，使噪声诱导跃迁移至有限频率，从而抑制低频涨落。在代表性噪声强度下，测量构型中的X宇称切换寿命达到微秒量级，即便对应的裸tetron相干时间仅数十纳秒。近共振区中快速跃迁将能谱组织为两个长寿命流形，近似对应组合点-tetron X环宇称的扇区，尽管存在多能级结构仍表现为有效两态切换。

Conclusion: 测量诱导的稳定化（measurement-induced stabilization）可显著延长观测到的X宇称切换寿命，使连续读出下测得的寿命大幅超过孤立Majorana tetron量子比特的固有闲置T_2^*相干时间。该机制为理解实验中较长的宇称寿命提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A recent experiment [arXiv:2507.08795] has reported measurements of the interwire $X$-parity lifetime in a Majorana tetron device via the quantum capacitance of an auxiliary readout dot. Motivated by these measurements, we theoretically study the interwire parity lifetime in a minimal model of the coupled dot-tetron system subject to electrostatic noise. We show that coupling to the measurement dot can itself strongly modify the parity dynamics by generating an energy splitting that shifts noise-induced transitions to finite frequency and thereby stabilizes the measured states against low-frequency fluctuations. For representative noise strengths motivated by disordered nanowire devices, we find $X$-parity switching lifetimes of order microseconds in the measurement configuration even when the corresponding bare-tetron coherence time is only tens of nanoseconds long. We analyze both the dispersive regime, where the dot can be integrated out and the dynamics reduces to an effective two-level system, and the near-resonant regime, where all four low-energy states participate. In the latter case, rapid transitions organize the spectrum into two long-lived manifolds that approximately correspond to sectors of the combined dot-tetron $X$-loop parity, yielding effective two-state switching despite the underlying multilevel structure. Our results show that measurement-induced stabilization can substantially enhance the observed $X$-parity switching lifetime, such that the lifetime measured under continuous readout can greatly exceed the intrinsic idle $T_2^*$ coherence time of the isolated Majorana tetron qubit.

</details>


### [173] [Accessing electronic entanglement via photo-assisted two-electron tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12286)
*M. Paulet,G. Rebora,M. Bienioszek,G. Ménard,G. Fève,A. Feller,P. Degiovanni*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出“光辅助双电子层析（photo-assisted two-electron tomography）”协议，仅凭直流电流（dc-currents）与时均电流关联即可重建双电子相干性（two-electron coherence），并在库仑耦合量子霍尔边缘通道（Coulomb-coupled quantum Hall edge channels）中揭示相互作用诱导的能量纠缠（energy entanglement）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测量子电路中传播电子的纠缠需要获取其双电子相干性，该相干性编码了库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions）产生的双粒子量子关联。然而，双电子相干性的实验重建仍是主要挑战，现有途径需依赖电流噪声测量。

Method: 以小型射频信号（rf tone）驱动能量滤波器，将频域中的非对角相干性编码进直流电流响应，从而实现无需电流噪声测量的单电子层析（single-electron tomography）；将该方案扩展至两个通道，在两级光辅助滤波器后利用电流关联重建双电子维格纳分布（two-electron Wigner distribution）并分离其不可约两体部分。另采用广义 Franson 几何（generalized Franson geometry）将方法推广至通道内双电子相干性。

Result: 应用于库仑耦合量子霍尔边缘通道时，协议揭示出相互作用诱导的能量纠缠；该纠缠特征表现为基于柯西–施瓦茨不等式（Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities）的纠缠目击者（entanglement witness）被违反，以及维格纳函数负值（Wigner function negativities），且在现实器件参数下仍可在热背景之上被分辨。

Conclusion: 该工作为在弹道量子电路（ballistic quantum circuits）中直接探测与表征电子纠缠开辟了可行途径。

Abstract: Witnessing entanglement between electrons propagating in quantum circuits requires accessing their two-electron coherences, which encode the two-particle quantum correlations generated by Coulomb interactions. Yet, experimental reconstruction of two-electron coherence remains a major challenge. We introduce photo-assisted two-electron tomography, a quantum coherence reconstruction protocol from dc-currents and time-averaged current correlations, routinely measured in electron quantum optics experiments. Driving an energy filter with a small rf tone encodes off-diagonal coherences in the frequency domain into the dc-current response, enabling single-electron tomography without resorting to current-noise measurements. Extending it to two channels, current correlations after two photo-assisted filters reconstruct the two-electron Wigner distribution and isolate its irreducible two-body part. When applied to Coulomb-coupled quantum Hall edge channels, the protocol reveals interaction-induced energy entanglement. Signatures of this entanglement are visible through the violation of an entanglement witness based on Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities, as well as Wigner function negativities, which remain resolved above the thermal background for realistic device parameters. A generalized Franson geometry further extends the approach to intra-channel two-electron coherences. This opens a direct pathway to detect and characterize electronic entanglement in ballistic quantum circuits.

</details>
