### [174] [Axiomatic Incompatibility Between Thermal Equilibrium and Volume-Dependent Entropy in Ideal Gas Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10645)
*Andrea Paglietti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文指出理想气体（ideal gas）熵（entropy）的传统体积依赖公式违反可逆等温过程中热平衡（thermal equilibrium）公理，并通过区分严格等温与准等温过程，主张完美气体的热力学熵仅是温度的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典热力学（classical thermodynamics）的宏观分析中存在一个被忽视的基础性矛盾：理想气体熵的传统体积依赖公式在可逆等温（reversible isothermal）过程中与热平衡基本公理相冲突，需予以解决。

Method: 建立严格等温（strictly isothermal）与准等温（quasi isothermal）过程之间的严格操作区分；对理想气体绝热自由膨胀（adiabatic free expansion）进行新分析；并引入伪等温过程（pseudo isothermal process，即由有限序列的小绝热与小等容步骤构成）以衔接标准热力学实践。

Result: 为与经典热力学定律严格一致，完美气体（perfect gas）的热力学熵必须仅是温度的函数，与体积无关；对理想气体绝热自由膨胀的分析表明该过程本质上是可逆的（intrinsically reversible）；经典功与热的方程严格适用于伪等温过程，而非严格等温过程。

Conclusion: 修正后的热力学熵与麦克斯韦动理论（Maxwell's kinetic theory）完全一致，但与普朗克-玻尔兹曼公式（Planck-Boltzmann formula）存在本质差异；热力学熵与统计微观态计数（microstate counting）是两种不同的物理量，该分析为经典热力学摆脱微观无序（microscopic disorder）悖论提供了简明途径。

Abstract: A rigorous macroscopic analysis of classical thermodynamics reveals an overlooked contradiction at the foundations of ideal gas theory: the traditional volume dependent formula for entropy violates the fundamental axiom of thermal equilibrium during reversible isothermal processes. This paper resolves this inconsistency by establishing a strict operational distinction between strictly isothermal and quasi isothermal processes. We show that in order for the perfect gas to be strictly consistent with the laws of classical thermodynamics, the thermodynamic entropy of a perfect gas must be a function of temperature only. This independence of volume is also confirmed by a new analysis of the adiabatic free expansion of an ideal gas, which is found to be intrinsically reversible. To reconcile these results with standard thermodynamic practice, we introduce the concept of a pseudo isothermal process (a finite sequence of small adiabatic and isochoric steps) and show that the classical equations for work and heat apply rigorously to these real processes, as opposed to strictly isothermal ones. We conclude by discussing the profound implications of these findings for statistical mechanics, showing that while the corrected thermodynamic entropy is in perfect agreement with Maxwell's kinetic theory, it is essentially different from the Planck Boltzmann formula. Thus, thermodynamic entropy and statistical microstate counting are two different physical quantities, and the present analysis provides a simple way to free classical thermodynamics from the paradoxes of microscopic disorder.

</details>


### [175] [Efficiency fluctuations in quantum heat engines with non-quasi-static adiabatic strokes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10647)
*Bikram Keshari Behera,Shyamal Biswas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文对量子卡诺机（quantum Carnot engine）与量子奥托机（quantum Otto engine）在非准静态绝热膨胀/压缩过程下的效率涨落（efficiency fluctuations）进行了分析与数值研究，揭示了活塞速度引入的额外量子涨落对平均效率的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有量子热机研究多关注准静态过程下的效率与涨落，而对于以恒定速度进行的非准静态绝热冲程所导致的随机性及其对效率的影响尚缺乏明确刻画，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 采用两点测量方案（two-point measurement scheme）处理绝热冲程；将活塞建模为一维盒中单量子粒子且边界可非准静态移动，借此捕捉非平衡过程中系统量子态幺正演化（unitary evolution）所产生的功输出随机性，并结合解析与数值方法分析卡诺机（等温过程为准静态）与奥托机（等容过程为准静态）的效率。

Result: 非准静态冲程中功输出呈随机分布，其涨落源于量子态在非平衡过程中的幺正演化；活塞速度在量子非平衡冲程中会引入额外的量子涨落，并显式地改变两台热机的平均效率。

Conclusion: 活塞速度是量子热机效率涨落的关键控制参数，非准静态绝热过程不仅带来功的随机性，还会通过附加量子涨落修正平均效率；该结论为量子热机在有限时间运行下的性能评估提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We have presented an analytical and numerical study of efficiency fluctuations in quantum Carnot and Otto engines with pistons operating under non-quasi-static adiabatic expansion and compression processes at constant speed, along with quasi-static isothermal (for the Carnot engine) or isochoric processes (for the Otto engine). We have considered the two-point measurement scheme during the adiabatic strokes in this regard. By considering each of the pistons as a single quantum particle in a 1-D box with a non-quasi-statically movable boundary, we capture the stochastic nature of the work output resulting from the unitary evolution of the quantum mechanical state of the system during non-equilibrium processes. Furthermore, we have explicitly shown how the piston speed during quantum non-equilibrium strokes introduces additional quantum fluctuations that modify the average efficiency of both the engines.

</details>


### [176] [A general framework for crystallization in maximal hard-core models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11042)
*Alexander Barg,Qidong He,Geyang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究周期性格点型图上的通用最大硬核模型（maximal hard-core models），提出统一假设框架，用于建立 Peierls 条件与配分函数的收敛集团展开，从而导出 Pirogov-Sinai 理论结论，并给出高、低活度下相共存区域界定的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大模型（maximal models）与标准模型不同，其相共存（phase coexistence）可能出现在足够低的活度（activity）下，而非仅在高活度时出现，因此需要一套适用于此类模型的统一分析框架。

Method: 借助体积分配（volume allocation）概念，构建一组统一假设，使其蕴含 Peierls 条件（Peierls condition）以及配分函数（partition function）的收敛集团展开（convergent cluster expansion）；并在多个示例（含标准格点型周期图）上验证这些假设；同时为界定相图中相共存区域，重写了 Pirogov-Sinai 理论推导中一个技术性结果的证明并给出活度估计。

Result: 统一假设成立时可得 Peierls 条件与收敛的集团展开，从而获得该类模型的 Pirogov-Sinai 理论结论；在含标准格点型周期图在内的若干示例中验证了假设，并证明高活度与低活度下均发生结晶（crystallization）。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一假设框架可同时刻画高、低活度下的相共存与结晶现象，并给出界定相图中相共存区域的活度估计，拓展了 Pirogov-Sinai 理论在最大硬核模型上的适用范围。

Abstract: We study general maximal hard-core models on periodic lattice-type graphs. While for standard models, phase coexistence may arise for high activity, for maximal models this behavior may occur also for sufficiently low activity values. Relying on the concept of volume allocation, we develop a {\em unified set of assumptions} that imply the Peierls condition, a convergent cluster expansion for the partition function, and hence the conclusions of Pirogov-Sinai theory for this class of models. We further check these assumptions for a number of examples including standard lattice-type periodic graphs, proving crystallization for both high and low activity. We also derive estimates for the values of activity that bound the phase coexistence regions in the phase diagram, rewriting for this purpose the proof of a technical result in the derivation of Pirogov-Sinai theory.

</details>


### [177] [High-resolution mapping of the phase boundary of the honeycomb-lattice Blume--Capel ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11068)
*Eluiz C. M. Filho,Claudio J. DaSilva*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文对蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）上自旋-1 Blume–Capel 铁磁体进行高分辨率数值研究，确定三临界点（tricritical point）位置及普适临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在高精度刻画配位数 z=3 的蜂窝晶格 Blume–Capel 模型的一级相变线与三临界行为，并检验三临界 Ising 普适性（tricritical Ising universality）及场混合参数 r 的标度规律。

Method: 采用 Wang–Landau 方法估计联合态密度（joint density of states）Ω(E₁,E₂)并结合场混合（field mixing）映射一级相变线；以 L=36、48 的 Metropolis 模拟直方图重加权（histogram reweighting）作独立校验；并用由态密度构造权重的 multicanonical 模拟扩展至 L=64。

Result: 三临界点定位为 (Δ_t,T_t)=[1.4819(2),0.4022(7)]，重加权交叉温度约 0.4039；场混合给出 r=−1.586(19)，共存线斜率给出更精确的 r=−1.587(5)；归一化后 r/Δ₀≈−1.06，比平均场值 −1 大约 6%，未发现对配位数或维度的系统性依赖；σ_Q 的有限尺度标度给出 y_t=1.795(7)（与 9/5 相符），四阶累积量标度兼容 y_g=4/5，一级区累积量趋近 U_Q→2/3，界面张力指数 μ=1.265(26)（与 5/4 相符）；连续分支给出 T_c(Δ=0)=1.157。

Conclusion: 结果支持蜂窝晶格 Blume–Capel 铁磁体的三临界 Ising 普适类，场混合比 r/Δ₀ 在多个晶格间近似普适聚集于约 −1.06；靠近三临界点的估计受 Ising 到三临界标度交叉（crossover）影响。

Abstract: We present a high-resolution numerical study of the spin-1 Blume--Capel ferromagnet on the honeycomb lattice ($z=3$). Wang--Landau estimates of the joint density of states $Ω(E_1,E_2)$, combined with field mixing, are used to map the first-order transition line and locate the tricritical point at $(Δ_t,T_t)=[1.4819(2),\,0.4022(7)]$. Histogram reweighting of Metropolis simulations for $L=36$ and $48$ provides an independent consistency check, with a crossing temperature near $0.4039$, compatible with the Wang--Landau extrapolation. From the field-mixing analysis we obtain $r=-1.586(19)$, while the slope of the multicanonical coexistence line yields the consistent and more precise estimate $r=-1.587(5)$. Normalizing by the zero-temperature field $Δ_0=zJ/2$, the available honeycomb, square, triangular, and simple-cubic estimates cluster near $r/Δ_0\simeq-1.06$, corresponding to a magnitude about $6\%$ larger than the mean-field value $-1$; no systematic dependence on coordination number or dimensionality is resolved over the range examined. Finite-size scaling of $σ_Q$ gives $y_t=1.795(7)$, consistent with the tricritical Ising value $9/5$, while the fourth-order cumulant scaling is compatible with $y_g=4/5$. Deep in the first-order regime, the cumulant approaches the expected two-phase limit $U_Q\to2/3$. Multicanonical simulations up to $L=64$, using weights constructed directly from the Wang--Landau density of states for $L\le24$ and rescaled and refined for larger systems, yield an interface-tension exponent $μ=1.265(26)$, consistent with the tricritical prediction $(d-1)/y_g=5/4$. Finally, we map the continuous branch of the phase boundary from the same density-of-states data, obtaining $T_c(Δ=0)=1.157$; estimates sufficiently close to the tricritical point are affected by crossover from Ising to tricritical scaling.

</details>


### [178] [Markov length can diverge in systems whose universal physics is spatially Markovian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11235)
*Yu-Hsueh Chen,Tarun Grover*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 条件互信息（conditional mutual information, CMI）所呈现的空间非马尔可夫性（spatial non-Markovianity）可以在同一重整化群（renormalization-group, RG）不动点下表现出定性差异；一类由破坏细致平衡（detailed balance）的 RG-无关微扰产生的幂律 CMI 尾巴会被截断（cutoff）压低，并在连续极限下消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有基于局域可逆性（local reversibility）的混合态相（mixed-state phases）框架通过 CMI 的指数衰减与代数衰减来区分相，但默认流向同一 RG 不动点的态具有相同定性行为。本文旨在检验这一假设，考察同一 RG 不动点下 CMI 行为是否可能定性不同。

Method: 以有限程局域经典哈密顿量的临界 Gibbs 态为出发点（其 CMI 在相互作用程之外为零），施加破坏细致平衡的 RG-无关微扰；构造可解模型加以验证，并预测 Toom 元胞自动机（Toom's cellular automata）的 Ising 对称临界点也普遍呈现该行为；此外在长程 Ising 顺磁体的高温顺磁相中分析类似现象，并推导一维情形下 CMI 衰减与马尔可夫性（Markovianity）关系的一般结论。

Result: 破坏细致平衡的 RG-无关微扰普遍生成代数衰减的 CMI；这些幂律尾巴在每个固定格点截断下导致无限马尔可夫长度（Markov length），但在连续极限下以截断的正幂次消失。相同现象出现在长程 Ising 顺磁体的高温顺磁相，而该模型有限温临界点的真实幂律 CMI 可存续至连续极限。一般性结果：一维中 CMI 衰减快于缓冲长度（buffer length）的平方反比，当且仅当长程物理是马尔可夫的。

Conclusion: 流向同一 RG 不动点的态可具有定性不同的 CMI，因此仅凭代数衰减的 CMI 判断相不等价在截断敏感的意义上可能失效。区分“截断压低的 CMI”与“连续极限下存续的真实幂律 CMI”，对基于局域可逆性的混合态相分类至关重要。

Abstract: Conditional mutual information (CMI) probes spatial non-Markovianity and plays a central role in a recently developed framework for defining mixed-state phases based on local reversibility. This framework sharply distinguishes exponentially decaying from algebraically decaying CMI, since the latter obstructs equivalence to a state with finite Markov length. Here we show that states that flow to the same renormalization-group (RG) fixed point can nevertheless exhibit qualitatively different CMI. Starting from a critical Gibbs state of a finite-range local classical Hamiltonian, whose CMI vanishes beyond the interaction range, we show that an RG-irrelevant perturbation that breaks detailed balance generically generates algebraically decaying CMI. Crucially, these power-law tails are cutoff-suppressed: although they lead to an infinite Markov length at every fixed lattice cutoff, they vanish as a positive power of the cutoff in the continuum limit. We construct solvable models that exhibit this phenomenon and predict that the Ising-symmetric critical point of Toom's cellular automata generically exhibits such behavior. We also find analogous cutoff-suppressed CMI in the high-temperature paramagnetic phase of the long-range Ising paramagnet, in contrast with the genuine power-law CMI at the finite-temperature critical point in this same model that survives the continuum limit. We also derive a general result that in one spatial dimension, CMI decays faster than the inverse square of the buffer length if and only if the long-distance physics is Markovian.

</details>


### [179] [Quantum annealing of the random transverse-field Ising model in one, two, and three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11411)
*István A. Kovács,Neil Shah,Róbert Juhász*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用强无序重整化群（SDRG）研究随机横场伊辛模型中量子退火（quantum annealing）的缺陷形成问题，得到避免缺陷形成所允许的极限退火速率，并给出一维至三维的数值验证与标度理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火（quantum annealing）必须足够缓慢才能抑制缺陷（defect）的形成，但避免缺陷形成所需的极限速率此前缺乏系统刻画。本文旨在确定该极限速率，并揭示缺陷形成的基本微观事件。

Method: 基于绝热定理（adiabatic theorem），采用强无序重整化群（strong-disorder renormalization group, SDRG）方法，将退火过程视为强耦合集团（cluster）的逐步聚集过程；建立一般维度下这些事件统计的标度理论（scaling theory），并在1、2、3维进行大规模数值分析，同时在一维用自由费米子（free-fermion）技术的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）加以验证。

Result: 结果表明误差密度随退火时间呈对数式缓慢下降，其特征由模型相应无限无序不动点（infinite-disorder fixed point）的维度依赖临界指数（critical exponent）决定；SDRG数值结果与一维精确对角化一致。此外给出了潜在缺陷形成事件在激发能–控制参数平面上的分布图。

Conclusion: SDRG 框架可有效刻画随机横场伊辛模型中缺陷形成事件及其统计规律，所获得的指数与事件分布图为设计最优退火路径（optimal annealing path）提供了依据。

Abstract: Quantum annealing must be sufficiently slow to prevent defect formation. We study this problem in the random transverse-field Ising model via the adiabatic theorem to obtain the limiting rate at which defect formation can be avoided. Our approach based on the strong-disorder renormalization group (SDRG) technique views the annealing process as a gradual aggregation of strongly coupled clusters, giving access to the elementary events of error formation. We present a scaling theory of the statistics of these events in general dimensions and confront it to an extensive numerical analysis carried out in one, two, and three dimensions by an efficient implementation of the SDRG method. This approach is also validated by exact diagonalization in one dimension by means of the free-fermion technique. Our results consistently show a logarithmically slow decrease of the error density with the annealing time, characterized by the dimension-dependent critical exponents of the corresponding infinite-disorder fixed point of the model. Furthermore, we provide a map of individual events of potential defect formation in the excitation energy - control parameter plane, which may help in the design of optimal annealing paths.

</details>


### [180] [Controlling transitions between nonequilibrium states through active bath engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11831)
*Dima Boriskovsky,Salambô Dago,Cyriaque Genet,Rémi Goerlich,Vincent Hardel,Paul-Antoine Hervieux,Harry L. F. Ip,Giovanni Manfredi,Laurent Mertz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出以噪声色（noise color，即相关率）作为唯一控制参数，实现活性非平衡稳态（active nonequilibrium steady states）之间的有限时间转变，并通过光镊实验验证了远快于自然弛豫的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工程化快速平衡（engineered swift equilibration）方法主要面向平衡态，向远非平衡稳态的有限时间控制扩展仍属空白，而活性浴的时间相关性提供了新的控制自由度。

Method: 基于活性 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程（active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process），反演其二阶矩动力学（second moment dynamics），求解实现预设系统状态演化所需的时间依赖相关率；并在微米级光镊捕获粒子与工程化活性浴耦合的平台上开展实验。

Result: 实验中在保持束缚势、噪声幅度与温度固定的条件下，实现了显著快于自然弛豫的稳态间转变；同时揭示物理约束对有限时间转变施加的速度极限（speed limit），并表明可通过选择预设演化来消除控制不连续、最小化可允许转变时间或优化热力学代价（thermodynamic cost）。

Conclusion: 通过直接调控活性环境的时间相关性，工程化快速平衡方法可推广至远非平衡稳态，为活性系统的有限时间控制与热力学优化提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We develop a family of control protocols for finite-time transitions between active nonequilibrium steady states using the noise-color (correlation rate) as the sole control parameter. By reverse-engineering the second moment dynamics of an active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, we determine the time-dependent correlation rate required to realize a prescribed evolution of the system's state. We experimentally implement these protocols with a micrometer-sized optically trapped particle coupled to an engineered active bath, demonstrating transitions substantially faster than the natural relaxation while keeping the confining potential, noise amplitude, and temperature fixed. Our approach extends engineered swift-equilibration methods to far-from-equilibrium steady states by directly controlling the temporal correlations of an active environment. We show how physical constraints impose a speed limit on finite-time transitions, while the freedom in choosing the prescribed system evolution can be exploited to eliminate control discontinuities, minimize the admissible transition time, or optimize a thermodynamic cost.

</details>


### [181] [The Fiedler dimension of networks of networks: from fractal to small-world architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11909)
*Lorenzo Grimaldi,Andrea Gabrielli,Pablo Villegas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究探讨模块化网络（modular network）的弛豫过程由模块本身决定还是由连接模块的网络决定。作者提出“捆绑网络（bundled networks）”模型，证明两个层级依次作用，平衡时间（equilibration time）为逃逸时间与基网络弛豫时间之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解模块化网络的弛豫时间尺度（time scales）对扩散（diffusion）、共识（consensus）与同步（synchronization）过程至关重要，而这些尺度均由 Fiedler 特征值（Fiedler eigenvalue）决定。核心问题是：弛豫究竟由模块主导，还是由连接模块的网络主导。

Method: 作者构建“捆绑网络”框架：随机游走者（random walker）须先逃出所在模块，再在连接模块的基网络（base）上扩散。基于此，研究者解析推导了有限维（finite-dimensional）与小世界（small-world）基网络与纤维（fiber）的所有组合，并与各类别代表性网络的数值精确谱（numerically exact spectra）进行对比。

Result: 平衡时间为两部分之和：从纤维内部到达基网络所需的时间，以及基网络的弛豫时间（被纤维质量拖慢）。出乎意料的是，无论模块多大，小世界核心（small-world core）始终强加其自身的 Fiedler 维度；模块仅作为对数修正（logarithmic correction）存留，从而减慢平衡并掩盖真实指数（true exponent），其影响范围远超真实网络尺度。

Conclusion: 在有限模块化系统（finite modular systems）上测得的有�效维数（effective dimensions）可能系统性地产生偏差。该工作通过解析与数值谱的对照，为模块化网络中扩散、共识与同步的时间尺度提供了统一刻画。

Abstract: Is the relaxation of a modular network dictated by its modules or by the network that connects them? The answer fixes the time scales of diffusion, consensus, and synchronization, all set by the Fiedler eigenvalue. We show that, for bundled networks, the two levels act one after the other: a random walker must first escape from its module and then spread over the network that connects them. The equilibration time is the sum of these two times, the time needed to reach the base from inside a fiber and the relaxation time of the base, slowed down by the mass of the fibers. The consequences are unexpected. However large the modules are, a small-world core always imposes its own Fiedler dimension; the modules survive only as a logarithmic correction, which slows down equilibration and hides the true exponent up to sizes far beyond any real network. Effective dimensions measured on finite modular systems can therefore be systematically biased. We analytically work out all combinations of finite-dimensional and small-world bases and fibers, and compare them with numerically exact spectra of representatives of each category.

</details>


### [182] [Mutual Linearity of Complexes in Chemical Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11970)
*Pedro E. Harunari,Guilherme Fiusa,Matteo Polettini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究将“互为线性（mutual linearity）”从跳过程推广到非线性质量作用动力学的化学反应网络（chemical reaction networks）。作者证明在零亏格（zero-deficiency）且单一连接类（single linkage class）的网络中，调控单一反应的速率常数会使任意三个稳态复合物活度（complex activities，即质量作用单项式）满足线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远离平衡（far from equilibrium）时，系统对速率常数变化的响应缺乏线性响应之外的精确结论，而这类结论对生化网络（biochemical networks）的调控至关重要。跳过程中的互为线性是一个例外，但化学反应网络因非线性质量作用动力学与守恒律（conservation laws）取代归一化条件，被认为不满足该性质。

Method: 作者不再从物种浓度（species concentrations）出发，而是转向复合物活度（complex activities，即质量作用单项式）这一变量，考察在零亏格、单一连接类的网络中调控单一反应速率常数时，稳态活度所满足的关系。

Result: 在零亏格且单一连接类的网络中，调控一个反应使任意三个稳态活度满足线性关系，其系数独立于被调控速率常数、守恒量（conserved quantities，因而也独立于初始状态），甚至独立于复合物的化学计量（stoichiometry of complexes）。

Conclusion: 该关系可预测隐藏活度（hidden activities）并界定调控所能达到的活度比范围，而其违背则标志着非零亏格或多连接类。

Abstract: Exact statements about how a system far from equilibrium responds to changes in its rate constants are scarce beyond linear response, yet they are central to the control of biochemical networks. A notable exception is mutual linearity: in a jump process, controlling the rates of a single transition makes any two stationary probabilities obey an exact affine relation, arbitrarily far from equilibrium. With nonlinear mass-action kinetics, and conservation laws in place of a normalization, chemical reaction networks seem to defy mutual linearity since species concentrations obey no such relation. We show that it was there all along, in the complexes' activities (mass-action monomials) rather than in the concentrations of species. For zero-deficiency networks with a single linkage class, controlling one reaction makes any three stationary activities obey a linear relation whose coefficients are independent of the controlled rate constants, the conserved quantities (hence the initial state), and even of the stoichiometry of complexes. This predicts hidden activities and bounds the activity ratios the control can reach, while a violation signals nonzero deficiency or multiple linkage classes.

</details>

### [183] [Disorder-induced nuclear-transition doublet at nominally cubic $^{229}$Th sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11010)
*Andrei Derevianko*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上阐明，在标称立方对称且理想晶格电场梯度（electric-field gradient）为零的位点上，稀薄静态点缺陷（point-defect disorder）会导致 $^{229}$Th 核钟跃迁（nuclear clock transition）出现双峰（doublet）结构，并在无四极相互作用频率处保留一个零点。该零点的可分辨性有望成为核钟判别器（discriminator）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $^{229}$Th 核钟跃迁在标称立方对称位点上仍呈现非均匀展宽（inhomogeneous broadening），而理想晶格在此处的电场梯度为零，说明展宽来源需另行解释。论文旨在建立缺陷无序与谱线形状之间的联系，并寻找可用于核钟的参考特征。

Method: 采用稀薄静态点缺陷模型，区分各向同性近似（isotropic approximation）下的对称双峰、尺寸失配（size misfit）缺陷引入的瓣不对称性（lobe asymmetry），并分析带电缺陷与尺寸失配缺陷的标度行为（inverse-cube scaling）与归一化谱形（normalized profile）。线宽被分解为宿主控制的背景项与正比于 $^{229}$Th 密度的项。

Result: 带电与尺寸失配缺陷均呈反立方标度并具有近似共同的归一化谱形。在重掺杂 $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$ 晶体中，与钍相关的贡献与实测 1.1 MHz 线宽相当；在轻掺杂晶体中，0.9 MHz 线宽给出了宿主背景的上限。两种晶体的实测谱均未出现理论预言的凹陷（dip），暗示其中存在扩展缺陷（extended defects）。

Conclusion: 点缺陷无序可解释立方对称位点上的非均匀展宽，并预言在无四极相互作用频率处存在双峰零点。若该凹陷得以分辨，可作为以无四极核跃迁频率为基准的核钟判别器；当前实测谱未见凹陷，提示晶体中存在扩展缺陷。

Abstract: The $^{229}$Th nuclear clock transition is inhomogeneously broadened even at sites of nominally cubic symmetry, where the electric-field gradient of the ideal lattice vanishes. We show that dilute static point-defect disorder produces a doublet with a zero at the quadrupole-free frequency. The doublet is symmetric in the isotropic approximation; size misfit point defects introduce a small lobe asymmetry without removing the zero. Charged and size-misfit defects share inverse-cube scaling and an approximately common normalized profile, whose width separates into a host-controlled background and a term linear in the $^{229}$Th density. In a heavily doped $^{229}$Th:CaF$_2$ crystal [Nature 657, 626 (2026)], the thorium-linked contribution is comparable to the measured 1.1 MHz width; in a lightly doped crystal [arXiv:2606.08870], the 0.9 MHz width bounds the host background. Neither spectrum shows the predicted dip, which points to the presence of extended defects in both crystals. If the predicted dip is resolved, it can provide a nuclear-clock discriminator referenced to the quadrupole-free nuclear transition frequency.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Hamiltonian framework for Chiral Gauge Theories on a Disk Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11035)
*Srimoyee Sen*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文提出一个基于欧几里得表述的手性规范理论（chiral gauge theories, CGT）哈密顿框架：在 2n+1 维圆盘边界上放置 2n 维手性费米子，并给出一种可将规范场延拓至体内部（bulk）的新方案，使其同时兼容哈密顿框架与欧几里得路径积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始欧几里得表述中，边界规范场需借助 2n+1 维规范场运动方程（equations of motion, EOM）延拓到体内部，这为构建哈密顿量造成瓶颈。作者旨在突破该瓶颈，为手性规范理论开辟哈密顿处理与量子模拟（quantum simulation）的路径。

Method: 提出一种替代性的规范场体内部延拓方案，使其同时兼容哈密顿框架与欧几里得路径积分。将该方法应用于阿贝尔规范场（Abelian gauge fields），得到内部场作为边界场泛函的精确表达式，可直接用于哈密顿表述。

Result: 对新的规范场延拓进行欧几里得分析显示，其可保持原构造的非微扰（non-perturbative）内容，包括拓扑荷（topological charge）的行为、相关联的手性费米子零模（chiral fermion zero modes），以及应用于标准模型（Standard Model）时不存在强 CP 问题（strong CP problem）。

Conclusion: 该构造为圆盘边界上手性规范理论的哈密顿处理及未来的量子模拟开辟了途径。

Abstract: I propose a Hamiltonian framework for chiral gauge theories (CGT) based on a Euclidean formulation which uses 2n dimensional chiral fermions on the boundary of a 2n+1 dimensional disk. In the original Euclidean formulation, boundary gauge fields were extended into the bulk using $2n + 1$ dimensional gauge field equations of motion (EOM) which creates a bottleneck for constructing a Hamiltonian. I present an alternate proposal for extending the gauge fields into the bulk that is compatible with both a Hamiltonian framework and a Euclidean path integral. Applying this to Abelian gauge fields produces exact expressions of interior fields as a functional of the boundary fields, which can be directly used in a Hamiltonian formulation. Euclidean analysis of the new gauge field extension shows that it can preserve the non-perturbative content of the original construction, including the behavior of topological charge, associated chiral fermion zero modes and an absence of the strong CP problem when applied to the Standard Model. This construction opens up a route to a Hamiltonian treatment and future quantum simulation of CGTs on a disk boundary.

</details>


<div id='physics.gen-ph'></div>

# physics.gen-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Inflationary Dynamics and CMB Constraints in $f(T,L_m)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10565)
*Suchita Patel,Sai Swagat Mishra,P. K. Sahoo*

Main category: physics.gen-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非最小耦合挠率（torsion）与物质部门下的改性远平行（teleparallel）框架中的慢滚暴胀（slow-roll inflation），聚焦 Starobinsky 型平台暴胀。结果表明弱耦合基本不改变标准预言，强耦合显著移动标量谱指数（scalar spectral index）；模型在较宽参数空间内与 Planck 及 BICEP/Keck 约束相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于考察挠率与物质部门之间的非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）如何影响暴胀动力学与原始观测（primordial observables），尤其是在保留 Starobinsky 型平台暴胀成功现象学的前提下寻找可检验的偏离。摘要未提供更具体的文献背景或观测动机细节。

Method: 在改性远平行框架中引入挠率—物质非最小耦合，推导修正后的背景动力学与相应慢滚可观测量，并分析引力耦合对标量谱指数、张量—标量比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）及谱指数跑动（running of the spectral index）的影响。同时检验二次挠率修正（quadratic torsion correction）在受控低能区（controlled low-energy regime）内的作用。

Result: 模型在适当弱耦合极限下连续回到标准 Starobinsky 情形；物质—几何耦合（matter–geometry coupling）对暴胀预言产生非单调修正（non-monotonic modification）。弱耦合下标准预言基本不变，较强耦合可显著移动标量谱指数并最终与当前宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）约束不相容。二次挠率修正影响相对温和，主要作用于张量振幅（tensor amplitude）。结果与 Planck 及 BICEP/Keck 约束对比后，模型在广泛暴胀参数空间内仍属可行（viable）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，物质—几何耦合可能在原始可观测量上留下可检验的印记，同时保留 Starobinsky 型平台暴胀成功的现象学。摘要未给出进一步结论或未来工作方向。

Abstract: We investigate slow-roll inflation in a modified teleparallel framework with a non-minimal coupling between torsion and the matter sector. Focusing on Starobinsky-type plateau inflation, we derive the modified background dynamics and the corresponding slow-roll observables, and examine how the gravitational couplings affect the scalar spectral index, tensor-to-scalar ratio, and running of the spectral index. The model continuously recovers the standard Starobinsky scenario in the appropriate weak-coupling limit, while the matter--geometry coupling produces a non-monotonic modification of the inflationary predictions. We find that weak couplings leave the standard predictions essentially unchanged, whereas stronger couplings can significantly shift the scalar spectral index and eventually become incompatible with current cosmic microwave background constraints. The quadratic torsion correction has a comparatively mild impact within the controlled low-energy regime, primarily affecting the tensor amplitude. We confront the resulting predictions with the Planck and BICEP/Keck constraints and find that the model remains viable over a broad range of the inflationary parameter space. Our results demonstrate that the matter--geometry coupling can leave a potentially testable imprint on primordial observables while retaining the successful plateau-inflation phenomenology of the Starobinsky scenario.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Coupling Independence Implies Zero-Freeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12456)
*Shuai Shao,Ke Shi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在最大度不超过 Δ 的有限简单图上，反铁磁 q-态 Potts 配分函数在 [0,1] 的图一致复邻域内无 Fisher 零点，条件为 Δ≥2 且 q≥11Δ/6；主要工具是分离子-壳层转移定理（separator-shell transfer theorem），并推广至 Lee-Yang 多圆盘与 Boolean Holant 问题的无零点区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于确定反铁磁 Potts 模型配分函数零点（Fisher zeros）在图一致意义下的无零点区域，并统一处理彩色场与 Holant 型计数问题的无零点性。摘要以 Δ 与 q 的不等式条件刻画该区域的适用范围。

Method: 方法上，对 q>11Δ/6 使用 Vigoda 翻转动力学（flip dynamics）的软版本，在整个 [0,1] 上建立耦合独立性（coupling independence）。核心技术工具为有界度图的诱导子图封闭类上 Potts 模型的分离子-壳层转移定理，条件为 q≥Δ+1，由 0 处的 Hamming 耦合独立性及每个区间 [δ,1] 上的一致耦合独立性界推出图一致无零点邻域。

Result: 结果为：在 Δ≥2 且 q≥11Δ/6 时，反铁磁 q-态 Potts 配分函数在 [0,1] 的图一致复邻域内无 Fisher 零点。此外获得均匀场附近针对顶点与边彩色场的无零点 Lee-Yang 多圆盘（polydiscs）；对有界度图上来自固定有限族对数凹对称特征（log-concave symmetric signatures）f 且 f(0)>0 的 Boolean Holant 问题（如 b-匹配），得到每个非负活动量有界盒周围的图一致无零点多管（polytubes），其并集构成非负正交象限的开无零点邻域。

Conclusion: 结论表明分离子-壳层转移定理可由耦合独立性输入导出图一致无零点区域，并适用于 Potts 零点、Lee-Yang 多圆盘及 Holant 多管等情形；附录进一步汇总了其他耦合独立性输入及其给出的无零点区域。

Abstract: For $Δ\ge2$ and $q\ge11Δ/6$, we prove that the antiferromagnetic $q$-state Potts partition function on finite simple graphs of maximum degree at most $Δ$ has no Fisher zeros in a graph-uniform complex neighbourhood of $[0,1]$. For $q>11Δ/6$, the proof establishes coupling independence throughout $[0,1]$ using a soft version of Vigoda's flip dynamics. Our main tool is a separator-shell transfer theorem for the Potts model on induced-subgraph closed classes of graphs of maximum degree at most $Δ$, with $q\geΔ+1$. It yields a graph-uniform zero-free neighbourhood of $[0,1]$ from Hamming coupling independence at $0$ and a uniform coupling-independence bound on each interval $[δ,1]$, $δ\in(0,1]$. We also obtain zero-free Lee-Yang polydiscs around the uniform field for vertex- and edge-colour fields. For Boolean Holant problems on bounded-degree graphs whose signatures come from a fixed finite family of log-concave symmetric signatures $f$ with $f(0)>0$, such as $b$-matchings, we obtain graph-uniform zero-free polytubes around every bounded box of nonnegative activities; their union is an open zero-free neighbourhood of the nonnegative orthant. An appendix summarizes further coupling-independence inputs and the zero-free regions they yield.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Optical absorption spectra from the $GW$-Bethe-Salpeter framework for thousands of atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11767)
*Maximilian Graml,Ritaj Tyagi,Rémi Pasquier,Štěpán Marek,Jan Wilhelm*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个结合实时传播（real-time propagation）、原子中心基组（atom-centered basis sets）与局部优化的实空间恒等式分辨（resolution of the identity）的 $GW$-BSE 框架，使每步传播成本随体系尺寸二次增长且约等于一次半局域泛函密度泛函自洽场迭代，并据此计算了含 4616 个原子的纳米石墨烯吸收谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一性原理预测光学性质仍是计算难题，尤其针对大尺寸与无序体系；$GW$-BSE 方法虽具预测精度，但传统实现随体系增大而成本过高。因此需要降低 $GW$-BSE 的计算标度，以扩展其可处理体系规模。

Method: 构建 $GW$-BSE 框架，融合实时传播、原子中心基组，以及局部优化的实空间恒等式分辨（resolution of the identity）。据摘要，该组合使每步传播成本随体系尺寸呈二次标度（quadratic scaling），并约等于密度泛函计算中一次自洽场迭代的成本。

Result: 该计算效率使作者能够计算含 4616 个原子的纳米石墨烯（nanographene）的吸收光谱（absorption spectrum），展示了对大体系的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为大型无序体系光学性质的常规计算开辟了路径，表明低成本 $GW$-BSE 光谱计算在大体系中的可行性。

Abstract: The frequency-dependent absorption and emission of light determine the colors of matter and underpin today's technologies, from solar cells to light-emitting diodes. Predicting optical properties from first principles remains a computational challenge, especially for large and disordered systems. The $GW$ plus Bethe-Salpeter equation ($GW$-BSE) approach provides predictive accuracy for optical properties, but conventional implementations become costly as system size grows. Here, we present a $GW$-BSE framework combining real-time propagation, atom-centered basis sets, and a locally optimized real-space resolution of the identity. The cost of each propagation step scales quadratically with system size and roughly equals the cost of one self-consistent-field iteration in a density-functional calculation with a semilocal functional. This computational efficiency allows us to calculate the absorption spectrum of a nanographene containing 4616 atoms and opens a path toward routine calculations of optical properties for large disordered systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Closed Universe, Quantum Entanglement and Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10667)
*Hao Geng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将全息量子电路模型中作为“观察者”的纠缠子系统，统一到因微分同胚自发破缺而出现的戈德斯通玻色子（Goldstone boson）描述之下，表明“观察者”的出现是“大质量岛屿”（Massive Islands）范式的体现，且引力子质量（graviton mass）属量子态特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力中闭合宇宙的希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）被认为是一维平凡的，为解释现实宇宙的丰富性，需引入“观察者”（observer）以恢复非平凡希尔伯特空间；已有三类具体模型，其中第二类与第三类的关系尚待厘清。

Method: 通过理论论证，将第二类模型（全息量子电路模型中与外部非引力系统纠缠的子系统）纳入第三类模型（微分同胚（diffeomorphisms）自发破缺时涌现的戈德斯通玻色子）框架，即由实现第一类的尝试所发现的描述方式。

Result: 证明第二类模型亦可统一至第三类；该结果指示“观察者”的出现是“大质量岛屿”（Massive Islands）范式的表现，并说明引力子质量（graviton mass）是量子态的特征。

Conclusion: 应将岛屿（islands）视为闭合宇宙，将伴随引力子质量的戈德斯通玻色子视为“观察者”，此情形正对应蒸发黑洞（evaporating black holes）的晚期；该工作暗示“观察者”能够“观察”量子引力中的整体对称性（global symmetries）。

Abstract: It has been argued that in quantum gravity the Hilbert space associated with any closed universe is trivially one-dimensional. To reconcile this argument with the richness of our own universe, it was suggested that a nontrivial Hilbert space will emerge once an ``observer" is included. Such an ``observer" has been modeled in different ways. There are three concrete classes of models: $\textbf{1)}$ The ``observer" is modeled by a term in the full Hamiltonian of the system which is linear in a phase space variable; $\textbf{2)}$ The ``observer" is modeled in the quantum circuit model of holography as a subsystem in the closed universe which is entangled with an external non-gravitational system; $\textbf{3)}$ The ``observer" is argued to be described by the emergent Goldstone bosons when the diffeomorphisms are spontaneously broken. In fact, the third class was discovered by an attempt to physically realize the first class. In this paper, we will show that the second class can also be unified into the third class. Our result indicates that the emergence of the ``observer" is a manifestation of the $\textit{Massive Islands}$ paradigm. It demonstrates that the graviton mass is a feature of the quantum state. One should think of the islands as closed universes and the Goldstone boson associated with the graviton mass as the ``observer", which is exactly the case at late-times for evaporating black holes. An implication of this work is that the ``observer" can ``observe" global symmetries in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [189] [Thermodynamics of interacting magnetic chain molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10671)
*Alexios P. Polychronakos,Konstantinos Sfetsos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文以平均场方法研究由 $m$ 个磁活性原子构成的链状分子磁体（nearest-neighbor couplings），推导自旋-$j$ $SU(2)$ 磁性原子链的热力学并分析相结构与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对链构型下具有任意数量磁活性原子的分子磁体，需要建立可解析的热力学框架，并厘清耦合强度比对平衡态与相变序列的影响，同时考察链端杂质导致的边界效应。

Method: 采用平均场方法（mean-field approach）处理最近邻耦合（nearest-neighbor couplings）的 spin-$j$ $SU(2)$ 磁性原子链，求解平衡态与稳定条件；对长链引入连续近似（continuum approximation）以解析相结构。摘要未给出具体哈密顿量形式或计算细节。

Result: 系统呈现丰富相结构，包含级联的（cascade）铁磁相变，其数量随耦合相对强度从 $m$（最大铁磁体）变化至零（顺磁体）。链端存在杂质（modified couplings）时，出现与顺磁相或完全铁磁相共存的边界态。连续描述中，低于其出现临界温度的磁化态对应扭结孤子（kink solitons）。

Conclusion: 链状分子磁体的相行为由耦合强度比控制，并可产生多级铁磁转变；端部杂质引入共存边界态；长链极限下的连续近似可将磁化态理解为扭结孤子。

Abstract: We study magnets composed of molecules with an arbitrary number $m$ of magnetically active atoms in a chain configuration interacting with nearest-neighbor couplings. We derive the thermodynamics of a chain of spin-$j$ $SU(2)$ magnetic atoms using a mean-field approach and determine the equilibrium states and stability conditions. The system exhibits a rich phase structure with a cascade of ferromagnetic phase transitions, their number ranging from $m$, for maximal ferromagnets, to zero, for paramagnets, depending on the relative strengths of the couplings. In the presence of impurities (modified couplings) at the ends of the chain, boundary states emerge that coexist with the paramagnetic phase or the fully ferromagnetic phase. For long chains, a continuum approximation can be taken that clarifies the structure of the phases. In the continuum description, magnetized states at temperatures lower than the critical temperature at which they appear correspond to kink solitons.

</details>


### [190] [Prescriptive Master Integrals of Maximal Weight at Two Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10672)
*Jacob L. Bourjaily*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套无质量粒子在四维情形下二圈阶（two loops）的平面主积分（planar master integrals）张成集，其中每个积分单独为纯积分（pure），并涵盖全部最大权（maximal-weight）贡献。在该基底下，红外发散（infrared divergence）对应的每个独立区域均由特定的主积分单独匹配，其余主积分在四维下显式为有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出所构建的对象及其性质。可推断的关切在于：将无质量粒子二圈平面主积分组织为一个逐项纯、且能按红外发散区域单独对应并隔离发散与有限部分的基底。

Method: 以构造性方式给出一个张成集（spanning set）：选取单个为纯的（individually pure）、平面的（planar）主积分，包含所有最大权贡献，并在该基底上实现红外发散独立区域与特定主积分的一一匹配。

Result: 所提出的基底中，与红外发散相关的每个独立区域均被特定主积分单独匹配；除这些匹配发散区域的主积分外，其余所有主积分在四维中显式有限（manifestly finite）。

Conclusion: 该基底把二圈无质量平面主积分按纯性与红外发散结构组织起来，使发散区域可逐项对应、有限部分可显式区分，从而为相关计算提供结构化表示。摘要未给出具体应用或验证结果。

Abstract: We describe the construction of a spanning set of individually pure, planar master integrals involving massless particles in four dimensions which include all maximal-weight contributions at two loops. In this basis, every independent region associated with infrared divergence is individually matched by specific masters, with all other masters being manifestly finite in four dimensions.

</details>


### [191] [Gluons and Knots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10673)
*Alfredo Guevara*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 单负螺旋度胶子（single minus helicity gluon）与任意多个正螺旋度胶子的散射振幅被论证为拓扑性的：振幅在运动学空间的各个胞腔（chamber）内为常数，共振位于壁上，因子化由穿壁（wall-crossing）决定。Yangian 不变性将该振幅归结为一个二维零 Wilson 环（2d null Wilson loop），并可提升为三维正横向链环（positive and transverse 3d link）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于揭示最大螺旋度破坏（maximally helicity violating）类振幅的深层几何与拓扑起源：将振幅的胞腔常值性、共振位置与因子化行为统一理解为穿壁现象，并借助 Yangian 不变性把振幅与 Wilson 环及链环拓扑联系起来，从而寻找状态计数式的组合描述及其与拓扑场论、弦论的潜在联系。

Method: 方法上，将穿壁（wall-crossing）与 Weinberg 软定理（Weinberg soft theorem）重新解释为子纽结（subknots）的绞链关系（skein relations）与 Reidemeister 移动，从而构造由 HOMFLY-PT 多项式（HOMFLY-PT polynomials）的“角”（corners）相加构成的状态计数描述。新公式通过 Lean 形式化的依赖图（DAG）严格推导，明确列出包括 Reidemeister III 不变性在内的精确假设，并进行了穷尽性检验。

Result: 结果显示，新公式经 Lean 形式化依赖图严格推导，并接受穷尽测试；其中包含新颖的“星形”（star）运动学情形，在此情形下振幅与状态计数被证明成为卡塔兰数（Catalan numbers），与纽结理论文献一致。超出经典极限后，该构造被认为提供了与拓扑场论（topological field theory）和弦论（string theory）之间一个猜想性但精确的联系。

Conclusion: 结论是：单负螺旋度胶子振幅具有本质的拓扑性质，可由基于 HOMFLY-PT 多项式角的态计数公式刻画，并在星形运动学下给出卡塔兰数；该框架在经典极限之外给出了通往拓扑场论与弦论的精确猜想性连接，且其推导假设已被形式化验证与穷尽测试。

Abstract: We argue that the scattering amplitude of a single minus helicity gluon with arbitrarily many positive helicity gluons is topological. The amplitude is constant in chambers of kinematic space, its resonances are located at walls and its factorization is determined by wall-crossing. Yangian invariance dictates the amplitude to be determined by a 2d null Wilson loop that can be lifted into a positive and transverse 3d link. By reinterpreting wall-crossing and the Weinberg soft theorem as skein relations and Reidemeister moves of the subknots, we construct a state-counting description consisting of adding the `corners' of their HOMFLY-PT polynomials. Beyond the classical limit, we argue that this provides a conjectured but precise connection to topological field and string theory.
  The new formula is rigorously derived via a Lean-formalized dependency graph (DAG) as detailed in a companion paper, laying out the precise assumptions including Reidemeister III invariance. It is further tested exhaustively, including novel `star' kinematics where the amplitude and state count are shown to become Catalan numbers, in agreement with knot theory literature.

</details>


### [192] [Hawking Amplitudes: Subtle Points About Saddle Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10678)
*Owen Lear,Daniel McLoughlin,Sebastian Mizera,John Staunton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究霍金振幅（Hawking amplitudes）中半经典近似（semi-classical approximation）与弱场近似（weak-field approximation）的相互作用，发现两者极限不可交换，并据此计算多种黑洞背景下的半经典霍金振幅，提出基于斯托克斯线（Stokes lines）确定热谱温度的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍金振幅为确定黑洞霍金谱（Hawking spectrum）提供了新途径，其借用散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）文献中的在壳（on-shell）技术。此前用于计算霍金振幅的半经典近似与弱场近似之间的关系尚不明确，需要厘清其相互影响。

Method: 论文考察半经典近似与弱场近似两种极限的相互作用，分析支配半经典极限的鞍点分析（saddle point analyses）中的微妙之处，并在解决相关问题后计算多种黑洞背景下的半经典霍金振幅；同时描述不同能量下支配霍金振幅的鞍点贡献。

Result: 发现半经典极限与弱场极限并不必然可交换（do not necessarily commute），揭示了鞍点分析中的微妙问题；在包括 Tangherlini、Reissner-Nordström 与 Hernquist 度规在内的多种黑洞背景中成功计算了半经典霍金振幅。

Conclusion: 通过解决鞍点分析中的问题，可将霍金振幅的半经典计算推广至多类黑洞背景，并提出一种依据霍金振幅的斯托克斯线（Stokes lines）来确定所得热谱（thermal spectrum）温度的新方法。

Abstract: Hawking amplitudes provide a new way of determining the Hawking spectrum of black holes, using on-shell techniques borrowed from the literature on scattering amplitudes. In this paper, we investigate the interplay between the semi-classical and weak-field approximations previously used to compute Hawking amplitudes. We find that these limits do not necessarily commute, highlighting subtleties with the saddle point analyses governing the semi-classical limit. Resolving these issues allows us to compute semi-classical Hawking amplitudes in a range of black-hole backgrounds, including Tangherlini, Reissner-Nordström, and Hernquist metrics. Moreover, we describe the saddle-point contributions dominating Hawking amplitudes for different energies and propose a new method for determining the temperature of the resulting thermal spectrum based on Stokes lines of Hawking amplitudes.

</details>


### [193] [Covariant BCFW for All Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10686)
*Jacob L. Bourjaily,Michael Plesser*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用大质量旋量-螺旋度形式（massive spinor-helicity formalism），提出一种新的大质量旋量构造，用以连接涉及大质量粒子振幅表示中的多种基选择，并由此推导出适用于任意质量情形的通用 BCFW 位移（BCFW shift）新表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涉及大质量粒子（massive particles）的振幅计算中，大质量旋量的基（basis）选择多样，缺乏统一联系；同时 BCFW 位移的既有表述在粒子有质量或无质量情形下适用性受限，需要更通用的框架。

Method: 采用大质量旋量-螺旋度形式，构建一种新的大质量旋量构造，使其能够沟通振幅表示中多种常用基的选择；基于该构造推导通用 BCFW 位移的新表述，该表述不依赖于所涉粒子是否为大质量或无质量（massless），也不依赖于具体哪些粒子具有质量。

Result: 得到一种可连接多种大质量旋量基的新构造，以及一个对质量情形普遍适用的 BCFW 位移新表述。

Conclusion: 该形式体系有助于在四维（four dimensions）中更一致地实现壳上递推（on-shell recursion），用于计算涉及大质量粒子的振幅。

Abstract: We use the massive spinor-helicity formalism to describe a novel construction for massive spinors which allows one to connect the many choices of bases useful in the representation of amplitudes involving massive particles, and we use this to derive a new formulation for the general BCFW shift that is valid regardless whether or which of the particles involved are massive or massless. This formalism should help facilitate a more coherent implementation of on-shell recursion for computing amplitudes involving massive particles in four dimensions.

</details>


### [194] [General Spin-1 Dynamics During Inflation: Microcausality Constraints and Cosmological Collider Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10693)
*Trevor Cheung,Carlos Duaso-Pueyo,David Stefanyszyn,Dong-Gang Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了在暴胀背景上传播的自旋-1场（spin-1 field）的普适理论，称为无推进Proca理论（Boostless Proca theory），将标准大质量Proca理论推广为含5个自由参数的拉格朗日量，并研究其与暴胀子（inflaton）耦合对宇宙学关联函数（cosmological correlators）的印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在保持标度不变性（scale invariance）、旋转不变性、Bunch-Davies条件与微观因果性（microcausality）的前提下，系统刻画暴胀背景上自旋-1场的普适行为，并寻找模型无关的新物理探针。

Method: 从出发点即固定三个自由度，构造含5个自由参数的Boostless Proca拉格朗日量（4个宇称偶项与1个宇称奇项）；施加微观因果性要求（即场算符在光锥外消失），并据此分析动能项的条件；随后将该自旋-1态与暴胀子耦合，计算其在宇宙学关联函数中的信号。

Result: 微观因果性要求动能项必须是规范不变（gauge invariant）的。对于双谱（bispectrum），未发现新信号，说明已知信号即为最一般形式，从而为非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）的这一主导形式提供了干净的模型无关探针；对于三谱（trispectrum）及更高点函数，该理论产生新形状，且5个参数原则上均可测量。

Conclusion: Boostless Proca理论给出了暴胀自旋-1场的普适描述，其双谱无新信号使其成为非高斯性主导形式的模型无关检验工具，而三谱及以上关联函数则可区分并测量全部5个参数。

Abstract: We construct the general theory of a spin-1 field propagating on an inflationary background consistent with scale invariance, rotations, Bunch-Davies conditions and microcausality. Our Boostless Proca theory generalises the usual massive Proca theory to a Lagrangian with 5 free parameters: 4 corresponding to parity-even terms and a single parity-odd term. We impose three degrees of freedom from the outset and find that microcausality, the statement that field operators vanish outside the lightcone, requires the kinetic terms to be gauge invariant. We couple this spin-1 state to the inflaton and study the signatures it imprints on cosmological correlators. We find no new signals for the bispectrum meaning that the known signals are the most general thereby providing a very clean model-independent probe of new physics in this leading form of non-Gaussianity. For the trispectrum and higher-point functions, the boostless Proca theory produces new shapes with all 5 parameters in principle measurable.

</details>


### [195] [Scattering entanglement entropy between one particle species and the others](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10704)
*Yuan-Hao Zheng,Jiayin Gu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作构建了一般散射过程中单个粒子种类 A 与其余末态之间的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy），并将冯诺依曼熵表示为散射概率与微分截面（differential cross section）的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在给出一个模型无关（model-independent）的散射纠缠熵框架，通过考虑完整末态的时间演化而非特定结果，克服对具体过程的依赖。

Method: 假设纯动量本征态初态，证明粒子 A 的 Fock 空间中约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrix）在零、单、多粒子态下块对角，单粒子态在动量基下对角，并据此用散射概率与微分截面写出 S_A。

Result: S_A 同时包含总散射概率（或截面）信息与微分分布信息，后者依赖相空间分辨因子（phase-space resolution factor），源于无限维动量希尔伯特空间；n≥2 的 Tsallis 与 Rényi 熵在领头阶正比于总散射概率且不含微分信息。

Conclusion: 结果具有一般性与模型无关性，适用于产生多个粒子 A 的概率为次领头（subleading）的情形。

Abstract: We construct the entanglement entropy of a general scattering process between one particle species (denoted as $A$) and the rest of the final state. Assuming a pure initial momentum eigenstate, we show that the reduced density matrix in particle $A$'s Fock space is block-diagonal in zero-, one- and multi-particle states, and is diagonal in the momentum basis for the one-particle state. As a result, we could write down the Von Neumann entropy of particle $A$, $S_A$, in terms of scattering probabilities and differential cross sections, assuming no more than one particle $A$ is produced in the final state. $S_A$ contains the information of both the total scattering probabilities (or cross sections) and the differential distributions --- the latter depends on a phase-space resolution factor as a result of the infinite-dimensional momentum Hilbert space. Our result is general and model-independent since we consider the full final state from time evolution ($S|i\rangle$) rather than a specific outcome, and is applicable as long as the probability for producing multiple particle $A$ is subleading. We also obtain the $n\geq2$ Tsallis and Rényi entropies in our framework and find out that, at the leading order, they are proportional to the total scattering probability and carry no differential information.

</details>


### [196] [Large Charge Jets in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM Energy Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10707)
*Alba Grassi,Cristoforo Iossa,Luigi Tizzano,Alexander Zhiboedov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 $\mathcal{N}=4$ $SU(N)$ SYM 中，针对大电荷规范算子所制备的态，计算了有限 $N$、大电荷 't Hooft 耦合下的能量-能量关联函数，并揭示其随探测角呈现多层次的物理区域结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由高标度量纲规范算子（canonical operator）触发的共形对称性自发破缺与希格斯机制（Higgs mechanism）如何影响能量-能量关联函数（energy-energy correlator），并探讨大电荷极限下不同角度尺度上的物理行为。

Method: 从已知的局域算子四点函数（four-point function）有限耦合结果出发，施加探测变换（detector transform）与傅里叶变换（Fourier transform），将所得关联函数表示为源-探测道（source-detector channel）中的无穷 OPE 求和，并进行数值与解析分析。

Result: 关联函数随 $z=(1-\cos\theta)/2$ 呈现分层区域：$z\ll\lambda^{-1}$ 时为小角度真空主导行为；$\lambda z\sim1$ 时出现非单调的介质诱导瞬态行为；在 $\lambda^{-1}\lesssim z\lesssim\lambda^{-1/3}$ 范围内呈幂律行为 $\sim1/(\lambda z^3)$；更大角度则趋于与角度无关的平台值。

Conclusion: 有限耦合能量-能量关联函数展现出由真空主导、介质瞬态到大电荷喷注（jet）区再到角度无关平台的一系列清晰物理区域，其标度由 $\lambda$ 与探测角共同决定。

Abstract: We compute the energy-energy correlator in a state created by a heavy canonical operator of scaling dimension $Δ_H$ in $\mathcal{N}=4$ $SU(N)$ SYM, at fixed $N$ and arbitrary values of the large charge 't Hooft coupling $λ\sim Δ_H g_{\rm YM}^2$. The state created by the canonical operator triggers spontaneous breaking of conformal symmetry and the Higgs mechanism, with squared W-boson masses proportional to $λ$. We start with the known finite-coupling result for the underlying four-point function of local operators, perform the detector and Fourier transforms and express the resulting energy-energy correlator as an infinite OPE sum in the source-detector channel, which we then analyze both numerically and analytically. We show that the finite-coupling energy-energy correlator exhibits a hierarchy of distinct physical regimes as a function of $z=(1-\cosθ)/2$, where $θ$ is the angle between the detectors. A small-angle vacuum-dominated behavior at $z\llλ^{-1}$ is followed by a non-monotonic medium-induced transient behavior at $λz \sim1$. A large charge jet regime follows, where the energy-energy correlator displays a power-law behavior $\sim1/(λz^3)$ for $λ^{-1}\lesssim z \lesssim λ^{-1/3}$, before approaching an angle-independent plateau at larger angles.

</details>


### [197] [Reconstructing BPS String Charge Cone in 6d N=(1,0) Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10711)
*Yuta Hamada,Hee-Cheol Kim,Kai Xu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出一套仅依赖有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）数据的系统方法，用于重建任意六维 $\mathcal N=(1,0)$ 超引力理论中本原 BPS 弦生成元，并确定完整的闭 BPS 弦锥（BPS string cone）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六维 $\mathcal N=(1,0)$ 超引力的张量分支（tensor branch）由带电于二形式规范场的 BPS 弦谱刻画，其荷构成张量荷格（tensor charge lattice）中的锥，极射线由在张量模空间边界处变为无张力（tensionless）的本原 BPS 弦荷生成。重建该锥对刻画低能理论有基础意义。

Method: 方法完全基于 EFT 数据：利用普适的 H-弦（H-string）将固定扇区与规范中性 E-弦（E-string）扇区分离，前者由分类的 LST 块（LST blocks）与规范反常数据决定；并结合完备性猜想（Completeness Conjecture）的 BPS 精化，以保证相关荷射线的布居。

Result: 该重建程序可确定任意 6d $(1,0)$ 超引力理论的完整闭 BPS 弦锥，并对低能理论给出新约束，可排除那些通过此前所有已知局域格点与反常一致性条件的候选 EFT。对具有几何张量基的理论，同一重建还可确定相应 Kähler 曲面的完整森锥（Mori cone）。

Conclusion: 基于 EFT 数据重建 BPS 弦锥提供了超越既有局域格点与反常条件的新一致性约束，并建立了几何张量基情形下与 Kähler 曲面森锥的对应关系。

Abstract: The tensor branch of six-dimensional $\mathcal N=(1,0)$ supergravity is characterized by a spectrum of BPS strings charged under the two-form gauge fields. Their charges span a cone in the tensor charge lattice, whose extremal rays are generated by primitive BPS string charges that become tensionless at the boundaries of the tensor moduli space. We develop a systematic procedure, based entirely on effective field theory data, to reconstruct the primitive BPS string generators and thereby determine the complete closed BPS string cone of any 6d $(1,0)$ supergravity theory. The key ingredients are the universal {\it H}-string, which allows us to isolate a fixed sector determined by classified LST blocks and gauge anomaly data from the remaining gauge-neutral E-string sector, and a BPS refinement of the Completeness Conjecture that ensures population of the relevant charge rays. We further show that this reconstruction provides new constraints on the low-energy theory and can rule out candidate EFTs that pass all previously known local lattice and anomaly consistency conditions. Separately, for theories admitting geometric tensor bases, we explain how the same reconstruction determines the complete Mori cone of the corresponding Kähler surface.

</details>


### [198] [All-multiplicity completion of maximally supersymmetric gauge amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10712)
*Ilmo Sung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明在平面四维最大超对称规范理论中，已知的六点约束（six-point constraints）对四点振幅（four-point amplitudes）的完备化（completion）在任意多重数下是充分的，并给出基于KLT双线性与球面积分归一化矩阵的构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清四点振幅系数需满足何种约束才能被完备化为任意多重数的无质量树振幅，并判定六点条件是否既必要又充分。

Method: 以导数展开（derivative expansion）中的形式级数构造无质量树振幅，固定超对称Yang-Mills三点振幅、未破缺R对称性（R-symmetry）与纯标量振幅的宇称不变性；利用场论Kawai-Lewellen-Tye双线性（field-theory KLT bilinear）及球面积分（sphere integrals）的归一化矩阵，通过对矩阵对数齐次分量取指数实现奇数系数的独立调节。

Result: 当领头四点接触系数为正时，对数参数化（logarithmic parameterization）中的偶数系数被固定，而任意实数奇数系数序列均可在所有导数阶上实现；指数化操作在保持局域性（locality）与经由完整无质量多重态的因子化（factorization）的同时独立改变奇数系数。

Conclusion: 六点条件对上述完备化是充分且必要的；一旦更高点接触相互作用按相同对称性与因子化条件一致选取，更高多重数一致性不对这些四点系数施加额外限制。

Abstract: We show that the known six-point constraints on four-point amplitudes are sufficient for completion at arbitrary multiplicity in planar, four-dimensional maximally supersymmetric gauge theory. We construct massless tree amplitudes as formal series in the derivative expansion, with the standard supersymmetric Yang-Mills three-point amplitudes, unbroken R-symmetry, and parity invariance of amplitudes with only external scalars, holding the scalar labels fixed. For a positive leading four-point contact coefficient, the even coefficients in the logarithmic parameterization are fixed, while every real sequence of odd coefficients is realized by a completion at all derivative orders. The construction uses the field-theory Kawai-Lewellen-Tye bilinear and normalized matrices of sphere integrals. Exponentiating homogeneous components of their matrix logarithms varies the odd coefficients independently while preserving locality and factorization through the complete massless multiplet. The six-point conditions are therefore necessary and sufficient for such a completion. Higher-multiplicity consistency imposes no further restrictions on these four-point coefficients once the higher-point contact interactions are chosen consistently with the same symmetries and factorization conditions.

</details>


### [199] [Polynomial and Eigenspace Factorisations for the One-loop Dilatation Operator at Finite $N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10745)
*Adrian Padellaro,Sanjaye Ramgoolam,Ryo Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超杨-米尔斯理论（super Yang-Mills theory）$su(2)$ 扇区中规范不变双矩阵多项式的代数结构，证明单圈膨胀算符（one-loop dilatation operator）特征多项式的整除关系，并给出 $SU(N)$ 与 $U(N)$ 本征态之间的联系与因子化猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限 $N$ 下规范群 $U(N)$ 或 $SU(N)$ 的谱问题缺乏直接的置换（permutation）描述框架，尤其 $N<L$ 时态空间受杨图（Young diagram）截断限制，需要厘清其与 $N\ge L$ 情形的关系。

Method: 将态空间表述为代数 $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$，即置换中心化子代数（permutation centraliser algebras）的直和，视为群代数 $\mathbb{C}[S_L]$ 的子代数；对 $U(N)$ 与 $SU(N)$ 分别考察单圈膨胀算符的本征空间，并通过维数计数（dimension counting）检验交集猜想。

Result: 第一，$\mathcal{A}_N(L)$ 上单圈膨胀算符的特征多项式整除自 $\mathcal{A}(L)$ 延拓至 $N<L$ 的特征多项式；第二，$SU(N)$ 的每个本征态通过单字母迹（single-letter trace）乘法生成 $U(N)$ 中的无穷本征态塔，给出按迹数标记的本征空间因子化（eigenspace factorisation）；并猜想 $SU(N)$ 不变多项式空间同构于 $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$ 与错排（derangements）张成子空间的交集。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限 $N$ 的 $SU(N)$ 谱问题提供了直接的置换框架，并给出其上单圈膨胀算符的构造；基于该提议计算的光谱与 $U(N)$ 理论本征空间因子化的预言一致。

Abstract: We study gauge-invariant two-matrix polynomials of degree $L$, corresponding to the states in the $su(2)$ sector of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory with gauge group $U(N)$ or $SU(N)$ at finite $N$. For the $U(N)$ theory, the state space has the structure of an algebra $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$, which is a direct sum of permutation centraliser algebras, i.e. sub-algebras of the group algebra $\mathbb{C}[S_L]$ of $S_L$. For $N \ge L$, $\mathcal{A}_N (L) = \mathcal{A} (L)$ is independent of $N$, and the one-loop dilatation operator acts on $\mathcal{A}(L)$ with $N$ as a parameter. For $N < L$, $\mathcal{A}_N (L)$ is a subspace of $\mathcal{A}(L)$ defined using a finite-$N$ cutoff on Young diagrams with $L$ boxes. We establish two results on the eigenspaces of the one-loop dilatation operator. Our first main result is that the characteristic polynomial of the one-loop dilatation operator on $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$ divides the characteristic polynomial from $\mathcal{A}(L)$, continued to $N < L$. Our second result is that each eigenstate of the one-loop dilatation operator in the $SU(N)$ theory generates an infinite tower of eigenstates in the $U(N)$ theory, through the multiplication of single-letter traces. These towers define an eigenspace factorisation of the $U(N)$ theory labelled by the number of such traces. Further, we conjecture that the space of $SU(N)$-invariant polynomials in two traceless matrices at finite $N$ is isomorphic to the intersection of $\mathcal{A}_N(L)$ with the subspace of $\mathbb{C}[S_L]$ spanned by derangements. This intersection conjecture is supported by dimension counting. Finally, we propose a one-loop dilatation operator on the intersection, thus providing a direct permutation framework for the $SU(N)$ spectral problem at finite $N$. The computed spectra based on the proposal agree with the prediction from the eigenspace factorisation in the $U(N)$ theory.

</details>


### [200] [Generalised Gibbs Ensembles from the ODE/IM Correspondence: the Lee--Yang Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10716)
*Sujay K. Ashok,Max Downing,Faisal Karimi,Adarsh Sudhakar,Roberto Tateo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一套系统方案，用于构造广义吉布斯系综（generalised Gibbs ensembles）的 ODE/IM 对应物，并将其应用于 Lee–Yang 共形场论（conformal field theory）的手征部分。该方法通过形变三次振子并对 WKB 周期施加逐阶匹配条件，预测局域守恒荷的形变本征值，结果与共形场论独立计算及 TBA 数值解一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODE/IM 对应（ODE/IM correspondence）已被用于联系可积模型的热力学与常微分方程谱问题，但其向广义吉布斯系综的推广尚缺乏系统构造。本文旨在建立该推广的一般框架，并以 Lee–Yang 共形场论作为具体检验场景。

Method: 将化学势（chemical potentials）编码进三次振子（cubic oscillator）的形变中，逐阶要求大能量 WKB 周期精确复现相应热力学 Bethe ansatz（thermodynamic Bethe ansatz, TBA）方程的驱动项；次阶 WKB 周期则给出局域守恒荷的形变本征值。对两个态详细计算自旋-5 系综（spin-5 ensemble）：基态（φ 主场的模，对偶于形变三次振子）与恒等模的主场（对偶于同一振子附加“表观奇点”即双极点，其位置由平凡单值性条件固定）。

Result: 两种情形下 Stokes 乘子（Stokes multiplier）均满足 Lee–Yang Y-系统（Y-system），且形变本征值与独立的共形场论计算在所检验阶数上一致。基态能量级数的 Borel 重求和（Borel resummation）在化学势的多个数量级范围内重现 TBA 数值解。

Conclusion: 该方案仅依赖 ODE/IM 对应的一般性质，原则上可推广至其他可积模型中的广义吉布斯系综，并为形变守恒荷谱提供可预测、可交叉验证的解析途径。

Abstract: We introduce a systematic scheme for constructing the ODE/IM counterpart of generalised Gibbs ensembles, and apply it to the chiral sector of the Lee--Yang conformal field theory. We encode the chemical potentials in a deformation of the cubic oscillator, fixed order by order by requiring that the large-energy WKB period reproduces exactly the driving term of the corresponding thermodynamic Bethe ansatz equation; the subleading WKB periods then yield, as predictions, the deformed eigenvalues of the local conserved charges. We work out the spin-5 ensemble in detail for two states of the model: the ground state, in the module of the primary $φ$, dual to the deformed cubic oscillator, and the primary of the identity module, dual to the same oscillator supplemented by an apparent singularity, a double pole whose position is fixed by a trivial-monodromy condition. In both cases, the Stokes multiplier obeys the Lee--Yang Y-system, and the deformed eigenvalues match, to the orders checked, an independent conformal-field-theory computation. The Borel resummation of the resulting series for the ground-state energy reproduces the numerical TBA solution over many orders of magnitude in the chemical potential. The scheme relies only on general features of the ODE/IM correspondence and applies to generalised Gibbs ensembles in other integrable models.

</details>


### [201] [The three- and four-loop equal-mass sunset integrals and local Calabi--Yau fourfolds and fivefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10821)
*Hartmut Maennel,Pierre Vanhove*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用局部 Calabi–Yau 四重与五重流形（local Calabi–Yau fourfold and fivefold）的亏格零 Gromov–Witten 理论（genus-zero Gromov–Witten theory），给出了大欧几里得动量下等质量二维三圈与四圈 sunset Feynman 积分的表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两圈 sunset 积分由局部 Calabi–Yau 三重流形的预势（prepotential）支配，但这一框架无法直接推广到更高圈数。当 L≥3 时，无标记稳定映射的模空间具有正虚拟维数且不存在预势，因此需要寻找替代的数学对象来刻画相应的 Feynman 积分。

Method: 以局部 Calabi–Yau 四重、五重流形 X_L = Tot(K_{F_L}) 的亏格零 Gromov–Witten 理论为工具，其中 F_L 为 (P^1)^{L+1} 中多重次数 (1,…,1) 的光滑超曲面；引入带插入 γ_L = H^{L-1}/(L-1)! 的单点势 F_{γ_L}、其引力后代（gravitational descendants）以及来自 Gamma 类的 ζ(3) 边界项。三圈情形下还借助 Picard–Fuchs 方程与 Mellin 变换的极点部分独立推导解析归一化。

Result: 在镜坐标（mirror coordinate）R 的 Frobenius 展开中，R^{L-1} 的系数为 -θ_Q F_{γ_L}，其中 Novikov 变量 Q = exp(R)。三圈时整个 Feynman 积分仅为 F_{γ_3} 的显式表达式，因亏格零递推（genus-zero recursion）可由其确定所有后代级数；四圈时需两个对角级数，且 ζ(3) 项本身被 θ_Q F_{γ_4} 修正。三圈情形下还证明了任意整插入的多重覆盖变换的整性（integrality）并给出显式量子-K 公式，计算了精确的周期联络多项式。此外给出欧几里得轴上的端点值、Novikov 变量 Q 中 Taylor 半径的界、四圈端点非解析性的计算机辅助证明，以及二、三、四圈归一化的比较。

Conclusion: 高圈 sunset Feynman 积分可由高维局部 Calabi–Yau 流形的亏格零 Gromov–Witten 理论描述，其中预势被带插入的单点势、其引力后代及 ζ(3) 边界项所取代；该框架同时提供了归一化、整性与解析性质等方面的定量结果，并将二、三、四圈的归一化纳入统一比较。

Abstract: We determine the equal-mass two-dimensional three- and four-loop sunset Feynman integrals $I^{(3)}_\circleddash$ and $I^{(4)}_\circleddash$ at large Euclidean momentum in terms of the genus-zero Gromov--Witten theory of the local Calabi--Yau fourfold and fivefold $X_L=\operatorname{Tot}(K_{F_L})$, where $F_L\subset(\mathbb P^1)^{L+1}$, $L=3,4$, is a smooth hypersurface of multidegree $(1,\ldots,1)$. At two loops the sunset integral is governed by the prepotential of a local Calabi--Yau threefold. For $L\geq3$ the moduli spaces of unmarked stable maps have positive virtual dimension and there is no prepotential, therefore the Feynman integral is governed instead by the one-point potential $\mathcal F_{γ_L}$ with insertion $γ_L=H^{L-1}/(L-1)!$, by its gravitational descendants, and by $ζ(3)$ boundary terms coming from the Gamma class. At both loop orders the coefficient of $R^{L-1}$ in the Frobenius expansion in the mirror coordinate $R$ is $-θ_Q\mathcal F_{γ_L}$ with the Novikov variable $Q=\exp(R)$. At three loops the whole Feynman integral is an explicit expression in $\mathcal F_{γ_3}$ alone, because genus-zero recursion determines all descendant series from it; at four loops two diagonal series suffice, and the $ζ(3)$ term is itself corrected by $θ_Q\mathcal F_{γ_4}$. At three loops we also derive the analytic normalization independently, from the Picard--Fuchs equation and the polar part of a Mellin transform, prove integrality of the multiple-cover transforms for arbitrary integral insertions together with an explicit quantum-$K$ formula, and compute the exact period connection polynomials. Finally we give endpoint values along the Euclidean axis, bounds on the Taylor radii in the Novikov variable $Q$, a computer-assisted proof of the four-loop endpoint nonanalyticity, and a comparison of the normalizations at two, three and four loops.

</details>


### [202] [Carroll-Weyl gauged null strings in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10717)
*Sarthak Duary,Sourav Maji*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 Poincaré 补丁中 AdS_D 上的 Carroll-Weyl 规范零弦（gauged null string）及其 BRST 量子化，构造径向适配约束基并获得扩展 BMS_3 代数，分类中心扩展后在最高权一阶表示中求得临界维数 D=27。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 Carroll-Weyl 零弦的残余规范对称性与反常结构，并与平坦时空中的最小 Carroll-Weyl 理论进行结构性对比，以明确 AdS 径向流与膨胀约束所导致的差异。

Method: 采用 Poincaré 坐标 X^A=(z,x^i)，在共形相关标架下将径向共形 Killing 向量 K=∂_z 化为伸缩型；对依赖场的伸缩型哈密顿约束作重标度，得到径向适配基 M, L, S。随后分类残余代数允许的中心扩展，并构造含径向 Carroll-Weyl 约束鬼对的 BRST 复形（BRST complex）。

Result: M, L, S 的模式构成被一个阿贝尔权一电流扩展的 BMS_3 代数；残余代数允许四个独立上同调类，分别位于 LL、LM、LS、SS 通道。在正则最高权一阶表示中仅 Virasoro 型中心扩展实现，即 (c_LL,c_LM,c_LS,c_SS)=(2D-54,0,0,0)，对应非平凡 BRST 余圈抵消给出 D=27。

Conclusion: 在最高权一阶表示下，AdS 径向适配的 Carroll-Weyl 零弦临界维数为 D=27。该结果与最小平坦 Carroll-Weyl 理论在结构上不同，后者因膨胀约束 X·P 而产生额外反常通道。

Abstract: We study the Carroll-Weyl gauged null string in the Poincaré patch of $\mathrm{AdS}_D$ and its BRST quantization. In Poincaré coordinates $X^A=(z,x^i)$, the radial conformal Killing vector $K=\partial_z$ becomes homothetic in a suitable conformally related frame. A field-dependent rescaling of the homothetic Hamiltonian constraint then leads to the radial-adapted basis $\mathcal{M}=\frac{1}{2}(P_z^2+P_iP^i)$, $\mathcal{L}=-P_AX'^A$, and $\mathcal{S}=P_z$, with $\mathcal{M}$ invariant along the Carroll-Weyl radial flow. Their modes form a $\mathrm{BMS}_3$ algebra extended by an abelian weight-one current. We classify the central extensions allowed by this residual algebra and find four independent cohomology classes in the $LL$, $LM$, $LS$, and $SS$ channels. We then construct the associated BRST complex, including the ghost pair for the radial Carroll-Weyl constraint. In the canonical highest-weight first-order representation, only the Virasoro-type central extension is realized: $(c_{LL},c_{LM},c_{LS},c_{SS})=(2D-54,0,0,0)$. Cancellation of the corresponding nontrivial BRST cocycle therefore gives $D=27$ in this representation. This differs structurally from the minimal flat Carroll-Weyl theory, where the dilation constraint $X\cdot P$ gives extra anomaly channels.

</details>


### [203] [Surface defects in $\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills: exact results and holographic bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10723)
*Lorenzo Bianchi,Davide Bonomi,Maddalena Ferragatta,Valentina Forini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超杨-米尔斯（Super Yang-Mills）中半 BPS 面缺陷（half-BPS surface defect）存在时手征原初算符（chiral primary operator）的两点函数，求解超共形 Ward 恒等式并构造所有扇区的体与缺陷超共形块（superconformal block），通过映射到手征代数（chiral algebra）非微扰地确定全纯扇区，并结合缺陷色散关系重构大 N 强耦合下维数二算符的完整关联函数，提取大量新的缺陷 CFT 数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该缺陷仅保留横向旋转与全局 U(1) R 对称性（R-symmetry）的对角组合，使关联函数多依赖一个交比并按确定角荷分扇区，其中带电扇区在仅保持横向旋转的缺陷中并无对应物，因而需要发展可处理带电扇区的超共形块方法。

Method: 求解超共形 Ward 恒等式（superconformal Ward identities），并在各扇区（含带电扇区）构造体与缺陷超共形块；将全纯扇区映射到手征代数关联函数以非微扰确定，并将该映射推广至破坏横向旋转的缺陷（需重定义二维流代数（current algebra）的模）；再利用缺陷色散关系（defect dispersion relation）重构大 N 强耦合下维数二手征原初算符的完整关联函数，并用 Witten 图自举独立验证。

Result: 在带电扇区中长多重态（long multiplet）缺失，所得结果在 N 与耦合常数上被猜想为精确；弱耦合展开与已知结果完全一致，并从关联函数中提取出无穷多个新的缺陷 CFT 数据；强耦合结果也由旋转不变轨迹上的 Witten 图自举独立重现。

Conclusion: 半 BPS 面缺陷上的手征原初算符关联函数可由超共形块与手征代数映射系统求解，带电扇区的无长多重态结构使结果具有非微扰精确性，为缺陷 CFT 数据提供了新的解析来源。

Abstract: We study two-point functions of chiral primary operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills in the presence of a half-BPS surface defect. Such a defect preserves only the diagonal combination of transverse rotations and of a global U(1) R-symmetry, so that the correlator depends on one additional cross-ratio and splits into sectors of definite angular charge. We solve the superconformal Ward identities and construct the bulk and defect superconformal blocks in all sectors, including the charged ones, which have no analogue for defects preserving transverse rotations. The solution contains a holomorphic sector, which we fix non-perturbatively by mapping it to a chiral algebra correlator. We extend this map to defects breaking transverse rotations, where it requires a redefinition of the modes of the two-dimensional current algebra, and use it to fix the charged sectors as well. We then use the defect dispersion relation to reconstruct the full correlator of the dimension-two chiral primary at leading order at large N and strong coupling. In the charged sectors long multiplets are absent and the answer is conjecturally exact in both N and the coupling; expanding it at weak coupling, we find perfect agreement with known results. From these correlators we extract infinitely many new pieces of defect CFT data. Finally, we reproduce the strong-coupling answer by an independent Witten diagram bootstrap on the rotation-invariant locus.

</details>


### [204] [The hidden sunrise in the energy-energy correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10734)
*Matthew D. Schwartz,Xiaoyuan Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文补全了 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超杨-米尔斯（super Yang-Mills, SYM）理论中能量-能量关联函数（energy-energy correlator, EEC）在 NNLO 阶的完整解析结果，包含此前缺失的椭圆扇区（elliptic sector），并以迭代爱森斯坦积分（iterated Eisenstein integrals）表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEC 是可高阶级数解析计算的少数对撞机可观测量之一，能揭示散射振幅无法呈现的量子场论特征并可直接与数据对比；此前 Henn 等人的 NNLO 结果仅以调和多重对数（harmonic polylogarithms, HPLs）和一个含椭圆曲线的两重积分表示，椭圆部分尚未完成。

Method: 补全剩余椭圆扇区的解析计算，该扇区与日出费曼积分（sunrise Feynman integrals）密切相关；通过比较 EEC 与日出积分的 $j$-不变量（$j$-invariants），并借助模曲线与迭代爱森斯坦积分的表示方法给出结果。

Result: 发现在莫比乌斯映射（Möbius map）下，EEC 与日出积分的 $j$-不变量完全一致，故 EEC 的椭圆扇区位于日出积分的 $\Gamma_1(6)$ 模曲线（modular curve）上；解析结果可用迭代爱森斯坦积分表示，并支持高精度快速数值求值。

Conclusion: 该解析形式首次支持 EEC 朗道自举（Landau bootstrap）的研究；对 $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM 中函数空间的理解为处理 QCD 中 EEC 的椭圆扇区提供了具体抓手。文中许多技术性结果由 AI 在人类监督下完成。

Abstract: The energy-energy correlator (EEC) is one of a handful of collider observables that can be computed analytically to high orders. As an energy-weighted cross-section, it exposes features of quantum field theory that scattering amplitudes do not, and it can be compared directly to data. As with scattering amplitudes, the analytic expressions require special functions beyond polylogarithms. In Henn et al., the EEC was computed to next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) for $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills (SYM) theory, and the result is expressed in terms of both harmonic polylogarithms (HPLs) and one two-fold integral, which contains elliptic curves. In this work, we report a complete result including the remaining elliptic sector, which is closely related to the sunrise Feynman integrals. We find the $j$-invariants of the EEC and the sunrise agree identically under a Möbius map, and thus the elliptic sector in the EEC lives on the $Γ_1(6)$ modular curve of the sunrise integral. We then express the answer in terms of iterated Eisenstein integrals, which can be evaluated to high precision quickly. The analytic form also allows a first study of the EEC Landau bootstrap, and the understanding of its function space in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM offers a concrete handle on the elliptic sector of the EEC in QCD. Many of the technical results in this paper were completed with AI under human supervision.

</details>


### [205] [Quantum geometry at arbitrary genus - I: Anharmonic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10940)
*Mustafa Türe,Mithat Ünsal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文借助复代数几何（complex algebraic geometry）与精确 WKB 方法，解决了高亏格势中 P/NP 关系是否成立这一开放问题，给出了适用于任意亏格势的全阶量子 P/NP 关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在亏格-1 势的量子力学中，围绕拓扑平庸鞍点（saddle）的微扰论与非微扰瞬子（instanton）鞍点可通过 P/NP 关系构造性地联系起来；但该关系能否推广到更高亏格势一直是未解问题。

Method: 经典能量守恒关系定义亏格-g 黎曼面（Riemann surface）X，其周期由雅可比簇（Jacobian variety）经 Abel-Jacobi 映射组织，并独立地由 Picard-Fuchs 方程的解基组织；研究证明 WKB 活跃周期与 Picard-Fuchs 基之间的线性变换属于 SL(2g,Z) 但不属于辛群（symplectic group）Sp(2g,Z)（当 g≥2），并通过构造修正的黎曼双线性恒等式（Riemann bilinear identity）加以解决。

Result: 该群不匹配是导致高亏格 P/NP 关系难以显式给出的障碍；通过基于适当变换后相交矩阵的修正黎曼双线性恒等式可将其消除，WKB 活跃周期精确满足该恒等式。据此导出了适用于任意亏格势的全阶量子 P/NP 关系，并对亏格-2（五阶与六阶）及亏格-3（七阶与八阶）非谐振子（anharmonic oscillator）作了显式推导。

Conclusion: 高亏格势中的 P/NP 关系确实存在，其显式构造的障碍源于 WKB 活跃周期基与 Picard-Fuchs 基之间的变换不再属于辛群；借助修正的双线性恒等式可规避该障碍，从而将 P/NP 关系推广至任意亏格。

Abstract: In quantum mechanics of genus-1 potentials, perturbation theory around the topologically trivial saddle and non-perturbative instanton saddle are constructively related via the P/NP relation. Whether such a relation persists for higher-genus potentials has remained an open problem. In this work, we resolve it using tools from complex algebraic geometry combined with exact WKB. The classical energy conservation relation $p^2 = 2(E-V(q))$ defines a genus-g Riemann surface $X$, whose periods are naturally organized by its Jacobian variety $J(X) = \mathbb{C}^{\text{g}}/Λ$ via the Abel-Jacobi map, and independently by the solution basis of the associated Picard-Fuchs equations. We show that the physically relevant, WKB-active cycles are generally related to the Picard-Fuchs basis by a linear transformation that lies in $\text{SL}(2\text{g},\mathbb{Z})$ but not in the symplectic group $\text{Sp}(2\text{g},\mathbb{Z})$ once $\text{g}\geq2$, in contrast to the genus-1 case, where the two groups coincide. This mismatch is the obstruction that has made an explicit higher-genus P/NP relation elusive. We show that it can be resolved by passing to a modified Riemann bilinear identity, built from a suitably transformed intersection matrix, which the WKB-active periods do satisfy exactly. The result is an all-orders quantum P/NP relation valid for potentials of arbitrary genus, which we derive explicitly for genus-2 (quintic and sextic) and genus-3 (septic and octic) anharmonic oscillators.

</details>


### [206] [Fourier analysis of compact oscillons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10746)
*Pawel Klimas,Leandro Roza Livramento,João Saldanha Streibel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用傅里叶方法（Fourier methods）重新推导了符号-戈登模型（signum-Gordon model）中精确的紧致振荡子（compact oscillon），给出离散模态级数表示，并推广至边界周期运动的摇摆振荡子（swaying compact oscillon）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前解析势（analytic potentials）中的微扰级数无法收敛，且存在关于空间局域、小振幅周期态的不存在定理（non-existence theorem）；本文旨在为紧致振荡子提供收敛的精确描述并规避该定理限制。

Method: 采用傅里叶方法（Fourier methods），将振荡子分解为基频及其奇数高次谐波（odd higher harmonics）构成的无穷一致收敛离散模态级数；并分析级数截断精度，进而推广到具有周期运动边界的摇摆振荡子。

Result: 主模态（primary mode）单独即可给出对精确解的高精度近似，加入最初两个非零谐波模态后一致性显著提升；推广情形下摇摆振荡子需要偶数阶模态（even-order modes），其与奇源项的耦合引发动态调制，产生摇摆运动，而空间傅里叶剖面（spatial Fourier profiles）本身不随时间变化。

Conclusion: 傅里叶级数框架可精确描述紧致振荡子并规避不存在定理，且通过奇偶模态耦合可自然推广至具有周期运动边界的摇摆振荡子。

Abstract: We present a new derivation of the exact compact oscillon in the signum-Gordon model using Fourier methods. This approach decomposes the oscillon into an infinite, uniformly convergent series of discrete modes consisting of a fundamental frequency and its odd higher harmonics, in contrast to standard analytic potentials, where the corresponding infinite perturbative series fails to converge. Crucially, our oscillon evades the non-existence theorem for spatially localized, small-amplitude periodic states. We investigate the accuracy of truncating the Fourier series and find that the primary mode alone provides a remarkably accurate approximation of the exact solution, with agreement improving significantly when the first two non-vanishing harmonic modes are included. Furthermore, we generalize this framework to a swaying compact oscillon with a periodically moving boundary. We show that describing such an oscillon requires even-order modes, whose coupling with the odd source terms induces a dynamic modulation that generates the swaying motion, even though the spatial Fourier profiles themselves are time-independent.

</details>


### [207] [Reconstruction of Black Hole Islands from Hawking Radiation via the Algebraic Petz Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10769)
*Niloofar Vardian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用代数量子信息方法，通过 Petz 映射构造了岛屿（island）重构协议，实现了从霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）中恢复黑洞内部算符，并将该结果与 Papadodimas-Raju 镜像算符（mirror operators）结构相统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 岛屿方案（island proposal）虽保证蒸发黑洞满足幺正的 Page 曲线（Page curve），但视界深处算符需可从渐近霍金辐射完全重构；然而内部因果不连通且高度动力学化，依赖全局等距（global isometries）或连续模流（continuous modular flow）的标准纠缠楔重构（entanglement wedge reconstruction）方法无法给出该映射。

Method: 采用代数 Petz 映射（algebraic Petz map）构造岛屿重构协议：运用模理论（modular theory）将岛屿算符反射至可及的补集，再将其输运至边界并映射入辐射代数（radiation algebra）。

Result: 该纯代数推导自然导出了 Papadodimas-Raju 镜像算符（mirror operators）的结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为岛屿公式（island formula）提供了严格的操作性字典（operational dictionary），证明黑洞内部所困信息可以被解码。

Abstract: The island proposal guarantees a unitary Page curve for evaporating black holes, implying that operators deep behind the horizon must be fully reconstructable from asymptotic Hawking radiation. However, because the interior is causally disconnected and highly dynamical, standard entanglement wedge reconstruction methods, relying on global isometries or continuous modular flow, fail to provide this mapping. We resolve this by constructing a protocol for island reconstruction using the algebraic Petz map. By using modular theory, we reflect island operators into the accessible complement, transport them to the boundary, and map them into the radiation algebra. Furthermore, we demonstrate that this purely algebraic derivation naturally yields the structure of Papadodimas-Raju mirror operators. This framework provides a rigorous operational dictionary for the island formula, proving that information trapped inside a black hole can be decoded.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond gauge-parameter dependence: Further evidence for asymptotic safety in Scalar-Tensor theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10791)
*Samuel M. P. Antunes,Antonio D. Pereira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作检验标量-张量理论（Scalar-Tensor theories）渐近安全（Asymptotic Safety）框架下重整化群（renormalization group）不动点在计算设定被显著修改时的稳健性，采用显含动力学标量场的规范条件并考察不同场参数化，发现四维时空中属于高斯物质类（Gaussian Matter class）的不动点与前人研究一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近安全为标量-张量理论的非微扰可重整量子化提供了有前景的框架，且已有越来越多的证据支持多种标量-张量模型中存在非平凡重整化群不动点。因此需要检验这些不动点在计算设定发生显著改变时是否依然稳健。

Method: 采用显式依赖于动力学标量场的规范条件，从而超越文献中常用的标准线性协变规范（linear covariant gauges）的纯粹规范参数变形；并探索不同的场参数化（field parameterizations），对相关临界指数（critical exponents）进行计算与分析。

Result: 在四维时空中识别出属于高斯物质类的不动点，且与先前研究一致。与标量场质量和非最小标量-曲率耦合（non-minimal scalar-curvature coupling）相关的临界指数，其实部绝对值非常小；分析还表明标量场反常维度（anomalous dimension）与场参数化会影响这些临界指数，并出现具有两个或四个相关方向（relevant directions）的不动点候选者。

Conclusion: 所考察的不动点在规范条件显著修改与不同场参数化下仍可复现，表明其具有一定的稳健性；同时这些不动点候选者可对应两个或四个相关方向，其临界指数受标量场反常维度与参数化选择的影响。

Abstract: Asymptotic Safety provides a promising framework for the quantization of Scalar-Tensor theories as non-perturbatively renormalizable quantum field theories. Over the past years, growing evidence has accumulated for the existence of non-trivial renormalization group fixed points in a variety of Scalar-Tensor models. In this work, we investigate the robustness of these fixed points under significant modifications of the computational setup. In particular, we employ a gauge condition that explicitly depends on the dynamical scalar field, going beyond a mere gauge-parameter deformation of the standard linear covariant gauges commonly used in the literature. Exploring different field parameterizations, we identify fixed points belonging to the Gaussian Matter class in four spacetime dimensions that are consistent with previous studies. The real part of the critical exponents associated with the scalar-field mass and the non-minimal scalar-curvature coupling turn out to have very small absolute value. We provide a comprehensive exploration of how the anomalous dimension of the scalar field and field parameterization impact those critical exponents showing that fixed-point candidates with two or four relevant directions emerge.

</details>


### [209] [Massive de Sitter Correlators as Finite Mellin-Barnes Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10921)
*Amara McCune*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用德西特传播子（de Sitter propagator）的梅林-巴恩斯表示（Mellin-Barnes representation），将树级重粒子交换约化为在物理运动学区收敛的有限积分，并将单圈图（one-loop diagram）约化为同一被积函数乘以平直空间动量因子，从而无需解析延拓即可计算三重交换双谱（triple-exchange bispectrum）及一般质量下的完整单圈三角形图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙碰撞器（cosmological collider）预测需要对暴胀期间重粒子交换与圈图信号进行精确计算，而传统方法通常依赖解析延拓，在计算多重交换与任意质量圈图时存在技术困难。

Method: 采用德西特传播子的梅林-巴恩斯表示（Mellin-Barnes representation），在质量无关的围道上（mass-independent contours）将树级重粒子交换化为有限积分，并将单圈图化为相同被积函数与平直空间动量因子的乘积。

Result: 在物理运动学下积分收敛；无需解析延拓即完成三重交换双谱（triple-exchange bispectrum）的求值，并计算了任意质量下的完整单圈三角形图（one-loop triangle）。

Conclusion: 该方法为多重交换与任意质量圈图提供了宇宙碰撞器预测，即重粒子在暴胀期间的振荡特征（oscillatory signatures）。

Abstract: Using the Mellin-Barnes representation of de Sitter propagators, we reduce tree-level heavy-particle exchange to finite integrals that converge at physical kinematics on mass-independent contours, and one-loop diagrams to the same integrand times a flat-space momentum factor. We evaluate the triple-exchange bispectrum without analytic continuation and compute the complete one-loop triangle at general mass. These give cosmological collider predictions, the oscillatory signatures of heavy particles during inflation, for multiple exchanges and loops at arbitrary masses.

</details>


### [210] [Quantum Gravity in Asymptotically Flat Spacetimes from Unstable D-Branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11493)
*Roji Pius*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出利用不稳定D膜上的Witten三次开放弦场论（OSFT），在渐近平坦时空中构建量子引力的非微扰表述，并通过将开弦关联函数映射到闭弦S矩阵的亏格展开，论证闭弦理论（即量子引力）可从开弦快子真空中动力学涌现。


<details>
