  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭弦谱中的无质量自旋-2扇区天然编码量子引力的自由度，但缺乏可在平坦时空中直接计算的非微扰框架。作者希望借助OSFT建立平坦空间版的AdS/CFT对应，以填补这一空白。

Method: 将on-shell闭弦激发直接映射为OSFT中的规范不变算符，并在快子真空附近微扰计算这些算符的OSFT关联函数。

Result: 所计算的关联函数系统地再现了相关态闭弦S矩阵的全阶亏格展开（all-orders genus expansion）。

Conclusion: 闭弦理论及量子引力可动力学地从开弦快子真空中涌现，表明Witten的OSFT是平坦空间量子引力的可完全计算、非微扰框架。

Abstract: We present a non-perturbative formulation of quantum gravity in asymptotically flat spacetimes using Witten's cubic open string field theory (OSFT) on unstable D-branes. Given that the massless spin-2 sector of the closed string spectrum inherently encodes the degrees of freedom of quantum gravity, we establish this connection by mapping on-shell closed string excitations directly to gauge-invariant operators in OSFT, realizing a concrete flat-space analogue of the AdS/CFT correspondence. By evaluating the OSFT correlation functions of these operators perturbatively around the tachyon vacuum, we systematically reproduce the all-orders genus expansion of the closed string S-matrix for the associated states. This demonstrates that closed string theory-and thus quantum gravity-emerges dynamically from the open string tachyon vacuum, establishing Witten's OSFT as a fully calculable, non-perturbative framework for flat-space quantum gravity.

</details>


### [211] [Fermionic Spectral Functions in a Two-Current Gubser-Rocha Model with Axion Momentum Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11323)
*Cai-E Liu,Defu Hou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在全息（holographic）框架下研究双流 Gubser-Rocha 背景中带线性轴子（linear-axion）动量弛豫的费米子谱函数，比较偶极耦合与两种轴子依赖的非最小耦合通道对谱重构的差异化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清电流混合（current mixing）、轴子诱导的动量弛豫（axion-induced momentum relaxation）与非最小费米子耦合（non-minimal fermion couplings）如何共同重塑全息费米子激发。以偶极耦合 p1 作为对照通道，凸显轴子依赖通道在谱重构中的独特性。

Method: 在双流 Gubser-Rocha 背景中引入线性轴子实现动量弛豫，计算费米子谱函数。比较三通道：偶极耦合 p1（对照）、由电流混合调制的张量通道 p2 与矢量通道 p3，并扫描动量弛豫强度 β/μ 与掺杂参数 χ。

Result: p1 产生软的、不对称的重构，但未打开清晰的硬 Mott 能隙（hard Mott gap）。p2 张量通道抑制零能费米峰（Fermi peak），将主导谱权重移至有限频率，形成平底重构。p3 矢量通道在动量空间产生强烈谱不对称，生成开放的类费米弧（Fermi-arc-like）片段。增大 β/μ 通常使 p2 响应展宽，而 p3 的单侧特征随背景参数呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: 结果表明谱重构对不同耦合通道及背景参数具有通道依赖性响应，揭示了电流混合、轴子诱导动量弛豫与非最小费米子耦合联合调控全息费米子激发的机制。

Abstract: We investigate fermionic spectral functions in a two-current Gubser-Rocha background with linear-axion momentum relaxation. Serving as a comparison channel, the dipole coupling $p_1$ is found to produce a soft asymmetric reconstruction without opening a clean hard Mott gap. Our main analysis then focuses on two axion-dependent non-minimal couplings modulated by current mixing. The $p_2$ tensor channel suppresses the zero-energy Fermi peak and shifts the dominant spectral weight to finite frequencies, resulting in a flat-bottom reconstruction. In contrast, the $p_3$ vector channel creates a strong spectral asymmetry in momentum space, generating open, Fermi-arc-like segments. Varying the momentum-relaxation strength $β/μ$ and the doping parameter $χ$ reveals a channel-dependent response to the two-current background. Increasing $β/μ$ generally broadens the $p_2$ response, whereas the one-sided $p_3$ feature varies non-monotonically under changes of the background parameters. These results clarify how current mixing, axion-induced momentum relaxation, and non-minimal fermion couplings jointly reshape holographic fermionic excitations. These results clarify how current mixing, axion-induced momentum relaxation, and non-minimal fermion couplings jointly reshape holographic fermionic excitations.

</details>


### [212] [Extremal black hole mimickers: geometry, stability, and long-delay signal trains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11664)
*Sergey N. Solodukhin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类极端黑洞（extremal black hole）的无视界模仿体（horizonless mimicker），其几何为单侧渐近平坦区连接无限长喉部，并在晚期产生特征间隔为 $t_{\rm delay}\sim1/\lambda$ 的长延迟信号序列（long-delay signal train），而非非极端模仿体的回波时间尺度 $\ln(1/\lambda)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有黑洞模仿体研究多针对非退化视界（non-degenerate horizon），其几何为连接两个渐近平坦区的虫洞型（wormhole type）结构；极端黑洞模仿体的时空结构此前研究不足，且其扰动稳定性与信号特征尚待澄清。

Method: 构建具有单侧渐近平坦端与另一侧无限长喉部的时空，喉部趋近直积几何 $M_{1,1}\times S^2$；分析标量场动力学（scalar-field dynamics）及 Aretakis 不稳定性（Aretakis instability）的命运，并将构造推广至极端 Kerr 情形。

Result: 喉部的局部分析虽出现类 Aretakis 模式，但不构成物理不稳定性；初始定域于喉部的标量扰动在晚期给出一系列信号，间隔为 $t_{\rm delay}\sim1/\lambda$（$\lambda\ll1$ 为形变参数），显著大于非极端模仿体的回波时间 $t_{\rm echo}\sim\ln(1/\lambda)$，该周期性在几何上对应 $S^2$ 上的周期测地线（periodic geodesics）。极端 Kerr 情形下喉部趋近含 $M_{1,1}$ 与二维面 $\Sigma_2$ 的广义直积几何，预期信号呈准周期序列。

Conclusion: 极端黑洞模仿体表现出与非极端情形定性不同的信号结构（长延迟信号序列而非回波），并讨论了向非极端黑洞虫洞型模仿体的推广及对 AdS/CFT 对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）中幺正性（unitarity）问题的可能含义。

Abstract: We present a spacetime that serves as a horizonless mimicker of extremal black holes. Unlike previously studied mimickers of black holes with non-degenerate horizons, whose geometries are of the wormhole type and consist of two asymptotically flat regions connected by a very long throat, the mimicker of an extremal black hole possesses a single asymptotically flat region at one end and an infinitely long throat at the other. In the throat region, the spacetime approaches the direct-product geometry $M_{1,1} \times S^2$, where $M_{1,1}$ is two-dimensional Minkowski spacetime and $S^2$ is the two-sphere. We investigate scalar-field dynamics and the fate of the Aretakis instability. Although Aretakis-like modes arise in a local analysis of the throat, they do not lead to a physical instability of the mimicker. A scalar perturbation initially localized in the throat gives rise, at late times, to a sequence of signals with a characteristic separation $t_{\rm delay}\sim 1/λ$, where $λ\ll 1$ is a deformation parameter. This timescale is parametrically larger than the echo time $t_{\rm echo}\sim \ln(1/λ)$ characteristic of non-extremal black-hole mimickers. We refer to this sequence as a {\it long-delay signal train}. Geometrically, the periodicity of the long-delay signals is associated with periodic geodesics on $S^2$. We then extend the construction to extremal Kerr. In this case, the throat region approaches a generalized product geometry involving $M_{1,1}$ and a two-dimensional surface $Σ_2$. The corresponding signals are expected to form a quasi-periodic signal train, reflecting the quasi-periodic geodesic motion on $Σ_2$. We also comment on the generalization to wormhole mimickers of non-extremal black holes and on the implications of these results for unitarity in the context of the AdS/CFT correspondence.

</details>


### [213] [No Particle Production and Integrability in AdS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11623)
*De-liang Zhong,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出二维反德西特空间（anti-de Sitter space）中“无粒子产生条件（no particle production condition）”的类比，并证明该准则可通过一般多重度全息关联函数（general-multiplicity holographic correlators）唯一重建弯曲空间中的Liouville理论与Toda理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平直空间中，“无粒子产生条件”可作为约束理论结构的强判据；本文试图将其推广至弯曲时空（curved space），以检验其是否仍具备确定理论形式的判别力。目标是为弯曲空间共形场论（conformal field theory）的可积模型提供一种基于物理条件的重建途径。

Method: 方法上引入二维反德西特空间（two-dimensional anti-de Sitter space）中无粒子产生条件的类比形式，并借助一般多重度（general-multiplicity）的全息关联函数（holographic correlators）作为约束工具，考察其对理论的重建能力。

Result: 结果显示，该准则能够唯一地（uniquely）重建弯曲空间中的Liouville理论（Liouville theory）与Toda理论（Toda theory），表明该条件在弯曲背景下仍具有充分的约束强度。

Conclusion: 无粒子产生条件可自然推广至二维反德西特空间，并作为唯一确定Liouville与Toda这类可积（integrable）场论结构的准则。该结论提示全息关联函数可作为连接物理条件与具体理论形式的有效桥梁。

Abstract: We propose an analogue of the no particle production condition in two-dimensional anti-de Sitter space, and demonstrate how this criterion uniquely reconstructs the curved-space Liouville and Toda theories via general-multiplicity holographic correlators.

</details>


### [214] [Einstein-Cartan and Palatini-Cartan Gravity from Graded Poisson Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11844)
*Johannes Moerland,Peter Schupp*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文借助分级泊松几何（graded Poisson geometry）构建统一框架，将 Einstein-Cartan 引力表述为受约束的 Palatini-Cartan 引力变体，并通过分级流形微分同胚与 BV 扩展实现理论间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为 Einstein-Cartan 与 Palatini-Cartan 等场论提供简洁表述，并厘清二者之间的结构关系，同时为 BV（Batalin-Vilkovisky）量化扩展提供几何基础。

Method: 采用分级泊松几何的概念，通过分级流形（graded manifolds）的微分同胚（diffeomorphism）将相关理论相互关联，并在分级几何框架内引入对 vielbein 场的对称性约束。

Result: 证明了 Einstein-Cartan 引力等价于一种变体 Palatini-Cartan 引力，即 vielbein 场满足特定对称性关系的“受约束 vielbein 引力（constrained vielbein gravity）”；移除该对称约束后可得到完整的 Palatini-Cartan 理论。

Conclusion: 分级几何框架可直接纳入受约束 vielbein 理论与 Palatini-Cartan 理论的最小 BV 扩展（minimal BV extension），表明该框架在表述与量化此类引力理论方面具有适用性。

Abstract: Using notions from graded Poisson geometry, we develop a framework that allows for concise formulations of field theories such as Einstein-Cartan and Palatini-Cartan gravity. We show that Einstein-Cartan gravity is equivalent to a variant of Palatini-Cartan gravity in which the vielbein fields are constrained to satisfy appropriate symmetry relations, which we refer to as constrained vielbein gravity. To that end, we relate these theories via a diffeomorphism of graded manifolds. We then show how to obtain the full Palatini-Cartan theory by removing the symmetry constraint. Finally, we show how our graded geometric framework allows for a direct incorporation of the minimal BV extension of the graded constrained vielbein and Palatini-Cartan theories.

</details>


### [215] [Dielectric constant of holographic insulator from quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11635)
*Debabrata Ghorai,Taewon Yuk,Sang-Jin Sin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文从基态量子几何（quantum geometry）出发研究全息绝缘体（holographic insulator）的介电性质，通过计算介电常数（dielectric constant）刻画其输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标为理解全息绝缘体的介电性质，并尝试用基态量子几何这一视角对其进行描述。

Method: 通过全息费米子（holographic fermions）的赝标量相互作用（pseudoscalar interaction）实现绝缘态，计算费米子格林函数（fermionic Green function）以提取有效哈密顿量（effective hamiltonian），进而导出量子度规（quantum metric）与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）以计算输运量。

Result: 由输运计算得到电导率（conductivity），并最终从其虚部（imaginary part）提取出介电常数。

Conclusion: 摘要未显式给出结论性陈述，仅表明可通过量子几何与输运量的结合得到全息绝缘体的介电常数。

Abstract: We study dielectric properties of holographic insulators from the quantum geometry of the ground state. We realize the insulating state by the pseudoscalar interaction of holographic fermions and calculate the fermionic Green function to extract the effective hamiltonian. We then derive the quantum metric and Berry curvature to compute the transports and finally get the dielectric constant from the imaginary part of conductivity.

</details>


### [216] [Entanglement entropy of black holes from the first law of thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11777)
*D. V. Diakonov,Gleb S. Zverev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在具有Killing视界的时空上，通过沿以Hawking温度标记的平衡背景族积分热力学第一定律，完全在壳（on-shell）计算量子物质的纠缠熵，得到一个由重整化应力能量张量与Killing矢量表达的闭合、构造上有限的结果，无需复制方法（replicas）与锥形奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠熵计算常依赖复制方法（replica method）与锥形奇点（conical singularities）等技巧，存在技术复杂性。该文旨在寻求一种完全在壳（on-shell）、构造上有限且无此类奇点的替代计算途径。

Method: 沿由Hawking温度标记的单参数平衡背景族，对热力学第一定律（first law of thermodynamics）进行积分，从而获得纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的闭合表达式；结果以重整化应力能量张量（renormalized stress-energy tensor）与Killing矢量表示。

Result: 所得表达式构造上有限，其协变形式与Bardeen–Carter–Hawking质量变分公式的物质项一致；在de Sitter空间静态区（static patch）与非旋转BTZ黑洞（non-rotating BTZ black hole）两个案例中，精确重现了锥形熵（conical entropy），且包含接触项（contact term）$Q$。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一种无复制、无锥形奇点的在壳纠缠熵计算框架，其协变结构与已知质量变分公式的物质项吻合，并通过de Sitter静态区与BTZ黑洞的精确验证表明结果的可靠性。

Abstract: We compute the entanglement entropy of quantum matter in spacetimes with a Killing horizon entirely on-shell, by integrating the first law of thermodynamics along a one-parameter family of equilibrium backgrounds labelled by the Hawking temperature. This yields a closed expression in terms of the renormalized stress-energy tensor and the Killing vector, finite by construction, with no replicas and no conical singularities. Its covariant form agrees with the matter terms of the Bardeen--Carter--Hawking mass variation formula. We show in details that for static patch of de Sitter space and for the non-rotating BTZ black hole it reproduces the conical entropy exactly, contact term $Q$ included.

</details>


### [217] [Multipartite Entanglement Geometry in AdS/BCFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11921)
*Debarshi Basu,Qiang Wen,Mingshuai Xu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息边界共形场论（holographic BCFT）中物理边界如何重塑多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）结构，发现世界末端膜（end-of-the-world brane）为多体纠缠开辟新的相对同调通道（relative-homology channels），导致体（bulk）中连接网络的重组与分解发生在不同于常规 Ryu–Takayanagi 转变的尺度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠度量（multipartite entanglement measures）可揭示超出两两熵（pairwise entropies）所能推断的量子关联组织方式，但这一结构在存在物理边界时如何被改变尚不明确，故本文在 BCFT 框架下考察边界的重塑作用。

Method: 在静态 AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$ 中对全息 BCFT 进行分析，考察世界末端膜为多体纠缠提供的相对同调通道，并分析体中的连接网络（connected bulk webs）如何重组与分解；同时在热场双态（thermofield-double state）中讨论通过爱因斯坦–罗森桥（Einstein–Rosen bridge）支持真正连接网络的情形。

Result: 在静态 AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$ 中得到特征性的多体相层级（hierarchy of multipartite phases），且真多熵（genuine multi-entropy）对边界呈现非平凡依赖；在热场双态中，真正连接的网络可由爱因斯坦–罗森桥支持，其增长、重连（reconnection）与饱和尺度不同于通常的 Hartman–Maldacena 转变。

Conclusion: 多体纠缠不仅对极值曲面面积（extremal-surface areas）敏感，也依赖于在存在边界时体纠缠结构被允许如何连接、分裂与终止。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement measures probe how quantum correlations are organized beyond what can be inferred from pairwise entropies. We study how this structure is reshaped by a physical boundary in a holographic BCFT. The end-of-the-world brane opens new relative-homology channels for multipartite entanglement, allowing connected bulk webs to reorganize and factorize at scales that need not coincide with any ordinary Ryu--Takayanagi transition. In static AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$, this produces a characteristic hierarchy of multipartite phases and a nontrivial dependence of genuine multi-entropy on the boundary. In the thermofield-double state, genuinely connected webs can instead be supported through the Einstein--Rosen bridge, leading to growth, reconnection and saturation scales distinct from the usual Hartman--Maldacena transitions. These results show that multipartite entanglement is sensitive not only to extremal-surface areas but also to how bulk entanglement structures are allowed to join, split and terminate in the presence of a boundary.

</details>


### [218] [Mass quench in interacting finite-size quantum field system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12045)
*Grachik A Simonian,Alexey G. Mikhaylenko,Andrew G. Semenov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在理论层面研究有限尺寸系统中量子标量场（quantum scalar field）经历质量量子淬火（mass quantum quench）后关联函数（correlation functions）的非平衡演化，并在单圈近似（one-loop approximation）下数值求解 Keldysh（统计）关联函数的自洽方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限尺寸系统中量子场在量子淬火后的非平衡动力学，关注关联函数随时间的演化及其空间-时间效应，尤其是周期性边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）所导致的特殊行为。

Method: 在单圈近似（one-loop approximation）下建立 Keldysh（统计）关联函数的自洽方程（self-consistent equation），并进行数值求解。

Result: 观察到一种特定的空间-时间效应（spatio-temporal effect）：在时间约等于系统尺寸一半时，有效质量（effective mass）出现强烈扰动。该效应由在光锥（light cone）内传播的涨落波前（wave fronts of fluctuations）的相向干涉（interference of counter-propagating wave fronts）所驱动，并由周期性边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）诱导。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸系统中的量子淬火动力学可产生由边界条件与光锥内波前干涉共同决定的特征性时空效应，表现为有效质量在特定时刻的显著扰动。

Abstract: In this work, we theoretically investigate the non-equilibrium evolution of the correlation functions of a quantum scalar field following a mass quantum quench in a finite-size system. The self-consistent equation for the Keldysh (statistical) correlation function is solved numerically in the one-loop approximation. A specific spatio-temporal effect is observed: at a point in time approximately equal to half the system size, the effective mass experiences a strong disturbance. It is shown that this effect is driven by the interference of counter-propagating wave fronts of fluctuations that propagate within the light cone and is induced by periodic boundary conditions.

</details>


### [219] [Probability measures for de Sitter JT gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12174)
*Wilfried Buchmuller,Alexander Westphal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究正宇宙学常数下的Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT)引力，将其作为从量子态涌现出膨胀de Sitter宇宙的玩具模型。通过比较双喇叭振幅密度矩阵与无边界波函数的预言，发现符合全息的双喇叭振幅给出宇宙尺度的平坦概率分布，与暴胀模型一致，而无边界波函数则预言大宇宙被压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正宇宙学常数的JT引力被用作研究膨胀de Sitter宇宙从量子态涌现的玩具模型，旨在检验不同量子态（密度矩阵与无边界波函数）对经典膨胀宇宙的预言差异。

Method: 将JT引力的密度矩阵表示为双喇叭振幅（double-trumpet amplitudes）的叠加，这些振幅是Wheeler-DeWitt方程的解；并将其对经典膨胀宇宙的预言与无边界波函数（no-boundary wavefunctions）的相应预言进行比较。

Result: 符合全息的双喇叭振幅，其半经典极限与Schwarzian边界涨落的结果一致，给出宇宙尺度的平坦概率分布，与暴胀模型一致；这与无边界波函数所预言的大宇宙压低形成对比。

Conclusion: 在正宇宙学常数JT引力框架下，基于全息双喇叭振幅的混合态预言平坦的宇宙尺度分布，与暴胀模型相符，并与无边界波函数对大宇宙压低的预言相区别。

Abstract: Jackiw-Teitelboim (JT) gravity with positive cosmological constant is a toy model to study the emergence of expanding de Sitter universes from a quantum state. Its density matrix is a superposition of double-trumpet amplitudes which are solutions of a Wheeler-DeWitt equation. We compare predictions of such mixed states for classical expanding universes with the corresponding predictions of no-boundary wavefunctions. Double-trumpet amplitudes consistent with holography, whose semiclassical limit matches the result from Schwarzian boundary fluctuations, lead to a flat probability distribution for the size of the universe, consistent with models of inflation. This is in contrast to the suppression of large universes predicted by no-boundary wave functions.

</details>


### [220] [Asymptotic Safety in Gauge Theories beyond Four Dimensions: Perturbative Tests and Fixed-Point Mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12225)
*Aldo Deandrea,Jie Liu,Roman Pasechnik,Zhi-Wei Wang,Yu-Bo Zhao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用延续至 D=4+2ε 的四圈 $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ beta 函数，研究非阿贝尔规范理论在四维以上的相互作用紫外不动点，界定微扰可控边界，并探讨五维渐近安全（asymptotic safety）的合并（merger）情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维以上非阿贝尔规范理论可能通过正则标度（canonical scaling）与规范场反屏蔽（antiscreening）的竞争产生相互作用紫外不动点，但其高维渐近安全区域的范围及微扰可控性需要界定。

Method: 采用延续至 D=4+2ε 的四圈 $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ beta 函数，确定满足指定比值判据的最大维度 $D_{\mathrm{pt}}=4+2ε_{\mathrm{pt}}$，并映射于 $SU(N)$ 基础表示费米子的色数与味数；在 D=5 时构造分母为固定极点正则化（受 fRG 启发）的有理反常维度，与 $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ 系列作一至三圈匹配。

Result: 一至三圈匹配使紫外–红外合并点（merger，即红外与紫外不动点重合处）的临界味数与耦合逐级减小，三圈构造给出大 N 下 $N_f^{\mathrm{cr}}\simeq 2.09N$；四圈匹配则在三圈合并边界之外仍保留相互作用紫外不动点，对整数 2≤N≤15 与 0≤N_f/N≤11/2 未发现合并。

Conclusion: 不动点结构对高阶项敏感，使合并与不合并两种情景均可能；不动点湮灭（fixed-point annihilation）或构成五维渐近安全的潜在约束，所得色–味映射为五维模型构造提供了参考。

Abstract: Non-Abelian gauge theories above four dimensions may admit an interacting ultraviolet fixed point through the competition between canonical scaling and gauge-field antiscreening. Using the four-loop $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ beta function continued to $D=4+2ε$, we determine the largest dimension $D_{\mathrm{pt}}=4+2ε_{\mathrm{pt}}$ below which successive loop corrections to the fixed point remain hierarchically ordered under a specified ratio criterion, and map $D_{\mathrm{pt}}$ over color and flavor numbers for fermions in the fundamental representation of $SU(N)$. At $D=5$, we construct rational anomalous dimensions with a fixed, pole-regularized denominator inspired by the functional renormalization group (fRG). Matching to the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ series through one, two, and three loops produces progressively smaller shifts in the critical flavor number and coupling at the ultraviolet--infrared merger (the point where the infrared and ultraviolet fixed points coincide), yielding $N_f^{\mathrm{cr}}\simeq 2.09 \, N$ at large $N$ in the three-loop construction. This low-order stabilization, together with qualitative evidence from fRG flows including higher gauge operators, suggests a potential merger scenario that bounds the candidate asymptotically safe region in flavor number. The resulting color--flavor map provides guidance for five-dimensional model building by displaying both the baseline perturbative-control boundary and the candidate three-loop merger boundary. Four-loop matching, however, retains an interacting ultraviolet fixed point beyond the three-loop merger boundary, with no merger found for integer $2\le N\le15$ and $0\le N_f/N\le11/2$. The sensitivity of the fixed-point structure to higher-order terms leaves both scenarios open, highlighting fixed-point annihilation as a potential constraint on five-dimensional asymptotic safety.

</details>


### [221] [Horizon symmetries of exact hydrodynamic actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12262)
*Mike Blake,Arpit Das,Richard A. Davison*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文说明如何在对偶应力张量（dual stress tensor）的流体力学作用量（hydrodynamic action）中实现 AdS 黑洞视界对称性（horizon symmetries），并在 JT 与 AdS₃ 引力中给出显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期获取流体力学作用量的方法需要选定视界边界条件（horizon boundary conditions），而其与对称性的关系尚需澄清，本文即针对此问题展开研究。

Method: 先选定视界边界条件，再指出保持这些条件的微分同胚（diffeomorphisms）会给出相应流体力学作用量的对称性；随后用该方法构造 JT 与 AdS₃ 引力中黑洞小振幅扰动（small amplitude perturbations）的显式流体力学作用量。

Result: 在 JT 与 AdS₃ 引力中显式验证了这类对称性的存在；对一种自然的边界条件选择，所得作用量明显实现了此前为最大混沌（maximal chaos）有效理论提出的规范对称性（gauge symmetries）。

Conclusion: 视界对称性可经由适当的视界边界条件统一体现在对偶流体力学作用量中，并与最大混沌有效理论的规范对称性相衔接。

Abstract: We demonstrate how horizon symmetries of AdS black holes are realised in the hydrodynamic actions for the dynamics of the dual stress tensor. A recent approach for obtaining such actions involves a choice of horizon boundary conditions, and we show that diffeomorphisms that preserve these give rise to symmetries of the corresponding hydrodynamic action. We obtain explicit hydrodynamic actions for small amplitude perturbations of black holes in JT and AdS$_3$ gravity using this approach and demonstrate explicitly the existence of these symmetries. For a natural choice of boundary conditions, the resulting actions manifestly realise gauge symmetries previously proposed for effective theories of maximal chaos.

</details>


### [222] [Stringy resolution of bouncing singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12311)
*Ilija Burić,Ivan Gusev,Andrei Parnachev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究近极端 NS5 膜（near-extremal NS5 branes）近地平线几何，利用 $SL(2,\mathbb{R})_k/U(1)$ 陪集（coset）世界面描述证明：超引力极限下时间域反射振幅的弹跳奇点（bouncing singularity）可被有限弦长效应非微扰地完全消除，使振幅成为复时间的整函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端 NS5 膜具有可控的世界面描述，可为研究黑洞奇点相关问题提供精确可算的框架；超引力极限下反射振幅出现的弹跳奇点类似渐近 AdS 时空中边界关联函数的弹跳奇点，其是否被量子/弦效应平滑化是关键问题。

Method: 在 $SL(2,\mathbb{R})_k/U(1)$ 陪集世界面理论（worldsheet theory）中分析近极端 NS5 膜的近地平线几何，考察解析延拓至复时间的时间域反射振幅，并引入有限弦长（finite string length）修正（等效于对陪集能级 $k$ 的 $1/k$ 修正）来研究奇点的命运。

Result: 超引力极限下振幅含与零测地线（null geodesic）从黑洞奇点弹回相关的奇点；纳入有限弦长效应后，振幅成为复时间的整函数（entire function），弹跳奇点被完全消除。该消解对陪集能级的倒数是非微扰（nonperturbative）的：在任何有限阶展开中奇点依然存在。

Conclusion: 有限弦长效应以非微扰方式完全消除近极端 NS5 膜反射振幅的弹跳奇点，表明该类奇点是超引力近似的产物；其非微扰性质意味着无法通过陪集能级倒数（$1/k$）的有限阶展开捕捉。

Abstract: We study the near-horizon geometry of near-extremal NS5 branes, which admits an exact worldsheet description in terms of the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})_k/U(1)$ coset. In the supergravity limit, the analytically continued time-domain reflection amplitude exhibits a singularity associated with a null geodesic bouncing off the black-hole singularity, analogous to the bouncing singularities of boundary correlators in asymptotically AdS spacetimes. We show that finite-string-length effects render this amplitude an entire function of complex time, completely resolving the bouncing singularity. This resolution is nonperturbative in the inverse coset level: the singularity persists at every finite order in the expansion.

</details>


### [223] [Differential $T_2$-Duality and Spans of Principal 3-Bundles with Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12383)
*Gianni Gagliardo,Christian Saemann,Roberto Tellez-Dominguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出 T-duality 的推广形式 \(T_2\)-duality，用以刻画 M-theory 中 U-duality 的某些方面，其核心结果是借助带联络的分类化主丛（categorified principal bundles with connections）的 span 刻画 \(T_2\)-对偶对，并给出构造规则与示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 T-duality 推广至可涵盖 M-theory（M-theory）中 U-duality（U-duality）部分特征的形式，尤其是包含主环面丛（principal torus bundle）上 2-gerbe 的十一维超引力背景结构。

Method: 方法上，作者引入 \(T_2\)-duality，并证明一个定理，通过带联络的分类化主丛的 span 来刻画 \(T_2\)-对偶对；同时以多个显式示例说明各特征类（characteristic classes）在左右背景间的映射方式。

Result: 主要结果为给出 \(T_2\)-dual pairs 的刻画定理与显式构造规则；对于二维环面纤维（two-dimensional torus fibers），将构造与连接十一维超引力（eleven-dimensional supergravity）到 IIB 型超引力（type IIB supergravity）的降维加 T-duality 组合相关联；并讨论 \(T_2\)-duality 如何由 3d sigma 模型（3d sigma models）的类 Buscher（Buscher-like）span 产生。

Conclusion: 结论层面，\(T_2\)-duality 作为 U-duality 部分特征的推广框架，在特例中亦以精确意义纳入 S-duality（S-duality），并通过分类化主丛 span 与 3d sigma 模型 span 得到统一刻画。

Abstract: We present a generalization of T-duality, called $T_2$-duality, that captures certain aspects of U-duality in M-theory. In particular, it features backgrounds that contain a 2-gerbe over a principal torus bundle, structures familiar from eleven-dimensional supergravity backgrounds. In special cases, it also incorporates S-duality in a precise sense. Our main result is a theorem characterizing $T_2$-dual pairs through spans of categorified principal bundles with connections, giving explicit rules for constructing a $T_2$-dual for a given background. A number of explicit examples illustrate how the various characteristic classes are mapped between the left and right backgrounds. For two-dimensional torus fibers, we also relate our construction to the combination of dimensional reduction and T-duality that links eleven-dimensional supergravity to type IIB supergravity. Finally, we discuss how $T_2$-duality arises from a Buscher-like span of 3d sigma models.

</details>


### [224] [Cornering the Heterotic String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12318)
*Li-Yuan Chiang,Yu-tin Huang,He-Chen Weng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维 $\mathcal N=4$ 杨–米尔斯理论（Yang–Mills theory）耦合 $\mathcal N=4$ 超引力（supergravity）的树级完备化，通过六点因子化与超对称 Ward 恒等式推出 Wilson 系数间的非线性关系，并借助色道正性将参数空间压至异弦与无限自旋塔振幅所界定的窄带内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四胶子振幅的单迹（single-trace）与双迹（double-trace）Wilson 系数在四点层面可与超对称相容，但其在更高点及引力耦合下的自洽性缺乏系统约束。本文旨在确定引力分支上这些系数的完备结构，并检验已知紫外完备（异弦、无限自旋塔）是否实现该结构。

Method: 采用六点因子化（six-point factorization）与超对称 Ward 恒等式（supersymmetry Ward identities），并附加标量接触项的特殊宇称（peculiar-parity）限制，导出系数 $a_{k,q}$ 与 $b_{k,q}$ 的非线性关系；随后结合色散关系（dispersion relations）与 $SO(N)$ 色道分波正性（partial-wave positivity），并对最简 IST 振幅做解析幺正性（unitarity）分析。

Result: 最简单关系 $\kappa^2 a_{0,0}=0$ 排除非零引力耦合下的单迹 $F^4$ 相互作用。在引力分支上，由五个双迹参数决定直至 $a_{7,q}$ 与 $b_{8,q}$ 的全部系数，二者由共同指数生成形式给出，异弦与一类无限自旋塔（infinite-spin-tower, IST）振幅均实现该结构。正性分析给出窄数值带，其边界紧邻异弦与最简 IST 振幅；自旋依赖质量间隙（mass gap）排除 IST 谱并进一步定位于异弦轨迹附近。最简 IST 的幺正性给出 $N\leq48$、$g^2/(\kappa^2 m^2)\leq4$，与异弦振幅上限一致。

Conclusion: 树级完备化结构强烈限制 $\mathcal N=4$ 杨–米尔斯–超引力耦合的 Wilson 系数，并排除单迹 $F^4$ 项；色道正性与幺正性把可行参数区压缩至异弦轨迹附近，且在 $N=48$ 处对规范–引力耦合比给出尖锐约束，显示异弦振幅在该分析中具有基准地位。分析未超出摘要所给结论，未涉及完整证明或数值细节。

Abstract: We study tree-level completions of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ Yang--Mills theory coupled to $\mathcal N=4$ supergravity. Six-point factorization and supersymmetry Ward identities, supplemented by a peculiar-parity restriction on scalar contact terms, impose nonlinear relations between the single- and double-trace Wilson coefficients, $a_{k,q}$ and $b_{k,q}$, of the four-gluon amplitude. The simplest relation, $κ^2a_{0,0}=0$, excludes the single-trace $F^4$ interaction at nonzero gravitational coupling, despite its compatibility with four-point supersymmetry. On this gravitational branch, five double-trace parameters determine all coefficients through $a_{7,q}$ and $b_{8,q}$, which are reproduced by a common exponential generating form. Both the heterotic string and a family of infinite-spin-tower (IST) amplitudes realize this structure. Combining these relations with dispersion relations and partial-wave positivity in $SO(N)$ color channels yields a narrow numerical band closely bounded by the heterotic and simplest IST amplitudes. A spin-dependent mass gap excludes the IST spectrum and further localizes the region near the heterotic trajectory. An analytic unitarity analysis of the simplest IST gives $N\leq48$ and $g^2/(κ^2 m^2)\leq4$, matching the upper limits of the heterotic amplitude, where $m$ is the mass of the first massive level. Along the heterotic trajectory, the numerical bounds also sharply constrain the gauge-to-gravity coupling ratio at $N=48$.

</details>


### [225] [Platonic self-dual solitons in a (1+1)-dimensional $\mathbb{CP}^1$ model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12385)
*L. A. Ferreira,G. Marcelino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文利用柏拉图几何（Platonic geometry）构造了一族自对偶（self-dual）的 (1+1) 维 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ sigma 模型，其势（potential）$V$ 由预势（pre-potential）$U$ 决定。通过正多面体顶点与单位外接球的关系生成对称不变的预势，并研究其对真空结构与自对偶解的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为具有势的 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 模型提供一种几何处方，用以控制其真空结构与自对偶扇区，通过柏拉图立体几何将预势的构造系统化。

Method: 采用 $\mathbb{S}^2\simeq\mathbb{CP}^1$ 上的圆度量（round metric）与广义自对偶（generalized self-duality）形式，从每个正多面体与单位外接球相交的单位顶点构造对称不变的预势 $U$；顶点、面心与棱中点方向对应的正格点（regular-lattice sites）为 $U$ 的临界点及标量势 $V$ 的退化真空。

Result: 对偶立体共享同一正格点但生成不同的预势、荷（charges）、势与度量梯度流（metric-gradient flows）。数值积分显示不同的自对偶轨迹与能量密度分布，包括路径靠近鞍点时的多峰结构；具有相同端点差 $\Delta U$ 的解具有相同总能量，且 $V$ 的正极大值也被组织为对称轨道（symmetry orbits）。

Conclusion: 该构造为控制带势 $\mathbb{CP}^1$ 模型的真空结构与自对偶扇区提供了几何处方。

Abstract: We construct a family of self-dual $(1+1)$-dimensional $\mathbb{CP}^1$ sigma models whose potentials $V$ are determined from pre-potentials $U$ by Platonic geometry. Using the round metric on $\mathbb{S}^2\simeq\mathbb{CP}^1$ and the generalized self-duality formalism, we build symmetry-invariant pre-potentials $U$ from the unit vertices of each Platonic solid intersecting the unit circumsphere. The regular-lattice sites associated with vertex, face-center, and edge-midpoint directions are critical points of $U$ and degenerate vacua of the scalar potential $V$. Dual solids share the same regular lattice but generate different pre-potentials, charges, potentials, and metric-gradient flows. Numerical integration reveals distinct self-dual trajectories and energy-density profiles, including multi-peak structures for paths passing near saddle points, while solutions with the same endpoint difference $ΔU$ have the same total energy. The positive maxima of $V$ are also organized into symmetry orbits. This construction provides a geometric prescription for controlling the vacuum structure and self-dual sectors of $\mathbb{CP}^1$ models with a potential.

</details>


### [226] [Energy-energy correlators in the large-charge limit of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12414)
*Alessandro Georgoudis,Max Jackson,Congkao Wen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维 $\mathcal N=4$ 超杨-米尔斯理论（super Yang–Mills theory）的大荷 't Hooft 极限下研究能量-能量关联子（energy-energy correlator, EEC），利用已知的全阶四点关联子结果，通过重求和得到有限耦合下的精确 EEC 表达式，并分析其随有效耦合与角度分离的物理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重态（heavy states）中的 EEC 在强耦合与有限耦合区域的行为尚缺乏精确可控的研究框架。该工作旨在借助大荷 't Hooft 极限下已知的全阶关联子结果，获得 EEC 的有限耦合精确信息，以刻画其动力学结构。

Method: 采用大荷 't Hooft 极限，令重算符维数 $\Delta$（等价于其 $R$-荷）趋于无穷而有效耦合 $\lambda_Q \propto g_{\mathrm{YM}}^2\Delta$ 固定。基于该类重-重-轻-轻（heavy-heavy-light-light）四点关联子在该极限下的全阶结果，对某些算符类别的微扰级数进行重求和（resummation）以获得精确有限耦合表达式，并由四点关联子的双重不连续性（double discontinuity）提取 EEC 的动力学部分。

Result: 对特定算符类别，微扰级数可被重求和为精确的有限耦合表达式；由此确定对应的 EEC，并给出其动力学部分在有限有效耦合下的结果。EEC 的物理行为随有效耦合 $\lambda_Q$ 与探测器间角度分离变化，涵盖一般角度以及共线（collinear）与背对背（back-to-back）极限。

Conclusion: 大荷 't Hooft 极限为在有限有效耦合下精确确定重态 EEC 提供了可行途径，其动力学信息可由四点关联子的双重不连续性提取。所得 EEC 在一般角度及共线、背对背极限下的行为，为理解该理论中能量关联的可解析结构提供了依据。

Abstract: We study the energy-energy correlator (EEC) in heavy states of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory in the large-charge 't Hooft limit, in which the dimension $Δ$ of the heavy operator (or, equivalently, its $R$-charge) is taken to infinity with the effective coupling $λ_Q \propto g_{\mathrm{YM}}^2Δ$ held fixed. The relevant heavy-heavy-light-light four-point correlators are known to all orders in $λ_Q$ in this limit. For certain classes of operators, the perturbative series can be resummed to obtain exact finite-coupling expressions. We use these results to determine the corresponding EEC, whose dynamical part can be extracted from a double discontinuity of the four-point correlator. We determine the EEC at finite effective coupling and study its physical behavior as a function of $λ_Q$ and the angular separation between the detectors, both at generic angles and in the collinear and back-to-back limits.

</details>


### [227] [A real superpotential for M-theory on eight-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12439)
*Anthony Ashmore,Savdeep Sethi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究十一维超引力在八维内空间上紧致化至三维 Minkowski 或 AdS 真空、保持 N=1 超对称的情形，针对具有局部 G$_2$ 结构的背景，在不做有限 Kaluza-Klein 截断的前提下导出了 off-shell 实超势泛函，并证明其临界点方程等价于十一维 Killing spinor 方程给出的全部超对称条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于理解十一维超引力在八维流形上保持 N=1 超对称的紧致化结构，并希望在无需有限 Kaluza-Klein 截断的条件下刻画超势，从而更完整地描述内部几何与通量（fluxes）对真空的约束。

Method: 研究聚焦具有局部 G$_2$ 结构的背景，通过两条独立路径导出 off-shell 实超势泛函：一是维度约化（dimensional reduction）引力微子（gravitino）变分，二是约化十一维引力微子作用量；该泛函以内部几何与通量表示。

Result: 所导出的超势满足：其临界点方程与超势的真空取值，共同等价于十一维 Killing spinor 方程所蕴含的完整超对称条件；当局部 G$_2$ 结构增强为整体 Spin(7) 结构时，结果退化为已知的 Spin(7) 流形上 M 理论超势。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在八维流形上以局部 G$_2$ 结构描述的十一维超引力紧致化中，存在与完整超对称条件一致的 off-shell 实超势表述，且该表述与 Spin(7) 情形下的已知结果相容，构成其自然推广。

Abstract: We study warped compactifications of eleven-dimensional supergravity on eight-manifolds to Minkowski or AdS vacua preserving $N=1$ supersymmetry in three dimensions. For backgrounds characterised by a local G$_2$ structure, we derive an off-shell real superpotential functional in terms of the internal geometry and fluxes without performing a finite Kaluza-Klein truncation. We obtain this expression in two independent ways: from the dimensional reduction of the gravitino variation and from the reduction of the eleven-dimensional gravitino action. We show that the critical-point equations, together with the vacuum value of the superpotential, are equivalent to the full set of supersymmetry conditions implied by the eleven-dimensional Killing spinor equations. When the local G$_2$ structure enhances to a global Spin(7) structure, our result reduces to the known superpotential for M-theory on Spin(7) manifolds.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Finite- and long-range spin-wave free-energy asymptotics for the two-dimensional quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10736)
*Andreas Klippel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有渐近径向幂律交换（asymptotically radial power-law exchange）的二维量子海森堡铁磁体（quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet），在所有固定自旋下、在弱场标度上一致地证明了低温自由能（free energy）的主项，得到与[16,17]所预言的色散相联系的分数律与对数修正律。量子蒙特卡洛（Quantum Monte Carlo）模拟支持自旋1/2的异常磁振子色散（anomalous magnon dispersion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画二维量子海森堡铁磁体在长程幂律交换下的低温热力学行为，特别是弱磁场中自由能的主阶规律。其出发点是此前文献[16,17]对该类色散的理论预言以及[20]给出的量子蒙特卡洛证据，需要严格的数学证明加以支撑。

Method: 采用严格解析手段，针对渐近径向幂律交换核，在所有固定自旋取值下、并在弱场标度上一致地导出低温自由能主项。对具有有限二阶矩（finite second moment）的交换核，进一步推广最近邻定理（nearest-neighbour theorem，文献[14]），并推广Takahashi二次律（quadratic law，文献[21]）及其弱场依赖关系（文献[11]）。

Result: 证明了低温自由能的主项，得到分数律与对数修正律，二者分别对应[16,17]所预言的磁振子色散。这些异常色散获得自旋1/2的量子蒙特卡洛模拟支持[20]。对有限二阶矩核，最近邻定理得到扩展，Takahashi二次律及其弱场依赖亦被推广。

Conclusion: 结果表明，幂律交换导致的异常磁振子色散在低温自由能中表现为分数幂与对数修正，且该规律在固定自旋与弱场标度下具有一致性。有限二阶矩情形下最近邻定理的推广，将已有二次律框架纳入更一般的交换核设定。

Abstract: For the two-dimensional quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet with asymptotically radial power-law exchange, we prove the leading low-temperature free energy at every fixed spin, uniformly on the weak-field scale. We obtain fractional and logarithmically corrected laws associated with the dispersions predicted in [16, 17]. Quantum Monte Carlo simulations support the associated anomalous magnon dispersions for spin one-half [20]. For kernels with finite second moment, we also extend the nearest-neighbour theorem [14], generalizing Takahashi's quadratic law [21] and its weak-field dependence [11].

</details>


### [229] [Uniformity theory of weighted composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11157)
*Marcelo Epstein,Mohammadjavad Javadi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种双群胚（double groupoids）数学框架的扩展，用于处理组分体积分数（volume fractions）空间变化的复合材料（composites），即功能梯度介质（functionally graded media），目标是为失配缺陷（defects of misalignment）及其时间演化（time evolution）的分类奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过引入组分体积分数的空间变化，可获得类似功能梯度介质（functionally graded media）的复合材料。此类材料中失配缺陷（defects of misalignment）及其时间演化（time evolution）的刻画需要相应的数学工具，而现有双群胚（double groupoids）方法尚未涵盖体积分数空间变化的情形。

Method: 提出对双群胚（double groupoids）数学工具（mathematical apparatus）的一种扩展，将体积分数（volume fractions）具有空间变化的复合材料纳入该形式体系之中。

Result: 摘要未报告具体结果，仅说明所提出的扩展旨在最终实现对失配缺陷（defects of misalignment）及其时间演化（time evolution）的分类。

Conclusion: 该扩展的双群胚（double groupoids）框架为功能梯度介质（functionally graded media）类复合材料提供了形式化描述途径，其最终目标是完成失配缺陷（defects of misalignment）及其时间演化（time evolution）的分类。

Abstract: By including spatially varying volume fractions of the constituents, composites are obtained that are akin to functionally graded media. An extension of the mathematical apparatus of double groupoids is proposed to incorporate these materials with the aim of eventually classifying defects of misalignment and their time evolution.

</details>


### [230] [Extended States on the Bethe Lattice Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11611)
*Vojkan Jaksic,Yoram Last*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该短文给出 Anderson 模型（Anderson model）在 Bethe 格（Bethe lattice）上弱无序（weak-disorder）情形下，自由谱（free spectrum）内部紧区间上谱为纯绝对连续（purely absolutely continuous）的简捷证明；方法为前向格林函数（forward Green function）稳定性论证结合树递归（tree recursion）的零一律（zero–one law），并指出该结构性论证可简化近期 OpenAI 预印本中从边界正性到纯性的推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结论虽属经典，但作者延续前作 [6] 的思路，寻求更简短的证明：前作仅借助 [8] 的循环性判据（cyclicity criterion）证明非平凡绝对连续分量存在，此处希望进一步以更直接的论证得到整个区间上的绝对连续谱与纯性。

Method: 对前向格林函数（forward Green function）施加稳定性论证（stability argument），再利用树递归（tree recursion）的零一律（zero–one law），得到其边界虚部（boundary imaginary part）的正性；随后结合 [7] 的结构性结果排除奇异谱（singular spectrum）以确立纯性。

Result: 边界虚部的正性直接给出该区间上谱的绝对连续（absolutely continuous）性；配合 [7] 的结构性结果排除奇异谱，从而得到自由谱内部紧区间上的纯绝对连续谱。

Conclusion: 该证明较前作更为简短，且同样的结构性论证可缩短近期关于 $\zz^d$（$d\geq3$）弱无序 Anderson 模型的 OpenAI 预印本 [10] 中从边界正性到纯性的最后一步推导。

Abstract: We give a short proof of the classical weak-disorder result that the Anderson model on the Bethe lattice has purely absolutely continuous spectrum on compact intervals in the interior of the free spectrum. This note continues [6], where we gave a minimal proof of the existence of a nontrivial absolutely continuous component using the cyclicity criterion of [8]. There, Hellinger overlap yields the required non-cyclicity. Here, a stability argument for the forward Green function, followed by the zero--one law for the tree recursion, yields positivity of its boundary imaginary part. This positivity already gives absolutely continuous spectrum throughout the interval; the structural result of [7] excludes singular spectrum and yields purity. We also observe that the same structural argument shortens the final passage from boundary positivity to purity in the recent OpenAI preprint on the weak-disorder Anderson model on $\zz^d$, $d\geq3$ [10].

</details>


### [231] [Wrinkling of a bilayered cylinder under tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11681)
*Chiara Lonati,Sairam Pamulaparthi Venkata,Kevin Moerman,Olga Petrenko,Michel Destrade,Valentina Balbi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究结合建模、数值计算、有限元模拟与受控实验，分析由不可压缩芯层与薄可压缩膜构成的双层圆柱在受拉伸时的近表面张力失稳（near-surface tension instability）。结果显示褶皱幅值与波长依赖包覆层泊松比（Poisson ratio）及膜/基底剪切模量比，且低泊松比使褶皱更早出现，进而催生对负泊松比（auxetic）薄膜的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标为理解双层圆柱在轴向拉伸下外表面出现周向褶皱（circumferential wrinkles）与轴向波前（axial wavefronts）的失稳机制，并探究材料参数（泊松比、剪切模量比）对失稳行为的影响，以寻求调控褶皱出现时机与形态的途径。

Method: 采用建模、数值计算（numerics）、有限元模拟（finite element simulations）与受控实验（controlled experiments）相结合的方式；依据低泊松比促进褶皱的预测，在有限元与实验中引入具有定制微结构的负泊松比薄膜，并制备带刚性负泊松比外包覆层的3D打印软圆柱进行轴向加载。

Result: 褶皱幅值与波长取决于包覆层泊松比以及膜与基底的剪切模量（shear moduli）之比；包覆层泊松比越低，拉伸下褶皱出现越早。模拟预测出现清晰褶皱，但受限于褶皱振幅较小，实验观测更为困难。

Conclusion: 双层圆柱拉伸下的近表面褶皱可通过泊松比与模量比进行调控，负泊松比薄膜有望促进褶皱更早形成；然而褶皱的小幅值导致实验验证仍具挑战性，模拟与实验之间存在观测难度的差距。

Abstract: We study near-surface tension instabilities in bilayered cylinders composed of an incompressible core coated with a thin compressible film. In theory, circumferential wrinkles with axial wavefronts may appear on the outer surface when the cylinder is stretched. We analyze these instabilities through modelling, numerics, finite element simulations, and controlled experiments. We show that the wrinkle amplitude and wavelength depend on the Poisson ratio of the coating and the ratio of the film and substrate shear moduli. We find that the lower the film's Poisson ratio, the earlier the wrinkles emerge under tension. This observation motivates us to use auxetic films (with negative Poisson ratios) with bespoke microstructures in the finite element simulations and the experiments. For the latter, we fabricated 3D-printed soft cylinders with stiff auxetic outer layers and subjected them to axial loading. Although simulations predicted clear wrinkle formation, experimental observation proved more elusive due to the small amplitude of the wrinkles.

</details>


### [232] [Multipartite entanglement spreads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12271)
*Sylvain Carrozza,Johann Chevrier,Luca Lionni*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入纠缠展宽（entanglement spread）的多体推广，以 $D$-边着色图对为索引，构造由两个多体 Rényi 纠缠熵之差构成、且在局域操作下单调的量；并证明超图张量（hypergraph-tensor, HT）态可视为二分平坦态的多体对应物，即可由某固定纠缠展宽的极小化唯一刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分纯态的纠缠结构由纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）或整数阶 Rényi-$k$ 纠缠熵族刻画，而两个此类熵之差定义的纠缠展宽在局域操作下单调、且仅当纠缠谱平坦时为零。作者希望将这一二分框架推广到多体情形，并为已有的一类超图张量态寻找类似的变分刻画。

Method: 对固定份数 $D\geq 3$，以满足特定条件的 $D$-边着色图（$D$-edge-colored graph）对为索引，构造一族映射；每个映射为两个多体 Rényi 纠缠熵之差，各自对应一个局域酉多项式不变量（trace-invariant），并证明其局域操作单调性。随后引入谱超图张量（spectral hypergraph-tensor, SHT）态的新拟设（Ansatz），将 HT 态嵌入其中，并分析 SHT 态在局域酉变换下的轨道；同时计算 Haar 随机态（局域维度 $N$）中若干纠缠展宽的渐近大 $N$ 期望值。

Result: 当 $D=3$ 时，三分纯态为 HT 态当且仅当它极小化某一（无限）族纠缠展宽；对任意 $D\geq 3$，HT 态可被唯一刻画为 SHT 态中某个固定纠缠展宽的极小化者。此外给出了 SHT 态在局域酉变换下的轨道特征，并计算了 Haar 随机态中若干纠缠展宽的渐近大 $N$ 期望值。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多体纠缠展宽的框架，并将 HT 态置于与二分平坦态相对应的地位，同时通过 SHT 态给出更广泛的状态族及其局域酉轨道刻画。

Abstract: The entanglement structure of a bipartite pure state is characterized by its entanglement spectrum, or equivalently, by the family of Rényi-$k$ entanglement entropies with integer $k$. Taking the difference of two such entropies defines a quantity known as an entanglement spread, that is monotonous under local operations, and that vanishes if and only if the entanglement spectrum of the state is flat. We introduce a multipartite generalization of this notion which, for a fixed number $D\geq 3$ of parties, results in a collection of maps indexed by pairs of $D$-edge-colored graphs (obeying some condition). Each member of this collection takes the form of a difference of two multipartite Rényi entanglement entropies, each associated to a local unitary polynomial invariant (known in this context as a trace-invariant), and is shown to be monotonous under local operations. We then argue that a previously introduced family of so-called hypergraph-tensor (HT) states can be understood as a multipartite counterpart to the family of flat bipartite states. Indeed, we first prove that, when $D=3$, a tripartite pure state is HT if and only if it minimizes a particular (infinite) family of entanglement spreads. Second, we embed the set of HT states into the much larger family of spectral hypergraph-tensor (SHT) states, defined by a new Ansatz we introduce. We then prove that HT states can be uniquely characterized as the minimizers of some fixed entanglement spread among SHT states, for arbitrary $D\geq 3$. We also investigate SHT states in their own right: in particular, we characterize their orbits under local unitary transformations. Finally, along the way, the asymptotic large-$N$ expectation values of a number of entanglement spreads are computed in the Haar-random state of local dimension $N$.

</details>


### [233] [Wave packets asymptotics in $κ$-minkowski space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12331)
*Adith A,A. Bhagwat*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文借助奇点理论（singularity theory）研究 $\\kappa$-Minkowski 空间中波包（wave packets）的性质与衰减行为，发现其衰减率依赖系统控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是刻画 $\\kappa$-Minkowski 空间中波包的衰减特性，并将其与交换（commutative）时空中的行为进行区分。

Method: 采用奇点理论工具：用拉格朗日奇点（Lagrangian singularities）与焦散（caustics）理论研究能级流形的拓扑，并用振荡积分（oscillatory integrals）理论中的牛顿多面体（Newton polyhedron）方法分析波包衰减。

Result: 对于一般的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），波包衰减率依控制参数（control parameter）取值而不同：当控制参数位于尖点（cusps）上时，衰减阶数为 $t^{-1/4}$；否则为 $t^{-1/2}$。

Conclusion: $\\kappa$-Minkowski 空间中波包的这种独特行为使其与交换时空存在本质差异。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the nature of wave packets and their decay behavior in the $κ$-Minkowski space. We employ techniques from singularity theory throughout our analysis. Specifically, we use methods from the theory of Lagrangian singularities and caustics to study the topology of the energy-level manifold. The Newton polyhedron method from the theory of oscillatory integrals is applied to analyze the decay behavior of wave packets. We find that for a generic Hamiltonian in the $κ$-Minkowski space, the associated wave packets exhibit different decay rates depending on the control parameter of the system. When the control parameter takes values on the cusp, the system exhibits decay of order $t^{-1/4}$; otherwise, it exhibits decay of order $t^{-1/2}$. This distinctive behavior of wave packets in the $κ$-Minkowski makes it fundamentally different from commutative spacetime.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Three-Point Trace Positivity:Schatten Norm Compression and Interpolating Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11021)
*Trung Dung Vuong,Hiroyuki Osaka*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 论文证明核心不等式：对有限迹冯诺依曼代数（finite tracial von Neumann algebra）中三者矩阵 $[\mathcal K_r(P_i,P_j)]\ge0$ 在 $1/2\le r\le1$ 成立，$1/2<r<1$ 情形即使对矩阵亦为新结果，且阶数三为最优；该机制一举解决 Schatten 范数压缩猜想与半有限迹代数上 $d_t$ 度量分类两个问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于统一处理两个悬而未决的问题：Lin--van den Driessche 关于 Schatten 范数压缩（Schatten norm compression）的猜想，以及 Komálovics 与 Molnár 的 Problem 2，即 Hellinger 与 Bures 端点之间 $1<t<2$ 区间上 $d_t$ 的度量性。二者被认为需要单一机制加以解决。

Method: 方法基于定义在有限迹冯诺依曼代数 $(\mathcal N,\nu)$ 上的核 $\mathcal{K}_r(P,Q)=\nu((P^{1/2}QP^{1/2})^r)$，证明三阶矩阵 $[\mathcal K_r(P_i,P_j)]_{i,j=1}^3$ 在 $1/2\le r\le1$ 时半正定；随后将同一机制应用于算子绝对值的 $p$ 次幂迹不等式与半有限迹代数上的 $d_t$ 距离。

Result: 得到三项结果：其一，对 $H=[H_{ij}]\in M_3(\mathcal R)_+$ 有 $[\omega(|H_{ij}|^p)]\ge0$（$1\le p\le2$），解决 Lin--van den Driessche 猜想；其二，$d_t$ 在 $L^1(\mathcal M,\tau)_+$ 上为完备度量当且仅当 $t\in[1,2]$ 或 $\mathcal M$ 为交换代数，其中 $1<t<2$ 为此前未决区间，且其拓扑与 Cauchy 序列与 $L^1$ 一致；其三，同样分类对含单位迹 $C^*$-代数（unital tracial $C^*$-algebra）成立。

Conclusion: 结论表明，单一核半正定性机制可同时解决 Schatten 范数压缩猜想与 $d_t$ 度量分类问题；结合已知的 $t=0$ 障碍，Komálovics 与 Molnár 的 Problem 2 得到完整解答，并确立阶数三与区间 $[1,2]$ 的最优性。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal N,ν)$ be a finite tracial von Neumann algebra and set
  $\mathcal{K}_r(P,Q)=ν((P^{1/2}QP^{1/2})^r)$. We prove that
  $$
  [\mathcal{K}_r(P_i,P_j)]_{i,j=1}^3\ge0,
  \qquad 1/2\le r\le1.
  $$
  The range $1/2<r<1$ is new even for matrices, and order three is sharp
  for $1/2\le r<1$. This single mechanism resolves two problems. First, if
  $(\mathcal R,ω)$ is finite tracial and
  $H=[H_{ij}]_{i,j=1}^3\in M_3(\mathcal R)_+$, then
  $$
  [ω(|H_{ij}|^p)]_{i,j=1}^3\ge0,
  \qquad 1\le p\le2,
  $$
  settling the Lin--van den Driessche conjecture on Schatten norm
  compression. Second, for a semifinite tracial von Neumann algebra
  $(\mathcal M,τ)$, define
  $$
  d_t(A,B)^2=\frac{τ(A)+τ(B)}2-
  \|B^{t/4}A^{t/4}\|_{2/t}^{2/t}.
  $$
  We prove that $d_t$ is a complete metric on $L^1(\mathcal M,τ)_+$ for
  $1\le t\le2$, including the previously open range $1<t<2$ between the
  Hellinger and Bures endpoints, and that
  $$
  d_t\text{ is a metric on }L^1(\mathcal M,τ)_+
  \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
  t\in[1,2]\ \text{or}\ \mathcal M\text{ is abelian}.
  $$
  Its topology and Cauchy sequences agree with those of $L^1$. The same
  classification holds for unital tracial $C^*$-algebras and, with the known
  $t=0$ obstruction, resolves Problem~2 of Komálovics and Molnár.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Kinetic Reduction and Hydrodynamics under Time-Dependent Helical Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10605)
*Qingyun Zeng,Fan Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在目标分子动力学（objective molecular dynamics）框架下，为具有时变螺旋对称性（time-dependent helical symmetry）的稀薄气体推导约化玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation），并通过流体力学展开得到欧拉（Euler）与纳维-斯托克斯-傅里叶（Navier-Stokes-Fourier）方程，进而考察圆柱几何与轴向变形对输运性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移对称流已被动力学与连续介质框架充分研究，但螺旋流中微观动力学与连续介质行为之间的联系尚不完善。本文旨在弥补这一空白，刻画螺旋对称下微观动力学如何映射到连续介质描述。

Method: 采用目标分子动力学框架，在螺旋对称下保留径向变化并保持经典碰撞算子，推导约化玻尔兹曼方程；随后通过形式化的流体力学展开，导出相应的欧拉方程与纳维-斯托克斯-傅里叶方程。

Result: 所得模型揭示了圆柱几何与轴向变形对黏性输运和热传导的影响，以及变形如何在与碰撞弛豫（collisional relaxation）竞争下产生沿轴与跨轴的不等应力。与直接模拟蒙特卡洛（direct simulation Monte Carlo）解的比较表明，一阶流体修正可捕捉主导的应力各向异性并改进径向温度变化的预测，而较大克努森数（Knudsen number）下该近似存在局限。

Conclusion: 螺旋对称下微观动力学与连续介质输运之间存在可系统建立的对应关系；一阶流体模型在中等克努森数范围内有效，但在较大克努森数时近似失效。该工作为螺旋流的连续介质建模提供了从微观出发的推导路径。

Abstract: Translational and helical symmetries provide idealized descriptions of many flows encountered in nature and engineering. While translationally symmetric flows have been extensively studied within kinetic and continuum frameworks, the corresponding connection between microscopic dynamics and continuum behaviour for helical flows is less developed. Here, we investigate this connection for a dilute gas with time-dependent helical symmetry. Using the objective molecular dynamics framework, we derive a reduced Boltzmann equation that retains radial variations while preserving the classical collision operator under helical symmetry. Formal hydrodynamic expansions then yield the corresponding Euler and Navier-Stokes-Fourier equations. The resulting models describe how cylindrical geometry and axial deformation influence transport, including viscous transport and heat conduction, and how deformation generates unequal stresses along and across the axis in competition with collisional relaxation. Comparisons with direct simulation Monte Carlo solutions examine both spatially uniform and radially varying flows. In the cases studied, the first-order fluid corrections capture the leading stress anisotropy and improve the prediction of radial temperature variations, while larger Knudsen numbers expose limitations of the approximation.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Frame-dependent coherence of Gaussian quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05623)
*Nicolae Cotfas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用所有正则相干态（canonical coherent states）构成的紧框架（tight frame）描述连续变量高斯态（Gaussian states），并据此给出混合高斯态框架相关相干性（frame-dependent coherence）的显式公式，同时构造出快速收敛的离散变量近似态序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）通常以正交归一基描述，而更一般的描述可用紧框架；连续变量高斯态的相干性通常用厄米-高斯基（Hermite-Gauss basis）刻画，该文意在寻找一种更简洁的替代描述。

Method: 采用所有正则相干态构成的紧框架来描述高斯态，并将该方法与基于厄米-高斯基的描述进行对比；对离散情形，则相对于所有离散相干态（discrete coherent states）构成的紧框架测量相干性。

Result: 相干态表述比厄米-高斯基表述更简洁，由此得到混合高斯态的框架相关相干性关于协方差矩阵（covariance matrix）的行列式与迹的显式公式；并针对每个混合高斯态构造一列离散变量量子态，在希尔伯特空间维度足够大（15 及以上）时，在纯度（purity）与相干性上快速收敛于该高斯态。

Conclusion: 以相干态紧框架描述高斯态可简化相干性刻画，并给出依赖协方差矩阵行列式与迹的显式结果，同时提供由离散变量量子态序列逼近连续变量高斯态的有效途径。

Abstract: A Hilbert space is usually described using an orthonormal basis, but a more general description can be defined using a tight frame. In the case of continuous-variable Gaussian states, the coherence is usually described using the Hermite-Gauss basis. We present an alternative description using the tight frame of all canonical coherent states. The description of Gaussian states in terms of coherent states is simpler than in terms of Hermite-Gauss basis, and this allows us to obtain an explicit formula describing the frame-dependent coherence of a mixed Gaussian state as a function of the determinant and the trace of covariance matrix. For each mixed Gaussian state, we construct a sequence of discrete-variable quantum states rapidly convergent to it (as concern the purity and coherence) when the dimension of Hilbert space becomes large enough (15 or more). In the discrete case, the coherence is measured with respect to the tight frame of all discrete coherent states.

</details>


### [237] [Geometry-optimized hyperbolic codes for modular fault-tolerant quantum architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10948)
*Ahmed Adel Mahmoud,Steven Rayan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出有限族双曲表面码（hyperbolic surface codes），利用其最优效率标度，通过优化周期识别使码距翻倍、效率提升四倍，并引入拓扑感知路由算法实现模块化布局，电路级模拟表明其具备可行的纠错阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现量子容错（quantum fault tolerance）需在编码率（encoding rate）、码距（code distance）、校验权重（parity-check weights）、码平面性（code planarity）与路由（routing）等多重理论及实验参数间权衡优化，而表面码（surface codes）因其几何结构与实验可行性成为有力候选，但其效率仍有提升空间。

Method: 构造有限族双曲表面码，利用有界局部几何下表面码可达的最优效率标度 $kd^2/n = C(\log k)^2$；通过优化周期识别（periodic identifications）提升码距；并引入拓扑感知（topology-aware）算法，将双曲码划分为有界平面模块以降低长程通信需求。

Result: 优化周期识别可在固定量子比特数、编码率与校验权重下使码距翻倍，效率提升四倍；电路级蒙特卡洛（circuit-level Monte Carlo）模拟给出模块化与整体式布局的有限尺寸阈值估计，显示三倍化长程门错误概率会轻微降低但不消除纠错阈值。

Conclusion: 结果表明双曲表面码是具有结构化、高效率特征的大规模量子纠错码（quantum error correction codes），可支撑未来容错架构的实现。

Abstract: Quantum fault tolerance is a prerequisite to harness the full potential of quantum computers. However, achieving fault tolerance remains a major challenge since it requires optimizing tradeoffs between several intertwined theoretical and experimental parameters, including the encoding rate, code distance, parity-check weights, code planarity, and routing. Surface codes remain strong candidates for fault tolerance due to their simple geometric structure, established decoding methods, locality, and experimental attainability. In this work, we present finite families of hyperbolic surface codes that leverage the optimal efficiency scaling $kd^2/n = C (\log{k})^2$ attainable by surface codes with bounded local geometry. We show that optimizing the periodic identifications can double the code distance and thereby increase the code's efficiency by a factor of four, at fixed qubit count, encoding rate, and parity-check weight. We also address the routing problem of hyperbolic surface codes by introducing a topology-aware algorithm that partitions a hyperbolic code into bounded planar modules while reducing long-range communications demand. Circuit-level Monte Carlo simulations yield finite-size threshold estimates for both modular and monolithic layouts, showing that tripling the long-range gate error probability mildly lowers, but does not eliminate, the estimated error-correction thresholds. Together, these results strengthen the case for hyperbolic surface codes as structured and highly efficient large-scale quantum error correction codes for future fault-tolerant architectures.

</details>


### [238] [Mutually unbiased bases: common states, completion, and extension obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12180)
*Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究哪些纯态在多个相互无偏测量（mutually unbiased measurements）中给出均匀结果，以及这些态何时能构成新的测量基。作者区分了共同无偏态、其正交归一完备化与进一步扩展，并在维度 2m 与六维情形给出显式的不可扩展性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是理解共同无偏态（common unbiased states）的结构及其能否扩展为新的相互无偏基（mutually unbiased bases）。这一问题的背景在于相互无偏基的全局最大数目与三元组分类尚未解决，作者希望通过局部构造与不可扩展性刻画推进相关认识。

Method: 方法上，作者考察维度 2m 中的相位变形 Fourier 对（phase-deformed Fourier pair），利用时钟对称性（clock symmetry）与斜反酉（skew antiunitary）将给定共同无偏态完备为显式正交归一基。在六维中引入幅度轮廓障碍（amplitude-profile obstruction）；同时研究保持代数重数的辅助复方程、六阶平坦酉（flat unitary）的射影射线概形、临界代数恒等式，以及离散赋值环（discrete valuation ring）上的特化。

Result: 主要结果包括：六维中当给定相互无偏三元组的第三基射线在三元时钟（ternary clock）下不变时，幅度轮廓障碍证明其强不可扩展性；给出精确非乘积例子及单 Fourier 频率局域的伴随应用。对固定参考矩阵某闭逐元素邻域内的每个精确 Hadamard 矩阵，坐标基与 Hadamard 基恰有 48 条共同无偏射线但不存在第三测量基，并给出每个六列候选平方 Gram 缺陷的定量下界。此外，六阶平坦酉在三阶子式非零时射影射线概形有限，每个奇异二阶子式贡献两个约化孤立边界点，仿射坐标代数在 C 上有限维；各阶临界代数恒等式保持重数，六维中给出固定相位纤维的有限性。

Conclusion: 作者强调这些结果并不确定相互无偏基的全局最大数目，也不对全部三元组进行分类，而是提供局部的不可扩展性判据、代数有限性与重数保持的结构性结论。非物理的 Tao/Potts 集合被用来区分代数解与物理解。

Abstract: Which pure states give uniform outcomes in several mutually unbiased measurements, and when can they form another measurement basis? We distinguish common unbiased states, their orthonormal completion, and further extension. For a displayed phase-deformed Fourier pair in dimension $2m$, a clock symmetry and skew antiunitary complete every prescribed common unbiased state to an explicit orthonormal basis. In dimension six, an amplitude-profile obstruction proves strong unextendibility of a given mutually unbiased triple when its original third-basis rays are invariant under the ternary clock. We give an exact nonproduct example and an application to a companion whose columns are localized at single Fourier frequencies. For every exact Hadamard matrix in a specified closed entrywise neighborhood of a fixed reference matrix, the coordinate and Hadamard bases have exactly 48 common unbiased rays but no third measurement basis, with a quantitative lower bound on the squared Gram defect of every six-column candidate.
  We also study auxiliary complex equations for common states, retaining algebraic multiplicities. For a flat unitary of order six with all $3\times3$ minors nonzero, the projective ray scheme is finite; each singular two-minor contributes two reduced isolated boundary points, and the affine coordinate algebra is finite-dimensional over $\mathbb{C}$. In every order, a critical-algebra identity preserves multiplicities; in dimension six it gives finiteness for a fixed phase fibre. Specialization over a discrete valuation ring controls complete reciprocal-orthogonal collections. A nonphysical Tao/Potts collection separates algebraic from physical solutions. These results do not determine the global maximum number of mutually unbiased bases or classify all triples.

</details>


### [239] [Quartic Certificates for Pure-State Tomography with Pauli Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12320)
*Hengjian Li,Baisong Sun,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在仅测量部分非恒等泡利可观测量时，纯态能否被唯一确定，并给出基于四次对称多项式的可检验判据。核心结论：n≥3 时若被省略的泡利可观测量中不存在四个两两对易且乘积为 ±I 的算符，则所有纯态均满足 UDA；三比特情形该条件对 UDP 亦为充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整泡利基层析（Pauli-basis tomography）需测量所有非恒等泡利可观测量，成本随比特数增长。若已知态为纯态，可省略部分可观测量而保持唯一性，但何种省略仍能保证唯一性缺乏结构性判据，故需研究 UDP 与 UDA 两类唯一性。

Method: 将省略的泡利可观测量取实线性张成，考察其中算符的第四初等对称多项式，并由此导出四次唯一性证书（quartic uniqueness certificate）与一个精确的四次恒等式，用于判定纯态在纯态集合或全态集合中的唯一性。

Result: 对 n≥3 的 n 比特系统，只要被省略的泡利可观测量中不含四个两两对易且乘积为 ±I 的不同算符，则每个纯态均为 UDA。三比特情形下该条件对 UDP 亦为必要条件，从而给出 UDP 与 UDA 的精确刻画；此外，当被省略的泡利可观测量两两对易时，任意比特数下 UDP 与 UDA 一致。

Conclusion: 研究提供了可直接检验的结构性判据，用于判定受限泡利测量下纯态层析的唯一性，将唯一性问题归结为被省略可观测量之间的对易与乘积结构。

Abstract: Full Pauli-basis tomography of an arbitrary $n$-qubit state uses the expectation values of all nonidentity Pauli observables. For multiqubit systems, prior knowledge that the state is pure allows some of these observables to be omitted while retaining uniqueness. We study two resulting notions of uniqueness: whether every pure state is uniquely determined among pure states (UDP), and whether every pure state is uniquely determined among all states, including mixed states (UDA). We derive quartic uniqueness certificates from the fourth elementary symmetric polynomial of operators in the real span of the omitted Pauli observables. An exact quartic identity shows that, for $n$-qubit systems with $n\geq 3$, every pure state is UDA whenever the omitted Pauli observables contain no four distinct operators that commute pairwise and multiply to $\pm I$. For three qubits, this condition is also necessary for UDP, yielding an exact characterization of both UDP and UDA. We further prove that UDP and UDA coincide for any number of qubits whenever the omitted Pauli observables commute pairwise. These results provide directly checkable structural criteria for certifying pure-state tomography from restricted Pauli measurements.

</details>


### [240] [Spin-outcome correlations and memory-assisted entropic uncertainty in Bhabha scattering with an entangled spectator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11629)
*Zan Cao,Meng-Long Song,Xue-Ke Song,Liu Ye,Dong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究树级 Bhabha 散射（tree-level Bhabha scattering）中的自旋-结果关联（spin-outcome correlations）与记忆辅助熵不确定度（memory-assisted entropic uncertainty），揭示初态 B-C 纠缠可通过增强旁观电子 C 的关联来降低高能背散射区域的总体不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于区分量子记忆（quantum memory）中“相互作用粒子”与“旁观粒子”在散射过程中所扮演的不同角色，并厘清测量结果随机性变化与其与各记忆关联性变化之间的区别。

Method: 针对固定角度后选择（post-selected）末态，比较电子 A 上三种替代泡利测量（Pauli measurements）下的总记忆辅助不确定度 U_A 与 A|BC 划分下的纠缠熵 S(ρ_A)；将 U_A 分解为 U_B 与 U_C，并计算相应的结果-记忆互信息（mutual-information）之和 G_B 与 G_C，辅以熵完备互补性（entropic complete complementarity）分析。

Result: 在所研究参数区域内，A|BC 纠缠增强伴随 U_A 降低，尤其在高能背散射（high-energy backscattering）附近最为显著；该极限的解析处理表明 B 从 AC 中因子化，G_B 趋于零，而 G_C 随初始 B-C 纠缠增大。

Conclusion: 初态 B-C 纠缠可通过增强自旋-结果与旁观者 C 的关联来降低高能背散射下的总记忆辅助不确定度，从而确立相互作用粒子与旁观粒子作为量子记忆的各自不同作用。

Abstract: We investigate spin-outcome correlations and memory-assisted entropic uncertainty in tree-level Bhabha scattering, where an incident electron $A$ scatters with a positron $B$ initially entangled with a spectator electron $C$. For the fixed-angle post-selected final state, we compare the total memory-assisted uncertainty $U_A$ for three alternative Pauli measurements on $A$ with the entanglement entropy $S(ρ_A)$ across the $A|BC$ partition. We resolve $U_A$ into the separate uncertainties $U_B$ and $U_C$ and evaluate the corresponding outcome-memory mutual-information sums $G_B$ and $G_C$. This separates changes in the randomness of the outcomes on $A$ from changes in their correlations with each memory. In the parameter regions studied, enhanced $A|BC$ entanglement is accompanied by lower $U_A$, most prominently near high-energy backscattering. An analytic treatment of this limit shows that $B$ factorizes from $AC$: $G_B$ vanishes, whereas $G_C$ increases with the initial $B$-$C$ entanglement. An entropic complete complementarity analysis characterizes the accompanying loss of local helicity predictability in $ρ_A$ and changes in the coherence, predictability, and mixedness of the interacting pair $ρ_{AB}$. These results show that initial $B$-$C$ entanglement can lower the total memory-assisted uncertainty in high-energy backscattering by strengthening spin-outcome correlations with the spectator $C$, thereby identifying the distinct roles of the interacting particle and the spectator as quantum memories.

</details>


### [241] [Quantum non-Markovian response spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10684)
*Aaron Sander,John F. Kam,Gauthameshwar S.,Robert Wille,Kavan Modi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出响应矩阵（response matrix）框架，仅通过对量子系统施加受控干预并观测系统本身，揭示环境记忆（environmental memory）的时间结构；无需直接测量或层析（tomography）环境即可刻画记忆的持续性与结构，并在相互作用自旋过程（interacting spin process）与两比特基准（two-qubit benchmark）中加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统难以与周围环境隔离，隐藏环境可保留并返还信息，导致量子计算中的关联错误（correlated errors）并干扰实验解读；即使量子比特已重置，先前任务仍可能在环境留下影响后续任务的痕迹。直接测量环境通常不可行，而通过层析完整重构其效应又迅速变得不切实际，因此亟需一种仅依赖系统观测来刻画隐藏影响的方法。

Method: 引入响应矩阵，利用受控干预（controlled interventions）与系统观测记录未来观测在不同受控历史下的变化；通过其谱（spectrum）刻画记忆的结构及在指定系统重置下的持续性，并用其元素的适当线性组合排除特定经典记忆模型；由于历史—未来查询可独立评估，数值响应矩阵可并行组装。框架在理论上建立，并在相互作用自旋过程中刻画响应谱、在两比特基准中认证量子记忆（quantum memory）。

Result: 在理论上确立了该框架；在相互作用自旋过程中刻画了响应谱；并在两比特基准中认证了量子记忆。响应矩阵的谱可表征记忆结构与在指定重置下的持续性，特定线性组合可排除指定经典记忆模型，且其历史—未来查询支持并行计算。

Conclusion: 该方法将系统观测转化为探测不可及环境的工具，有望支持关联噪声（correlated noise）诊断、开放系统模型检验以及记忆感知控制设计（memory-aware control design）。

Abstract: Quantum systems are rarely isolated from their surroundings. These hidden environments can retain information and return it later, producing correlated errors in quantum computers and complicating the interpretation of experiments.On a quantum processor, for example, one job can therefore leave an environmental trace that affects the next, even after the qubits themselves have been reset. Learning how this hidden influence persists and affects later behavior is therefore important, but the environment is often impossible to measure directly, and fully reconstructing its effects through tomography rapidly becomes impractical. Here, we introduce a response matrix that uses controlled interventions and observations of the system alone to reveal the temporal structure of environmental memory. The matrix records how future observations change across different controlled histories. Its spectrum characterizes how memory is structured and how it persists under specified system resets, while suitable linear combinations of its entries can rule out specified classical models of that memory. Because its history-future queries can be evaluated independently, numerical response matrices can be assembled in parallel. We establish the framework theoretically, characterize response spectra in an interacting spin process, and certify quantum memory in a two-qubit benchmark. This approach turns system observations into a probe of inaccessible environments and could support the diagnosis of correlated noise, tests of open system models, and memory-aware control design.

</details>


### [242] [On linearity or non-linearity in machine learning for quantum chaotic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10697)
*Francesco Perciavalle,Agostino Gallo,Francesco Plastina,Gianluigi Greco,Nicola Lo Gullo,Carlo Adornetto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将混沌量子多体动力学（chaotic quantum many-body dynamics）预测形式化为时间序列预测（time-series forecasting）问题，以可由里德堡原子阵列（Rydberg-atom arrays）实现的少比特 PXP 链为基准，比较非线性 Transformer 与线性 DLinear 模型。结果显示 DLinear 在全初始态范围内保持高精度，而 Transformer 在接近疤痕（scarring）极限时性能下降，表明复杂量子演化未必对应同等复杂的预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法模拟混沌量子多体动力学面临重大计算挑战，原因在于演化过程中纠缠（entanglement）的快速积累与空间扩散。这引出机器学习（machine learning）能否作为预测量子动力学的有效替代方案这一问题。

Method: 将量子动力学表述为时间序列预测问题，采用可经里德堡原子阵列实现的少比特 PXP 链作为基准。通过改变初始态使系统覆盖从遍历（ergodic）行为到量子多体疤痕（quantum many-body scarring）的不同动力学区间，并对比两种架构：表达力较强的非线性 Transformer 与简单线性预测模型 DLinear。

Result: Transformer 在较遍历的区间能准确预测动力学，但随着初始态接近疤痕极限，其性能逐步退化。相反，DLinear 在整个初始态族上保持准确，其主要偏差为小幅高频振荡，对整体预测误差影响很小。

Conclusion: 结果表明，由复杂量子多体动力学产生的可观测量（observables）可通过过去到未来观测的简单线性映射实现高精度预测，说明底层量子演化的复杂性不必转化为同等复杂的预测问题。

Abstract: Accurately simulating chaotic quantum many-body dynamics remains a major computational challenge for classical methods, due to the rapid buildup and spatial spreading of entanglement during the evolution. This raises the question of whether machine learning can provide an effective alternative for predicting quantum dynamics. We address this question by formulating quantum dynamics as a time-series forecasting problem, using a few-qubit PXP chain, realizable with Rydberg-atom arrays, as a benchmark. By varying the initial state, the system spans dynamical regimes ranging from ergodic behavior to quantum many-body scarring, providing a controlled setting for testing forecasting models across qualitatively different dynamics. We compare two contrasting architectures: an expressive nonlinear Transformer and DLinear, a simple linear forecasting model. The Transformer accurately predicts dynamics in the more ergodic regime, but its performance progressively deteriorates as the initial state approaches the scarred limit. In contrast, DLinear remains accurate across the entire family of initial states, with its main deviations consisting of small high-frequency oscillations that have little effect on the overall prediction error. Remarkably, these results show that observables generated by complex quantum many-body dynamics can be forecast with high accuracy through a simple linear mapping from past to future observations. This reveals that the complexity of the underlying quantum evolution need not translate into an equally complex forecasting problem.

</details>


### [243] [Coherence-assisted quantum state preparation and thermal control through repeated interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10794)
*Alessandro Prositto,Dvira Segal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在马尔可夫重复相互作用框架下，研究量子比特（qubit）通过能量守恒翻转-翻转（flip-flop）相互作用与相干环境耦合时的弛豫动力学与稳态，给出了对环境相干性非微扰的精确解析解，并证明环境相干性可作为制备耗散态、调控热流的资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注非相干环境对量子系统耗散动力学的影响，而相干环境作为资源在稳态制备与热力学调控中的作用尚待系统刻画。本文旨在揭示环境相干性（environmental coherence）如何扩展可达稳态集合并改变热传递行为。

Method: 采用马尔可夫重复相互作用框架（Markovian repeated-interaction framework），考虑量子比特与相干辅助系统（ancilla）间能量守恒的翻转-翻转相互作用（flip-flop interaction），推导演化态与稳态的精确解析解，该解对环境相干性非微扰（nonperturbative）有效；并进一步开展协议的热力学分析。

Result: 环境相干性扩大了可达稳态集合，可提升系统基态布居数至超过热浴水平（对应低于热浴温度的有效温度），并增强稳态纯度；这些效应受系统能标与系统-辅助系统耦合强度之间相互作用的控制。同时证明系统纯度始终不超过辅助系统纯度。热力学分析显示环境相干性可改变甚至逆转自然热传递方向，产生反常热流（anomalous heat flow）；在固定相互作用参数下，热传递方向由系统与辅助系统相干性的相对相位决定，而其幅度决定交换热量多少。

Conclusion: 结果确立环境相干性作为重复相互作用动力学中控制稳态制备与能量传递的一种资源，并揭示其在耗散态工程与热流调控中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate the relaxation dynamics and steady state of a qubit interacting with a coherent environment through an energy-conserving flip-flop interaction within the Markovian repeated-interaction framework. We derive an exact analytical solution for the evolving and stationary state, nonperturbative in the environmental coherence. We show that environmental coherence provides a resource for dissipative state preparation by expanding the set of accessible steady states. In particular, coherent ancillas can increase the system ground- state population beyond that of the bath, corresponding to an effective temperature below the bath temperature, and enhance its steady-state purity, with these effects controlled by the interplay between the system energy scale and the system-ancilla coupling. Nevertheless, we prove that the purity of the system can never exceed the purity of the ancilla. We further analyze the thermodynamics of the protocol and show that environmental coherence can modify and even reverse the natural direction of heat transfer, giving rise to anomalous heat flow. Remarkably, for fixed interaction parameters, the direction of the coherence-induced heat transfer is governed by the relative phase between the system and ancilla coherence, whereas their magnitudes determine the amount of heat exchanged. Our results establish environmental coherence as a resource for controlling steady-state preparation and energy transfer in repeated-interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [244] [Helicity decoupling ultrastrong Rabi splitting from quantum vacuum dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10944)
*Ayoub El-Amrani,Nasir Boubekraoui,Zakaria Mzaouali,Houssam Sabri,Herschel Rabitz,Abdelouahed El Fatimy,Dukhyung Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究证明，在支持单一圆偏振模式的手性腔（chiral cavity）中，通过调控回旋共振（cyclotron resonance）与腔场螺旋性（helicity）的匹配关系，可使极化激元（polaritons）与量子真空修饰（quantum vacuum dressing）相互独立地被控制。螺旋性因此成为在腔量子材料（cavity quantum materials）中分别调控极化激元形成与量子关联的手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化激元与量子真空修饰通常被视为超强光-物质耦合（ultrastrong light-matter coupling）相互交织的两种后果，二者能否被独立控制尚不明确。该工作旨在检验光谱杂化（spectral hybridization）与真空修饰是否为超强耦合中彼此可分离的表现。

Method: 考虑与手性腔中单一圆偏振模式耦合的回旋共振体系。通过反转磁场方向改变回旋运动方向而保持腔不变，从而改变腔场螺旋性与回旋运动的匹配关系，对比分析共振交换过程与反常成对产生/湮灭过程两种耦合情形。

Result: 当腔场螺旋性与回旋运动匹配时，光与物质共振交换能量，产生大拉比劈裂（Rabi splitting）与常规极化激元，而基态仍为裸真空。反转磁场后，同一模式通过成对产生和湮灭光与物质激发的反常过程耦合：常规极化激元反交叉消失，同时基态获得压缩（squeezing）与虚激发。

Conclusion: 光谱杂化与量子真空修饰是超强耦合的两种不同表现形式，大拉比劈裂本身并不意味真空被修饰。螺旋性为将极化激元形成与量子关联作为不同资源加以控制提供了途径。

Abstract: Polaritons and quantum vacuum dressing are usually regarded as intertwined consequences of ultrastrong light-matter coupling. Here we show that they can be controlled independently through helicity. We consider cyclotron resonance coupled to a chiral cavity supporting a single circular mode. When the helicity of the cavity field matches the cyclotron motion, energy is exchanged resonantly between light and matter, producing a large Rabi splitting and conventional polaritons, while the ground state remains the bare vacuum. Reversing the magnetic field reverses the cyclotron motion without changing the cavity. The same mode then couples through anomalous processes that create and annihilate light and matter excitations in pairs: the conventional polaritonic anticrossing disappears, while the ground state acquires squeezing and virtual excitations. These two regimes show that spectral hybridization and quantum vacuum dressing are distinct manifestations of ultrastrong coupling, and that a large Rabi splitting alone does not imply a dressed quantum vacuum. Helicity therefore provides a route to controlling polariton formation and quantum correlations as distinct resources in cavity quantum materials.

</details>


### [245] [Analog quantum simulation of $φ^4$ field theory with a superconducting transmission line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11071)
*Ilan T. Rosen,Neill C. Warrington,Max Hays,Christopher McNally,Joshua Lin,Stephen Sorokanich,Julian Bender*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用超导传输线（superconducting transmission line）对（1+1）维 φ⁴ 场论进行类比量子模拟（analog quantum simulation），通过调控电路参数实现单势阱与双势阱两种区间，分别用于粒子散射与拓扑孤子碰撞实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用量子场（interacting quantum fields）的动力学研究仍是基础性难题，需要可实时、空间分辨探测的模拟平台。

Method: 传输线由类磁通子（fluxonium-like）电路元件构成，其连续无界相位变量编码标量场且无希尔伯特空间截断（Hilbert-space truncation）；非谐势（anharmonic potentials）提供强相互作用。通过调节电路参数获得两种区间，并用格点蒙特卡洛（lattice Monte Carlo）计算比较电路与格点 φ⁴ 理论的低能谱以验证对应关系，同时给出态制备与测量方案。

Result: 第一参数区间对应单势阱 φ⁴ 理论，可开展反向传播粒子波包间的散射实验；第二参数区间给出双势阱势，对应对称破缺相（symmetry-broken phase）并具简并真空（degenerate vacua），真空界面形成拓扑扭结（topological kinks），可模拟扭结-反扭结碰撞。

Conclusion: 该方案可在相互作用量子场论中实现粒子与孤子（soliton）散射的空间分辨、实时研究。

Abstract: Understanding the dynamics of interacting quantum fields remains a fundamental challenge. We propose a superconducting transmission line for analog quantum simulation of $(1+1)$-dimensional $φ^4$ field theory. The transmission line comprises fluxonium-like circuit elements whose continuous and unbounded phase variables encode a scalar field without Hilbert-space truncation. Their anharmonic potentials provide strong interactions. Tuning the circuit parameters provides access to two distinct regimes. In the first, the transmission line emulates $φ^4$ field theory with a single-well potential. We confirm this correspondence through lattice Monte Carlo calculations of the low-energy spectra of both the circuit and lattice $φ^4$ theory. This regime enables scattering experiments between counter-propagating particle wave packets. The second parameter set yields a double-well potential that emulates $φ^4$ theory in the symmetry-broken phase and hosts degenerate vacua. Interfaces between the two vacua form topological kinks, and the transmission line can simulate kink-antikink collisions. We present state preparation and measurement protocols for both collision experiments, enabling spatially resolved, real-time studies of particle and soliton scattering in an interacting quantum field theory.

</details>


### [246] [Exact polariton-condensate states via nonlinear Stark coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11234)
*DaKai He abd Zhi Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究耦合腔（含/不含二能级原子）构成的杂化系统，借助非线性斯塔克耦合（nonlinear Stark coupling）构建可解析处理的多体本征态集合，揭示其在混沌谱中表现为量子疤痕（quantum scars），而在基态时表现为极化激元凝聚（polariton condensates），并给出斯塔克耦合对莫特绝缘体-超流转变的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意晶格几何与原子分布下寻找可解析处理的多体本征态，并理解光-物质杂化系统中非热化态与相干量子相的统一描述。

Method: 采用由非线性斯塔克耦合促成的“构建块”（building-block）构造方法，识别出一族精确多体本征态；结合局域激发数涨落的数值计算。

Result: 这些精确态依光谱位置呈现双重物理性质：嵌入混沌多体谱时表现为违反本征态热化假设（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis）并发生弱遍历性破缺的量子疤痕；在固定总激发数扇区作为基态时则表现出非对角长程序（off-diagonal long-range order）并形成极化激元凝聚。数值结果还表明负斯塔克耦合使莫特绝缘体到超流的转变移向更弱的腔间跃迁。

Conclusion: 该结果为在多种光-物质架构中工程化相干量子相与非热化态提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We study a hybrid system of coupled cavities, with or without two-level atoms doped inside. By employing a building-block construction facilitated by nonlinear Stark coupling, we identify a manifold of exact many-body eigenstates that remain analytically tractable across arbitrary lattice geometries and atom distributions. We demonstrate that these exact states exhibit a dual physical nature depending on their spectral positioning: when embedded within the chaotic many-body spectrum, they manifest as quantum scars that violate the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis and undergo weak ergodicity breaking; conversely, as ground states within fixed-total-excitation sectors, they exhibit off-diagonal long-range order and form polariton condensates. Furthermore, numerical calculations of local excitation-number fluctuations suggest that negative Stark coupling shifts the Mott-insulator to superfluid transition toward weaker inter-cavity hopping. Our findings provide a versatile framework for engineering both coherent quantum phases and non-thermalizing states in diverse light-matter architectures.

</details>


### [247] [Topological Superradiant Universality Classes in Cavity-Mediated Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11495)
*Yunhao Liu,Sheng Yang,Mingyuan Chen,Xue-Jia Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一类新的超辐射临界性（superradiant criticality），其在拓扑上与已知的超辐射相变（superradiant phase transition）本质不同，表现为临界线上存在受对称性保护的边缘简并（edge degeneracy），且在不能破坏保护对称性或穿越多临界点（multicritical point）的情况下无法与拓扑平庸的对应相变平滑连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超辐射相变（superradiant phase transition）是光-物质耦合系统（light-matter coupled systems）中的基本现象。已有研究已识别出拓扑平庸的超辐射相变，然而是否以及如何存在拓扑上不同的新一类超辐射临界性（superradiant criticality）尚不明确。

Method: 以腔介导的团簇-伊辛链（cavity-mediated cluster-Ising chains）为示例模型，通过数值方法研究分隔超辐射相与腔拓扑相（cavity topological phase）的临界线，并进一步推广至腔介导的 $\mathbb{Z}_{n}^{}$ 团簇-qudit 链，辅以晶格模拟（lattice simulations）加以验证。

Result: 分隔超辐射相与腔拓扑相的临界线承载了稳健的对称性保护边缘简并，这是传统超辐射相变所不具备的特征。尽管其局域体临界性质（local bulk critical properties）与已知的拓扑平庸超辐射相变无法区分，但二者若要保持保护对称性、且不经过多临界点，则无法平滑连接。该拓扑超辐射临界性具有普适性，也出现在腔介导的 $\mathbb{Z}_{n}^{}$ 团簇-qudit 链中。

Conclusion: 研究表明存在一类拓扑上区别于已知超辐射相变的新型超辐射临界性，其核心判据在于临界线上的对称性保护边缘简并；该现象不限于单一模型，而是可推广至更一般的 $\mathbb{Z}_{n}^{}$ 团簇-qudit 链，具有普遍性。

Abstract: Superradiant phase transitions are fundamental phenomena in light-matter coupled systems. Here, we uncover a novel class of superradiant criticality that is topologically distinct from previously known superradiant transitions. Using cavity-mediated cluster-Ising chains as an illustrative example, we numerically demonstrate that the critical line separating the superradiant phase from the cavity topological phase hosts robust symmetry-protected edge degeneracies, a feature absent in conventional superradiant transitions. Remarkably, although its local bulk critical properties are indistinguishable from those of the previously identified topologically trivial superradiant transition, the two criticalities cannot be smoothly connected without breaking the protecting symmetries or passing through a multicritical point. We further show that such topological superradiant criticality is generic, also occurring in cavity-mediated $\mathbb{Z}_{n}^{}$ cluster-qudit chains, as supported by complementary lattice simulations.

</details>


### [248] [Theory of the CH$_\text{S}^-$ Defect in MoS$_2$ Confirming the Origin of the Quantum Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12283)
*Chanaprom Cholsuk,Tobias Vogl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对单层MoS$_2$/hBN(2L)/石墨烯体系中约1.8 eV发射源自CH$_\mathrm{S}^{-}$缺陷的实验指认，开展了系统的第一性原理光物理描述，证明相邻层的存在对声子边带与发光线型起决定性作用，并首次在理论上重现了实验观测的发射线形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中局域发射的微观起源仍需实验与理论的一致印证。近期STM/STS-STML测量将单层MoS$_2$/hBN(2L)/石墨烯中约1.8 eV的发射归因于带负电的CH$_\mathrm{S}^{-}$缺陷，但该发射体缺乏完整的理论描述。

Method: 构建了CH$_\mathrm{S}^{-}$缺陷的理论描述，并系统计算其在单层MoS$_2$、两种双层构型以及MoS$_2$/hBN异质结中的光物理性质，涵盖电子跃迁、零声子线（zero-phonon line）、电子-声子耦合、辐射寿命与跃迁偶极取向。
